le/mbht wl_/kwos mu/Hsm_. mu/Hs we/rh1t. sh1dhi/ me. h1yo/d h2we/ksoh2 me/g'h2s h1e/soh2 h2e/k'h3esi-kwe me. mu/Hs moy h1e/h3si h1e/si lewbho/s-kwe moy h1e/si. me/g'h2m_ ne wo/ydh2e nu gwr_h3e/t to/m. so/ wl_/kwosyo h1e/h3si lewbho/s h1e/sti me/g'h2s-kwe lewbho/s ne h1e/sti.

we/g'het ne/h2us h2ne/rn_s mre/y nu dhg'hu/Hs ta:/m wide/t. wo/kwet se/h2 to/smey. dhg'hu/H h1eg'o/h2 h2ne/rn_s we/g'hoh2 tu/H-kwe to/ns ne we/g'hesi. ne/h2u wo/dr_ twe/ h1me/-kwe bhe/reti. wo/dr_ to/m bhe/reti h1yo/s bhe/reti.

k'e/yo sh2imo:/ h1ime/ni nu ma/rkos to/m spe/k'yet bhe/yh2t-kwe. mle/wHt. h2e/ngwhis h1e/sti nu bhe/wgt nu k'e/yo h2e/ngwhis h1ime/ni nu ma/rkos h2e/ngwhim ne kwe/k't to/m-kwe gwe/mt nu h2e/ngwhis to/m gwhe/nt. h1yo/s sh2imne/s bhebho/yh2e so/ h2e/ngwhim ne wide/t.

we/rh1t wl_/kwos k'une/y. po/rk'om moy dh3dhi/. wl_/kwe gwowsth2o/m kwse/pi gwm_ye/ po/rk'om-kwe toy de/h3oh2 nu gwowsth2o/m gwe/mt nu Hre/wdHt k'wo:/ nu wiHro:/s h1ludho/nt bhe/wgt-kwe. we/kwos wl_/kwom gwowsth2o/m de/wkti.

lm_bhe/nt h2r_/tk'os wl_/kwos h3e/ro:-kwe skwa/lom nu h2r_/tk'os h2e/g't. so/ moy h1e/sti. h1eg'o/h2 tewh2so/s h1e/smi. skwta:/m wl_/kwos h3e/ro:-kwe. tu/H tewh2so/s h1e/si we/-kwe ml_du/h1 h1swo/s nu linkwta:/m. ish1ro/s sle/h2gwt ml_de/wes-kwe ke/nkonti.

h1ita:/m k'e/ywoh1 h1ime/ni nu h2r_/tk'os to/h1 gwe/mt nu ste/yghet sm_/teros dre/wi k'e/ywos-kwe le/ght nu h2r_/tk'os h2e/nti h1e/rt le/ytt-kwe nu gwe/h2t sm_/teros ni/. kwi/d toy h2r_/tk'os we/wket? mle/wHt moy. to/m me: se/kweth2e. h1yo/s te line/kwti so/ k'e/ywos ne h1e/sti.

spe/k'yet wl_kwi/h2s ta/wrom we/dni. ta/wre te h2e/k'h3oh2. wl_/kwih2 gwm_ye/. stesto/h2h2e nu to/m dre/mt nu le/ytt so/ wi/ se/h2-kwe wo/dr_ h3sle/ydht. wl_/kwih2 to/d ph3dhi/. h1yo/s dre/mt ne-kwe spe/k'yeti so/ h3sle/ydht.

we/wket h2o/wis swe/h2ey dhugh2tre/y. dhu/gh2ter wo/dr_ kwse/pi me: gwe/h2s. wl_kwi/h2s h2e/n stesto/h2e. nu dhugh2te:/r swe/h2m me/h2term_ ne h2we/yst nu to/d h1r_sk'e/t nu le/mbht wl_kwi/h2s ta:/m nu dhugh2te:/r gwe/tet. me/h2ter me k'le/wse nu wl_kwi/h2s ta:/m gwr_h3e/t. spe/rt me/h2tr_s we/kwos ta:/m h1ye/h2 to/d h2we/yst.

le/ghyet h2r_/tk'os g'hye/mi nu h1ye/gos tine/h1t. wo/kwet h2e/wis dre/wi. bhudhdhi/. so/ tine/h1ti nu bhe/wdht h2e/g't-kwe. ke/nkoh2 nu ste/h2t. h1yo/d h1ye/gos tine/h1ti h2r_/tk'os bhe/wdht.

dukta:/m dwo/h1 h1e/k'woh1 kwe/kwlom. kwe/kwlos h2e/g't. h2ne/rm_ we/g'hoh2. se/kwt h1e/k'wos. de/wkmi te. yugo/m we/wket. to/h1 yune/gmi nu h2ne:/r h1e/k'woh1 le/h1t nu stne/h2t kwe/kwlos. h1yo/d h1e/k'wos ne de/wkti kwe/kwlos stne/h2ti.

wi/k'poteyes me/ne we/kwos k'lute/. tre/yes gho/steyes kwse/pi gwme/nt gwo:/s-kwe nos h2ew h1l_h2e/nt. kwi/d dhe/dheh1omes? ko/ryom h1e/yomes to/ybhos-we: de/h3o:r se/lg'o:mes? nu wik'po/tis ste/h2t we/rh1t-kwe. h1yo/d de/h3o:r se/lg'o:mes to/y h2ew gwm_yo:/nti. to/ns gwhe/nomes h1we/su-kwe k'le/wos we/nomes. kwi/s wos dedwo/ye? ne dedwo/yh2e. to/ns h1re/h1omes. h1yo/d me ne k'l_ne/wonti nu h1r_sk'o:/mes. kwi/s to/ybhos me/ne we/kwos we/g'he:ti? to/d we/g'ho:h2.

wi/k'poteyes me k'l_nute/. de/h3o:r h1we/dmn_-kwe bhe/ronts h3re:/g's bhe/rg'ho:d gwegwo/me. me/ne dhugh2te/rm_ gwhodhe/yeti. k'e/ywos h1e/sti gho/stis-we: h1e/sti? nu wik'po/tis se/kwt ta:/m h1ope/ye. nu te/we g'm_ro/s h1e/seti g'm_ro/s-kwe swo/m swe/k'urom ne gwhe/nti. nu wik'po/tis h2e/g't. to/smey te/we dhugh2te/rm_ me: de/h3s. kwi/s to/syo k'e:/r wo/yde? nu bhe/h2mi me/ne ph2we/ni se/dtu wo/dr_-kwe pe/h3tu bhoydhe/h2m-kwe moy de/deh3tu. nu me/ne dhugh2te:/r to/smey h1e/seti. dye/w ph2ter ta:/m bhoydhe/h2m dedr_k'dhi/. k'e/ywos nos h1sye/h1t wl_/kwos-kwe me: h1sye/h1t.

bhre/h2teres h3re:/g's de/k'm_ HeHk'u/bhis h1e/k'wo:ys gwm_sk'e/ti. kwo/r sth2e/h1yo:mes? nu wik'po/tis we/rh1t to/smi bhe/rg'hoy sth2e/h1yo:mes. bhe/rg'hos nos h2le/kseti h1e/k'wo:s-kwe to/m ne ste/yghonti. h1yo/s to/smi se/dyeti so/ k'le/wos ne we/nt. tro/mos to/ns h1e/peti. to/ns h2use/si stune/domes. h2e/g'rom ni/ gwe/h2omes. dye/w nos ph2ter me/g'h2 k'le/wos dh3ye/h1s.

bhre/h2teres moy me/ne bhre/h2te:r we/kwos se/lg'et. gwm_sk'e/. h3re:/g's me stune/dti. nu wik'po/tis we/rh1t wo/yk'os ta/wro:s-kwe n_sme/y h1se/nti. me: h1e/yomes. nu wik'po/tis h2e/g't. bhre/h2tr_s ko/ryos n_sme/y h1e/sti. h1yo/d me/reti gwm_yo:/nti nos. h1eg'o/h2 stighne/woh2. kwi/s me se/kwe:tor? twe/ se/kwo:h2er. dhe/g'hom me/h2ter me/ne bhre/h2term_ h2le/kse. gwi/h3woyh1t to/y-kwe sn_gwih1e/nt.

ph2teres de/k'm_ me/ne wiHro:/s h2yuHn_k'o/m h3re:g'e/s suHnu/m h1pe/nt. kwi/d dhe/dheh1omes? to/m more/yo:mes to/m-we: spe/romes? nu wik'po/tis se/kwt to/m gwhn_dhi/. h2yuHn_k'o/s wl_/kwos h2we/kst nu-kwe h2o/wins h1e:/dti. nu wik'po/tis h2e/g't to/m me: more/yes. h1yo/s me/rt so/ k'e/ywos ne h1e/sti. to/m spe/romes. to/syo nos ph2te:/r de/h3o:r we/g'hetu k'e/ywo:s-kwe h1e/somes.

ph2teres nos pe/nkwe h2ne/res wo/yk'oy stesth2e:/r. n_sme/y wo/dr_ gwhodhe/yonti. to/y nos gho/steyes h1e/sonti? nu wik'po/tis we/rh1t gwo/wes nos polh1e/wes ne h1se/nti. nu wik'po/tis se/kwt. h1yo/s gho/stim gwhe/nti deywo:/s to/m more/yonti. ph2we/ni nos sde/ntu h1e/dn_tu-kwe ph3e/ntu-kwe. gho/steyes gwmih1e/nt k'e/ywo:s-kwe h1yih1e/nt.

bhre/h2teres g'he/yo:m gwm_sk'e/ti. nu ko/ryom h1r_sk'o:/mes? nu wik'po/tis se/kwt. h1yo/d gwe/h2omes to/ns stune/domes. h1yo/d se/dyo:mes sno/ygwhos n_sme/ more/ye:ti. to/y ste/wdonti. nu wik'po/tis h2e/g't. sno/ygwhos nos h2e/g'roy k'e/yor. kwi/s nos gwo:/s h2e/le:ti? m_ne/h1yo:mes to/ns-kwe h2e/lo:mes. h1yo/d sno/ygwhos tine/h1eti so/h2wl_-kwe gwhe/reti nu stighne/womes. k'le/wos nos me/g'h2 h1e/seti.

ph2ter me k'ludhi/. h1eg'o/h2 h2yuHn_k'o/s h1e/smi ne-kwe bhebho/yh2h2e. ko/ryom we/dho:h2. su/Hnew h2yuHn_k'o/s h1e/si. h1ne/wn_ ke/h3tuns dedo/rk'h2e. te/we k'le/wos to/m h2le/kse:ti. me/ne ma/rkos to/m we/g'he:ti gwe/h2tu. me/ne su/Hnew ih2dhi/ k'luto/m-kwe h1no/mn_ dhh1dhi/. gwm_sk'o/yh1s wido/yh1m_-kwe te.

ph2teres ta:/m bhoydhe/h2m wide/. bhe/rg'hosyo nos h3re:/g's bhoydhe/h2m dhe/h1t ta:/m-kwe Hrune/pt. kwi/d dhe/dheh1omes? deywo:/s to/ns stune/donti. m_ne/h1yo:mes. deywo:/s bhoydhe/h2m dedr_k'e:/r h2n_re/s-kwe bheh2g'hu/s stune/dti. deywo:/m bheh2g'hu/s h1e/somes. h1n_/gwney to/ns dhe/gwhe. stighne/womes.

bhre/h2teres wiHro/s h2e/n stesto/h2e Hre/wdHti-kwe. h2ne:/r ke/h3tum me/lh3tu. kwi/s wos gwe/h2eti? ne kwis bhe/h2ti. to/y tr_me/nti. nu h2yuHn_k'o/s ste/h2t. h1eg'o/h2 gwe/h2oh2 ne dedwo/yh2e. so/ de/k'm_ wiHro/ns more/yet. stighne/woh2. h1idhi/ nu-kwe h1we/su k'le/wos wn_dhi/. h1n_gwni/s twe/ ph2sye/h1t.

h1eg'o/h2 h1o/gwhim gwhe/nm_. kwi/s tu/H h1e/si? me/ne bheh2g'hu/s HeHk'u/s h1e/sti me/ne-kwe k'e:/r me/g'h2 h1e/sti. ml_h3dhi/. h1yo/d h1me/ we/nesi me/ne h1e/k'wons h1pdhi/. h1yo/d twe/ we/noh2 k'wo/nes wl_/kwo:s-kwe twe/ h1e/donti. polh1u/h2 we/kwesh2 bhe/h2si. me/ne bheh2g'hu/s bhe/h2eti. h1yo/s me/reti n_/dhgwhitom k'le/wos to/smey m_ne/h1ye:ti.

kwi/s po/h2imen tu/H h1e/si? kwi/s te/we ph2te:/r h1e/sti? me/ne ph2te:/r h3re:/g's h1e/sti k'luto/s-kwe h1e/sti. te/we h2o/wins ph2sdhi/. h1eg'o/h2 poh2ime:/n h1e/smi. wl_/kwos h2o/wim h1e/pt. h1eg'o/h2 to/m me/ne bheh2g'hu/h1 stune/dm_. to/smo:d ne dedwo/yh2e. h2n_re/s dedwo/yh2e? h1me/ gwhn_dhi/ h2epo/-we: h1idhi/. h2ne:/r me/rt k'luto/m h1no/mn_ ne me/rt.

k'ludhi/ me. dwo/h1 me HeHk'u/h1 h1e/k'woh1 we/g'hetes. sh2we/ns kwe/kwlos lewko/s h1e/sti. me/ne ye/wgos h2use/si ye/wgm_. ne kwis me se/kwetor. me/ne h2e/g'rom gwm_dhi/. h2e/wsos diwe/s dhu/gh2ter n_sme/ spe/k'ye. HeHk'e/wes h1e/k'wo:s twe/ ne spe/ronti. me/ne bheh2g'hu/s twe/ h1e/peti te/we-kwe kwe/kwlos Hrune/petor.

te/we we/kwesh2 polh1u/h2 h1e/sti. h1eg'o/h2 pl_h1e/wih2n_s g'he/yomn_s dedo/rk'h2e ko/ryom-kwe wo/ydh2e. h2yuHn_k'o/s ma/rkos yugo/m ne wo/yde. septm_/ h2yuHn_k'o:/s to/m h2e/g'rom h1ludho/nt nu-kwe h2e/n k'e/yror. h1eg'o/h2 twe/d ne bhebho/yh2h2e. te/we me/h2term_ h1r_sk'e/. tk'idhi/-we: ke/h3tum-kwe h1ukdhi/.

k'wo/n me/ne gwo:/s kwse/pi ste/h2yes. wiHro/s ne h1e/si. gwm_ye/. k'wo/nes twe/ h1e/donti. dr_mdhi/. h1eg'o/h2 k'wo:/ h1e/smi h2o/wins-kwe h2le/ksoh2. tu/H wl_/kwos h1e/si. me/ne gwo:/s pe/ruti h1e/lh2s. nu to/ns h1e/poh2.

h1o/gwhey me/ne gwo:/s gwr_h3e/s me/ne-kwe bhre/h2term_ gwr_h3e/s. te/we bhe/rg'ho:d ml_h3dhi/. h1eg'o/h2 twe/d ne dedwo/yh2e. dye/w moy ph2ter se/g'hos de/h3s. twe/ bhe/ydoh2. so/h2wl_ h1o/gwhim spe/k'ye. kwi/s tu/H h1e/si? de/k'm_ me h2ne/res gwme/nt. to/ns gwr_h3o/m twe/-kwe h1e/doh2. nu n_/dhgwhitom k'le/wos me/g'hye h1e/seti.

sth2e/h1yoh2. ne kwosmo:d bhe/wgm_ twe/d-kwe me: bhe/wgoh2. h1yo/s bhe/wgt. so/ k'le/wos ne we/nt. h1yo/s kh3te/wi stesto/h2e so/ k'luto/m h1no/mn_ dhe/h1t. sth2e/h1yoh2. dhe/g'hom me/h2ter to/m h1pdhi/ h1yo/s se/ngweti.

h1eg'o/h2 me/ne-kwe bhre/h2te:r stesth2we/. ph2te:/r nos wik'po/tis h1e/sti. wl_/kwons more/yowe ne dedwiwe/. n_sme/y nos gwo:/s h1ope/yete usme/-we: gwhe/nowos. we/y polh1e/wes h1sme/s. yu/ dwo/h1 h2ne/rh1e h1ste/s.

k'ludhi/ me. ko/ryos n_sme/ h1ente/r me: h1sye/h1t. te/bhye HeHk'u/ns h1e/k'wons de/deh3mi. me/g'hye ta/wrons h1ope/ye. bhoydhe/h2m de/h3omes. dye/w ph2ter n_sme/ k'le/wse. dhe/g'hom me/h2ter n_sme/ k'le/wse. so/h2wl_ n_sme/ k'le/wse. h1n_/gwney n_sme/ k'le/wse. h1yo/s ta:/m bhoydhe/h2m Hrune/peti swo/m k'le/wos mr_ye/h1t. twe/ k'l_ne/wmi. me/ne bhoydhe/h2 te/bhye h1e/sti.

wi/k'potey twe/ we/nm_. te/we k'le/wos h1we/su h1e/sti. te/we me/ne-kwe wiHro:/s h2e/n k'e/yror. to/m ke/h3tum line/kwomes wo/dr_-kwe pe/h3omes. me/g'hye de/k'm_ h1e/k'wons dedh3dhi/ te/bhye-kwe k'e/ywos h1e/soh2. to/ns de/deh3mi. de/ywo:s h1use/wo:m de/h3tores ta:/m bhoydhe/h2m dedr_k'te/.

dhugh2te:/r moy h1e/sti. te/we bhre/h2te:r me/ne g'm_ro/s h1e/stu. h1we/dmn_ h1ope/ye. h1we/dmn_ h1ope/yoh2. de/h3o:r septm_/-kwe ta/wrons se/lg'oh2. se/h2 me/ne bhre/h2trey lewbhe/h2 h1sye/h1t. h2e/wsos ta:/m k'o/ymom we/dhe. so/h2wl_ ta:/m bhoydhe/h2m dedr_k'dhi/. lewbho:/s h1sih1e/nt.

twe/ g'n_ne/h3mi. te/we ph2te:/r me/ne ph2tre/s gho/stis h1e/st. ke/h3tum me: h1r_sk'o:/mes. we/ gho/stih1 h1swo/s. me/ne do/h3r_ te/bhye h1e/stu te/we-kwe do/h3r_ me/g'hye h1e/stu. to/smi ph2we/ni se/dyo:mes.

wi/k'potey bhe/rg'hos te/bhye h1e/stu wo/dr_-kwe me/g'hye h1e/stu. h2e/g'ros n_sme/ h1ente/r k'e/yor. te/we ta/wro:s me/ne we/dni ph3e/ntu me/ne-kwe h2o/weyes te/we h2e/g'roy h1e/dn_tu. te/we we/kwos h1we/su h1e/sti. h1e/stu. so/h2wl_ h1e/yti dhe/g'ho:m-kwe stesto/h2e. bhoydhe/h2 nos m_ne/h1yoyh1t.

k'ludhi/ me. h1yo/y sn_gwe/nt to/y h2e/n k'e/yror. to/ns ph2we/nh1 dhe/gwho:mes to/ns-kwe Hre/wdHomes. me/g'hye bhoydhe/h2m dedh3dhi/. ta:/m de/deh3mi. h1n_/gwney n_sme/ k'l_nudhi/.

wi/k'potey de/h3o:r bhe/ronts h1eg'o/h2 gwegwo/mh2e. me/g'hye me/ne bhre/h2term_ h1ope/ye. te/we ph2te/rm_ memn_dhi/. sddhi/ h1ddhi/-kwe ph3dhi/-kwe. h1eg'o/h2 te/bhye te/we bhre/h2term_ h1ope/yoh2. deywo:/s me twe/ we/dhont.

te/we wiHro/s me/ne bhre/h2term_ more/yet. me/g'hye de/k'm_ ta/wrons bhe/re. to/d me/ne bhre/h2tr_s misdho/s h1e/sti. h1yo/d to/ns bhe/re:si te/bhye k'e/ywos h1e/soh2. h1yo/d to/ns ne bhe/re:si twe/ pro/ti stighne/woh2. twe/ k'l_ne/wmi. h1eg'o/h2 ta/wrons bhe/roh2. bhoydhe/h2 n_sme/ h1ente/r m_ne/h1yoyh1t.

ph2ter ko/ryom h1e/ymi. h1me/ me: Hre/wdHs. h1yo/d me/roh2 me/ne h1no/mn_ k'luto/m h1e/seti to/d-kwe mle/wHesi. h1yo/d gwi/h3wo:h2 me/ne k'o/ymom h2ew gwe/moh2. me/ne toy ma/rkos tk'e/yti. pl_h1e/wih2n_s g'he/yomn_s gwi/h3woyh1s. me/ne su/Hnew ih2dhi/ deywo:/s te/bhye me/g'h2 k'le/wos dh3ih1e/nt twe/-kwe h2le/ksoyh1ent.

me/h2ter h1me/ h2e/les. h1eg'o/h2 ne bhebho/yh2h2e. me/ne tre/yes bhre/h2teres gwm_sk'o/nti. me/d me: tre/ses. te/bhye k'luto/m h1no/mn_ dhe/h1oh2. dhe/g'hom ta:/m h2le/kse. ke/h2ilos h1sye/h1s h1me/-kwe wido/yh1s.

po/tni nos suHnu/m h2e/le. pe/h2wr_ nos lewko/m h1e/stu. me: Hre/wdHs. h1yo/d h2ew me: gwe/moh2 te/we dayh2we:/r twe/ h2le/kse:ti. dhe/g'hom to/syeh2ey h1we/suh2 dh3ye/h1s.

wi/Hre stighnudhi/ h2ew-kwe gwm_sk'e/. h1n_gwni/s twe/ we/dhoyh1t te/we-kwe bheh2g'hu/s Hwr_dhye/h1t. ph2we/ni nos se/dyo:h2 twe/-kwe h2use/si h2isk'o:/h2. h2e/wsos diwe/s dhu/gh2ter to/m k'o/ymom we/dhe. h1me/ memn_dhi/.

ih2dhi/. dye:/ws ph2te:/r h1use/wo:m de/h3to:r h1e/sti te/bhye me/g'h2 k'le/wos de/dh3ih1t. me: bhebhih2ye/h1s. kh3te/ws me: bhe/wgs. Hwr_dhdhi/. h2e/wso:s te/bhye lukye/h1t. te/we ph2te/rm_ memn_dhi/.

h2epo/ stighne/wsi m_ne/h1yo:h2-kwe. ta:/m we/stim we/so te/bhye h1ye/h2m me/ne h2wl_/h1neh2s dhe/h1m_. twe/ bhe/rg'ho:d spe/k'yo:h2. h1n_gwni/s twe/ we/dhoyh1t. so/h2wl_ te/bhye gwhr_ye/h1t.

ph2ter me/h2te:r-kwe h1me/ k'luto/m. ye/h2mi me/ne-kwe wos bhre/h2te:r tk'e/yti. me/ne po/tnih2m ph2ste/. h1yo/d se/ngwoh2 me: HrudHte/. h1me/ dhe/gwhete. gwi/h3woyh1te.

po/tni me k'le/wse. te/bhye me/ne h2e/g'rom h2o/wins-kwe line/kwmi. dhugh2te/rm_ nos h2e/le. h1yo/d h1eh1le/wdheti wik'po/teyey ta:/m dh3dhi/. h1yo/d gwi/h3wo:h2 h2ew gwm_sk'o:/h2. h1yo/d se/ngwoh2 to/syeh2ey me/ne h1no/mn_ mluHdhi/. ke/h2ilos h1sye/h1t.

h2ew gwegwo/mth2e. ku/dhe gwe/h2s? neh2we/h1 gwa:/m. kwi/d wide/s? septm_/ wo/yk'ons wido/m. de/h3o:r we/g'hesi? te/bhye de/h3o:r we/g'hoh2. sddhi/. me/ne k'e:/r me/wdt.

gwegwo/mth2e. te/we bhre/h2te:r ku/dhe h1e/sti? h2e/n k'e/yor se/ngwt. sth2e/h1yet bhe/wgt-we:? sth2e/h1yet. ne bhe/wgt. n_/dhgwhitom k'le/wos to/smey m_ne/h1ye:ti me/ne-kwe k'e:/r Hre/wdHti.

gwi/h3wesi? gwi/h3woh2. te/we k'le/wos me/g'h2 h1e/sti? me/ne k'le/wos me/g'h2 ne h1e/sti. gwegwo/mh2e. me/ne k'e:/r me/wdt. ph2we/ni nos sddhi/ wo/dr_-kwe ph3dhi/.

bhre/h2ter gwegwo/mth2e. h1ne/wn_ g'he/yomn_s lelo/ykwth2e. wr_h1me/. ne/k't. me/h2te:r nos twe/ Hre/wdHt. nu twe/ dedo/rk'h2e me/ne-kwe k'e:/r me/wdt. h1eg'o/h2 ke/h2ilos h1e/smi. wo/yk'oy h2e/n tk'e:/m.

po/h2imen gwegwo/mth2e. h2o/weyes ku/dhe h1se/nti? te/h2es h2e/n h1se/nti. kwi/d kwse/pi wide/s? wl_/kwom wido/m. ta:/s gwm_ye/t. k'wo:/ h1eg'o/h2-kwe to/m h2epo/ h1l_h2we/. bhebho/yh2th2e? ne bhebho/yh2h2e. sddhi/ wo/dr_-kwe ph3dhi/.

kwi/s tu/H h1e/si? twe/ ne g'n_ne/h3mi. ph2ter h1eg'o/h2 te/we suHnu/s h1e/smi. me/ne suHnu/s lelo/ykwe. ke/h2ilos h1e/smi. h1no/mn_ moy dhe/h1s. bhh2dhi/. te/we we/kwos g'n_ne/h3mi. me/ne k'e:/r twe/ wo/yde.

g'he/yo:m we/sni tine/h1ti h2ne:/r-kwe h2e/rh3tro:y gwo:/s ye/wgt. h1i/ns we/dhonts h1e/y h2e/g'rom h2e/rh3yeti. sth2ro/m h2e/rh3trom swo:/ys g'ho/sto:ys kapye/ti swidye/ti-kwe. h2e/weyes h2e/rh3trom se/kwontor. h1erkwo/m bhe/h2ti. nu h1e/syo po/tnih2 se/h1mn_ se/h1yeti lo/yseh2-kwe h1i/d k'e/leti. h2we/rst. h1yo/d so/h2wl_ n_bhro/s-kwe gwm_ta:/m g'r_h2no/m h2we/kst h1e/syo-kwe k'e:/r me/wdt.

to/smi ye/h1ni ne h2we/rst. bhre/h1wr_ te/rst. h2e/ng'hos wiHro/syo k'e:/r le/mbht. gwhrenso/s h2e/g'rom h1e/wset gwo/wes-kwe tr_syo/nt. h1e/y swo/h1 g'ho/stoh1 tl_ne/h2t we/rh1t-kwe. dye/w ph2ter wo/dr_ ku/dhe h1e/sti? n_bhro/m nos dh3dhi/. nu dye:/ws h1i/m k'le/wt. ghro/mos h1ludhe/t n_bhro/s-kwe ste/h2t. h2we/rst. gwo/wes bhre/h1wr_ dr_me/nt ph3e/nt-kwe. h1e/y me/wdt.

kwi/d k'o/d h1e/sti? kwi/d h1e/nod h1e/sti? k'o/m h2e/g'rom spe/k'ye. k'o/s puHro/s h1e/sti h1e/nod h2e/lbhit h1e/sti. k'o/d yugo/m h1e/sti h1e/nos kwe/kwlos h1e/sti. g'r_h2no/m kwi/h1 h2we/kst? dhe/g'ho:m h1e/smey wre/h2dn_s de/h3t n_bhro/s h1e/smey wo/dr_ h1a/yt so/h2wl_ h1i/d gwhr_ne/wt. nu h2e/k'esh2 pe/kweti. k'm_to/m dhoHne/h2es h2e/k'esi h1se/nti. h1i/h2 skine/domes. yo/h1r_ mudro/m h1e/sti.

h2ne:/r po/tnih2-kwe h2e/g'rom h2use/si h1e/g'hs gwh2ta:/m. h1e/y puHro/syo h2e/k'esh2 skine/dt h1i/h2 bhore/h2m bhe/ret. nu h1e/y h2e/lbhit skine/dt. so/h2wl_ dhe/gwhet h1e/y-kwe swidye/t. dhugh2te:/r to/ybhos wo/dr_ bhore/yet ph3e/nt-kwe. kwe/kwlos ple/h1t. h1i/h2 k'e/mh2t. HeHk'e/wes h1e/k'wo:s kwe/kwlom duke/nt. h1yo/d kwse/ps h1ludhe/t g'r_h2no/m do:/m kwe/kwloh1 we/g'hont. h2Hse/h2i sde/nt gwhre/nt-kwe. h1e/y h2e/g't. dhg'hme/s do/h3r_ me/g'h2 h1e/sti.

g'he/ymn_ gwe/mt. g'he/yo:m bhl_He/h2 k'e/yor ste/wdt-kwe. gwo/wes gwowsth2o/y stesth2e:/r h2o/weyes-kwe gho/rtoy le/ghyonti. h2ne:/r h1e/syo po/tn_yeh2h1 h2Hse/h2i k'o/m se/dyeti. pe/h2wr_ dhe/gwheti dhuh2mo/s-kwe ste/ygheti. puHro/m h2e/lbhit wo/sh2r_ h2e/boln_s-kwe h1e/dn_ti me/dhu-kwe pi/ph3onti. h1e/y yugo/m de/meti h2e/rh3trom-kwe h2re/ydheti. wo/sr_ memo/ne. bhe/h2ti. g'he/ymn_ h2epo/ gwe/h2eti wo/sr_ h1ludhe:/ti.

dwo/h1 bhre/h2terh1e h1sta:/m. k'o/s h2e/g'rom pe/ruti h2e/rh3yet se/h1mn_ se/h1yet. h1e/nos h2Hse/h2i le/ghyet. nu h2we/rst. g'r_h2no/m h2we/kst h1i/d-kwe pe/kwet. k'o/s dhoHne/h2h1 h3pe/wst. h1e/y g'r_h2no/m g'hime/ni h1e:/dt. h1e/nos kwid ne wide/t. h1e/y swo/m bhre/h2term_ gwe/h2t se/kwt-kwe. bhre/h2ter moy dhoHne/h2m dh3dhi/. so/ we/rh1t. h2e/rh3yom le/ghyes. h1yo/s h2e/rh3yeti so/ g'r_h2no/m gwr_h3e/ti.

h1yo/d so/h2wl_ h2use/si h1rowdho/m h1e/sti h1yo/d gwo/wes le/ghyonti h1yo/d dhuh2mo/s k'e/yor nu h2we/rst. h1yo/d gerh2e/nes HrudHe/nti g'he/ymn_ gwm_sk'e/ti. h1yo/d spe/res h2e/lbhiti se/dyonti g'r_h2no/m pe/kweti. h1yo/d ghro/mos we/sni gwm_sk'e/ti yo/h1r_ mudro/m h1e/sti. h1yo/s gerh2e/nn_s spe/k'yeti so/ ghro/mo:d ne dedwo/ye.

h1regwesye/t. ghro/mos gwm_ye/t. nu n_bhro/s upe/r ste/h2t. h2e/lbhit tre/mt. wiHro/s h2e/g'roy sth2e/h1yet. tr_mo/nts h1e/y Hre/wdHt. n_/bhre h2e/k'esh2 me: Hrune/ps. h2epo/ gwh2dhi/. h1e/y h1i/m k'le/wt. h1e/y h2epo/ gwe/h2t h1i/h2-kwe ne Hrune/pt. le/wkt. n_th2gro:/s h1e/st. h1e/y dhe/mbht me/wdt-kwe.

h2e/wso:s le/wkt. poh2ime:/n gwo:/s gwowsth2o:/d h1e/g'hs we/dhet. HeHk'u/s ka/pros pro/ h1e/yt. h2o/weyes h1e/k'wos-kwe se/kwonto. h2e/g'rom h1r_sk'o/nt. h2k'h3e/nt. h1e/y se/dt to/ns-kwe spe/k'yet. nu h1e/y to/ns bhre/h1wr_ h2e/g'et ph3e/nt-kwe. sh2e/nt. h1yo/d kwse/ps h1ludhe/t h1e/y to/ns gho/rtom g'he/yt.

supno/s poh2ime/nm_ kwse/pi h1e/pt. h2k'h2owsyo/nts k'wo:/ bhe/wdhet. h1e/y wl_/kwom h1re/gwesi k'le/wt h2o/wins-kwe gwe/h2t. tr_me/nt. wl_/kwos h1i/m kwe/k't bhe/wgt-kwe. poh2ime:/n h1e/syo lo/ysa:s wide/t. lo/yseh2es se/lesi n_k'e/nt. h1e/y we/rh1t h1we/sew k'wo/n tewo/m h1no/mn_ k'luto/m h1e/sti. h1e/y h1e/smey do/h3r_ h1a/yt.

h1regwesye/t. h2o/weyes gho/rtoy le/ghyont. nu wl_/kwos h1re/gwesos h1ludhe/t. nu h1e/y h1i/h2n_s se/rpet. tr_me/nt. moyso/s h2e/nti ste/h2t. h1e/syo h3o/kwih1 h1rowdho/yh1 h1sta:/m. wl_/kwos h1i/m h1e/pt. nu k'wo:/ gwm_ye/t. wl_/kwos h1i/m le/h1t bhe/wgt-kwe. moyso/syo h2wl_/h1neh2 kruh2se/h1 h1rowdhe/h2 h1e/st. moyso/s gwi/h3wet.

h1yo/d kwse/ps h1ludhe/t gwo/wes gwm_sk'o/nti. mudre/h2 gho/rtoy sth2e/h1yontih2 gwe:/n to/ns h2me/lg'ti. h1e/syeh2s dhugh2te:/r h2o/wins h2me/lg'ti. polh1u/ de/dh3n_ti. h1i/h2 mle/wHti. gwo/wes sth2e/h1yete. stesth2e:/r. wiHro/s se/dyeti. ne kwis gwm_sk'e/ti. l_ghe/nt. sh2e/nt.

poh2ime:/n de/k'm_ h2o/wins h2use/si h1e/g'hs we/dhet. h1yo/d h1e/y h1i/h2n_s gho/rtom h2e/g'et h1e/y h1ne/wn_ h2o/wins wide/t. tre/mt we/rh1t-kwe. moyso/s lelo/ykwe. nu h1e/y se/los gwe/h2t moyso/m-kwe h2e/n h2isk'e/t. h1e/y h1i/m se/lesi kwe/k't. h1i/m swo:/ys h2r_Hmo:/ys tl_nh2o/nts h1e/y h1i/m gho/rtom bhe/ret. h2o/wis kwis ne lelo/ykwe.

h1regwesye/t. h2o/weyes l_ghte/. gwo/wes l_ghte/. spe/k's bhe/wdheti. pe/h2wr_ h1rudhe/h1ti. supno/s wos h1pye/h1t. g'hwe:/r me: gwm_sk'o/yh1t. h1e/y h1i/h2n_s me: wido/yh1t. h2we/seti.

g'he/slom gwo/wes k'm_to/m-kwe h1e/k'wo:s h3re:g'e/y h1se/nt dwo/h1 gwo/wh1e poh2imne/y h1sta:/m. tr_no/m g'he/ymn_ gwe/mt. g'he/slom h3re:g'e/s gwo/wes h2e/g'roy m_re/nt. poh2ime:/n dwo/h1 swo/h1 gwo/wh1e do:/m we/dhet gwi/h3weta:m-kwe. h3re:/g's to/m do:/m we/sni gwm_sk'e/t. poh2ime:/n h1e/smey me/dhu de/h3t. h3re:/g's h1i/d pe/h3t me/wdt-kwe. h1e/y h1e/smey k'm_to/m h2o/wins h1a/yt.

bhre/h2ter h2o/wins ku/dhe h2e/g'esi? h1i/h2n_s bhre/h1wr_ h2e/g'oh2. h2e/g'ros h2sowso/s h1e/sti. g'hwe:/rm_ dedo/rk'th2e? h1i/m kwis ne dedo/rk'e. k'wo:/ h1i/m kwse/pi k'le/wt. ku/dhe tk'e/ysi? se/lesi h1e/pi tk'e/ymi. kwi/d bhe/resi? h2wl_/h1neh2m bhe/roh2. bhre/h2ter gwi/h3we. deywo:/s h1i/h2n_s h2le/ksoyh1ent.

nu He/Hk'ewes gwo/wes dukte/. h2e/g'r_yom h2e/rh3trom dukte/. yugo/m bhe/rete lo/yseh2m h3rog'e/yete. me: h3slidhte/ gwhrenso/s dhe/gwheti swidyo/mes. h2ne:/r wos se/kwetor nu-kwe se/h1mn_ se/h1yoh2. dhe/g'ho:m me/h2te:r dhoHne/h2m dh3ye/h1t. nu so/h2wl_ se/h1mn_ gwhr_nuye/h1t. nu-kwe mu/dro:s gwo/wes dukte/.

nu h2e/k'esh2 skine/domes. sth2ro/m-kwe bho/rom we/g'ho:mes. we/rg'om pl_h1u/bhis g'ho/sto:ys HeHk'u/ h1e/sti. gwhrenso/s dhe/gwheti puHro/s h2sowso/s h1e/sti. h2e/boles-kwe h1rowdho:/s h1se/nti. h1rowdho/ns h2e/boln_s wo/sh2r_-kwe h2e/k'h3omes. h1erkwo/m bhe/h2omes le/wbhe me/dhu pi/ph3e. mu/dra swe/sor g'r_h2no/m gwr_h3e/ me/wdomes.

su/Hnew tewo/syo ph2tre/s we/kwos k'ludhi/. se/h1mn_ h2sowse/h2i dhg'he/mi me: se/h1yes. h1yo/d h2we/rst se/h1ye. h2e/rh3tro:y dwo/h1 gwo/wh1e yugdhi/. to/h1 me: stune/ds. lo/yseh2m h3rog'e/ye. h2e/weyes se/h1mn_ gwr_h3o/nti. h1yo/s lo/yseh2m h3rog'e/yeti so/ g'r_h2no/m gwr_h3e/ti.

su/Hnew h2o/wins g'n_nh3dhi/. gwo:/s bhre/h1wr_ we/dhe. h1yo/d h2o/wis lele/ykweti h1i/h2m_ h2isk'e/. to/ns h1re/gwesi me: line/kws. k'wo:/ h1re/gwesi tewo/m h3o/kw h1e/sti. h1yo/d g'hwe:/r gwe/meti k'une/y HrudHdhi/. me: tre/ses. h1yo/s bhe/wdheti so/ swa:/s h2o/wins pe/h2sti.

h1yo/s ne wr_g'ye/ti so/ ne h1e:/dti. h1yo/s ne h2e/rh3yeti h2e/g'ros kwid to/smey ne de/deh3ti. h1yo/d dhuh2mo/s h1e/sti pe/h2wr_ h1e/sti. wl_/kwos h2e/wis h2wl_/h1neh2m we/stor wl_/kwos-ge h1e/sti. h1no/mn_ k'le/wos h1e/sti. h2e/g'ros g'r_h2no/syo me/h2te:r h1e/sti. g'r_h2no/m h2e/h2usom h1e/sti. h1yo/s dhro/wghom se/h1yeti so/ dhro/wghom gwr_h3e/ti. te/tk'o: h2e/rh3trom de/meti. kwoyne/h2 dhro/wghom se/kwetor. h1yo/s wr_g'ye/ti. so/ misdho/m h1a/yto. h3o/sdos wr_h2de/s bhuHye/ti. h2e/h2usom ne gwr_h3e/si dhoHne/h2m gwr_h3e/si. h3o/kwih1 spe/k'yetes g'ho/stoh1 wr_g'ye/tes po/dh1e bhe/retes. gwo/wes wiHro:/s-kwe m_re/nt. h1we/su k'le/wos ne me/rt.

po/teys suHnu/s h2o/wins h2e/g'rom h2e/g'et. Hre/wdHt. g'hwe:/r gwegwo/me. wiHro:/s h2e/n h1ludho/nt. ne kwid kwk'e/nt. me/wdt. h1i/ns tri/s dhre/wght. g'hwe:/r-ge gwe/mt. Hre/wdHt h1i/m-kwe kwis ne k'le/wt. h1e/y h1i/h2n_s h1e/pt. h1yo/s dhrughye/ti h1i/m kwis ne k'le/wseti.

kwi/s ph2ter h2e/g'rom h2e/leti? wo/dr_ h1i/m h2e/leti. kwi/s wo/dr_ h2e/leti? dye:/ws h1i/d h2e/leti. kwi/s dye:/m h2e/leti? ne kwis h1e/sti. h1e/y nos ph2te:/r h1e/sti.

po/tis suHno/wey we/rh1t. gwo:/s toy de/deh3mi. h2e/g'rom toy de/deh3mi. h1i/m h2e/rh3ye to/ns ph2sdhi/. tewo/m bhre/h2term_ me: dhrughye/s. me: ste/h2yes. h1e/syo te/rmn_ sth2ro/m h1e/sti. bhoydhe/h2m memn_dhi/. h1we/su toy k'le/wos h1sye/h1t.

h2e/Hsh2 de/ms k'e:/r h1e/sti. me/h2te:r pe/h2wr_ h2use/si h2le/kseti. h1i/h2 h1e/smey me/lit de/deh3ti. h1i/h2 h2Hse/h2i se/dyeti bhe/h2ti-kwe. pe/h2wr_ deywo/s h1e/sti. dhugh2te:/r k'le/wseti. h1i/d h1i/h2n_s gwhr_ne/wti dhuh2mo/s-kwe ste/ygheti. h1yo/d pe/h2wr_ me/rt h2e/Hsh2 h2e/Hsh2 ne h1e/sti.

h2ne:/r me/lit h2ewlo/y wide/t. h1e/y h1i/d swe/h2m po/tnih2m we/g'het. h1i/h2 m_lite/y wo/dr_ dhe/h1t. me/lit ye/set me/dhu-kwe bhu/Ht. h1i/d swe/h2dt. h1i/h2 h1i/d srobhe/yet. nu gho/steyes h1re/nt. wiHro/s deywo/ybhos g'huto/m me/dhu h1ope/yet. mudro:/s gho/steyes h1i/d ph3e/nt mude/nt-kwe.

h2wl_/h1neh2 h2e/wisu bhuHye/ti. h2wl_/h1neh2 bhl_He/h2 h1e/sti. po/tnih2 h1i/h2m_ tn_ne/wti. h1i/h2 h1e/syeh2s suHnu/m h2wl_/h1neh2h1 wose/yeti. h1e/y h1i/h2m_ g'hime/ni we/stor gwhe/rt-kwe. h2wl_/h1neh2 h1e/smey gwhrenso/m de/deh3ti. h1e/syeh2s g'ho/stoh1 HeHk'u/h1 h1ste/s. h2e/wyo:m do/h3r_ lewbho/m h1e/sti.

me/h2te:r ste/h2t. dhugh2te:/r bhe/wdht. me/h2te:r puHro/m pe/kweti puHro/s-kwe ye/seti h1e/dn_ti-kwe. ph2te:/r h1e/k'wons ye/wgt. h1e/y suHno/wey bhe/h2ti. to/ns h2e/g'rom h2e/g'e. h1e/y to/ns h2e/g'eti. dhugh2te:/r bhore/h2m bhe/reti. we/rg'om wr_g'yo/nti.

gho/stis kwse/pi h1e/rt. po/tis we/rh1t. gho/stey h1n_do/m h1idhi/. h2Hse/h2i sddhi/. h1ddhi/ pi/ph3e-kwe. gho/stis de/mi lewbho/s h1e/sti. kwi/s h1e/si? gho/stis h2e/g't. g'hwe:/r h2o/wins h1e/pt bhe/wgm_-kwe. po/tis h2e/g't. deywo:/s toy h1we/suh2 dh3ih1e/nt.

tre/yes lewbho:/s gho/steyes gwm_yo/nt. po/tis to/ns h2Hse/h2i sode/yet. h1e/syo po/tnih2 puHro/m wo/sh2r_-kwe pe/kwet to/ns-kwe bhore/yet. to/ns h2k'h3e/nt me/dhu-kwe tri/s pi/ph3ont. tetr_pe:/r. h2e/g't. dye:/ws ph2te:/r k'o/m do:/m h2le/ksoyh1t.

h1regwesye/t. h2ne:/r gwe:/n-kwe l_ghta:/m. supno/s to/h1 le/mbht. pe/h2wr_ h1rudhe/h1t. k'wo:/ h2Hse/h2i le/ghyet bhe/wdhet-kwe to/h1-kwe h2le/kset. h2e/wis Hre/wdHt. le/wkt. ste/h2t.

pik'te/h2 gwe:/n h2e/Hsh2m_ h1r_sk'e/t. h1i/h2 h1i/h2m_ pe/ri h1e/yt. h1e/syeh2s me/h2te:r Hre/wdHt h2e/g't-kwe. dhu/gh2ter po/teyey lewbhe/h2 bhuHdhi/. h1e/syeh2s bhre/h2te:r h1e/syeh2ey h2e/h2usosyo do/h3r_ h1a/yt. po/tis h1e/syeh2ey me/lit h1a/yt. h1i/h2 h1i/d h2e/k'h3t. nu h1e/y we/rh1t. gwe:/n k'o/syo de/ms po/tnih2 bhuHdhi/. tewo:/s suHne/wes polh1e/wes h1sih1e/nt. h2e/Hsh2 sth2re/h2 h1sye/h1t.

dye/w nos ph2ter h1use/wo:m de/h3tor k'ludhi/. nu k'o/s h2e/g'ros h2sowso/s h1e/sti. dhe/g'ho:m-kwe me/h2te:r tr_sye/ti. h1yo/s me/g'h2m_ h1o/gwhim gwhe/ns wo/dr_ nos dh3dhi/. h1yo/s me/g'h2m_ n_bhro/m bhe/yds nu h2e/g'roy h2wr_sdhi/. ghro/mos gwm_yo/yh1t dhoHne/h2 ple/h1dhoyh1t. h1we/su nos g'r_h2no/m h2uksye/h1t bhre/h1wr_ pl_h1ye/h1t. h1e/k'wo:s ka/pro:s-kwe pi/ph3oyh1ent. h2e/boles-kwe h1rudhe/h1ih1ent.

dhe/g'hom g'e/nh1mn_ tewo:/ys h2r_Hmo:/ys k'e/le. nu-kwe toy se/h1mn_ se/h1yomes. dhoHne/h2m nos dh3dhi/ puHro/m nos h1ope/ye. h1i/d nos h2uksye/h1t h1i/d me: mr_ye/h1t. wre/h2des sth2re/h2es h1sih1e/nt wo/sh2r_ ple/h1dhoyh1t. puHro/syo sth2re/h2 h2e/k'esh2 polh1u/h2 h1sye/h1t. wo/sh2r_ bhuHyo/yh1t h3o/sdos-kwe wr_h2de/s bhuHyo/yh1t.

sh2we/ns h1ro/wdhe kwe/kwle h2e/g'rom gwhr_nudhi/. polh1u/h2-kwe puHro/syo h2e/k'esh2 pe/kwe. nu kwe/kwle h2e/lbhit me: h1e/wsoyh1s. le/wbhe kwe/kwle me/g'h2s h1e/si h1i/m spe/k'ye. puHro:/y-kwe gwhrenso/m h1ope/ye. n_/dhgwhitom nos sth2ro/m k'le/wos h1ope/ye.

g'n_/h3ro:s de/ywo:s gwo:/s h2o/wins-kwe h2le/ksete. ka/prons h1e/k'wons-kwe h2le/ksete. gwi/h3woyh1ent polh1e/wes h1sih1e/nt. h2r_/tk'os-kwe to/ns kwse/pi me: h1pye/h1t. spe/k's bhe/wdhoyh1t moyso/s ka/pros-kwe gwi/h3woyh1ta:m. h2e/wsosm_-kwe-ge wido/yh1ent.

g'hwe:/r h2e/wibhos h2epo/ ih2dhi/. h1i/h2n_s me: h1e/ps moyso/m me: le/mbhs. k'o/ymom gho/rtom me: gwe/ms gwowsth2o/m-we: me: gwe/ms. dedo/rk'h2e-ge te nu me ne dedo/rk'th2e. sh2ito/s h1sdhi/ nu se/los ih2dhi/. h1e/y-kwe h1re/gwos h2epo/ bhugye/h1t.

dhe/g'hom toy puHro/m h2e/lbhit-kwe dedh3me/s. me/lit g'huto/m-kwe me/dhu h1ope/yomes. h1rowdho/ns-kwe toy h2e/boln_s h1ope/yomes. tewo/m do/h3r_ me/g'h2 h1e/sti. pik'to/m do/h3r_ h1a/yso gwe/rHtores h1sme/s. h2sowso/m nos g'e/nh1mn_ we/sni h1ope/ye. me/g'h2s-kwe-ge h1we/sus-kwe h1sye/h1s.

nu h1n_/gwney de/ywe do/h3r_ h1ddhi/. h2sowso/ns-kwe h3o/sdons h1ddhi/ lukdhi/. h2e/Hsh2m_-kwe h2le/kse to/ns ph2sdhi/. gwhr_nudhi/ nos h1i/d me: mr_ye/h1t. nu deywo/ybhos lewbho/s h1e/si.

h2e/wsos pi/k'ta diwe/s dhu/gh2ter lukdhi/. h1ye/h2 me/g'h2 h1re/gwos h2epo/ g'he/ys. gwo:/s h2e/g'rom gwowsth2o:/d h1e/g'hs we/dhe. gwi/h3woyh1ent polh1e/wes h1sih1e/nt. wido/yh1ent te so/h2wl_-kwe te se/kwetor.

h1ludhe/t gho/stis do:/m h1e/y-kwe tr_sye/t k'e/mh2t-kwe. po/tis h1i/m kwe/k't h1e/smey-kwe h2e/g't. gho/stey sddhi/. me:/ms h1ddhi/ we/yh1onm_-kwe pi/ph3e. se/dyonts h1e/y h1e:/dt pe/h3t-kwe. nu swe/pnos h1i/m h1e/pt. h1e/y h2use/si ste/h2t h2epo/-kwe gwe/h2t. nu po/tis h1e/smey do/h3r_ de/h3t.

gho/stis h1e:/dt pe/h3t-kwe. nu mle/wHt. nu gho/stey teto/rpth2e. kwi/s h1e/si? ku/dhe h1e/ysi? me/h2te:r toy gwi/h3weti? poh2imne/s suHnu/s h1e/smi. bhre/h2tr_s moy do:/m h1e/ymi. me/h2te:r moy gwi/h3weti ph2te:/r moy ne gwi/h3weti. se/desi moy l_ghdhi/ kwse/ps-kwe toy kwye/h1tim bhe/re:ti. nu h1e/y le/ght.

po/tnih2 gho/stim sode/yet h1e/smey-kwe wo/dr_ bhe/ret. nu h1e/y ste/wdt. h1i/h2 h1i/m h2wl_/h1neh2h1 wose/yet h1e/y-kwe gwhe/rt. nu h1i/h2 pe/yh2tu h2re/ydhet me:/ms-kwe pe/kwet. nu gho/stis we/wket. k'o/s moy do:/m priHo/s h1e/sti. h1e/y polh1u/h2 ye/h1o:r sth2e/h1yoyh1t.

kwe/syo suHnu/s h1e/si? ph2tre/s toy ghoste/ys suHnu/s h1e/smi. so/ moy se/desi h1e:/dt. k'o/s do:/m h1e/smey h1e/k'wom de/h3t h1e/y-kwe kwe/kwlom h1a/yt. nu te g'n_ne/h3mi priHo/s-kwe moy h1e/si. ph2we/ni moy h1ddhi/ de/h3nom-kwe toy h1ope/yo:h2. ye/wgmn_ do/mh1e h1ente/r ne Hrune/petor.

po/tis ghoste/yey yugo/m pe/ruti de/h3t. de/h3rom bhe/ronts gho/stis po/teys do:/m dhg'hye/s h2ew h1ludhe/t. nu h1e/y h1i/d spe/k'yet me/wdt-kwe h1e/y-kwe se/kwt. do/h3r_ moy de/ms h1e/g'hs gwe/h2t de/h3nom-kwe moy do:/m h2ew gwegwo/me. sddhi/ k'o/m-kwe h1ddhi/. nu to/h1 gwr_h3e/ta:m.

ph2te/rh1e k'o/m sdta:/m we/yh1onm_-kwe ph3ta:/m. k'o/s h1e/nosmey h2e/rh3trom de/h3t h1e/nos k'o/smey ne/h2wm_ h1a/yt. nu k'o/s we/wket. ye/wgmn_ do/mh1e h1ente/r h1e/stu. suHne/wes nos priHo:/s h1sih1e/nt. bhoydhe/h2m kwis me: Hrune/pt.

gho/stis deywo/ybhos priHo/s h1e/sti. h1yo/s swo/m gho/stim more/yeti so/ wl_/kwos h1e/sti h2ne:/r-kwe ne h1e/sti. h1e/smey kwis pe/h2wr_ wo/dr_ se/dos-kwe ne de/h3eti. se/dos wl_/kwo:y ne h1e/sti. kwi/s h1i/m h2le/kseti? dye:/ws h1i/m h2le/kseti. deywo:/s h1i/m dedr_k'e:/r.

h1regwesye/t gho/stis-kwe h3re:g'e/s dhe/h1mn_ gwe/mt. nu h3re:/g's ste/h2t h1e/syo-kwe h2r_Hmo/m h1e/pt h1i/m-kwe h1n_do/ we/dhet h1i/m-kwe se/desi sode/yet. nu h3re:/g'nih2 h1e/smey me:/ms me/lit-kwe bhe/ret. nu h1e/y h2e/k'h3t se/h2t-kwe. we/wket h3re:/g's. l_ghdhi/. h1yo/d h2e/wso:s le/wketi h1no/mn_ moy toy wr_h1dhi/.

dye/w ph2ter h1yo/s gho/stim h2le/ksesi h1i/m ste/yghi ph2sdhi/. dhe/g'hom me/h2ter h1i/m bhe/re. h1e/syo ste/yks h3reg'to/s h1sye/h1t h2ste:/r-kwe h1e/smey ste/yghm_ woyde/yoyh1t so/h2wl_-kwe h1i/m gwhr_nuye/h1t. wl_/kwos h1i/m me: lm_bhye/h1t. swo/m do:/m gwm_ye/h1t h1e/syo-kwe me/h2te:r h1i/m wide/h1yoyh1t.

gho/stis ste/h2t po/tis-kwe h1i/h1 gwe/h2t we/wket-kwe. k'o/d moy pe/rwr_ h2e/g'roy sth2e/h1yeti h1e/nos toy ste/yks h1e/sti. kwih1te/y h1idhi/. k'o/m do:/m memn_dhi/. h1yo/d moy do:/m h2ew gwe/mesi sepelyo:/h2 te.

dye/w ph2ter h1me/ k'ludhi/. dhe/g'hom me/h2ter k'le/wse. k'o/ymosyo moy h1e/k'wom ne ste/h2yom. h1yo/d dhro/wghom we/rh1oh2 dye/w me gwhn_ye/h1s dhe/g'ho:m-kwe me me: h2e/loyh1t. h1yo/d h1so/nt we/rh1oh2 gwi/h3woyh1m_ h2e/wsosm_-kwe wide/h1yoyh1m_ dhe/h1mn_-kwe moy sth2e/h1yoyh1t.

h2ne:/r ph2we/ni se/dt h1o/ytom-kwe dhe/h1t. so/h2wl_ me dedo/rk'e pe/h2wr_ me k'le/wt wo/dr_ me wo/yde. kwe/kwlom ne ste/h2yom. h1yo/d dhro/wghom bhe/h2oh2 pe/h2wr_ me h1e/wsoyh1t wo/dr_ me gwr_h3o/yh1t so/h2wl_-kwe me me: kwk'ye/h1t.

wiHro/s h1o/ytom dhe/h1t h1i/m-kwe Hrune/pt. deywo:/s h1i/m wido/nt. nu h1e/syo h2e/lbhit ne/k't h1e/syo-kwe dhe/h1mn_ se/ngwt h1e/syo-kwe g'e/nh1mn_ lelo/ykwe. nu me/rtis h1i/m le/mbht h1i/m-kwe kwis ne sepelye/t. h1n_gwni/s h1o/ytom pe/h2sti. wiHro/s me/rt h1o/ytos ne me/rt.

h1o/ytom dhe/h1esi? h1i/m dhe/h1oh2. kwo/smo:d bhebho/yh2th2e? diwe/s ph2tre/s bhebho/yh2h2e. h1e/y kudhto/m dedo/rk'e. h1yo/d h1i/m dhrughyo:/h2 gwhn_ye/h1t me g'e/nh1mn_-kwe moy me: h2uksye/h1t.

po/tey moy bhoydhe/h2m k'le/wse. h2o/wins toy yo/h1r_ pe/h2soh2. h1i/h2n_s wo/dr_ we/dho:h2 h1i/h2n_s-kwe do:/m kwse/pi h2e/g'o:h2 kwse/pi-kwe bhe/wdho:h2 wr_g'yo:/h2-kwe toy. h1yo/d wl_/kwos h2o/wim le/mbheti h2o/wim toy h1ope/yo:h2. h1yo/d te dhrughyo:/h2 deywo:/s me gwhnih1e/nt. misdho/m moy dh3dhi/.

h3re/g' h1so/nt we/rh1oh2. k'wo/nm_ we/dhontm_ h2ne/rm_ k'o:/ys h3kwbhi/s h2e/g'ro:d wido/m. h1e/y moy bhre/h2te:r ne h1e/sti h1e/smey-kwe priHo/s ne h1e/smi. h1yo/d kwe/k'm_ to/d we/rh1yoh2. h1yo/d dhro/wghom bhe/h2oh2 h3o/kwih1 moy so/h2wl_ me: wide/h1yoyh1ta:m h2e/wso:s-kwe moy me: lukye/h1t.

h1yo/s k'o/m h1o/ytom Hrune/peti. h1e/syo h2e/g'roy me: h2wr_sye/h1t. h1e/syo h2e/lbhit me: h2uksye/h1t. h1e/syo dhugh2te/res lelikwih1e/nt. pe/h2wr_ h1e/syo dhe/h1mn_ h1e/wsoyh1t. pe/rkwunos h1i/m gwhn_ye/h1t. swe/pnos h1i/m me: gwm_ye/h1t. h1e/syo-kwe kwis h1no/mn_ me: sepelyo/yh1t. h1yo/s h1i/m pe/h2seti do:/m to/smey sth2e/h1yoyh1t.

h3re/g' k'a:/m bhoydhe/h2m dhe/h1m_. pro/ h1e/yoh2. me: bhugye/h1m_. me/roh2 we/noh2-we: n_/dhgwhitom k'le/wos. dye/w me dedo/rk'th2e. h1yo/d bhe/wgoh2 dhe/g'ho:m me gwr_h3o/yh1t. h1no/mn_ moy k'luto/m bhu/Heti.

bhre/h2terh1e h3re/g'm_ h2e/nti h1sta:/m. k'o/s h2e/g't. k'o/y moy gwo/wes h1se/nti. h1e/nos mle/wHt. h1e/yes toy ne h1se/nti h1e/yes moy h1se/nti. nu se/kwt. gwo/wes swo/m po/tim wide:/r. h1i/ns HrudHto/m. nu k'o/s Hre/wdHt ne-kwe gwh2e/nt. h1e/nos Hre/wdHt h1e/syo-kwe wo/kwm_ k'luwe/nt h1i/m-kwe gwh2e/nt. nu h1e/nosmey we/wket. h1e/yes toy h1se/nti.

gwe/nh2h1e h3re/g'm_ h1r_sk'e/ta:m skwta:/m-kwe. h2o/wis moy h1e/sti. nu h2e/g't. po/h2imen h2o/wim dwi/s bhiddhi/. nu k'e/h2 mle/wHt. h1i/h2m_ bhiddhi/. h1e/neh2 Hre/wdHt. h1i/h2m_ me: bhe/yds. h1i/h2 k'o/syeh2ey h1e/stu h1i/h2-kwe gwi/h3wetu. nu we/wket. h1e/syeh2s po/tnih2 h1e/si. h1i/h2m_ h1a/yso.

k'o/ymoh1 h3re/g'm_ h1r_sk'e/ta:m. k'o/s h1e/nosyo k'wo/nm_ gwhe/nt. nu bhe/h2t. h1yo/d gho/stis k'wo/nm_ gwhe/neti dwo/h1 k'wo/nh1e de/deh3tu. h1yo/d bhre/h2te:r bhre/h2tr_s k'wo/nm_ more/ye:ti k'wo/nm_ h1ope/yetu. h1e/smey de/k'os dhedhh1dhi/. nu bhre/h2terh1e h2ew bhuHta:/m.

gwe:/n h3re/g'm_ h1r_sk'e/t h2e/g't-kwe. h1e/k'wos moy lelo/ykwe. nu poh2imne/y mle/wHt. h1i/m se/h2gye. nu poh2ime:/n h1i/m ste/yghm_ e/nu se/h2gyet h1i/m-kwe h2n_re/s kaghyo/y wide/t. nu se/kwt. h1e/k'wos toy kaghyo/y kwi/h1 h1e/sti? h1i/m ste/yghi wido/m. gwne/h2ey mle/wHs? h1e/syeh2ey ne mle/wHm_. gwne/h2ey h1i/m h2ew dh3dhi/. h1yo/s wide:/ti ne-kwe bhe/h2eti so/ ste/h2yeti.

swe/k'uros g'm_ro/s-kwe h3re/g'm_ h2e/nti h1sta:/m. swe/k'uros h2e/g't. h1e/y moy h1we/dmn_ ne bhe/ret. g'm_/re h1we/dmn_ h1ope/yes? de/k'm_ gwo:/s h1ope/yom. kwi/s h1i/d wide/t? h1i/d wido/m. g'm_/re h1so/nt we/rh1s swe/k'ure dhro/wghom bhe/h2s. g'm_ro/m toy sepelye/.

wiHro/s h3re/g'm_ h1r_sk'e/t bhe/h2t-kwe. bhre/h2tr_s moy gwo/wes me/ne h2e/lbhit h2k'h3e/nt. kwi/d moy de/deh3eti? nu se/kwt. h2e/lbhit toy de/deh3tu kaghyo/m-kwe de/metu. kaghyo/m kwye/h1tim pe/h2sti. nu h1e/y kaghyo/m de/met.

k'o/ymo:s moy we/kwos k'le/wsete. h1yo/s h2e/rh3trom ste/h2ye:ti so/ tri/h2 h2e/rh3treh2 de/deh3tu. h1yo/s kwe/kwlom ste/h2ye:ti so/ swe/k's kwe/kwlons bhe/retu. h1yo/s dhe/h1mn_ h1e/wse:ti so/ pe/nkwe dhe/h1mn_h2 de/metu. h1yo/s h1le/wdherom wiHro/m more/ye:ti so/ k'm_to/m gwo:/s de/h3tu. h1yo/s h3re/g'm_ more/ye:ti so/ g'he/slom gwo:/s de/h3tu. h1yo/d-kwe ne de/deh3eti h1e/syo se/dos h1e/poh2.

k'o/s h1e/nosyo ne/h2wm_ Hrune/pt. nu h3re:/g's se/kwt. h1yo/d kwis swo/syo bhre/h2tr_s ne/h2wm_ sle/h2gweti ne/h2us-kwe se/ngweti so/ h1e/smey ne/h2wm_ h1ope/yetu. h1yo/d bhre/h2te:r h1i/h2m_ de/h3eti h1i/h2-kwe se/ngweti me: kwid de/h3t. ne/h2us bhre/h2tr_s h1e/sti.

ke/h3tus polh1u/h2 ye/h1o:r do/mh1e h1ente/r h1e/st. nu to/h1 k'o/m gwm_ta:/m. k'o/s h1e/nosmey h2e/g't. bhre/h2te:r toy moy g'e/nh1mn_ more/yet bhre/h2te:r-kwe moy toy g'e/nh1mn_ gwhe/nt. wo/sn_ h1a/yso wo/sn_-kwe-ge h1a/yoh2er. kwye/h1tis n_sme/y h1e/seti. nu we/yh1onm_ k'o/m ph3ta:/m.

ye/mHoh1 h2e/g'roy h1sta:/m. kwi/d k'o/yme h2isk'o/mes? ph2tre/s nos h2e/g'rom h2isk'o/mes. h2e/g'ros me/g'h2s h1e/sti bhre/h2terh1e-kwe h1swo/s. swo/m bhre/h2term_ me: stundye/h1m_. dhe/g'ho:m noh3 me/h2te:r h2e/leti. nu h1e/nos mle/wHt. h1so/nt we/rh1s. se/domes k'o/m-kwe pe/h3omes. nu to/h1 si/sdeta:m ph3ta:/m-kwe.

h3re/g'h1e pe/runi g'n_h3ta:/m. nu k'o/s h2e/g't. ke/h3tus nos do/mh1e h1ente/r h1e/st. kwye/h1tis n_sme/y h1e/stu. so/h2wl_ nos wide/h1yoyh1t. h1yo/s bhoydhe/h2m Hrune/peti me/rtis to/m le/mbhtu. nu h1e/nos se/kwt. bhoydhe/h2m dhe/h1m_ h1i/h2-kwe sth2e/h1yoyh1t. nu wo/yk'oh1 priHo/h1 bhuHta:/m.

suHnu/h1 pro/ti sth2ta:/m. nu me/h2te:r h1ente/r ste/h2t we/wket-kwe. me/d g'eg'n_h1e/. h2e/lom wos. su/Hnuh1 me stundto/m. swo/m kwis bhre/h2term_ me: more/yet. nu to/h1 h1i/h2m_ k'luta:/m dhg'he/mi-kwe sdta:/m. kwye/h1tis to/h1 gwm_ye/t.

ke/h3tus do/mh1e me/g'h2s h1ente/r h1e/st. nu to/h1 k'o/m sdta:/m. suHno/wey moy toy dhugh2te/rm_ dh3dhi/ h1we/dmn_-kwe toy de/k'm_ h1e/k'wons h1ope/yo:h2. dhugh2te/rm_ toy moy de/h3m_. dhugh2te:/r moy toy snuso/s h1e/seti. k'o/ymo:s h1e/somes. nu ke/h3tus ne/k't.

k'o/ymo:s k'le/wsete. ke/h3tus wiHro/ns gwr_h3e/t. dhe/g'ho:m gwhn_to/ns k'e/letu kwye/h1tis-kwe wos k'le/ytreh2 h1e/stu. ke/h3tus le/ghtu. h2e/rh3trom tl_nh2te/ h2e/g'rom-kwe h2e/rh3yete. k'o/ymoh1 gwi/h3woyh1ta:m.

dye:/ws gho/stim h2le/kseti. h1i/m sepelye/ deywo:/s-kwe te sepelyo/nti. pe/h2wr_ gho/stim gwhr_ne/wti dhe/h1mn_-kwe h1i/m h2e/leti. h1yo/s gho/stim h2epo/ h2e/g'eti so/ deywo/ns h2epo/ h2e/g'eti. kwi/s wo/yde? gho/stis deywo/s h1sye/h1t.

h1so/nt we/kwos sth2e/h1yeti dhro/wghos se/ngwt. h1i/d h3reg'to/m h1e/sti dhro/wghos k'leywo/s h1e/sti. h2n_re/s we/kwos ye/wgmn_ h1e/sti. h1we/su k'le/wos h1so/nt we/kwos se/kwetor.

h1yo/s ste/h2yeti kwse/ps to/m k'e/leti so/h2wl_-ge h1i/m dedo/rk'e. h1yo/s h1e/k'wons h2epo/ h2e/g'eti k'wo:/ to/m g'n_ne/h3ti. wl_/kwos kwse/pi gwm_ye/ti poh2ime:/n bhe/wdheti. ste/h2yonts h2ne:/r h1e:/dti ne-kwe teto/rpe.

de/h3rom de/h3rom h2isk'e/ti. h1yo/s de/deh3ti so/ h1a/yto. h1yo/s ne de/deh3ti so/ ne h1a/yto. do/h3r_ ne ne/k't. de/h3nom h1use/ws k'r_de/s lewbho/m h1e/sti. h1use/wo:m de/h3to:r priHo/s h1e/sti.

bhre/h2te:r bhre/h2tr_s h2r_Hmo/s h1e/sti. h1yo/s bhre/h2term_ more/yeti so/ swo/m h2r_Hmo/m skine/dti. h2r_Hmo/h1 h2n_re/y h1ste/s bhre/h2terh1e de/mey h1ste/s. somHe/h2 h2o/wis wl_/kwosyo me:/ms h1e/sti.

yugo/m dwo/h1 h1e/k'woh1 yune/gti to/h1 k'o/m dr_mta:/m bhoydhe/h2 dwo/h1 do/mh1e yune/gti. h1yo/d bhoydhe/h2 Hrune/petor ke/h3tus to/h1 gwm_ye/ti. bhoydhe/h2m ph2sdhi/.

h3re:/g's wiHro:/m poh2ime:/n h1e/sti. poh2ime:/n h2o/wins wl_/kwo:d pe/h2sti h3re:/g's h1i/ns dhro/wgho:d h2le/kseti. h3re:g'e/s we/kwos h3reg'to/s ste/yks h1e/sti. h1yo/s we/kwos h3rog'e/yeti so/ h3re:/g's h1e/sti. h1yo/s k'leywo/m we/kwos bhe/h2ti so/ h3re:/g's ne h1e/sti.

gwo/wes m_re/nt k'o/ymo:s m_re/nt wiHro/s me/rt. h1we/su k'le/wos ne me/rt. h1yo/s h1we/su k'le/wos we/neti h1e/syo h1no/mn_ gwi/h3weti. so/h2wl_ h2ew gwm_ye/ti h1e/y h2ew ne gwm_ye/ti.

pr_h2wo/s h2ne:/r yemHo/s-kwe bhre/h2terh1e h1sta:/m. h2o/wins gwo:/s-kwe h2e/g'rom k'o/m h2e/g'eta:m ste/rh3mn_-kwe pr_kwe/wi Hn_dhi/ wide/ta:m. pe/rkwus g'e/rh2onts h1e/st. nu h2ne:/r h2e/Hsh2m_ h2e/n de/met. ph2we/ney g'huto/m me/lit h1ope/yet. deywo:/s h1e/smey wr_h1e/nt. ne/mos wr_g'dhi/. lewbho/m toy yemHo/m h1pdhi/. h1e/yso:m we/kwos k'le/wt h1e/syo-kwe k'e:/r tre/mt. nu h1e/pt yemHo/m h1i/m-kwe gwhe/nt. yemHo/s ne Hre/wdHt. to/d pr_h2wo/m ne/mos h1e/st. h2Hse/h2i se/dt ne-kwe bhe/h2t. nu dhe/g'ho:m yemHo/syo me/msos me/lh3t. dye:/ws h1e/syo k'r_h2sne/s kwr_to/. h1e/y pl_th2u/s h1e/st. nu mo/ri h1e/syo h1sh2e/ns bhwe/h2t. h2n_re/y-kwe h1we/su k'le/wos dh3e/nt. h1ludhe/ys g'e/nh1to:r bhu/Ht. nu yemHo/syo h1no/mn_ k'luto/m bhu/Ht.

yemHo/s str_h3me/ni k'e/yo. deywo:/s h1ludho/nt h1i/m-kwe pe/ri sde/nt. nu h1i/m skl_He/nt. me:/ms h3sth1bho/s luHe/nt. dhe/g'ho:m h1e/smo:d wr_g'e/nt h3e/sth1h2-kwe Hrumpe/nt. nu h2e/k'monn_s h1e/ymos kwre/nt. kre/wh2s h1e/g'hs dhe/nh2t. nu h2e/kwa:s h1e/smo:d dhh1ke/nt. h1e/syo k'e/rh2sonm_ skide/nt. nu dye:/m h1e/smo:d te/tk'n_t. lo/wksneh2 h1e/syo me/nesos h1ne/dht. nu n_bhro:/s h1e/syo h2k'ru/bhos ml_h3e/nt. nu h2we/rst. so/h2wl_ upe/r dhh1e/nt h2ste/rn_s-kwe kwse/pm_ Hih1ke/nt. nu swo/m we/rg'om kwk'e/nt. h1i/d-kwe h1we/su h1e/st. h1e/syeh2i ste/yghont. h2e/kwa:s ph3e/nt mude/nt-kwe.

nu h2ne:/r yemHo/m ste/rh3mn_ we/dhet. yemHo/s ste/rh3mn_ spe/k'yet. bhe/h2t. pe/h2wr_ pe/rkwum-kwe dedo/rk'h2e. h2o/weyes ku/dhe h1se/nti gwo/wes ku/dhe h1se/nti? h2ne:/r we/rh1t. bhre/h2ter ne/mos h1e/si. nu yemHo/s bhe/yh2t. ne bhe/wgt str_h3me/ni-kwe le/ght. nu more/yet h2ne:/r h1i/m. h1e/syo k'e:/r tre/mt. h1e/syo h1e/sh2r_ ste/rh3mn_ h1e/g'hs dhe/nh2t. nu dhe/g'ho:m h1i/d pe/h3t. nu h1regwesye/t. h2ste/res luke/nt. nu h2ne:/r h2e/n ste/h2t. swo/m bhre/h2term_ ne wide/t.

h2ne:/r h2Hse/h2i se/dt Hre/wdHt-kwe. bhre/h2ter ku/dhe h1e/si? nu wo:/kws Hn_dhi/ h1e/rt. nu h1i/h2 bhe/h2t. me: Hre/wdHs. dhe/g'ho:m h1e/smi bhe/roh2-kwe te. h2e/loh2 te. me/lit toy sokwo/m-kwe de/deh3mi. h2wl_/h1neh2h1 te wose/yoh2. dye:/ws moy k'e/rh2so: h1e/sti. lo/wksneh2 moy me/nos h1e/sti. n_bhro:/s moy h2e/k'ruh2 h1se/nti. wo/dr_ moy pi/ph3e. h2e/ndhesh2 moy h1ddhi/. h1yo/d me/resi h3e/sth1h2 toy k'e/loh2. me: bhe/yh2s. bhre/h2te:r toy h1e/smi. wo/kwm_ k'le/wt. nu h1e/syo k'e:/r me/wdt. nu ste/h2t.

kwe/syo me:/ms dhe/g'ho:m h1e/sti? dhe/g'ho:m yemHo/syo me:/ms h1e/sti. kwe/syo h3e/sth1h2 h2e/k'mones h1se/nti? h2e/k'mones h1e/syo h3e/sth1h2 h1se/nti. kwe/syo h1e/sh2r_ mo/ri h1e/sti? mo/ri h1e/syo h1e/sh2r_ h1e/sti. kwe/syo k'e/rh2so: dye:/ws h1e/sti? dye:/ws h1e/syo k'e/rh2so: h1e/sti. kwe/syo me/nos lo/wksneh2 h1e/sti? lo/wksneh2 h1e/syo me/nos h1e/sti. kwi/s yemHo/m gwhe/nt? h2ne:/r yemHo/m gwhe/nt. kwi/d pr_h2wo/m ne/mos h1e/st? yemHo/s pr_h2wo/m ne/mos h1e/st. kwi/d yemHo:/d loykwo/m h1e/sti? n_/dhgwhitom k'le/wos h1e/smo:d loykwo/m h1e/sti.

deywo:/s h2Hse/h2i sde/nt. ste/rh3mn_ pr_kwe/wi k'e/yo. nu h2ne:/r yemHo/m pe/h2wr_ we/dhet. h1i/m str_h3me/ni loghe/yont ph2we/ney-kwe g'huto/m me/lit h1ope/yont. nu h1e/smey sokwo/m h1ope/yont. h1i/d dhe/gwhet me:/ms-kwe pe/kwet. se/dyontes h1e/yes h1i/d h1e/dn_t kre/wh2s-kwe ph3e/nt. h2ne:/r sth2e/h1yet h3e/sth1h2-kwe spe/k'yet. h3e/sth1h2 h1e/smi k'e/yo. h3e/sth1h2 le/yg'ht. nu h2e/k'mones h3sth1bho/s bhuh2e/nt. mude/nt. h1e/smey we/wkont. pr_h2wo/m ne/mos we/rg's. nu sth2e/nt. nu h2we/st.

h2ne:/r bhe/h2t. bhre/h2term_ moy h1e/pm_. h1i/m h2e/g'rom we/dhom. h1i/m more/yom. dhe/g'ho:m dye:/ws-kwe h1e/smo:d kwrn_to/. k'e:/r moy tre/mti. ku/dhe h1e/sti? h1e/nod mo/ri h1e/syo h1e/sh2r_ h1e/sti. k'e/h2 lo/wksneh2 h1e/syo me/nos h1e/sti. h2ste/rn_s spe/k'yoh2. h2ste/res me spe/k'yonti. nu ste/yghoh2. h1e:/dmi pi/ph3oh2. bhre/h2ter me k'le/wsesi? bhe/royh1s me. nu dhe/g'ho:m h1i/m bhe/ret.

dye:/ws gwhn_to/m yemHo/m wide/t Hre/wdHt-kwe. h1e/syo h2e/k'ruh2 me/g'h2h2 h1e/st. nu n_bhro:/s h1e/syo h2k'ru/bhos ml_h3e/nt. h2o/weyes tr_syo/nt. nu h2we/rst. dhe/g'ho:m wo/dr_ pe/h3t. nu ple/h1t. nu mre/y ple/h1dhet. h2e/ndhesh2 bhwe/h2t. nu sno/ygwhos tine/h1t. h2o/weyes h2e/ndhesh2 h1e/dn_t Hwr_dhe/nt-kwe. nu h1i/d ph3e/nt. h1yo/d Hre/wdHeti h2we/rst. nu dhe/g'ho:m h2e/g'mn_ h1i/h1 h2e/leti. h2ne/res h2e/g'rom h2e/rh3yonti. k'o/d we/rh1yonti.

yemHo/s h2n_re/y bhe/h2t. bhre/h2ter me h1pdhi/ tm_h1dhi/-kwe me. me:/ms moy dhe/g'ho:m bhuHye/h1t. h3e/sth1h2 moy h2e/k'mones bhuh2ye/h1t. h1e/sh2r_ moy mo/ri bhuHye/h1t. k'e/rh2so: moy dye:/ws bhuh2ye/h1t. h1no/mn_ moy k'luto/m h1sye/h1t. h2ne:/r yemHo/syo we/kwos k'le/wt Hre/wdHt-kwe. h1e/syo k'e:/r tre/mt. nu h2e/k'monm_ sle/h2gwt. nu h1i/m h1i/h1 te/mh1t. dhe/g'ho:m dye:/ws-kwe h1e/smo:d ml_h3ta:/m. nu deywo:/s yemHo:/y k'luto/m h1no/mn_ dhh1e/nt. le/ytt h2ste/rn_s-kwe spe/k'yet.

deywo:/s gwhn_to/m yemHo/m wido/nt. h1e/smey wr_h1e/nt. h2ne/r toy bhre/h2term_ me: gwhe/ns. gwo:/s nos dh3dhi/. ne/mos h1se/ntu. nu h1e/smey bhoydhe/h2m kwre/nt. nu h2ne:/r h1i/ns pe/h2wr_ h2e/g'et. h2e/n stn_h2e/nt. h1e/smey g'huto/m me/lit h1ope/yet h1e/smey-kwe sokwo/m h1ope/yet. h2e/g't. h1i/ns h1a/ydhwe. h1e/smey h1we/suh2 de/dh3n_t. nu h1e/syo k'e:/r me/wdt. nu h2ne/res h1e/ymos gwo:/s de/dh3n_ti.

dye:/ws dhe/g'ho:m-kwe k'o/m k'e/yro. suHne/wes h1ente/r tk'iye/nt. tr_me/nt. nu Hwr_dhe/nt. nu me/g'h2s suHnu/s ste/h2t. h1i/ns spe/k'yet bhe/h2t-kwe. dye:/m u/d tl_ne/h2omes. h1i/h2m_ ni/ sode/yo:mes. sth2e/h1yontes h1e/yes h1i/m u/d tl_nh2e/nt. h1i/h2m_-kwe ni/ sode/yont. nu le/wkt. nu h3mi/ghleh2 le/ytt. so/h2wl_ kwk'e/nt mude/nt-kwe. h1e/y upe/r sth2e/h1yet h1i/h2 Hn_dhi/ k'e/yo. so/h2wl_ h1ente/r h1e/yti.

dye:/ws dhg'he/mi k'e/yo. h2ne/res h1e/smi Hn_dhi/ k'e/yro. ne sth2e/nt. tre/sont. h1e/yso:m k'e/rdh2 tre/mt. me/g'h2 te/wh2os wiHro:/y h1e/st. h2e/k'monm_ h1e/pt h1i/m-kwe tl_ne/h2t. nu dye:/m h1i/h1 stune/dt. h2e/k'mo: Hrumpto/. nu wi/ bhidta:/m. h1e/y u/d gwe/h2t h1i/h2-kwe le/ght. nu k'woyto/s h1ente/r gwe/mt. sth2e/nt. so/h2wl_ kwk'e/nt mude/nt-kwe. nu wiHro/syo h1no/mn_ k'luto/m bhu/Ht. wiHro/syo we/rg'om we/rh1yonti.

dhh2bhro/s dye:/m upe/r tn_ne/wt dhe/g'ho:m-kwe Hn_dhi/ loghe/yet. h1i/h2 pl_th2e/wih2 h1e/st. nu h3mi/ghleh2m h1ente/r dhe/h1t. h2ste/rn_s kwse/pm_ Hye/h1kt lo/wksneh2m-kwe upe/r dhe/h1t. sh2we/ns kwe/kwlo:y HeHk'u/ns h1e/k'wons ye/wgt. nu sh2we/ns h3reg'to/m sto/yghom h3rog'e/yet. nu sh2we/ns kwe/kwlos h3mi/ghleh2i h1e/yti. h1e/k'wo:s kwe/kwlom duke/nti. nu so/h2wl_ se/ngwt. nu dhh2bhro/s swo/m we/rg'om kwe/k't. dhh2bhro/s se/dt. dhh2bhro/syo we/rg'om me/g'h2 h1e/sti.

suHne/wes HrudHe/nt. dye/w ph2ter u/d gwh2dhi/. dhe/g'hom me/h2ter Hn_dhi/ l_ghdhi/. so/h2wl_ ne dedr_k'me/. tr_mme/s. dedwime/. h1e/y k'le/wt u/d-kwe gwe/h2t. nu h1i/h2 le/ght. h3mi/ghleh2 le/ytt. nu le/wkt. k'woyto/m kwk'e/nt mude/nt-kwe. h2ew bhh2e/nt. ph2ter h1use/wo:m de/h3tor upe/r sth2e/h1ye. me/h2ter nos bhe/re.

dye:/ws ku/dhe h1e/sti? upe/r h1e/sti. dhe/g'ho:m ku/dhe h1e/sti? Hn_dhi/ h1e/sti. kwi/d h1ente/r h1e/sti? h3mi/ghleh2 h1ente/r h1e/sti so/h2wl_-kwe h1ente/r h1e/yti. kwi/s h1i/m u/d tl_ne/h2t? me/g'h2s suHnu/s h1i/m u/d tl_ne/h2t. kwe/syo suHnu/s h1e/st? diwe/s dhg'hme/s-kwe suHnu/s h1e/st.

dye:/ws dhg'hme/y wo/dr_ de/deh3ti. h1i/d pi/ph3eti. nu h2e/g'mn_ h2o/wins-kwe h2e/leti. poh2ime:/n h1i/d h2e/g'rom h2e/g'eti. h2o/wins h2me/lg'ti. h2wl_/h1neh2 h1i/h2mos bhuHye/ti. h2ne/res h2wl_/h1neh2h1 wose/yontor. k'wo:/ h1i/h2n_s h2le/kseti. k'wo:/ poh2imne/y lewbho/s h1e/sti. wl_/kwos ne gwm_ye/ti. nu g'hwe:/res k'une/s bhuge/nt. ph2ter nos me/h2ter h1we/suh2 dh3to/m. wo/dr_ nos h2wl_/h1neh2m-kwe h1ope/yetom. polh1e/wes h1se/ntu.

deywo:/s pe/h2wr_ mo/rtoybhos k'e/lont. g'hime/ni stude/nt me:/ms-kwe ne pe/kwont. nu bho:/r h1ludhe/t. nu pe/h2wr_ deywo:/m h2Hse/h2i ste/h2yonts h1e/y h1ludhe/yey h1i/d bhe/ret. kwse/ps h1i/m k'e/let. nu h1i/ns dre/mt. bho:/r HeHk'u/s h1e/st. h1i/d h1e/yso:m h2Hse/h2su dhe/gwhet gwhre/nt-kwe. mude/nt. pe/kwont h1e/dn_t-kwe. bhre/s h1no/mn_ k'luto/m h1e/sti.

pe/h2wr_ h2k'me/ni l_h2to/m h1e/st. h1i/d kwis ne g'n_ne/h3t. wiHro/s h1e/smi se/dt h1i/m-kwe h2k'm_ne/h1 stune/dt. nu h1i/d h1e/g'hs dhe/nh2t. tre/mt. nu me/wdt. me:/ms wo/dr_ Hwe/rt. ye/set le/wkt-kwe. nu h2ne/res h1i/d h1ludho/nt. h1i/d pe/ri sde/nt. h1e/dn_t sh2e/nt-kwe.

h1n_gwni/s h1ludhe/ys h2Hse/h2i dhh1to/ h1e/yso:m-kwe gho/stis bhu/Ht. h1e/syeh2i se/dyet. h1rudhe/h1t. nu ne/mos h1e:/dt. h1e/smey me/lit h1ope/yont. h2e/n se/dyont h1i/m-kwe spe/k'yont. nu h1n_gwni/s h1e/ymos te/pos de/deh3t. h1e/ymos lewbho/s h1e/sti. h1n_gwni/s se/desos h1esh2o/s h1e/sti. h1n_gwni/s h1e/yso:m k'e/ywos h1e/sti. se/dos h2le/kseti. h1n_gwni/s h1use/wo:m de/h3to:r h1e/sti. h1yo/s h1n_gwni/m pe/h2sti h1n_gwni/s to/m pe/h2sti.

gwo/wes Hn_dhi/ h1n_dhe/nt. nu pr_h2wo/s h2ne:/r h1i/ns h1a/yto. h1i/ns h2e/g'rom h2e/g'et h1i/ns-kwe h2me/lg't. g'hime/ni pr_kwe/wi stn_h2e/nt. polh1e/wes bhuh2e/nt h2ukse/nt-kwe. g'hwe:/res bhuge/nt. nu wl_/kwos h1ludhe/t. nu wl_/kwos h1i/ns se/rpet. bhidhto/s k'wo:/ wl_/kwom kwe/k't. nu k'wo:/ wl_/kwom h2epo/ h1e/lh2t. h2ne:/r k'wo/nm_ de/mh2t. k'wo:/ h1e/syo k'e/ywos h1e/st. nu yugo:/y ye/wgos ye/wgt. nu k'wo:/ h2Hse/h2i se/dyeti. poh2ime:/n h1i/ns pe/h2sti.

h3re:/g's mo/rtoybhos ne h1e/st. nu h3re:/g's h1e/ymos dhh1to/. pr_h2wo/s h3re:/g's h1e/st. h3reg'to/m sto/yghom h3rog'e/yet wik'po/tibhos-kwe bhoydhe/h2m kwe/rt. nu h1le/wdherom h1le/wdhim pe/h2st. h2e/g'mn_ h1n_do/ h2e/g'et h1e/ymos-kwe h1we/suh2 h1ope/yet. wik'po/teyes h1e/syo we/kwesh2 k'luwe/nt. nu polh1e/wes bhuHe/nt. m_g'h2e/s h1e/syo te/wh2os h1e/st. g'e/nh1os h1e/smo:d me/lh3t. nu h1e/syo suHnu/s h3re:/g's bhwe/h2t.

yemHo/s pr_h2wo/s h3re:/g's h1e/st. h1ludhe/ys poh2ime:/n h1e/st. h1yo/d h3re:/g's h1e/st ne kwis me/rt. h2o/weyes ne m_re/nt. h2e/kweh2es ne tr_se/nt. h2ne/res ne tr_syo/nt. g'he/ymn_ ne gwe/mt. mo/rto:s polh1e/wes h1se/nt. h2ne/res h1e/dn_t sh2e/nt-kwe. nu pe/h2wr_ h1e/yso:m se/desu dhe/gwhet. so/h2wl_ h1e/ysu le/wkt. nu me/rtis h1e/smey gwe/mt. le/ght me/rt-kwe. nu h1le/wdhis Hre/wdHt.

h1o/gwhis bhe/rg'hi k'e/yo. gwo:/s h1e/pt h2o/wins-kwe gwr_h3e/t. h2ne/res tre/sont. ne h1e/dn_t. nu wiHro/s bhe/rg'hm_ HeHk'u/bhis h1e/k'wo:ys h1ludhe/t. nu h1o/gwhim gwhe/nt. h1i/ns spe/rt h1i/ns-kwe bhr_g'he/s h2e/g'et. mude/nt. nu h1e/dn_t. h1e/gwhis h3e/sth1h2 h2e/g'roy k'e/yo. we/wkont. wi/Hre h1o/gwhim gwhe/ns. h1no/mn_ toy k'luto/m h1e/sti.

nu gwo/wes h2e/n sth2e/h1yoyh1ent me: bhugih1e/nt. wl_/kwos-ge h1e/ymos me: gwm_yo/yh1t. h2e/ngwhis bho:/r-kwe h1i/ns me: h1pih1ta:/m. k'wo/n h2o/wins h2e/g'mn_-kwe h2le/kse. po/h2imen h1i/ns h1n_do/ h2e/g'e. nu dye/w wo/dr_ dh3dhi/ dhe/g'hom h1i/ns h2e/le. nu polh1e/wes bhuHih1e/nt nu h2uksih1e/nt. n_/dhgwhitom k'le/wos h3re:g'e/y h1sye/h1t. h3re:g'e/s-ge h2e/g'mn_ me/g'h2 polh1u/-kwe h1sye/h1t.

yemHo/s le/ght ne-kwe ste/h2t. nu me/rt. pr_h2wo/s mo/rtos h1e/st. sto/yghom kwis ne g'n_ne/h3t. h1i/m wide/t h1i/m-kwe gwe/h2t. sto/yghos dl_h1gho/s h1e/st. ne kwe/lh1t. nu h1i/m se/kwonto. nu yemHo/s ne/k'uns sto/yghom we/dheti. n_k'e/wo:m h3re:/g's h1e/sti. h1e/syo h1no/mn_ k'luto/m h1e/sti. h1e/syo k'le/wos n_/dhgwhitom h1e/sti.

ne/k'ew sto/yghom gwh2dhi/ yemHo/m se/kweso. nu yemHo/s h1yo/m gwe/h2t to/m gwh2dhi/. dwo/h1 k'wo/nh1e sto/yghom h2le/ksetes. me:-ge bhe/yh2s nu ne/h2wm_ wik'dhi/. me: kwe/lh1s lewbho/s te h1n_gwni/s h1a/yih1to. dhe/g'ho:m-ge toy h3e/sth1h2 k'e/loyh1t. k'lute/h2 te h2e/wso:s wog'e/yoyh1t. nu toy k'le/wos n_/dhgwhitom h1sye/h1t.

me/h2te:r tre/mt. swo/m suHnu/m h2e/g'roy h2isk'e/t h1e/syo-kwe h1no/mn_ Hre/wdHt. su/Hnew moy ku/dhe h1e/si? wo:/kws ne gwe/mt. nu g'e/rh2ontm_ wide/t. pr_kwe/wi se/dyet. g'e/rh2ont h1i/m dedo/rk'th2e? g'e/rh2onts we/wket. sto/yghom h1yo/ntm_ h1i/m wido/m. yemHo/s h1i/m we/dhet. lelo/ykwe. me/h2te:r h2e/n le/ght.

ne/k'ewes wo/dr_ h1ye/nti. dhe/g'ho:m linkwe/nti. h3e/nHos ne bhore/yonti. ne bhh2e/nti. ne/h2us h2e/n stesto/h2e. ne/h2us h1ln_gwhre/h2 h1e/sti. ne/h2us sne/h2ti. ne/h2wi si/sdonti. ne/h2us h1i/ns we/g'heti. wo/dr_ pl_th2u/ h1e/sti. h2e/g'ros h2e/n h1e/sti. gwo/wes h2e/n sth2e/h1yonti. yemHo/s h2e/n se/dyeti h3re:/g's-kwe h1e/sti. gwm_sk'o/ntn_s ne/k'uns dedo/rk'e. h1e/ymos sto/lons de/deh3ti.

so/h2wl_ se/ngwt h2ew-kwe gwe/mt. lo/wksneh2 le/ytt h2ew-kwe h1ludhe/t. g'he/ymn_ le/ytt h2ew-kwe h1e/rt. mo/rtos gwe/h2t h2ew-kwe ne gwm_ye/ti. nu me/rtis h1e/smey gwe/mt. ne/k'ewes so/h2wl_ ne dedr_k'e:/r. h1e/syo n_/dhgwhitom k'le/wos loykwo/m h1e/sti. k'luto/m h1no/mn_ ne ne/k't. h1we/su k'le/wos h1yo/smey h1e/sti so/ ne me/rt.

g'e/rh2onts me/rt. h1e/syo po/tnih2 h1e/syo-kwe swe/so:r HrudHta:/m h1i/m-kwe str_h3me/ni loghe/yont. nu h1i/m pe/h2wr_ we/g'hont. g'r_h2n_te/s me:/ms h1e/wset. nu dhe/g'ho:m h1e/syo h3e/sth1h2 h1a/yto. we/wkont. ph2ter memn_me/. h1no/mn_ toy k'luto/m h1sye/h1t. nu h1e/smey g'huto/m me/lit h1ope/yont. h2Hse/h2i sde/nt.

wiHro/s me/rtim wide/t. we/wket. me/rtey ku/dhe h1e/ysi? me/rtis bhe/h2t. gwegwo/mh2e toy. ph2te/rm_ toy h1e/pm_. nu te kapyo/h2. wiHro/s we/wket. me: me h1e/ps. n_/dhgwhitom moy k'le/wos dh3dhi/. ne dedwo/yh2e. me/rtis we/wket. kapyo/h2 ne de/deh3mi. wiHro/s ne bhe/h2t. nu sto/yghom gwe/h2t.

h1yo/d kwse/ps gwm_ye:/ti mo/rtos le/ghyeti. supno/s h1i/m kapye/ti. nu bhe/wdht. h2e/wso:s h1i/m wog'e/yeti. ne/k'uns kwis ne wog'e/yeti. ne/k'ewes ne bhudhe/nt. ne/k'ewes dhg'he/mi k'e/yror. so/h2wl_ le/wkt h1i/d ne dedr_k'e:/r. supno/s mr_te/ys bhre/h2te:r h1e/sti.

dye/w nos ph2ter h1use/wo:m de/h3tor k'ludhi/. h1yo/s upe/r sth2e/h1yesi h1yo/s so/h2wl_ u/d dhe/h1s. ph2ter h1yo/s n_sme/y He/h2mr_ k'woyto/m-kwe de/h3s. nu-kwe toy h2e/wso:s dhugh2te:/r gwm_sk'e/ti. me/g'h2s te suHnu/s u/d tl_ne/h2t. n_bhro:/s toy mo/ri pl_nh1e/nti. k'le/wos toy n_/dhgwhitom h1e/sti. nu nos ph2ter we/kwos k'ludhi/. k'woyto/s toy n_sme/d me: n_k'ye/h1t. h1we/su-ge nos k'le/wos dh3dhi/. nu nos ph2ter n_sme/ h2e/g'mn_-kwe h2le/kse.

nu nos dhe/g'hom me/h2ter k'ludhi/. h1ye/h2 mo/rtons bhe/resi h1ye/h2 h2e/g'mn_ h2e/lesi. h1ye/h2 diwe/s wo/dr_ pi/ph3esi. gwhn_to/syo yemHo/syo lewbho/m me:/ms h1e/si. me/h2ter pe/rkwum h2e/ndhesh2-kwe bhe/resi. h2e/g'ro:s toy pl_th2e/wes h1se/nti. g'hwe:/res toy gwo/wes-kwe h2e/kwa:s pi/ph3onti. h2wl_/h1neh2m nos me/lit-kwe h1ope/ye. me/h2ter nos g'hwe:/rn_s-kwe bhe/royh1s. h1yo/d-kwe me/romes h3e/sth1h2-ge nos k'e/le.

sh2we/ns kwe/kwlos dye/wi h1e/yti. HeHk'e/wes-ge h1e/k'wo:s kwe/kwlom duke/nti. sh2we/ns kwe/kwlos HeHk'u/s h1e/sti. nu h2e/wso:s h2we/st nu so/h2wl_ le/wkt. so/h2wl_-ge kwse/pi se/ngwt He/h2m_ni-kwe le/wkt. nu sh2we/ns kwe/kwlos ne Hrumpto/r. h1e/syo sto/yghos h3reg'to/s h1e/sti. so/h2wl_-ge h2e/kwa:s gwhr_ne/wti. lewbho/m nos so/h2wl_ g'hwe:/rn_s-kwe dedo/rk'e. nu nos so/h2wl_ dedr_k'ye/h1t h1i/d nos k'luye/h1t.

le/wbha h2e/wsos diwe/s dhu/gh2ter h2usdhi/. nu h1ye/h2 h1le/wdhim g'hwe:/rn_s-kwe wog'e/yesi. h1ye/h2 kwse/pm_ h2epo/ h2e/g'esi. h1ye/h2-ge sh2we/ns HeHk'u/m k'woyto/m bhe/resi. nu pl_th2uye/h2h1 pl_/h2seh2h1 we/sth2er. h1yo/d gwe/mesi kwse/ps mine/wti. k'woyto/s-ge toy me/g'h2s h1e/sti. nu nos h2e/wsos h2e/Hsh2m_ gwm_dhi/. h2e/wsos nos diwe/s dhu/gh2ter bhowdhe/ye.

nu kwse/ps gwm_ye/ti He/h2mr_-kwe gwm_ye/ti. so/h2wl_ He/h2m_ni ste/ygheti. h2ste/res-ge dye/wi sth2e/h1yonti. g'he/ymn_ gwm_ye/ti h2e/kweh2es-kwe stude/nt. nu He/h2mo:r dl_h1ghe/h2 bhuHye/ti. te/pos h2e/kwa:s h2sowse/yeti. so/h2wl_ kwe/lh1t g'he/ymn_-kwe le/ytt. sno/ygwhos tine/h1ti h2e/ndhesh2 h2e/g'roy bhwe/h2t. h2o/weyes-ge h2ukse/nt h1e/k'wo:s-kwe h2ukse/nt. nu kwi/s h1ludhe/yey g'hwe:rbho/s-kwe k'o/d h2re/ydhet? deywo:/s-ge h1e/ymos k'o/d h2re/ydhont.

kwse/p h1ludhe/yey h2g'me/ney-kwe gwm_dhi/. h1ye/h2 dhe/g'ho:m k'e/lesi h1ye/h2 h2ste/rn_s bhe/resi. h2ste/res toy h1le/wdhim spe/k'yonti. nu lo/wksneh2 upe/r ste/ygheti. mo/rtons-ge g'hwe:/rn_s-kwe k'e/le. supno/s n_sme/y g'hwe:rbho/s-kwe gwe/mt. h2stro:/m h2use/s-kwe me/h2te:r h1e/si. kwse/p nos h2e/g'mn_ me: trose/yes. nu nos h2e/wso:s diwe/s dhugh2te:/r h1ludho/yh1t.

nu nos dye/w dhe/g'ho:m-kwe k'luto/m. ph2te:/r deywo:/m-kwe me/h2te:r h1ste/s. me/g'h2s dye:/ws upe/r teto/ne. pl_th2e/wih2-ge dhe/g'ho:m Hn_dhi/ teto/ne. nu h3mi/ghleh2 h1ente/r sth2e/h1yeti. sh2we/ns kwe/kwlos h1ente/r h1e/yti. h1ente/r h2we/rst gwo/wes pi/ph3onti. mo/rtons h2e/g'mn_-kwe h2e/letes. He/h2mr_ nos wo/dr_-kwe dh3to/m.

nu ko/h2mos pr_h2wo/s bhwe/h2t me/nos ko/h2mo:d bhwe/h2t. nu kwi/s dye:/m tn_ne/wt kwi/s dhe/g'ho:m Hn_dhi/ loghe/yet? kwi/s-ge mo/ri pe/rkwum-kwe we/rg't? kwi/s lewbho/m so/h2wl_ lo/wksneh2m-kwe upe/r dhe/h1t? nu kwi/s sh2we/ns kwe/kwlo:y HeHk'u/ns h1e/k'wons ye/wgt? kwi/s-ge mo/rtoybhos He/h2mr_ k'woyto/m-kwe de/h3t? nu deywo:/s h1i/d wide:/r h1i/d-kwe ne widme/. h1e/ymos-ge ne/mos we/rg'omes.

h2we/st. ph2te:/r h1e/syo-kwe suHnu/s k'wn_bhi/s h1e/g'hs gwh2ta:/m. k'wo/nes twork'o/m se/h2gyont. twork'o/s pr_kwe/wi Hn_dhi/ k'e/yo. nu h1i/m str_h3me/ns g'hye/nt. dhe/nh2t h1e/y suHnu/m pro/ti. suHnu/s ste/h2t gwe/ru-kwe Hye/h1kt. h1e/syo k'e:/r ske/rdhet. me/rt twork'o/s. ph2te:/r h1e/syo k'e/rh2sonm_ ske/ydt h1e/smey-kwe h1i/m de/h3t h1e/ymos-kwe h3e/sth1h2 h1a/yt. suHnu/s h1i/m swo/y h2me/si tl_ne/h2t. gwh2e/nt k'o/ymom. kwe/k't me/h2te:r me:/ms me/wdt-kwe. h1i/d ye/set.

sno/ygwhos h2e/g'roy k'e/yo. h3re:g'e/s k'wo/nes wl_/kwosyo sto/yghom sno/ygwhoy wido/nt. wl_/kwom se/h2gyont h3re:/g's-kwe h1i/ns se/kweto. h1e/rt wl_/kwos pe/rwr_. pe/ri sth2e/h1yont. sth2e/h1yonts wl_/kwos ne bhe/wgt. Hye/h1kt h3re:/g's gwe/ru h1i/d-kwe h1i/m more/yet. h1e/sh2r_ sno/ygwhoy h1rudhe/h1t. de/ret h1e/y wl_/kwom h1e/syo-kwe k'e/rh2sonm_ h2k'me/ni stne/h2t. h1e/yes l_ghe/nt. h1regwesye/t.

h2we/rst k'wo/nes-kwe h2r_/tk'osyo sto/yghom wido/nt. sto/yghos de/h2nu we/dhet. gwe/ru bhe/ronts g'e/rh2onts h1i/ns se/kweto. h2r_/tk'os de/h2newi sne/h2t. g'e/rh2onts h1i/d Hye/h1kt h1i/d-kwe h3sle/ydht. nu h1ne/dht h2r_/tk'os we/dn_s dre/mt-kwe h1i/m pro/ti. k'wo/nes h2r_/tk'om lm_bhe/nt. g'e/rh2onts g'hoyso/m sokwo/h1 h2line/Ht h1i/m-kwe h1i/h1 gwhe/nt. me/rt h2r_/tk'os. g'e/rh2onts h1e/ymos k'e:/r h1ope/yet k'e/rh2sonm_-kwe h2k'me/ni loghe/yet. swidye/t tr_sye/t-kwe. si/sdet wo/dr_-kwe pe/h3t.

yemHo/h1 pe/rwr_ k'une/h1 ste/ygheta:m. h1e/y wl_/kwom kwe/k't h1i/m-kwe se/kweto. h1e/st wl_/kwos HeHk'u/s ne/k't-kwe. si/sdeta:m yemHo/h1 tr_sye/ta:m-kwe. wr_h1ta:/m. wl_/kwos HeHk'u/s h1e/sti pe/rwr_-kwe me/g'h2 h1e/sti. k'o/ymom h1e/yomes. h2use/si h2ew gwm_yo:/mes.

h3mi/ghleh2 h2e/g'roy k'e/yo. dhugh2te:/r tn_tlo/m h2k'mn_su/ h1ente/r tn_ne/wt. si/sdet. h1ne/dht po/rk'os h3mi/ghleh2s h1i/m-kwe we/yk't. h1i/h2 ste/h2t h1i/m-kwe h2k'm_ne/h1 more/yet. Hre/wdHt h1e/syeh2s-kwe me/h2te:r h1e/syeh2s wo/kwm_ k'le/wt. h1e/rt me/h2te:r dhugh2te:/r-kwe me/wdt. h1i/m k'o/ymom dukta:/m.

h1e/rt h2r_/tk'os k'o/ymom h2o/wins-kwe ste/h2yet. tre/set h1le/wdhis. dhe/nwr_ bhe/ronts poh2ime:/n pe/rwr_ gwe/h2t. h2r_/tk'om kwe/k't h1i/m-kwe more/yet. h1le/wdhis h1e/smey k'luto/m h1no/mn_ h2r_tk'ogwhono/m dhe/h1t. me/g'h2 k'le/wos h1e/smey h1e/st. h1e/syo k'le/wos n_/dhgwhitom h1e/sti.

wiHro/s swo/m gwe/ru Hye/h1kt h1i/d-kwe Hrumpto/. dhe/nh2t wl_/kwos h1i/m pro/ti. wiHro/s bhe/yh2t. h1e/syo bhre/h2te:r g'hoyso/m h1e/pt. g'hoyso/s h1e/syo k'e:/r te/rdt. me/rt wl_/kwos. nu bhre/h2te:r wiHro:/y gwe/ru we/rg't.

k'wo/nes g'hwe:/rm_ de/h2nu h2e/g'ont. h1e/y we/dni sne/h2t. ne sn_h2e/nt. h1e/y h2e/n h1ne/dht. h1e/y h1i/ns spe/k'yet. de/wkt gho/stis dhe/nwr_ le/h1t-kwe. h1e/y bhe/wgt. me/g'h2 k'le/wos ghoste/yey ne h1e/st.

po/tnih2 twork'o/syo me:/ms pe/kwet. h1regwesye/t. ph2te:/r ghosti/h1 se/dt. ghoste/yey k'e/rh2sonm_ de/h3t swe/h2ey-kwe po/tn_yeh2ey k'e:/r h1a/yt. h1e/dn_t me/lit-kwe ph3e/nt. we/rh1t gho/stis. ph2ter h1use/wo:m de/h3to:r h1e/si. k'o/d me:/ms swe/h2dt.

h3mi/ghleh2 pe/rwr_ k'e/let. swe/so:r wl_kwi/h2m_ h3mi/ghleh2i kwe/k't. spe/k'yet wl_kwi/h2s h1i/h2m_. swe/so:r g'hoyso/m ne le/h1t. wl_kwi/h2m_ se/h1t wl_kwi/h2s-kwe le/ytt. we/wket swe/so:r k'une/y. me: dre/ms.

dwo/h1 bhre/h2terh1e de/h2nu tn_tlo/h1 h1ludhe/ta:m. h1i/m we/dni tn_nuta:/m sdta:/m-kwe. we/yk't bhe/bhrus h1i/m. bhre/h2terh1e sth2ta:/m bhe/bhrum-kwe h1pta:/m. more/yeta:m h1i/m. h1i/m ph2we/nh1 pe/kweta:m me:/ms-kwe h1e/dta:m wo/dr_-kwe ph3ta:/m.

h2r_/tk'os g'hime/ni str_h3me/ni le/ghyet. k'wo:/ h2r_/tk'osyo ste/rh3mn_ wide/t le/h2kt-kwe. dhugh2te:/r h1i/m k'le/wt h1e/syeh2s-kwe ph2tre/h1 h1e/rt. bhe/wdht h2r_/tk'os. h1i/h2 gwe/ru Hye/h1kt h1i/d-kwe h2r_/tk'osyo g'o/nu te/rdt. bhe/wgt h2r_/tk'os pe/rwr_. h2e/g't ph2te:/r. me/reti.

dhh2bhro/s h2e/yos sle/h2gwt h1i/d-kwe ph2we/ni Hwe/rt. h1i/d tope/yet. h1i/d h1rudhe/h1t. h1i/d tri/s stune/dt. gwe/ru h1e/smo:d te:/tk't h1i/d-kwe h3rog'e/yet. de/h3t h3re:g'e/y h1i/d h3re:/g's-kwe h1e/smey misdho/m h1a/yt. dhh2bhro/syo h1no/mn_ k'luto/m h1e/sti.

te/tk'o: pe/rkwum wide/t h1i/m-kwe te/mh1t. kwe/kwlom h1e/smo:d pe/yk't. h1i/m h1o/loh1 te/rdt. h1e/st kwe/kwlos me/g'h2s. po/tn_yeh2ey h1i/m h1ope/yet. po/tnih2 gwo:/s ye/wgt h1i/ns-kwe h2e/g'rom h2e/g'et. kwe/kwlos dhr_g'hto/s h1e/sti.

dhh2bhro/s wik'po/teyey dwo/h1 kwe/kwloh1 kwe/rt. Hro/th2o:y kwe/kwloh1 yune/gt. wik'po/tis HeHk'u/h1 swo/h1 h1e/k'woh1 ye/wgt. h1e/k'woh1 dr_mta:/m Hro/th2os-kwe dhe/nh2t. me/wdt wik'po/tis. h1we/su k'le/wos h1e/smey h1e/st.

me/h2te:r se/dyet. h2wl_/h1neh2m tn_ne/wt wr_g'ye/t-kwe. swe/h2ey dhugh2tre/y pl_/h2seh2m kwe/rt. dhugh2te:/r pl_/h2seh2m wose/yeto. g'hime/ni ne ste/wdt.

wiHro/s h2e/k'monm_ h2e/g'roy wide/t. h1i/m ske/lHt gwre/h2wonm_-kwe h1e/smo:d kwe/rt. gwre/h2wonm_ k'o/ymom bhe/ret. h1e/syo po/tnih2 h1i/m kwe/k't me/wdt-kwe.

te/tk'o: wik'po/teyey se/dos de/met. h1i/d stoh2e/yet. se/dos me/g'h2 h1e/st. we/yk't wik'po/tis h1i/d h1e/smi-kwe se/dt.

kwe/kwlos h2e/g'roy Hrumpto/. poh2ime:/n h2e/n sth2e/h1yet Hre/wdHt-kwe. h1e/rt te/tk'o: h1i/m-kwe kwe/k't. kwe/kwlom kwe/rt. poh2ime:/n h1e/smey h2wl_/h1neh2m h1ope/yet. nu kwe/kwlos dhe/nh2t.

wiHro/s pe/rkwum te/mh1t. dhe/nwr_ h1e/smo:d pe/yk't. sne/h1wr_ sle/h2gwt h1i/d-kwe dhn_we/ni tn_ne/wt. de/wkt dhe/nwr_. h1e/syo bhre/h2trey dhe/nwr_ h1a/yt.

poh2ime:/n dhh2bhro:/y h2wl_/h1neh2m de/h3t. dhh2bhro/s tetk'ne/y h1o/lom h1a/yt. te/tk'o: poh2imne/y kwe/kwlom h1ope/yet. h1yo/s de/deh3ti so/ h1a/yto.

dhh2bhro/s gwe/ru te:/tk't. h1e/smey k'luto/m h1no/mn_ wl_kwogwhono/m dhe/h1t. gwe/ru wl_/kwom gwhe/nt. h1e/syo h1no/mn_ k'luto/m bhu/Ht. h1e/st me/g'h2 k'le/wos h1e/smey.

su/Hnew me/ne we/kwos k'ludhi/. pe/rkwum tm_h1dhi/ dhe/nwr_-kwe h1e/smo:d pik'dhi/. sne/h1wr_ sl_h2gwdhi/ h1i/d-kwe dhn_we/ni tn_nudhi/. dhe/nwr_ me: bhine/ds. h1i/d dukdhi/. h1we/su dhe/nwr_ h1yo/smey h1e/sti so/ wl_/kwo:d ne dedwo/ye.

h1me/ spe/k'ye. pe/rkwum kapyo/h2. h1i/m skine/dmi. h1i/m h3rog'e/yoh2. nu gwe/ru wr_g'dhi/. h3reg'to/m twork'o/m gwhe/nti. h1yo/d te twork'o/s pro/ti dre/meti. sth2dhi/ h1i/d-kwe tetl_h2dhi/. h1i/d k'wo/nm_ me: Hye/h1ks.

pe/rkwum h2isk'e/. h1i/m tm_h1dhi/. kwe/kwlom h1e/smo:d pik'dhi/. h1i/m h1o/loh1 tr_ddhi/. h1yo/d kwe/kwlos Hrune/petor me: Hre/wdHs. kwe/kwlom h2ew wr_g'dhi/. h1yo/s ne Hrumpto/r so/ me/h2trey lewbho/s h1e/sti.

h2wl_/h1neh2m sl_h2gwdhi/ h1i/h2m_-kwe yungdhi/. tn_tlo/m h1e/syeh2s te/tk'dhi. h1i/m h2k'mn_su/ h1ente/r tn_nudhi/. sddhi/ me:-kwe Hre/wdHs. h1yo/d g'hwe:/r tn_tlo/m we/yk'eti. sth2dhi/ h1i/m-kwe stunddhi/. h1yo/s se/deti ne-kwe Hre/wdHeti g'hwe:/r to/smey gwm_ye/ti.

kwi/d su/Hnew dedo/rk'th2e? h2r_/tk'osyo sto/yghom dedo/rk'h2e. h1i/m se/kweso. de/h2nu kwse/pi me: gwe/h2s. k'wo/nm_ k'le/wse. h1e/y h2k'h2owsye/ti. h1yo/d h1e/y Hre/wdHeti sth2dhi/. h2r_/tk'o:d me: bhe/wgs. gwe/ru tetl_h2dhi/.

k'une/y h3e/sth1h2 h1ope/ye. h1i/m wo/dr_ we/dhe h1e/y-kwe pe/h3tu. h1i/m me: stune/ds. h1yo/s h1i/m stune/deti h1e/y to/m ne se/kwetor. h1e/y lewbho/s h1e/sti.

h2e/yos sl_h2gwdhi/ h1i/d-kwe tope/ye. h1yo/d h1i/d h1rudhe/h1eti h1i/d stunddhi/. h1yo/d h1i/d ne h1rudhe/h1eti h1i/d me: stune/ds. gwe/ru h1e/smo:d wr_g'dhi/ h1i/d-kwe h3rog'e/ye. h1yo/s h2e/yos g'n_ne/h3eti to/syo h1no/mn_ k'luto/m h1e/sti.

dhu/gh2ter sddhi/. h2wl_/h1neh2m sl_h2gwdhi/. h1i/h2m_ yungdhi/ wr_g'ye/-kwe. te/we swesre/y pl_/h2seh2m wr_g'dhi/. h1yo/d pl_/h2seh2m we/rg'esi g'hime/ni ne ste/wdesi.

pe/rkwune nos we/kwos k'ludhi/. nu nos HeHk'u/ns k'wo/nn_s dh3dhi/. me/ne-ge gwe/ru me: h3slidhye/h1t. g'hwe:/r-kwe-ge n_sme/d me: bhugye/h1t. nu-kwe me:/ms k'o/ymom we/dhoyh1me. nu-kwe te/bhye k'e:/r de/h3omes.

h1we/su dhe/nwr_ Hwr_dhdhi/ dhe/nwr_ me: Hrumpth2e/. nu dhe/nwr_ g'hoyso/m h1e/g'hs sh1dhi/. nu dhe/nwr_ h3re:g'e/y lewbho/m h1sye/h1t.

pl_/th2ew tn_/tle g'hwe:/rm_ lm_bhdhi/ h1i/m kapye/. twe/d kwid me: bhugye/h1t tn_tlo/s-kwe pl_nh1ih1to/.

nu k'wo/nes h2k'h2owsye/te sto/yghom se/kwedhwe. nu-kwe de/h2nu pro/ me: dr_mte/. nu wl_/kwos usme/ me: spe/k'yoyh1t.

h2e/yos h1rudhe/h1dhi h2e/yos h1o/los bhuHdhi/. nu me: bhe/yds k'le/wos wiHro:/y h1sye/h1t.

He/Hk'ew kwe/kwle dr_mdhi/ kwe/kwle me: Hrumpth2e/. nu dwo/h1 h1e/k'woh1 HeHk'u/h1 h1sih1ta:/m. nu-kwe poh2imne/s k'e:/r mudye/h1t.

di/wya h2e/wsos lukdhi/ sto/yghom nos de/yk'e. nu-kwe g'hwe:/r n_sme/ ni/ gwm_yo/yh1t. nu-kwe h1i/m h2e/n wide/h1yoyh1me.

nu h2wl_/h1na pl_/h2seh2es bhuHdhi/. nu swe/so:r g'hime/ni gwhr_ye/h1t me: studye/h1t.

nu pe/rkwunos h1o/gwhim h2e/n gwhe/nt. wl_/kwe h2e/g'ro:d litdhi/ h2o/wins me: gwm_ye/s. nu g'hoyso/s twe/ gwhn_ye/h1t gwe/ru twe/ gwhn_ye/h1t.

de/ywe h1use/wo:m de/h3tor gho/stis h1e/dih1t. nu po/tnih2 h1e/dih1t tr_pih1me/.

h2we/st. nu h2ne:/r ste/h2t h2e/wHt-kwe. nu de/h3reh2 sle/h2gwt. pe/kwet h1e/syo me/h2te:r. nu HeHk'u/h1 dwo/h1 h1e/k'woh1 ye/wgt. nu h1e/syo po/tnih2 h1e/smey we/rh1t. h1idhi/ n_sdhi/-kwe. nu h2e/g't h1e/syo ph2te:/r. deywo:/s te ph2sih1e/nt. nu k'o/ymo:d h1e/g'hs gwe/h2t k'wo:/-kwe h1i/m se/kweto. nu h1ne/dht so/h2wl_. sh2we/ns kwe/kwlos bhe/rg'hus h1e/st. h2e/wis pro/ ye/h2t. nu dweh2ro/m po/ntoh1m_ wide/t. po/ntoh1s dweh2ro/s h1e/st ne-kwe bhe/yh2t. h2isk'e/t me/g'h2 k'le/wos.

sto/yghos dweh2ro/s h1e/st bhe/rg'ho:s-kwe bhe/rg'hontes h1se/nt. g'he/ymn_ h1e/st. nu h2ne:/r ko/lHonm_ ste/yghet. sno/ygwhos bhe/rg'hoysu k'e/yo. ste/wdt. h1ne/dht h1e/syo pne/wmn_ k'e/mh2t-kwe. nu wl_/kwom kwe/k't. g'hwe:/r sno/ygwhoy sth2e/h1yet spe/k'yet-kwe h1i/m. nu h2ne:/r h2e/k'monm_ Hye/h1kt wl_/kwos-kwe bhe/wgt. nu se/dt h1e:/dt-kwe k'l_nito/-kwe h2k'me/ni. gwhe/rt sno/ygwhos-kwe tine/h1t. nu kwelso/m de/h2nu wide/t. nu gwe/h2t ni/.

h1regwesye/t. nu h2ne:/r dhg'he/mi se/dt h1e/syo-kwe tpe:/rsneh2h1 k'm_h2ta:/m. nu h2re/ydhet pe/h2wr_. pe/h2wr_ dhe/gwhet. ste/wdt. nu polh1e/wes h2ste/res luke/nt. kwse/ps dweh2re/h2 h1e/st te/mHos-kwe te/gu h1e/st. nu spe/k'yet h2ste/rn_s. h2ste/res me/ne do:/m dedr_k'e/? h2ste/res ne bhh2e/nt. me/ne do:/m memo/nh2e. dhe/g'hom me me/h2ter ph2sdhi/. k're/mh2t pe/h2wr_. nu swe/pnos h1i/m le/mbht. nu h2we/st sh2we/ns-kwe kwe/kwlos h1ne/dht. nu bhe/wdht ste/h2t-kwe.

gwe/mt gho/stis h2o/g'moh1. nu h2ne:/r h1i/m kwe/k't ste/h2t-kwe. nu gho/stis se/kwt. ku/dhe ye/h2si? we/wket. ye/h2mi k'o/ymom. me/ne do:/m dweh2ro/s h1e/sti. kwi/s h1e/si? gho/stis h1e/smi. kwi/d tewo/m h1no/mn_ h1e/sti? me/ne h1no/mn_ k'luto/m h1e/sti. h1i/d ne wo/ydh2e. nu k'o/m sdta:/m. de/h3t gho/stis h1e/smey wo/dr_ h1e/dta:m-kwe. nu gho/stis we/rh1t. deywo:/s te we/dhoyh1ent le/ytt-kwe. h2ne:/r pro/ gwe/h2t.

h1regwesye/t. nu h1ludhe/t gho/stis k'o/ymom. nu ph2te:/r h1i/m wide/t me/wdt-kwe. nu h2e/g't. gho/stey sddhi/. h1ddhi/ pi/ph3e-kwe. nu po/tnih2 h1e/smey wo/dr_ h3e/ngwn_-kwe de/h3t. dhe/gwhet pe/h2wr_. gho/stis h1e:/dt pe/h3t-kwe se/h2t-kwe. nu se/kwt. deywo:/s k'o/smey de/mey h1we/suh2 de/dh3ih1ent. nu k'le/ytreh2i le/ght. gho/stis deywo/ybhos lewbho/s h1e/sti.

h1regwesye/t. we/dhet poh2ime:/n h2o/wins bhe/rg'hom. nu k'wo:/ wl_/kwons k'le/wt Hre/wdHt-kwe. nu tre/yes g'hwe:/res se/rpont. kwe/k't poh2ime:/n h1i/ns. h2o/weyes tre/sont. nu we/wket. h1n_/gwney h2o/wins h1me/-kwe ph2sdhi/. nu h1n_gwni/s le/wkt. h1n_gwne/ys bhe/h2os pl_th2u/ h1e/st. wide/h1yont wl_/kwo:s pe/h2wr_ bhuge/nt-kwe. nu h2o/weyes l_ghe/nt. bhe/wdhet. nu h2we/st. h2o/weyes gwi/h3wont. h1n_gwni/s to/smey lewbho/s h1e/sti.

ih2ta:/m gho/stis h1e/k'wos-kwe h2o/g'moh1. nu so/h2wl_ to/h1 gwhr_ne/wt. tr_sye/ta:m swidye/ta:m-kwe. wo/dr_ h2isk'e/ta:m ne-kwe wide/ta:m h1i/d. nu h1e/k'wos pro/ gwe/h2t gho/stis-kwe h1i/m se/kweto. nu h1r_ta:/m wo/dr_. h1i/d pl_th2u/ h1e/st. nu h1e/k'wos pe/h3t gho/stis-kwe wo/dr_ pi/ph3et. nu se/dt gho/stis. nu tr_pta:/m. h1yo/s tr_sye/ti wo/dr_ to/smey lewbho/m h1e/sti.

h2eg'o/s kwe/kwlom h2e/g'et dukta:/m-kwe HeHk'u/h1 dwo/h1 h1e/k'woh1 h1i/m. nu ro/tos Hrumpto/. h1e/k'wo:s sth2e/h1yont. nu se/dt h2eg'o/s we/rh1t-kwe. kwi/s ro/tom de/me:ti? nu te/tk'o: gwe/mt. skidto/m ro/tom wide/h1yet. h2e/g't. ro/tom de/mo:h2. misdho/m moy h1ope/ye. h1a/yt h2eg'o/s h1e/smey misdho/m. te/tk'o: wr_g'ye/t ro/tom-kwe de/met h2line/Ht-kwe h1i/m sm_ru/h1. nu yugo/m h2re/ydhet. nu h2eg'o/s kwe/kwlom h2ew h2e/g'et. tetk'ne/s h3e/pos h1we/su h1e/sti.

gwe/h2t h3re:g'e/s HeHk'uh1e/k'wos suHnu/s dweh2ro/h1 h2o/g'moh1. h1e/k'wons pro/ h2e/g'et. nu h1o/gwhim kwe/k't k'e/yo h1yo/s h2e/n. nu h1o/gwhis me/g'h2s h1e/st dhe/g'ho:m-kwe tre/mt. h1o/gwhis gwo:/m gwr_h3e/t. tr_me/nt h1e/k'wo:s. nu ne bhe/yh2t. h1o/gwhey wo/dr_ Hwe/rs. nu twe/ gwhe/noh2. nu gwhe/nt h1o/gwhim wo/dr_-kwe h1le/wdherom bhu/Ht. nu dhe/h1t k'luto/m h1no/mn_ n_/dhgwhitom-kwe k'le/wos we/nt ne/st-kwe. wiHro/s me/rt. ne me/rt k'le/wos.

gho/stis bhe/rg'hoy se/dyontm_ poh2ime/nm_ wide/h1yet. nu h1e/smey se/kwt. wo/dr_ ku/dhe h1e/sti? nu we/wket poh2ime:/n. h1i/d h2e/n h1e/sti sto/yghom-kwe de/yk'et. ni/ gwh2dhi/ h2e/g'rom-kwe pr_dhi/. nu h1i/d wide:/si. nu gho/stis bhe/h2t. sto/yghos dweh2ro/s h1e/sti? sto/yghos dweh2ro/s ne h1e/sti. deywo:/s te h2le/ksoyh1ent. nu gwe/h2t gho/stis ni/.

de/h2nu pl_th2u/ HeHk'u/-kwe h1e/st. nu bhre/h2te:r swe/so:r-kwe h1i/d h1r_ta:/m de/k'm_-kwe gwo:/s h2e/g'eta:m. nu we/rh1t swe/so:r. te/rh2owos. nu bhre/h2te:r h2e/g't. h1i/d HeHk'u/ h1e/sti. wo/dr_ h1n_do/ me: h1e/ys tre/mt-kwe. nu swe/so:r se/kwt. ne bhebho/yh2h2e. h1e/lh2t h1i/ns wo/dr_ h1n_do/. gwo/wes sn_h2e/nt. h1i/d pre/nt tr_h2ta:/m-kwe bhre/h2te:r swe/so:r-kwe e/nu. nu h2e/n sth2ta:/m. gwo/wes wo/dr_ ph3e/nt.

h2we/rst. k'e/lont n_bhro:/s dye:/m. nu pe/rkwunos ghro/mom de/h3t. nu de/h2nu ple/h1t ple/h1dhet-kwe wo/dr_. nu gho/stis wo/dr_ h1ludhe/t. me/g'h2 wo/dr_ kwe/k't. nu we/wket. me: pe/roh2. k'le/ytreh2m de/mo:h2. nu le/ght k'le/ytreh2i h1e:/dt-kwe. ghro/mos le/ytt n_bhro:/s-kwe lite/nt. nu h2we/st wo/dr_-kwe me/g'h2 ne h1e/st. le/wkt so/h2wl_. nu h1i/d te/rh2t.

ph2te:/r h1e/syo-kwe suHnu/s wo/dr_ gwm_ta:/m. nu we/rh1t ph2te:/r. de/h2nu HeHk'u/ h1e/si. g'huto/m toy de/h3rom dedh3me/s. nu de/h3rom we/dni dhe/h1t. pe/romes. me: nos more/yes. nu tre/mt suHnu/s. h1i/d to/h1 k'le/wset. nu tr_h2ta:/m wo/dr_-kwe to/h1 ne h1e/pt. nu sth2ta:/m h2e/n. ph2te:/r h2e/g't. de/h2nu n_/dhgwhitom h1e/sti.

me/g'h2ih2 ne/h2us mre/y h1e/st. nu septm_/ wiHro:/s ne/h2wi se/dyont. g'he/yt h2eg'o/s ne/h2wm_. ne/h2us h1i/ns bhe/ret. ti/srn_s kwse/pn_s h1ye/nt k'm_h2e/nt-kwe. gwhr_ne/wt so/h2wl_ h1i/ns. h2eg'o/s h2ste/rm_ spe/k'yet ne/h2us-kwe h2ste/rm_ se/kweto. sn_h2e/nt pe/yskes mre/y. nu h2we/st. nu h2e/wis h1ludhe/t. h2eg'o/s h2e/wim de/yk'et. mude/nt.

h1e/yt ne/h2us mre/y. nu n_bhro:/s dye:/m k'e/lont te/mHos-kwe te/gu bhu/Ht. nu de/h3t pe/rkwunos ghro/mom. mo/ri Hyowdhe/yeto wo/dr_-kwe ple/h1dhet. nu bhih2e/nt wiHro:/s. sh2e/ymn_ Hrumpto/. ne/h2us bhindto/. nu Hre/wdHt h2eg'o/s. pe/rkwune nos spr_dhi/. nu h2r_g'n_to/m mo/ri Hih1ke/nt. nu ghro/mos le/ytt lite/nt-kwe n_bhro:/s. h2we/st. gwi/h3wont.

ne/h2us mre/y se/ngwt polh1e/wes-kwe k'e/ywo:s m_re/nt. nu sne/h2t h3re:g'e/s suHnu/s ti/srn_s kwse/pn_s we/dni. ste/wdt. h1e/syo k'e:/r ne tre/mt. h2ste/rn_s wide/h1yet. bhuHyo/nt h2ste/res. dye/w me ph2ter spr_dhi/. nu dye:/ws h1i/m h2we/yst. h2we/st. nu dhe/g'ho:m h1e/rt. le/mbht h1i/h2m_ g'ho/sto:ys. nu me/g'h2 k'le/wos h1a/yto. mo/ri ne/h2wm_ h1e/pt. ne sle/h2gwt h1e/syo k'le/wos.

h2we/st. nu h2eg'o/s dhe/g'ho:m wide/t. nu Hre/wdHt. nu mude/nt. nu ne/h2us h1i/h2m_ h1e/rt. gwh2e/nt neh2we/s h1e/g'hs. h1e/syeh2i sth2e/nt. mre/yey de/h3rom dh3e/nt. nu h2re/ydhont pe/h2wr_. pe/h2wr_ dhe/gwhet. h1e/dn_t ph3e/nt-kwe. nu l_ghe/nt. dhe/g'hom me/h2ter lewbhe/h2 h1e/si.

dwo/h1 wiHro/h1 de/h2nu gwm_ta:/m. nu se/dyet neh2uwiHro/s. nu skwta:/m. ne/h2uwiHre nos h2e/n bhe/re. nu we/wket. kwi/d moy h1ope/yete? h2r_g'n_to/m de/h3omes. ne/h2wi sddhi/. nu h1i/d h1a/yta:m ne/h2wi-kwe sdta:/m. me: bhih2te/. h2e/g'et neh2uwiHro/s ne/h2wm_. wo/dr_ pl_th2u/ h1e/st. nu neh2uwiHro/s ne/h2wm_ pro/ h1e/lh2t. nu h2e/n sth2ta:/m. wr_h1ta:/m. deywo:/s toy h1we/suh2 dh3ih1e/nt.

te/mHos mre/y h1e/st luke/nt-kwe polh1e/wes h2ste/res. nu ne/h2us h1e/yt. h2eg'o/s h2ste/rm_ spe/k'yet. h1ye/nt h1e/noy h2ste/res pe/ri. so/ h2ste:/r sth2e/h1yet. nu h2e/g't. h2ste/r nos we/dhe. bhebhidhme/. nu ne/h2us h2ste/rm_ se/kweto. nu h1rudhe/h1t dye:/ws. nu h2e/wso:s h1ludhe/t h2ste:/r-kwe le/ytt. le/wkt mo/ri.

mo/ri me/g'h2 h1e/sti. de/h2nuh2 mo/ri h1e/yti. mo/ri ne pl_nh1to/r. gwi/h3wonti pe/yskes mre/y. g'hwe:/res mre/y ne gwi/h3wonti. mo/ri kwis ne h2e/rh3yeti. po/ntoh1s mre/y ne h1e/sti. ne/h2us po/ntoh1s h1e/sti. de/yk'eti h2ste:/r neh2we/y po/ntoh1m_. so/h2wl_ mre/y se/ngwt. h2e/wso:s diwe/s dhugh2te:/r le/wkt. mo/ri piskbho/s lewbho/m h1e/sti. de/deh3ti mo/ri h2nr_bho/s pe/yskm_ me/rtim-kwe. mo/ri me/g'h2 h1e/sti.

h1esh2o/s k'o/ymoy se/dyet. nu wesowiHro/s h1esh2o/m gwe/mt. wide/h1yet dwo/h1 h1e/k'woh1. nu se/kwt. k'o/h1 h1e/k'woh1 h2yuHn_k'o/h1 h1ste/s? h2yuHn_k'o/h1 h1ste/s. kwi/d h1e/k'wo:m wo/sn_ h1e/sti? wo/sn_ de/k'm_ gwo/wes h1e/sti. wo/sn_ bhe/rg'hu h1e/sti pe/nkwe-kwe gwo:/s h1ope/yo:h2. h1ne/wn_ gwo:/s dedh3dhi/. septm_/ gwo:/s h1ope/yo:h2. to/d h1e/stu. nu bhoydhe/h2m dhh1ta:/m. nu h1i/ns de/h3t h1a/yto-kwe dwo/h1 h1e/k'woh1. nu h1esh2o/s h1i/ns h2epo/ h1e/lh2t. nu wesowiHro/s dwo/h1 h1e/k'woh1 ye/wgt. nu mudta:/m.

h1ludhe/t nigwte/h2m h2wl_/h1neh2m we/g'hontih2 po/tnih2 wesowiHro/m. h1i/h2m_ wide/h1yet. nu we/wket. h2wl_/h1neh2 h1use/wih2 h1e/sti? nu we/rh1t. h1i/h2 h1use/wih2 h1e/sti. h1i/h2m_ pe/rh2oh2. h1i/h2m_ moy dedh3dhi/. h2r_g'n_to/m toy h1ope/yo:h2. nu h1e/smey h1i/h2m_ de/h3t de/h3t-kwe h1e/syeh2ey h2r_g'n_to/m h1i/d-kwe sle/h2gwt. nu mudta:/m. nu wesowiHro/s pl_h1e/wih2m h2wl_/h1neh2m kwrine/h2t we/g'het-kwe h1i/h2m_ h2epo/. nu h1i/h2m_ pe/rh2t polh1u/-kwe h2r_g'n_to/m h1a/yto. nu h3pe/wst.

dhe/h1t wesowiHro/s sme/rum h3n_gwe/ni. nu h2yuHn_k'o:/y h3e/ngwn_ pe/rh2t. h2yuHn_k'o/s h3e/ngwn_ h1e:/dt ne-kwe swe/h2dt. nu le/mbht h2yuHn_k'o/s h1i/m. h1me/ dhre/wghs. twe/ ne dhre/wghm_. nu we/dhet h2yuHn_k'o/s h1i/m h3re/g'm_. h3re:/g's se/dyet. h3re:/g's to/h1 k'le/wt. h1e:/dt h3re:/g's h3e/ngwn_ ne-kwe swe/h2dt. nu h2e/g't. dhre/wghs. h2r_g'n_to/m h2ew dedh3dhi/ h2yuHn_k'o:/y-kwe de/h3rom h1ope/ye. nu wesowiHro/s h1i/d h2ew de/h3t de/h3rom-kwe de/h3t. ne kwis kwrine/h2t h1e/smo:d.

wl_/kwos poh2ime/nm_ h1e/rt. h2o/wim moy pr_h2dhi/. pe/rh2oh2 toy. nu k'wo/nm_ h2wl_/h1neh2h1 wose/yet. nu h1a/yt wl_/kwos ta/wrom k'wo/nm_-kwe sle/h2gwt. k'wo/nm_ ne g'n_ne/h3t. nu ye/h2t k'wo/nm_ bhe/ronts wl_/kwos bhe/rg'hom me/wdt-kwe. nu k'wo:/ wl_/kwom h1e/pt h1i/m-kwe de/ret. Hre/wdHt bhe/wgt-kwe. h1e/lh2t poh2ime:/n ta/wrom do:/m. h1yo/s dhrughye/ti so/ dhrughye/tor.

se/dyet poh2ime:/n kaghyo/y. nu h2ne:/r h1i/m gwe/mt. nu se/kwt. kwetwo/rn_s toy gwo:/s de/deh3mi. de/k'm_ moy h2o/wins h1ope/ye. nu we/wket poh2ime:/n. h2o/weyes pl_h1e/wih2es h1se/nti. bhoydhe/h2m dhe/h1omes. h1yo/s bhoydhe/h2m Hrune/peti deywo:/s to/m gwhnih1e/nt. nu poh2ime:/n kwetwo/rn_s gwo:/s kwre/yh2t h2ne:/r-kwe de/k'm_ h2o/wins kwre/yh2t. nu h1e/lh2t h2ne:/r h2o/wins h2epo/ poh2ime:/n-kwe h1i/ns kaghyo/m h1e/lh2t. nu mudta:/m.

wesowiHro/s h1e/k'wons ye/wgt. nu dhe/h1t h2wl_/h1neh2m kwe/kwloy. nu dweh2ro/h1 stighe/h1 gwe/h2t. de/h2nuh2 bhe/rg'hons-kwe pe/rt. nu h1ludhe/t kwelse/h2m dhe/g'ho:m. gho/stis h2e/n h1e/st. g'hime/ni h2e/n tk'e/yt. nu h2wl_/h1neh2m pe/rh2t kwrine/h2t-kwe h2r_g'n_to/m pl_/h2seh2m ma/rkom-kwe. nu pl_/h2seh2m wo/g'hoy dhe/h1t. nu ne/st. h1e/syo po/tn_yeh2ey pl_/h2seh2m de/h3t. bhu/Ht h1e/syo h1no/mn_ k'luto/m.

kwrinh2o/nts gho/stis h2wl_/h1neh2s h1esh2o/m h1e/rt. nu we/rh1t. tewe/h2 h2wl_/h1neh2 h1use/wih2 ne h1e/sti. nu h1esh2o/s h2e/g't. h1i/h2 h1use/wih2 h1e/sti. h1i/h2m_ spe/k'ye. h1i/h2 h1use/wih2 ne h1e/sti. h1i/h2 h1use/wih2 ne h1e/sti. h2epo/ le/ytoh2. h2epo/ me: h1e/ys. h1i/h2m_ toy de/deh3oh2. kwi/d wo/sn_ h1e/sti? wo/sn_ bhe/rg'hu ne h1e/sti. nu de/h3t h2r_g'n_to/m h1i/h2m_-kwe sle/h2gwt. nu le/ytt. nu h2ew gwe/mt kwrine/h2t-kwe pl_h1e/wih2m h2wl_/h1neh2m.

wesowiHro/s h1esh2o/s-kwe bhoydhe/h2m dhh1ta:/m. nu h1esh2o/s pe/nkwe h1e/k'wons h1a/yt. nu h1a/yto wesowiHro/s h1e/k'wons. h2r_g'n_to/m ne de/h3t. h2r_g'n_to/m ku/dhe h1e/sti? h1e/k'wo:s HeHk'e/wes ne h1se/nti. nu h1esh2o/s se/kwt. h1yo/s bhoydhe/h2m Hrune/peti dye/w ph2ter to/m gwhn_dhi/. nu h1ne/dht ghro/mos wesowiHro/syo-kwe k'o/ymos dhe/gwhet. nu bhe/yh2t. h1esh2o:/y h1i/d de/h3t. sth2e/h1yet bhoydhe/h2.

h1suk'le/we:s wiHro/s de/k'm_ ye/h1o:r de/ms dweh2ro/s h1e/st. nu h1ludhe/t se/dt-kwe. nu po/tnih2 gho/stim wide/h1yet h1e/smey-kwe wo/dr_ h1ope/yet. le/ghyet k'wo:/. k'wo:/ h1i/m g'ne/h3t h1i/m-kwe dre/mt le/yg'ht-kwe h1e/syo g'ho/stoh1. po/tnih2 h1i/m ne g'ne/h3t. nu po/tnih2 we/wket. kwi/s h1e/si? nu we/rh1t. tewo/s wiHro/s h1e/smi. me/ne h1no/mn_ k'luto/m h1e/sti. nu h1ne/dht h1e/syeh2s da/k'ru h1e/syo-kwe g'ho/stoh1 le/mbht. h1yo/s ne/st k'o/ymos to/smey lewbho/s h1e/sti.

se/dyet me/h2te:r de/mi. nu h2yuHn_k'o/s h2o/g'moh1 h1e/rt. h1i/h2 h1i/m ne g'n_ne/h3t. sth2e/h1yet Hre/wdHt-kwe me/h2ter. nu h1i/h2 we/kwos h2we/yst. h1e/syeh2s k'e:/r we/kwos g'ne/h3t. me/ne suHnu/s h1e/si? tewo/s suHnu/s h1e/smi. nu h1ne/dht h1e/syeh2s da/k'ruh2. h1e/syeh2s g'ho/stoh1 tr_mta:/m. h2e/g't. me/ne h2e/wso:s k'o/ymom gwegwo/me. nu dre/mt h1e/pt-kwe h1i/m g'ho/sto:ys. ph2te:/r to/d k'le/wt h1e/g'hs-kwe gwe/h2t.

h2e/rh3yet swe/so:r h2e/g'roy. nu wiHro/s stighe/h1 gwm_sk'e/t. h1i/m ne spe/k'yet. nu h2we/yst swe/so:r h1e/syo gwe/h2mn_ h1i/d-kwe g'ne/h3t. h1e/syeh2s k'e:/r tre/mt. Hre/wdHt bhre/h2ter. nu h2e/g'rom linkwn_ti/h2 swe/so:r dre/mt. bhre/h2te:r se/kwt. ne/sm_ swe/sor. we/wket. lewbho/s h1e/si. nu do:/m ih2ta:/m.

h1suk'le/we:s h3re:/g's de/k'm_ ye/h1o:r k'o/ymom ne kwe/k't. nu gwe/mt gho/stis-kwe h1e/st. nu wide/t po/tnih2 h1i/m ne-kwe g'n_ne/h3t. h1e/smey wo/dr_ de/deh3t h1i/d-kwe pe/h3t. nu we/rh1t. dedo/rk'h2e h3re/g'm_. h1i/m ku/dhe dedo/rk'th2e? h1i/m mre/y dedo/rk'h2e. gwi/h3weti. gwi/h3weti? ne/seti. deywo:/s h1i/m we/dhoyh1ent. nu h3re:/g's h1e/smi. me/ne h1no/mn_ k'luto/m h1e/sti. po/tnih2 h1i/m spe/k'yet g'ne/h3t-kwe h1e/syeh2s k'e:/r h1i/m. nu da/k'ruh2 h1ne/dht.

wiHro/s le/ytt ne-kwe ne/st. nu Hre/wdHt h1e/syo me/h2te:r. me/rt h1e/syo-kwe bhre/h2te:r tre/mt. septm_/ ye/h1o:r h1e/yt. nu h1ludhe/t gho/stis do:/m. me/h2te:r h1i/m wide/h1yet bhe/yh2t-kwe tre/mt-kwe. mr_wo/s h1e/si. me/ne k'o/ymom me: gwe/ms. nu se/dt h1e:/dt-kwe. nu pe/h3t. me/h2te:r h2e/g't. ne h1e/dn_ti mr_wo:/s. gwi/h3wesi. nu me/wdt.

po/tnih2 po/ntoh1m_ spe/k'yet. h2ste/rn_s kwse/pi wide/h1yet. se/kwt h1e/syeh2s k'r_de/y. gwi/h3weti? h1i/d bhe/h2t. gwi/h3weti. nu de/k'm_ ye/h1o:r h1e/yt. ne me/rt. nu h2we/st h1ne/dht-kwe so/h2wl_ wiHro/s-kwe h2o/g'moh1 h1e/rt. k'wo:/ h1i/m dre/mt. g'ne/h3t h1e/syeh2s k'e:/r h1i/m. me/ne k'e:/r h1me/ ne dhre/wght.

nu dye/w ph2ter me/ne po/ntoh1m_ ph2sdhi/. nu me/ne sto/yghom h3rog'e/ye. nu moy dhe/h1s h2o/g'mom woyde/ye. g'hwe:/r bho:/r-kwe h1me/ me: lm_bhih1ta:/m. me/ne h1e/k'woh1 HeHk'u/h1 h1sih1ta:/m. sh2we/ns kwe/kwlos upe/r lukye/h1t. nu-kwe me/ne k'e:/r me: tr_mye/h1t. me: tr_syo/yh1m_ n_sye/h1m_-kwe. dye/w me ph2ter h1use/wo:m de/h3tor k'ludhi/.

nu moy h2e/wsos diwe/s dhu/gh2ter gwm_dhi/. h1ye/h2 h2ste/rn_s h2epo/ h2e/g'esi. h1ye/h2 sh2we/ns kwe/kwlom we/dhesi h1ye/h2 h1rudhe/h1si. nu me/ne po/ntoh1i lukdhi/. le/wbha h2e/wsos me/ne h1e/k'woysu lukdhi/. nu moy h2e/wsos le/wkm_ bhe/re. nu h2e/wsos me/ne k'e:/r mudye/h1t. h2e/wsos me me/ne do:/m we/dhe. h2e/wsos te/we le/wkm_ kwk'ye/h1m_.

me/g'h2es de/ywo:s me/ne wiHro/m h2le/ksete. g'hwe:/r bho:/r-kwe h1i/m me: lm_bhih1ta:/m. nu de/h2nu h1e/smey gwm_to/m h1sye/h1t. h1e/syo lewbho:/s h1e/k'wo:s HeHk'e/wes h1sih1e/nt. nu-kwe de/ywo:s we/yHs h1e/smey h1sye/h1t. nu pe/rkwunos h1i/m me: gwhn_ye/h1t. h1e/syo sto/yghom h3rog'e/yete nu k'o/ymom n_sye/h1t. h1yo/d ne/seti nu wos de/h3rom h1ope/yo:h2.

nu wl_/kwe bho:/r-kwe bhugto/m ne bhebho/yh2h2e. nu me/ne h1e/k'wons me: dhgwhh2te/. nu-kwe pe/h2wr_ h2m_bhi/ h1e/sti. nu pe/rkwunos upe/r h1e/sti. dhe/g'ho:m-kwe-ge Hn_dhi/ h1e/sti. nu-kwe h1n_gwni/s to/h1 h1e/wseti. nu wl_/kwosyo pe/rkwunos g'o/mbhons gwhn_ye/h1t. me/ne pn_th1e/s h2epo/ litte/.

nu de/h2nu te/we wo/dr_ stoh2e/ye h1me/ me: le/mbhs. nu wo/dr_ bhe/rg'hu me: h1sye/h1t h1i/d gwm_to/m h1sye/h1t. nu pe/roh2 nu-kwe h2e/n ste/h2oh2. me/ne h1e/k'wo:s prih1e/nt de/h3rom toy h1a/yoh2.

nu dhe/g'hom me/h2ter ni/ le/ghoh2. le/wbha me dhe/g'hom me/h2ter kwse/pi ph2sdhi/. nu-kwe h2ste/res h1me/ spe/k'yete. nu g'hwe:/r h1me/ me: h2k'h3ye/h1t swe/pnos h1me/ sl_h2gwye/h1t. me/ne h1e/k'wo:s k'o/m le/ghyoyh1ent. kwse/ps h1me/ k'e/loyh1t pe/h2wr_ dhe/gwhoyh1t. me/g'h2i h2e/wsos te/we le/wki bhudhye/h1m_.

h2we/rst. nu h1ludho/nt deywo:/s k'o/m ph2te:/r-kwe upe/r se/dyet. nu dhe/g'ho:m me/h2te:r u/d ste/h2t dye:/m-kwe g'huHe/yet. ph2ter me h2wisdhi/ le/wbho:s-kwe de/ywo:s k'le/wsete. h2ne/res moy gwo/wes h2o/weyes-kwe gwr_h2e/wes h1se/nti. k'e:/r moy gwr_h2u/ h1e/sti k'e/mh2m_-kwe. kwi/s k'o/ns h2ne/rn_s me/d tl_ne/h2eti? nu se/kwt h1e/y h1e/syeh2ey. me/h2ter toy we/kwos k'le:/m. g'e/rh2ontes me/ronti dhg'he/mi-kwe ni/ le/ghonti. nu gwr_h2e/wih2 ne h1e/sesi. deywo:/s toy kwye/h1tim h1ope/yoyh1ent. nu h1e/syo we/kwos k'luwe/nt mude/nt-kwe. nu h1i/h2 kwih1te/y ni/ se/dt. gwh2to/s g'r_h2n_tbho/s h1r_sk'e/ti h1i/h2-kwe h1i/ns h2le/kseti. h2ne/res h1e/syeh2ey de/h3nom we/sni de/dh3n_ti.

ne/bhos upe/r se/rpet. nu g'huHe/yet dye:/ws deywo/ns k'o/m gwme/nt-kwe. nu ste/h2t so/h2wl_ u/d ph2te/rm_-kwe deywo/ns-kwe g'huHe/yet. dye/w me ph2ter de/ywo:s-kwe k'le/wsete h1yo/d me ne/bhos k'e/leti h1le/wdhis moy de/h3reh2 ne de/deh3ti ne-kwe me wide/h1yonti. nu h1e/y h1i/d h2we/yst h2e/nh1mo:y-kwe we/wket. h2we/h1n_t h2we/h1dhi. ne/bhos sh2we/ns h2epo/ h2e/g'e. nu bhe/h2t h2e/nh1mos. ph2ter h2we/h1oh2. nu h2we:/h1t ne/bhos-kwe ne/k't. nu so/h2wl_ le/wkt h2ne/res-kwe de/h3neh2 h1ope/yont h1erkwo/m-kwe te/tk'n_t. ne/bhos upe/r se/rpeti h2we/h1n_ts-kwe h1i/d h2epo/ h2e/g'eti.
