h1regwesye/t. nu h1ludho/nt deywo:/s ph2te:/r-kwe h1i/ns sode/yet. nu ste/h2t h1n_gwni/s u/d Hre/wdHt-kwe. dye/w me ph2ter k'le/wse h1yo/d h2Hse/h2i dhe/gwhoh2 h2e/kweh2 gwm_ye/ti more/yeti-kwe me. nu h2e/kweh2 bhe/h2t. ne te h2isk'o/h2. tu/H me h2isk'e/si. nu h2e/g't h1e/y. h1n_/gwney h2Hse/h2i ni/ sddhi/ h2e/kwa-kwe de/h2nuh2 gwh2dhi/ h2e/Hsh2m_-kwe me: gwe/ms. nu h1e/y h2Hse/h2i ni/ se/dt h2e/kweh2-kwe de/h2nuh2 gwe/h2t. h1n_gwni/s h2Hse/h2i gwi/h3weti h2e/kweh2-kwe de/h2nusu gwi/h3weti. pe/h2wr_ h1le/wdhim gwhr_ne/wti wo/dr_-kwe h1le/wdhim srobhe/yeti.

dhe/h1ses k'o/m se/dyont ph2te:/r-kwe h1re/h1t. kwi/s we/rh1eti? nu mle/wHt h2e/wso:s. ph2ter wiHro/ns gwm_sk'o/h2 h1e/ymos-kwe bhe/h2os bhe/roh2 h1yo/d gwm_sk'o/h2 wiHro:/s le/ghyonti de/h3rom-kwe kwis moy ne de/deh3ti. nu h1e/y bhe/h2t. h2e/wsos moy te dhu/gh2ter k'le:/m. h2ne/res h2use/y h1erkwo/m h1ope/yo:nti h1yo/d-kwe gwm_ye:/ti u/d ste/h2onti. nu mude/nt deywo:/s h2e/wso:s-kwe le/wkt. h2e/wso:s h2e/nti h1e/yti so/h2wl_-kwe h1i/h2m_ se/kwetor.

ghro/mos stonh2e/yet. nu dhe/h1ses dye:/m k'o/m gwm_sk'o/nt. nu stonh2e/yet pe/rkwunos Hre/wdHt-kwe. de/ywo:s h2wiste/. gwhe/nm_ h1o/gwhim we/do:r-kwe spe/rm_ h2ne/res-kwe gwi/h3wonti. misdho/s moy ku/dhe h1e/sti? pe/k'u kwis moy ne h1ope/yeti. nu se/kwt me/h2te:r. h1o/gwhim gwhno/ntm_ pe/rkwunom kwe/k'm_. nu ph2te:/r we/wket. pe/rkwune h1o/gwhim gwhe/ns. h2ne/res toy ukse/nn_s h1erkwo/m-kwe de/h3onti. deywo:/s toy n_/dhgwhitom k'le/wos h1ope/yoyh1ent. nu stonh2e/yet pe/rkwunos mude/nt-kwe. h2ne/res h1e/smey ukse/nn_s de/dh3n_ti h1e/syo-kwe h1no/mn_ k'luto/m h1e/sti.

h1e/rt de/h2nus de/ywih2 dye:/m h1i/m-kwe g'huHe/yet. dye/w me ph2ter k'ludhi/. mu/Htrom moy we/dni h1e/sti pe/yskes-kwe moy m_re/nt. nu h1e/y h1re/h1t. kwi/s toy mu/Htrom wo/dr_ h1n_do/ Hye/h1kt? nu mle/wHt h1i/h2. h2ne/res Hih1ke/nt. kwi/d dhe/dheh1oh2? nu pe/rkwunos we/rh1yet. dhe/h1ses k'o/ns wiHro/ns more/yete. nu bhe/h2t me/h2te:r. h1i/ns me: more/yete. nu ph2te:/r h2e/g't. de/ywo:s moy we/kwos k'le/wsete. h1yo/s mu/Htrom de/h2nu h1n_do/ Hye/h1keti so/ tr_syo/yh1t h2e/kweh2m-kwe me: ph3ye/h1t. nu me/wdt h1i/h2 h1e/syeh2s-kwe h2e/kweh2 nigwte/h2 bhu/Ht. pe/yskes h1e/syeh2s h2e/kweh2i sn_h2e/nti.

h1regwesye/t deywo:/s-kwe k'o/m si/sdont. lo/wksneh2 ph2tre/y mle/wHt. ph2ter wl_/kwos kwse/pi gwm_ye/ti poh2imne/s-kwe h2o/wins gwr_h3e/ti. poh2ime:/n mle/wHti. lo/wksneh2 wl_/kwo:y lewbhe/h2 h1e/sti. wl_/kwo:y lewbhe/h2 ne h1e/smi. wl_/kwom kwe/k'm_. nu h1e/y h1re/h1t. kwi/s h2o/wins pe/h2sti? nu se/kwt h1i/h2. poh2ime:/n h1i/h2n_s pe/h2sti. nu ph2te:/r bhe/h2t. po/h2imen k'wo/nm_ h1i/h2n_s we/dhe k'wo:/-kwe h1i/h2n_s pe/h2seti. h1yo/d wl_/kwos gwm_ye:/ti k'wo:/ Hre/wdHeti. nu bhe/wgt wl_/kwos lo/wksneh2-kwe le/wkt. poh2ime:/n lo/wksneh2ey h1erkwo/m te:/tk't k'wo:/-kwe dhwo/ri se/dyet.

deywo:/s k'o/m we/rh1yont. kwi/s nos me/g'h2s h1e/sti? nu pe/rkwunos we/rh1t. gwhe/nm_ h1o/gwhim me/g'h2s-kwe h1e/smi. nu Hre/wdHt h1n_gwni/s. h1ludhe/ys de/h3neh2 h1e:/dmi h1i/h2-kwe wos bhe/roh2. nu to/y ph2te/rm_ spe/k'yont. nu mle/wHt h1e/y. de/h3to:r me/g'h2s h1e/sti. h1use/wo:m de/h3to:r h1e/smi. nu h1i/d h2wise/nt we/kwos-kwe kwis ne we/rh1t. nu h1e/y h1e/syo h3bhre/wHh1e tl_ne/h2t.

nu sth2e/h1yeta:m suHnu/h1 h2e/nti. nu me/h2te:r to/h1 ph2te/rm_ we/dhet bhe/h2t-kwe. ph2ter moy suHnu/h1 h2e/nti sth2e/h1yetes. to/yso:m we/kwesh2 gwr_h2u/h2 h1e/sti. to/yso:m me/h2te:r h1e/smi ne-kwe wo/ydh2e. nu h1re/h1t h1e/y. kwi/s h2e/g'rom h2e/rh3yet? nu k'o/s we/wket. h1i/m h2e/rh3troh1 h2e/rh3yom. nu mle/wHt h1e/nos. pe/k'u moy h2e/g'roy h1e/sti. nu ph2te:/r h2e/g't. k'luto/m me. k'o/s h1i/m h2e/rh3ye:ti h1e/nos-kwe swo/m pe/k'u h1i/m we/dhe:ti. nu h1e/syo we/kwos h2wista:/m mudta:/m-kwe. h1i/h2 to/h1 kwe/k't swe/h2dos-kwe h1e/syeh2s k'e/rdi h1e/st.

deywo:/s ph2te/rm_ h1r_sk'o/nt suHnu/s-kwe diwe/s u/d ste/h2t ph2te/rm_-kwe g'huHe/yet. dye/w moy ph2ter h1no/mn_ ne h1e/sti. h1no/mn_ moy dhh1dhi/. nu bhe/h2t h2e/wso:s. h1e/syo h1no/mn_ HeHk'u/ h1sye/h1t. nu ph2te:/r se/kwt. suHnu/s moy h1e/sti h2ne/res-kwe h1e/syo h1no/mn_ k'le/wonti. nu h1e/smey k'luto/m h1no/mn_ dhe/h1t deywo:/s-kwe mude/nt. h1e/syo h1no/mn_ k'luto/m h1e/sti h1e/syo-kwe k'le/wos n_/dhgwhitom h1e/sti.

h2we/st pe/rkwunos-kwe h1n_gwni/s-kwe polh1u/ns gwo:/s h2e/g'eta:m. nu we/rh1yet pe/rkwunos. k'o/y moy gwo/wes h1se/nti. g'ho/stos moy h1i/ns h2e/g'et gwhe/noh2 te. nu h1n_gwni/s we/rh1yet. dhrughye/si me. h1o/h3s moy h1i/ns g'huHe/yet. gwo:/s h1e/g'hs we/dhom h1e/wso:h2 te. nu gwe/mt h2e/wso:s h1ente/r mle/wHt-kwe. pe/rkwune h1i/m me: gwhe/ns. h1n_/gwney h1i/m me: h1e/wses. dye:/m h1e/yomes h1i/m-kwe h1re/h1omes. nu h1i/m h1ludho/nt we/rh1t-kwe. k'o/y gwo/wes h2use/y h1se/nti. nu h2e/wso:s swo/ns gwo:/s h2epo/ h1e/lh2t. nu bhih2ta:/m.

h2e/nh1mos so/h2wl_-kwe k'o/m se/dyeta:m. nu ste/yghet we/smn_ we/sm_h1nos h2ne:/r h2n_g'he/wi sto/yghoy. h2we/h1n_ts we/wket. HeHk'u/s h1e/smi ne/bhesh2-kwe h2e/g'oh2. nu h1i/d h2e/g't. me/g'h2 h1e/smi wiHro/ns-kwe gwhr_ne/wmi. kwi/s memo/ghe? h2we/h1n_t h2we/h1dhi. h1yo/d h2ne:/r we/smn_ le/h1eti we/ns. nu h2we:/h1t h2we/h1n_ts tri/s so/-kwe swo/m we/smn_ kapye/t. nu h2ne:/r tre/set. nu gwhr_ne/wt h1i/d h1i/m so/-kwe swidye/t we/smn_-kwe le/h1t. nu so/h2wl_ we/nt.

dye:/ws h2use/y HeHk'u/ns h1e/k'wons de/h3t. nu gwe/mt h2e/nh1mos h1i/m se/kwt-kwe. dye/w ph2ter h2use/y HeHk'u/ns h1e/k'wons h1a/ys ne-kwe moy kwid h1a/ys. nu ph2te:/r h1re/h1t. kwi/d bhe/resi? nu bhe/h2t h2we/h1n_ts. ne/bhesh2 wiHro:/m-kwe pne/wmn_ bhe/roh2. nu ph2te:/r we/rh1t. we/kwos toy h2we/ysm_. ne/bhesh2 toy HeHk'e/wes h1e/k'wo:s h1e/stu h1i/h2-kwe yugdhi/. nu ye/wgt h2e/nh1mos ne/bhesh2 h1i/h2-kwe g'he/yt. nu h2e/wso:s h2we/h1n_ts-kwe k'o/m gwh2ta:/m h2ne/res-kwe h1i/ns wido/nt. ne/bhesh2 dre/mt ghro/mos-kwe stonh2e/yet.

h1o/gwhis se/lesi se/rpet mle/wHt-kwe. ne kwis me ge/lHt. we/do:r Hwe/rm_ me/g'h2s-kwe h1e/smi. polh1e/wes moy g'o/mbho:s h1se/nti. de/h2nuh2 ne ye/h2t h2ne/res-kwe tr_syo/nt. h2n_ro:/m h2e/k'ruh2 se/ngwt. nu h2k'h2owsye/t pe/rkwunos. nu h1e/syo we/kwos k'le/wt u/d-kwe ste/h2t h2e/yos-kwe h1e/pt gwe/mt-kwe. nu pe/rkwunos we/wket. te/wh2os moy h1e/sti. gwhe/noh2 te. nu h1e/pt h1o/gwhis pe/rkwunom. nu pe/rkwunos stonh2e/yet gwhe/nt-kwe h1o/gwhim h2e/yesh1. nu bhu/Ht we/do:r h1le/wdhereh2. nu h1e/smey me/g'h2 k'le/wos dhh1e/nt h2ne/res-kwe k'luto/m h1e/syo h1no/mn_ k'le/wsonti. h1e/y h1e/gwhis gwhe/nto:r h1e/sti h1e/smey-kwe h1erkwo/m te/tk'n_ti. h1e/syo sto/h2mos me/g'h2s h1e/sti.

dye:/ws me/h2te:r-kwe k'o/m se/dyeta:m. kwi/s nos wo/yde? nu h1e/y h1re/h1t. kwi/d me/d g'eg'o/nh1e twe/-kwe gwm_ye/ti. nu h2e/g't h1i/h2. wo/dr_ twe/d g'eg'o/nh1e h1me/-kwe gwm_ye/ti. nu h1i/h2 h1re/h1t. kwi/d dhg'he/mi le/ghyeti twe/-kwe h2we/kst. nu bhe/h2t h1e/y. dhoHne/h2 dhg'he/mi le/ghyeti h1me/-kwe h2we/kst. nu h2e/wso:s se/kwt. dye:/ws wo/dr_ wo/yde me/h2te:r-kwe dhoHne/h2m wo/yde. h1e/syeh2s wo/ydos me/g'h2s h1e/sti. nu mudta:/m.

sh2we/ns kwe/kwlos h2e/g'roy k'e/yo. nu we/rh1t ph2te:/r. dhe/h1ses h1yo/s kwe/kwlom tl_ne/h2eti to/smey de/h3rom de/deh3oh2. kwi/s memo/ghe? nu pe/rkwunos kwe/kwlom tl_ne/h2t ne-kwe memo/ghe. nu h1e/pt h1n_gwni/s h1i/m kwe/kwlos-kwe gwr_h2u/s h1e/st. nu h2e/wso:s u/d ste/h2t kwe/kwlom-kwe tl_ne/h2t h1i/m-kwe u/d bhe/ret. nu mle/wHt ph2te:/r. h2e/wsos diwe/s dhu/gh2ter kwe/kwlom tl_ne/h2s. h1we/su toy de/h3nom de/h3oh2. h2e/wso:s h1i/m u/d bhe/reti h2ne/res-kwe h1i/h2m_ wide/h1yonti.

kwse/ps h2e/wso:s-kwe swe/sorh1e h1ste/s. h1regwesye/t. nu kwse/ps h2n_g'he/wi sto/yghoy sth2e/h1yet h2e/wso:s-kwe gwm_sk'e/t. nu bhe/h2t kwse/ps. swe/sor moy k'o/s sto/yghos h1e/sti h2nr_bho/s-kwe kwye/h1tim h1ope/yoh2. nu h2e/wso:s we/wket. swe/sor sto/yghoy me: sth2e/h1yes. h2epo/ litdhi/. wiHro:/s me spe/k'yonti h2e/k'ruh2-kwe moy h2e/g'rom se/ngwt. nu mle/wHt me/h2te:r. dhugh2te/rh1e moy h1ste/s. kwse/p h2e/nti gwh2dhi/ h2e/wsos-kwe h1i/h2m_ se/kweso. nu kwse/ps se/ngwt h2e/wso:s-kwe le/wkt. kwse/ps h2e/nti ste/ygheti h2e/wso:s-kwe h1i/h2m_ se/kwetor.

dye:/ws dhe/g'ho:m-kwe k'o/m se/dyeta:m. kwi/s h2nr_bho/s dhoHne/h2m de/deh3ti? nu h2e/g't h1e/y. k'o/syeh2ey dhg'hme/y h2e/kweh2m de/deh3mi. h1yo/d h2e/kweh2 ne h1e/seti dhoHne/h2 me/rt. nu me/h2te:r se/kwt. dhoHne/h2m h2le/ksoh2 h1i/h2m_-kwe h2e/loh2 h1i/h2m_-kwe twe/ tl_ne/h2mi. h1yo/d h1i/h2m_ me: h2e/lo:h2 kwi/d de/deh3si? nu bhe/h2t h2e/wso:s. wo/dr_ h1e/nosmo:d diwe/s gwm_sk'e/ti dhoHne/h2-kwe k'o/syeh2s dhg'hme/s gwm_ye/ti. dye/w me/h2te:r-kwe wiHro/ybhos dhoHne/h2m k'o/m dedh3te/s. wiHro:/s h1i/h2m_ h1e/dn_ti. nu mudta:/m.

gwe/mt so/h2wl_ dye:/m h1i/m-kwe g'huHe/yet. ph2ter moy toy dhugh2te/rm_ dedh3dhi/. k'm_to/m toy gwo:/s h1ope/yo:h2. nu h1e/y mle/wHt. h1e/syeh2s h1we/dmn_ g'he/slom gwo/wes h1e/sti. nu we/rh1yet so/h2wl_. g'he/slom moy gwo/wes ne h1se/nti. septm_/ toy HeHk'u/ns h1e/k'wons h1a/yoh2. nu h1e/y we/rh1t. h1we/dmn_ h1we/su h1e/sti. dhugh2te/rm_ toy moy de/deh3oh2. nu se/dt sh2we/ns h2e/wso:s wo/g'hoy ni/ h1i/d-kwe h1i/h2m_ h2epo/ de/wkt. septm_/ HeHk'e/wes h1e/k'wo:s wo/g'hom we/g'hont. h1le/wdhis h1erkwo/m te:/tk't k'm_to/m-kwe gwo/wes se/kwonto.

dhe/h1ses se/dyont. nu ph2te:/r we/wket. h2e/nh1me h1n_gwni/s-kwe pro/ti dr_mto/m. kwi/s we/neti? nu gwe/h2t h2we/h1n_ts h2e/nti h1n_gwni/s-kwe Hre/wdHt. h2we/h1n_t me h2we/h1dhi. nu h2we:/h1t h1n_gwni/s-kwe h2e/g'rom pe/r dre/mt h1i/m-kwe h1e/wset. h2e/g'ros h1rudhe/h1t. nu h1n_gwni/s we/nt. to/y spe/k'yont. nu bhe/h2t me/h2te:r. h1yo/d h2e/nh1mos ne h2we/h1eti h1n_gwni/s ne h1e/yti. ph2ter h2uh1n_te/y h1n_gwne/yey-kwe misdho/m dh3dhi/. nu h1e/y to/ybhos misdho/m h1ope/yet.

HeHk'u/s deywo/s diwe/s gwo:/s kwse/pi ste/h2yet h1i/ns-kwe h2e/g'rom h1e/lh2t. nu h1i/ns de/h2nu h2e/g'et gwo/wes-kwe sn_h2e/nt. nu h1i/ns se/lesi k'e/let. g'e/rh2onts dhwo/ri se/dyet. g'e/rh2onts h2k'h2owsye/t. nu deywo/m wide/t h1i/m-kwe g'ne/h3t. nu spe/k'yet dye:/ws swo/ns gwo:/s h2use/si h1i/ns-kwe ne wide/t. nu h1e/y g'e/rh2ontm_ g'huHe/yet. kwi/s moy gwo:/s sle/h2gwt? nu h2e/g't g'e/rh2onts. deywo/s h1i/ns sle/h2gwt. h1i/m wido/m. nu ph2te:/r we/rh1yet. He/Hk'ew moy de/ywe gwo:/s ste/h2yes. h1i/ns h2ew we/dhe. nu bhe/yh2t HeHk'u/s deywo/s gwo:/s-kwe h2ew we/dhet dwo/h1-kwe ukse/nh1e h1a/yt. ph2ter me me: gwhe/ns. nu ph2te:/r se/kwt. gwi/h3we h2ew-kwe me: ste/h2yes.

HeHk'u/s deywo/s dhh1so:/m h2e/Hsh2m_ h1ludhe/t. pe/h2wr_ h2Hse/h2i dhe/gwhet h1e/syo-kwe bhe/h2os me/g'h2 h1e/st pe/h2wr_-kwe h2Hse/h2s ste/h2yet h1i/d-kwe h2ne/rn_s ni/ bhe/ret. nu ste/h2yet h1e/y pe/h2wr_ h2Hse/h2s. tu/H moy pe/h2wr_ h2Hse/h2s ste/h2yes? nu HeHk'u/s deywo/s we/rh1t. pe/h2wr_ ste/h2yom. h2ne/res stude/nt. nu h1e/ymos pe/h2wr_ bhe/rom gwhre/nt-kwe. nu we/wket ph2te:/r. pe/h2wr_ h2nr_bho/s do/h3r_ h1e/sti. dhh1sbho/s h1erkwo/m dh3dhi/. nu deywo/s dhh1sbho/s h1erkwo/m de/h3t to/y-kwe mude/nt. nu h1le/wdhis pe/h2wr_ swe/h2i h2Hse/h2i pe/h2sti pe/h2wr_-kwe h1i/ns gwhr_ne/wti.

pl_h1e/wih2es h2o/weyes me/h2trey h1se/nt. nu h2o/weyes lelikwe:/r. nu se/h2gyet me/h2te:r sto/yghom h2e/wyo:m-kwe k'oph2o/ns sto/yghoy wide/t. nu de/h2nu h1r_sk'e/t. de/h2nu dhubhno/m h1e/st deywo/s de/h2newi se/dyet nigwte/h2m-kwe h2wl_/h1neh2m bhe/ret. nu h1i/h2 h1re/h1t. kwi/s h1e/si? k'e/h2 kwe/syo h2wl_/h1neh2 h1e/sti? nu h2e/g't deywo/s. h2wl_/h1neh2 moy h1e/sti. nu h1i/h2 mle/wHt. dhrughye/si me. nu g'huHe/yet h1i/h2 swa:/s h2o/wins. nu h1i/h2es h2wise/nt h1i/h2m_-kwe dr_me/nt. nu bhe/yh2t deywo/s. me/h2ter h1i/h2n_s sle/h2gwm_. me: me gwhe/ns. nu h1i/h2 se/kwt. h2wl_/h1neh2m moy de/h3nom-kwe bhe/re. nu bhe/ret deywo/s me/h2trey h2wl_/h1neh2m de/h3nom-kwe h1i/h2-kwe swa:/s h1i/h2n_s k'o/ymom we/dhet.

poh2ime:/n diwe/s pe/k'u pe/h2st k'wo:/-kwe k'o/m h1e/st. k'wo:/ bhudhro/s h1e/st. nu poh2ime:/n ni/ le/ght. nu h1e/rt deywo/s h2e/g'rom. poh2imne/s we/smn_ ste/h2yet h1i/d-kwe wose/yeto. nu k'wo:/ we/smn_ wide/t deywo/m-kwe le/yg'ht. nu h1e/lh2t deywo/s pe/k'u h2epo/. nu poh2ime:/n bhe/wdht. pe/k'u ku/dhe h1e/sti? k'wo/n gho/stim le/yg'hs. nu wide/t ph2te:/r h1i/m sto/yghoy h1i/m-kwe h1e/pt. nu deywo/s pe/k'u h2ew we/dhet poh2imne/y-kwe we/smn_ de/h3t. k'wo:/ gho/stim Hre/wdHti.

diwe/s gwo/wes dhg'he/mi h1se/nt h1o/gwhis-kwe dhwo/rm_ pe/h2st. nu gwe/mt deywo/s dhwo/rm_ Hre/wdHt-kwe. h1o/gwhey toy k'e/ywos h1e/smi. pl_h1e/wih2n_s toy h2o/wins bhe/roh2. nu h1o/gwhis Hre/wdHt. wik'dhi/. nu we/yk't deywo/s h1o/gwhis-kwe h1i/h2n_s gwr_h3e/t se/h2t-kwe ni/-kwe le/ght. nu deywo/s gwo:/s h1e/g'hs h1e/lh2t. h1o/gwhim dhre/wght. nu bhe/wdht h1o/gwhis bhe/h2t-kwe. gwo/wes ku/dhe h1se/nti? nu dye:/ws deywo:/y h1we/su k'le/wos h1a/yt.

dwo/h1 deywo/h1 bhoydhe/h2m dhh1ta:/m. k'o/s gwo:/s de/h3eti h1e/nos-kwe h1e/k'wom h1a/yeti. nu de/h3t k'o/s gwo:/s h1e/nos-kwe h1i/ns le/mbht. nu k'o/s Hre/wdHt. h1e/k'wos moy ku/dhe h1e/sti? nu we/rh1t h1e/nos. gwo:/s toy ne le/mbhm_. dye:/ws moy bhoydhe/h2m wo/yde. h1yo/d te dhrughyo:/h2 dye:/ws me gwhn_ye/h1t. nu dye:/ws h1i/m k'le/wt stonh2e/yet-kwe. nu bhe/yh2t h1e/nos k'o/smey-kwe h1e/k'wom de/h3t. dye:/ws bhoydhe/h2s we/ydto:r h1e/sti.

lo/wksneh2s suHnu/s diwe/s gwo:/s kwse/pi ste/h2yet h1i/ns-kwe h2epo/ h1e/lh2t. nu dye:/ws h1i/m h1e/pt h1re/h1t-kwe. tu/H moy gwo:/s ste/h2yes? nu we/rh1yet deywo/s. gwo:/s ne wido/m. nu ph2te:/r mle/wHt. dhre/wghs me. g'ho/stos toy h1i/ns h2e/g'et. nu te:/tk't deywo/s h1we/su ke/h2nmn_ ph2te:/r-kwe k'l_ne/wt. swe/h2dos h1e/syo k'e/rdi h1e/st h1e/y-kwe bhe/h2t. lo/wksneh2s toy su/Hnew ke/h2nmn_ h2we/ysm_. gwo:/s moy h2ew we/dhe dwo/h1-kwe toy ukse/nh1e h1a/yoh2. nu deywo/s h1i/ns h2ew we/dhet.

h1regwesye/t. nu le/ght me/h2te:r swe/h2i dhwo/ri. nu HeHk'u/s deywo/s h1i/h2m_ wide/t mle/wHt-kwe. h1i/h2 le/ghyeti. ne me wide/h1yeti. h1e/syeh2s gwo:/s h2epo/ h2e/g'o:h2. nu h2e/g'et h1e/y h1e/syeh2s gwo:/s h2epo/. nu h1i/h2 h1e/syo g'ho/stom le/mbht we/wket-kwe. bhe/wdhom. spe/k'yom te. g'n_ne/h3mi te. nu bhe/yh2t deywo/s. me/h2ter toy gwo:/s pe/h2soh2. nu h1i/h2 h2e/g't. h1i/ns ph2sdhi/ h2e/g'rom-kwe we/dhe. pe/k'u toy de/h3oh2. me/h2te:r g'n_h3re/h2 h1e/sti. nu pe/h2st deywo/s h1e/syeh2s gwo:/s kwse/pi.

h2ne:/r h2e/g'rom h2e/rh3troh1 h2e/rh3yet. nu k'e/mh2t ni/-kwe se/dt. nu me/h2te:r h1e/smey bhuHye/t se/kwt-kwe. h2ne/r me h2e/rh3troh1 h2e/rh3yesi. twe/ toy gwo:/s-kwe h2e/loh2. h1yo/d moy de/h3nom de/h3esi puHro/m toy h1a/yoh2. nu h1re/h1t h2ne:/r. kwi/d toy h1a/yoh2? nu h1i/h2 we/rh1t. se/h1mn_ moy h1a/ydhi. nu dhe/h1t h2ne:/r se/h1mn_ dhg'he/mi h1i/h2-kwe h1e/smey polh1u/m puHro/m de/h3t. dhoHne/h2 bhwe/h2t. nu h2ne:/r h1e/syeh2ey h1erkwo/m te:/tk't.

h3re:/g's neh2we/h1 dhubhno/y lime/ni we/g'heto we/rh1yet-kwe. h2n_ro:/m h3re:/g's h1e/smi. me/g'h2s h1e/smi. dhh1sbho/s de/h3nom ne de/deh3mi. nu we/wket g'e/rh2onts ne/h2wi. h3re/g' me: we/rh1yes. dye:/ws te k'l_ne/wti. nu h3re:/g's g'e/rh2ontm_ ne k'le/wt. nu dye:/ws h3re/g'm_ h2we/yst stonh2e/yet h2e/g't-kwe. h3re/g' gwr_h2u/ we/kwos h2e/g's. h1yo/s dhh1sbho/s de/h3nom ne de/deh3eti h1e/syo ne/h2us se/ngweti. nu h2we:/h1t h2we/h1n_ts le/ymo:-kwe Hyowdhe/yeto. nu ne/h2us se/ngwt h3re:/g's-kwe me/rt wiHro:/s-kwe h1e/syo h1no/mn_ ne g'huHe/yont.

g'e/rh2onts po/tnih2-kwe sto/yghoy tk'ita:/m. po/tnih2 puHro/m pe/kwet. h2we/rst gho/stis-kwe kwse/pi gwe/mt. nu se/kwt g'e/rh2onts. gho/stey ni/ sddhi/. pokwto/m puHro/m h1ddhi/. h2e/kweh2m ph3dhi/. wr_h1e:/n moy h1e/sti. h1i/m more/yo:h2. nu gho/stis we/rh1t. wr_h1e/nm_ me: more/yes. gho/stis ne h1e/smi. dhe/h1s h1e/smi. g'e/rh2ont moy pokwto/m puHro/m h2e/kweh2m-kwe h1a/ys. polh1u/ toy pe/k'u de/h3oh2 h1we/su-kwe toy k'le/wos h1ope/yo:h2. sh2e/ytlom toy h2uksye/h1t. nu ne/k't gho/stis. nu g'e/rh2onts pe/k'u h2e/g'roy wide/t po/tnih2-kwe me/wdt.

h2ne:/r gho/stim more/yet h1e/syo-kwe gwo:/s h1e/pt. nu dye:/ws h1i/d dedo/rk'e. nu h1re/h1t h1e/y wiHro/m. gho/stis ku/dhe h1e/sti? nu h2ne:/r we/wket. ne wo/ydh2e. nu h2e/g't ph2te:/r. wo/ydth2e. dhrughye/si me. gho/stis diwe/s gwm_ye/ti. gwo/wes toy me/ronti dhg'he/mi-kwe le/ghesi. gwh2to/s twe/ gwm_sk'e:/ti. nu h1e/y stonh2e/yet h2ne:/r-kwe me/rt. h2ne/res gho/stim ph2se/nti.

po/tnih2 sto/yghoy ste/yghet Hre/wdHt-kwe. h1e/syeh2s wiHro/s me/rt h1e/syeh2s-kwe h2e/k'ruh2 se/ngwt. nu h2we/st h2e/wso:s-kwe h1i/h2m_ h1ne/dht. nu spe/k'yet po/tnih2 h1i/h2m_ g'huHe/yet-kwe. h2e/wsos me diwe/s dhu/gh2ter k'ludhi/. wiHro/s moy me/rt. kwi/d dhe/dheh1oh2? nu h2e/wso:s se/kwt. po/tni me: Hre/wdHs. h2we/ysm_ te. gwh2to/s h2nr_bho/s gwm_ye/ti k'le/wos-kwe ne me/rt. suHnu/m toy de/h3oh2. h1suk'le/we:s h1e/seti h1e/syo-kwe te/wh2os me/g'h2 h1e/seti. nu h1re/h1t po/tnih2. kwi/d toy de/h3oh2? nu h2e/wso:s we/rh1t. h1erkwo/m moy dh3dhi/. nu te:/tk't po/tnih2 h2use/y h1erkwo/m h1e/syeh2ey-kwe de/h3nom h1ope/yet. nu h2e/wso:s ne/k't. suHnu/s h1i/h2m_ ye/h1ni gwe/mt. h1e/syo h1no/mn_ k'luto/m h1e/st.

sto/yghos h2e/ng'hus h1e/st h1regwesye/t-kwe. h2ne:/r kwse/pi ste/yghet. h1i/m ne wide/t bhe/yh2t-kwe. nu Hre/wdHt wl_/kwos. nu lo/wksneh2 le/wkt h2n_re/y-kwe sto/yghom woyde/yet. nu Hre/wdHt h2ne:/r. kwi/s h1e/si? nu h1i/h2 we/wket. lo/wksneh2 h1e/smi. h2use/s swe/so:r h1e/smi. kwse/pi ste/yghoh2. bhe/h2os moy sto/yghoy h1e/sti. me: tre/ses. se/kweso me. nu h1re/h1t h1e/y. wo/yk'os ku/dhe h1e/sti? nu h1i/h2 h2e/g't. wo/yk'os h2e/n h1e/sti. nu se/kweto wiHro/s h1i/h2m_ wo/yk'om-kwe h1ludhe/t. nu h2ne:/r lo/wksneh2ey ne/mos de/h3t me/wdt-kwe.

h2we/st. nu ste/h2t diwe/s h2e/wso:s dhugh2te:/r u/d h1ludhe/yey-kwe we/rh1yet. h1le/wdhey bhudhdhi/. gwm_sk'o/h2 h1rudhe/h1mi-kwe bhe/h2os-kwe wos bhe/roh2. po/h2imen sth2dhi/ h2o/wins-kwe h1i/m we/dhe. h2ne/r bhudhdhi/ gwo:/s-kwe yugo/h1 yugdhi/. po/tni sth2dhi/ gwo:/s-kwe h2ml_g'dhi/ wo/dr_-kwe gwhr_nudhi/ puHro/m-kwe pe/kwe. su/Hnewes dhugh2te/res-kwe me: le/ghyete. g'e/rh2ont dhwo/ri ni/ sddhi/ sto/yghom-kwe spe/k'ye. h1yo/d ne ste/h2ete so/h2wl_ wos le/ghyontn_s wide:/ti. kwi/s ste/h2t? to/smey h1we/su k'le/wos h1ope/yo:h2. h1erkwo/m moy h1ope/yete h1we/suh2-kwe wos de/h3oh2. nu h1le/wdhis bhe/wdht so/h2wl_-kwe le/wkt. poh2ime:/n h2o/wins we/dhet h2ne:/r-kwe gwo:/s ye/wgt po/tnih2-kwe h1i/h2n_s h2me/lg't.

me/h2te:r dhe/g'ho:m swo/ybhos suHnu/bhos dhugh2tr_bho/s-kwe we/rh1yet. su/Hnewes dhugh2te/res-kwe k'l_nute/. h2e/loh2 wos h2le/ksoh2-kwe wos. puHro/s me/d gwm_ye/ti. bhre/h1wr_ me/d gwm_sk'e/ti. h2e/kweh2 me/d gwm_ye/ti. sri/Hgos gwm_ye/ti sno/ygwhos-kwe gwm_sk'e/ti. dhoHne/h2 we/sni me/d ste/h2t. h1yo/d moy do/h3r_ de/h3ete puHro/m wos h1a/yoh2. me: me bhindte/. ph2te/res wos dhg'he/mi le/ghyonti dhg'he/mi-kwe le/ghete. nu k'le/wt h1le/wdhis me/h2term_.

me/h2te:r swo/ns suHnu/ns g'huHe/yet h1i/h2m_-kwe h1ludho/nt. nu h1i/h2 we/rh1yet. su/Hnewes moy h2wiste/. me/h2te:r wos h1e/smi dye:/ws-kwe wos ph2te:/r h1e/sti. upe/r se/dyete Hn_dhi/-kwe le/ghyoh2. h1yo/s swo/m bhre/h2term_ gwhe/nti gwhe/nti me. k'e:/r moy gwr_h2u/ h1e/sti. me: gwhn_te/. k'o/m sdte/ h1e/dte-kwe k'o/m-kwe pi/ph3ete. bhre/h2te:r k'e/ywos h1e/sti. nu k'luwe/nt to/y swe/h2m me/h2term_ sde/nt-kwe. h1i/h2 h1i/ns spe/k'yet swe/h2dos-kwe h1e/syeh2s k'e/rdi h1e/st.

h2e/wso:s sh2we/ney we/rh1yet. so/h2wl_ gwm_dhi/. HeHk'u/ns toy h1e/k'wons yugdhi/. h2e/nti h1e/ymi tu/H-kwe me se/kweth2er. h1ludhe/yey bhe/h2os h1ope/ye. pe/k'u gwhr_nudhi/. h2e/k'ruh2 moy h2sowse/ye. nu h1i/d se/kwt. h2e/wsos te diwe/s dhu/gh2ter k'l_ne/wmi. ku/dhe h1e/yoh2 kwi/d-kwe dhe/dheh1oh2? nu we/rh1t h2e/wso:s. u/d h1idhi/ wo/yk'om-kwe sto/yghom-kwe spe/k'ye. h2ne/res le/ghyonti. nu h1i/d h1e/k'wons ye/wgt u/d-kwe h1e/yt. nu le/wkt h1i/d h2e/wso:s-kwe ne/k't. h1e/syeh2s h2e/k'ruh2 te/rst.

dhe/g'ho:m dye:/m g'huHe/yet. dye/w me me/ne wi/Hre h2wisdhi/. tr_syo/h2 h2e/g'ros-kwe te/rst. de/h2nuh2 ne ye/h2ti. h2o/weyes moy tr_syo/nti gwo/wes-kwe moy le/ghyonti. k'e:/r moy gwr_h2u/ h1e/sti. ne/bhesh2 h2e/g'e. ghro/mom h1ope/ye. h2we/rstu. h1yo/d h2we/rseti dhoHne/h2 bhwe/h2eti h2o/weyes-kwe pi/ph3o:nti. dhoHne/h2m toy h1erkwo/m-kwe h1a/yoh2. nu h1e/y we/wket. po/tni moy toy we/kwos k'le:/m. do/h3r_ moy pe/ruti ne de/h3s. nu h2e/g't h1i/h2. pe/ruti ne h2we/rst. h1yo/d h2we/rseti de/h3rom toy de/h3oh2. nu h1e/y se/kwt. ne/bhesh2 h2e/g'o:h2 ghro/mos-kwe stonh2e/ye:ti. nu h2we/rst de/h2nuh2-kwe ple/h1t. dhoHne/h2 h1e/smi h2we/kst h1i/h2es-kwe pi/ph3ont. h1i/h2 h1e/smey h1erkwo/m te:/tk't.

me/h2te:r h1ludhe/yey we/rh1yet. su/Hnewes moy dhugh2te/res-kwe k'le/wsete. wo/sr_ gwm_ye/ti sno/ygwhos-kwe tine/h1ti. de/h2nuh2 ple/h1dheti. se/h1mn_ h2e/g'rom Hih1kte/. gwo:/s h1i/m h2e/g'ete. h2o/wins ph2ste/ gwo:/s-kwe h2ml_g'te/. ph2te/rn_s wos dhe/h1sn_s-kwe memn_te/. h1yo/d dhh1sbho/s de/h3reh2 de/deh3ete to/y wos gwo:/s h2o/wins-kwe de/h3onti. me: sdte/. wr_g'ye/te. nu Hre/wdHt h2ne:/r. kwi/d me/h2ter h2e/g'rom Hye/h1komes? nu h1i/h2 we/rh1t. se/h1mn_ dhoHne/h2m-kwe Hih1kte/. h1i/m Hih1kte/. nu h2we/yst h1le/wdhis me/h2term_ h1i/m-kwe gwh2e/nt. h1le/wdhis we/sni wr_g'ye/t. h1i/h2 h1i/ns spe/k'yet swe/h2dos-kwe h1e/syeh2s k'e/rdi h1e/st.

h1regwesye/t. kwse/ps ste/wdt. poh2imne/s po/tnih2 de/mi le/ghyet h1e/syeh2s-kwe po/tis bhe/wdhet. nu h2e/wso:s le/wkt dye:/ws-kwe h1rudhe/h1t me/lh3t-kwe suHnu/s. Hre/wdHt. nu ph2te:/r h1i/m wide/t swo/m-kwe k'e:/r me/wdt. nu ph2te:/r h1i/m so/h2wl_ woyde/yet h1e/smey-kwe k'luto/m h1no/mn_ dhe/h1t we/rh1yet-kwe. polh1u/h2 we/tesh2 gwi/h3woyh1s te/we h1no/mn_ me: mr_ye/h1t n_/dhgwhitom toy k'le/wos h1sye/h1t. nu me/h2te:r h1i/m k'le/wt h2e/g't-kwe. h1e/y nos lewbho/s h1e/sti h2uksye/h1t Hwr_dhye/h1t-kwe.

sni/gwhs h2e/g'roy k'e/yo. dhugh2te/rm_ kapyo/nts ph2te:/r ph2we/ni se/dyet h2e/g't-kwe. dhugh2te:/r moy g'hime/ni g'eg'o/nh1e. te/we me/h2te:r kwse/pi k'e/mh2t. h1n_/gwney me k'ludhi/ h1yo/s nos pe/h2si. nu me/ne dhugh2tre/y k'luto/m h1no/mn_ dhe/h1m_. dhu/gh2ter me/ne k'o/ymoy h2uksye/h1s te/we-kwe po/teyey lewbhe/h2 h1sye/h1s h2wl_/h1neh2 toy pl_h1e/wih2 h1sye/h1t sri/Hgos te me: h1pye/h1t pe/h2wr_ te gwhr_nuye/h1t suHne/wes toy g'eg'n_h1ih1e/nt dhugh2te/res-kwe toy g'eg'n_h1ih1e/nt. nu mle/wHt h1n_gwni/s-kwe dhe/gwhet.

dye/w nos le/wbhe ph2ter k'le/wse. dhe/g'hom le/wbha me/h2ter k'o/m g'o/nh1om h2le/kse. h1use/wo:m de/h3tor h1e/smey sh2e/ytlom dh3dhi/. h2e/wsos diwe/s dhu/gh2ter h1e/smey lukdhi/. sh2we/ns He/Hk'ew kwe/kwle h1e/smey g'hleyno/s h1sdhi/. k'o/ymoy nos h2uksye/h1t Hwr_dhye/h1t-kwe. HeHk'e/wes lewbho:/s h1e/k'wo:s h1e/smey h1sih1e/nt. h1e/syo h2o/weyes pl_h1e/wih2es h1sih1e/nt. mo/rto:s h1e/syo h1we/su h1no/mn_ k'luwih1e/nt. g'e/rh2onts bhuHye/h1t nu-kwe mr_ye/h1t.

h2e/wh2os do:/m h1ludhe/t swo/m-kwe ne/potm_ wide/t. nu ph2tre/y we/rh1yet. kwo/d h1no/mn_ h1e/smey dhe/h1s? we/rh1t. h1e/smey me/ne ph2tre/s h1no/mn_ dhe/h1m_. k'luto/s h1e/st? k'luto/s h1e/st me/g'h2 k'le/wos h1e/smey h1e/st h1o/gwhim-kwe gwhe/nt. h1i/m ku/dhe gwhe/nt? h1i/m de/h2newi gwhe/nt. nu h2e/wh2os se/kwt. k'luto/s h1sye/h1t Hwr_dhye/h1t mo/rto:s-kwe h1e/syo h1no/mn_ k'le/wsoyh1ent. n_pte/y moyso/m h1ope/yet gwe:/n-kwe we/yh1onm_ bhe/ret si/sdeta:m-kwe me/dhu-kwe ph3ta:/m.

g'e/rh2onts se/dyet. sni/gwhs tine/h1t de/h2nu-kwe pl_nh1to/. snuso/s h1i/m gwe/mt we/rh1t-kwe. swe/k'ure moy suHnu/s g'eg'o/nh1e. g'e/rh2onts h1i/m spe/k'yet me/wdt-kwe se/kwt-kwe. se/nos h1e/smi me/roh2-kwe me/ne h1no/mn_ ne me/reti. me/ne n_pte/y me/ne h1no/mn_ dhe/h1m_ h1e/y h1i/d pro/ bhe/re:ti. mo/rto:s m_re/nt k'luto/m h1no/mn_ gwi/h3weti.

wl_/kwe me: gwe/ms g'o/nh1om me: h1e/ps. nu-kwe k'wo/n h1i/m ph2sdhi/ k'wo/n h1e/smi l_ghdhi/. h1yo/d wl_/kwos h1ludhe:/ti nu k'wo:/ h1i/m gwhn_ye/h1t. wl_/kwe bhugdhi/ nu h1n_/gwney kwse/pi dhe/gwhe. h2e/wsos kwse/pm_ h2epo/ h2e/g'e. g'e/rh2onts wl_/kwos h1i/m me: wido/yh1t. nu-kwe dhe/g'hom h1i/m tetl_h2dhi/. g'hwe:/res h1e/smo:d h2epo/ gwh2te/. nu-kwe so/h2wl_ h1i/m gwhr_nudhi/.

h1yo/d suHnu/s wiHro:/y g'eg'e/nh1eti h1e/syo de/h3reh2 bhe/rontes k'e/ywo:s h1e/syo k'o/ymom gwm_yo/nti. poh2ime:/n moyso/m we/dheti pe/kwto:r ghosti/bhos h1i/m pe/kweti gwe:/n me/lit bhe/reti dhh2bhro/s de/h3rom te:/tk'ti te/tk'o: h1e/smey yugo/m de/meti h1esh2o/s h1e/ymos me/dhu de/deh3ti pi/ph3onti-kwe h1e/dn_ti-kwe. h1yo/s do/h3r_ bhe/reti h1esh2o/s to/smey de/h3nom de/deh3ti. k'e/ywo:s polh1e/wes h1se/nti g'o/nh1os-kwe bhuHye/ti.

kwi/d mo/rtos bhe/reti? h1i/d h1e/syo h2me/si ne h1e/sti. h1e/syo ph2te:/r h1e/smey h1i/d g'n_h1te/y dhe/h1t. mo/rto:s h1i/d mluHe/nti. mo/rtos h1i/d k'le/wseti. h1i/d kwis ne h1e:/dti h1i/d kwis ne pi/ph3eti. pe/h2wr_ h1i/d ne h1e/wseti wo/dr_ h1i/d h2epo/ ne we/g'heti. h1yo/d mo/rtos dhg'he/mi k'e/yor h1i/d gwi/h3weti. kwi/d h1i/d h1e/sti? h1i/d h1no/mn_ h1e/sti.

h2n_re/s suHnu/s po/teys dhugh2te/rm_ we/nHt. swo:/y ph2tre/y bhe/h2t. nu ph2te:/r swo:/y bhre/h2trey we/rh1yet. po/tim gwe/h2omes me/ne-kwe suHno/wey h1e/syo dhugh2te/rm_ h2isk'o:/mes. nu gwh2ta:/m de/k'm_-kwe gwo:/s pro/ we/dheta:m. h1ludhe/ta:m po/tis-kwe to/h1 wide/t we/rh1yet-kwe. gho/stih1 sdto/m. si/sdeta:m to/ybhos-kwe me/dhu dhe/h1t. nu gho/stis mle/wHt. gwegwm_we/ te/we dhugh2te/rm_ h2isk'o/wos. dhugh2tre/s te/we h1we/dmn_ de/k'm_ gwo/wes h1e/sti. po/tis k'le/wset we/rh1t-kwe. k'l_ne/wmi te. we/yh1onm_ pi/ph3etom me/ne-kwe po/tn_yeh2h1 bhe/h2oh2.

po/tis h2ne:/r-kwe se/dyeta:m po/tis-kwe we/rh1yet. kwo/d h1we/dmn_ me/ne dhugh2tre/y de/deh3si? de/k'm_ gwo:/s de/k'm_-kwe h2o/wins de/deh3mi. me/ne dhugh2te:/r h1use/wih2 lewbhe/h2-kwe h1e/sti pl_h1e/wih2n_s h2o/wins h1a/ydhi. HeHk'u/h1-kwe dwo/h1 h1e/k'woh1 de/deh3mi. me/ne de/h3nom me/g'h2 h1e/sti. h1e/k'wo:s ku/dhe h1se/nti? de/h2newi sth2e/h1yonti. nu po/tis se/kwt. te/we de/h3rom me/g'h2 h1e/sti te/we-kwe suHno/wey me/ne dhugh2te/rm_ h1ope/yo:h2. h1i/ns me/ne h2e/g'rom we/dhe. to/d h1sye/h1t. h1e/y h1i/h2m_ h1e/syo do:/m we/dhe:ti. nu po/tis to/h1 spe/k'yet h1e/syo-kwe k'e:/r me/wdt.

h1yo/d h2ne:/r dhugh2tre/s h1we/dmn_ de/h3eti h1e/syeh2s-kwe ph2te:/r h1i/d h1a/yetor dhugh2te/rm_-kwe ne de/h3eti h1i/d h2ew dwi/s de/h3tu. h1yo/d gwe:/n h2e/lyom h1e/syeh2s po/teys h1r_sk'e:/ti h1e/syeh2s ph2te:/r po/teyey we/dhmn_ h2ew de/h3tu. h1yo/d wiHro/s gwe/nh2m_ h1e/syo k'o/ymom we/dhe:ti we/dhmn_-kwe ne de/h3eti h1e/syeh2s ph2tre/y de/k'm_ h2o/wins de/h3tu. h1yo/d wiHro/s bhoydhe/h2m Hrune/peti h2e/lyo:y de/k'm_ gwo:/s de/h3tu. h1yo/s h2e/lyosyo gwe/nh2m_ stune/deti h1e/syeh2s po/teyey moyso/m de/h3tu. h1yo/s do/h3r_ h1a/yetor ne-kwe kwid de/deh3eti so/ dusk'le/we:s h1e/sti. to/d dhe/h1mn_ h1e/sti.

dhugh2te:/r h1e/syeh2s ph2te/rm_ gwe/mt h1e/syeh2ey-kwe h2e/g't. h2ne:/r nos gwegwo/me h2isk'e/ti te. h1i/m ye/h2esi? dhugh2te:/r we/rh1t. ye/h2oh2 ghoste/yo:m-kwe dhh1me/ns dedwo/yh2e. nu me/h2te:r h1e/syeh2ey mle/wHt. me: tre/ses. h1yo/d k'o/m k'o/ymom h1ludho/m bhe/yh2m_. h2ne/rm_ dedo/rk'h2e h1we/sus-kwe h1e/sti. h1e/syo dhe/h1mn_ g'hleyno/m h1e/sti h1e/syo-kwe h1e/k'wo:s HeHk'e/wes h1se/nti. nu dhugh2te:/r ste/h2t h1e/syeh2s-kwe me/h2term_ gwe/h2t.

h2e/wso:s le/wkt. po/teys dhugh2te:/r h1ne/dht h1e/syeh2s-kwe me/h2te:r Hre/wdHt h1i/h2m_-kwe h2wl_/h1neh2s pl_/h2seh2h1 wose/yet. pl_/h2seh2 h1rudhe/h1t. h2ne/res kwe/kwlo:y gwo:/s yuge/nt. kwe/kwlos tetk'ne/s h3e/pos h1e/st yugo/m-kwe dhh2bhro/syo h3e/pos h1e/st. h1e/syeh2s po/tis h1i/h2m_ h1i/m u/d tl_ne/h2t h1e/syeh2s-kwe bhre/h2te:r h1i/h2m_ se/kweto. sh2we/ns kwe/kwlos g'hleyno/s h1e/st. dhugh2te:/r h2ew spe/k'yet h1e/syeh2s-kwe k'e:/r tre/mt. h2e/k'monm_ do/ru-kwe dhgwhh2e/nt. h1e/syeh2s swe/k'urosyo do:/m kwse/pi h1ludho/nt. swe/k'uros h2e/nti ste/h2t mle/wHt-kwe. dhu/gh2ter wik'dhi/.

ph2te:/r kwe/kwloy h2e/nti ste/h2t h1e/syo-kwe dhugh2tre/y h2e/g't. dhu/gh2ter te/we po/teys dhe/h1mn_ me/ne de/ms h1e/ysi. te/we swe/k'urom k'le/wse h1e/syo-kwe pe/h2wr_ ph2sdhi/. te/we po/tim me: dhrughye/s h1e/syo dayh2ure/y lewbhe/h2 h1sdhi/. h2wl_/h1neh2m pl_/h2seh2m moyso/m-kwe h1a/yso. te/we me/h2term_ memn_dhi/. te/we g'o/nh1om h2e/le. deywo:/s te/we sto/yghom h3rog'e/yoyh1ent. h1yo/d te h2e/ghos gwm_ye:/ti gwm_dhi/ nos. dhe/h1mn_ nos toy stesto/h2e. dhu/gh2ter h1idhi/.

nu po/tni swo/m dhe/h1mn_ wik'dhi/. po/tni te/we swe/k'uro:y h1use/wih2 h1sdhi/. po/teys dayh2ure/y lewbhe/h2 h1sdhi/. pe/h2wr_-kwe-ge toy g'hleyno/m h1sye/h1t. te/we h1e/k'woh1 HeHk'u/h1 h1sih1ta:/m. nu-kwe toy h3e/ngwn_ polh1u/ h1sye/h1t. pe/rkwune k'o/d dhe/h1mn_ dhgwhh2dhi/. te/we-ge se/h1mn_ polh1u/ h1sye/h1t. de/k'm_ lewbho/ns suHnu/ns h2e/loyh1s. polh1u/h2 we/tesh2 gwi/h3woyh1tom. se/noh1-kwe-ge k'o/m bhuHih1to/m. nu-kwe wos so/h2wl_ k'o/m spe/k'ye. h2e/wsos-kwe wos diwe/s dhu/gh2ter lukdhi/. dye/w nos ph2ter we/kwos k'ludhi/. nu-kwe to/ybhos h1we/suh2 dh3dhi/.

h2ne:/r po/tis-kwe sdta:/m. po/tis we/rh1yet. me/ne toy dhugh2te/rm_ de/deh3mi. te/we moy bhoydhe/h2m dh3dhi/. bhoydha:/s dh3ta:/m. nu h2ne:/r se/kwt. dye:/ws k'le/wseti dhe/g'ho:m-kwe me/h2te:r k'le/wseti. so/h2wl_ h1i/h2m_ dedo/rk'e de/h2nu-kwe h1i/h2m_ g'n_ne/h3ti. h1yo/s k'a:/m bhoydhe/h2m Hrune/peti h1e/syo gwo/wes n_k'ih1e/nt h1e/syo h1no/mn_ mr_ye/h1t. nu we/yh1onm_ ph3ta:/m kwse/ps-kwe gwe/mt.

h1n_gwni/s dhe/gwhet dhuh2mo/s u/d h1e/yt po/tnih2-kwe h1e/smi si/sdet. swe/k'uros h1i/h2m_ spe/k'yet h1i/h2-kwe tre/mt. nu h1e/syeh2s po/teys dayh2we:/r wo/dr_ bhe/ret h1e/syeh2ey-kwe h1i/d dhe/dheh1t. nu h1e/syeh2s po/teys swe/so:r h1e/syeh2ey pl_/h2seh2m de/h3t h1i/h2-kwe pl_/h2seh2h1 wose/yeto. swe/so:r h1e/syeh2ey h2wl_/h1neh2m woyde/yet. nu swe/k'uros h2e/g't. dhu/gh2ter nos dhugh2te:/r h1e/si. me/lit h3e/ngwn_-kwe h1ddhi/ me/dhu-kwe pi/ph3e pe/h2wr_ nos toy h1e/sti. h1e:/dt pi/ph3et h1e/syeh2s-kwe k'e:/r gwhe/rt. k'wo:/ h1e/syeh2i le/ght.

dwo/h1 bhre/h2terh1e k'o/m tk'ita:/m. po/tnih2h1 wo/dr_ de/h2nus dukta:/m. h1i/h2so:m po/teyes wr_g'yo/nt so/h2wl_ dhe/gwhet. bhre/h2tr_s po/tnih2 h2e/lyosyo po/tn_yeh2ey bhe/h2t. dhrughye/s me. we/wket. ne te dhrughyo/m. nu we/kwesh2 po/tins dre/mt. bhre/h2te:r h2e/lyom stune/dt kre/wh2s-kwe me/lh3t. gwe/nh2h1e HrudHta:/m swe/k'uros-kwe k'le/wt. h1ludhe/t h1ente/r-kwe ste/h2t. l_h1to/m. kwi/d dhh1to/m? h2e/ghos k'o/ymom pl_ne/h1t. sth2e/h1yeta:m ni/-kwe spe/k'yeta:m.

bhre/h2te:r bhre/h2trey bhe/rg'hs h1e/sti. dhrughyo/ntih2 dn_g'hwe/h2s bhre/h2tern_s bhine/dti. dn_g'hwe/h2s h1o/gwhis h1e/sti. g'r_h2n_te/s we/kwos sto/yghos h1e/sti. me/h2tr_s k'e:/r h1n_gwni/s h1e/sti. h1we/sus swe/k'uros g'hime/ni pe/h2wr_ h1e/sti. h1use/wih2 snuso/s me/lit h1e/sti. dusk'le/we:s dayh2we:/r h2be/li kwr_/mis h1e/sti. h1ye/h2 h1e/syeh2s po/tim dhrughye/ti se/h2 dhh1me/ni wl_/kwos h1e/sti. me/lit dhrughyo/ntey to/smey dn_g'hwe/h2i h1e/sti h1o/gwhis-kwe k'e/rdi h1e/sti. wl_/kwos k'wo:/ ne bhu/Ht. dwo/h1 bhre/h2terh1e kwe/kwlom yugo/y dukte/s. bhre/h2teres k'o/m kwo/r se/dyonti h1n_gwni/s h2e/n dhe/gwheti. k'o/d se/nom we/kwos h1e/sti.

gwe:/n h1e/syeh2s po/tim kwse/pi gwe/mt we/rh1t-kwe. k'ludhi/ me. te/we moy bhre/h2te:r we/wket. se/kwt. de/mi nos gho/stis h1e/si. me/lit nos h3e/ngwn_-kwe h1e:/dsi. gho/stis ne h1e/smi te/we po/tnih2 h1e/smi. h1i/h2m_ k'le/wset we/rh1yet-kwe. k'o/d we/wket? h1i/d we/wket. me/ne bhre/h2term_ h1r_sk'o:/h2 h1e/smey-kwe bhe/h2oh2. k'o/smi k'o/ymoy gho/stis ne h1e/si lewbhe/h2 moy h1e/si. nu ste/h2t h1i/m-kwe gwe/h2t. h1i/m h1e/k'wo:ys wide/t h1e/smey-kwe bhe/h2t.

dwo/h1 bhre/h2terh1e ph2te/ri sth2e/h1yeta:m. to/h1 k'le/wset nu-kwe we/rh1yet. h1yo/s swo/m bhre/h2term_ stune/dt so/ h2e/lyo:y moyso/m h1ope/yetu. h1ye/h2-kwe dhrughye/t se/h2 h2e/lyosyo gwne/h2ey wo/dr_ bhe/retu. nu h1e/syo we/kwos k'luta:/m. so/ moyso/m h1i/m we/dhet h1i/h2-kwe wo/dr_ bhe/ret. nu g'e/rh2onts mle/wHt. sdto/m we/yh1onm_-kwe pi/ph3etom. bhre/h2tr_s kre/wh2s wo/dr_ ne h1e/sti. si/sdeta:m ph3ta:/m-kwe.

po/tis h2e/g'mn_ h2e/g'rom g'hime/ni h2e/g'et. sni/gwhs gwe/mt h2ew-kwe ne gwe/mt. h1e/syo po/tnih2 se/dyet sto/yghom-kwe spe/k'yet. wl_/kwo:s kwse/pi HrudHe/nt. h1i/m h2use/si wide/t sni/gwhs h1e/smi k'e/yo sri/Hgos h1i/m h1e/pt mr_two/s h1e/st. h1i/h2 h1widhe/wh2 bhu/Ht Hre/wdHt-kwe. h1e/syeh2s po/teys dayh2we:/r h1ludhe/t. we/rh1yet. gwe:/n me: Hre/wdHs. te/we g'o/nh1om h2e/lo:h2 te/we-kwe h2e/g'rom pe/h2soh2. nu h1widhe/wh2 we/rh1t. h1we/sus h1e/si te/we-kwe k'o/ymoy tk'e/yoh2. h1widhe/wh2 h1e/syo k'o/ymoy tk'e/yt.

h1yo/d wiHro/s h1e/syo ph2te/rm_ stune/deti h1e/g'hs gwe/h2tu. h1yo/d wiHro/s bhre/h2tr_s h2e/g'rom h1a/yetor h1i/m moyso/m-kwe h2ew h1ope/yetu. h1yo/s h1e/syo swe:k'uro/m dhrughye:/ti h1e/smey kwis de/h3rom me: de/h3t. h1yo/d snuso/s swe/k'urom stune/deti swo/m dhe/h1mn_ h2ew gwe/h2tu. h1yo/d swe/k'uros h1use/wih2m snuso/m h2epo/ h2e/g'e:ti h1e/syeh2ey de/k'm_ h2o/wins h1a/ytu. h1yo/d wiHro/s h1e/syo bhre/h2term_ gwhe/neti k'o/ymoy me: tk'e/yt. h1yo/d wiHro/s h1e/syo swe:k'uro/syo h2o/wins ste/h2ye:ti h1i/h2n_s h2ew dwi/s de/h3tu.

ph2te:/r bhre/h2tr_bhos h2e/g't. k'o/m sdto/m gwe/nh2h1e-kwe si/sdeta:m. h1ye/h2 we/wketom de/h2nu te/h2 h2epo/ we/g'hetu. se/nos h1e/smi me/roh2-kwe wl_/kwo:s h2e/g'mn_ e/nu h1ludho:/nti h2e/g'mn_ k'o/m pe/h2setes. nu sdta:/m gwe/nh2h1e-kwe to:/ys sdta:/m. h1e/y to/h1 wide/t h1n_gwni/s-kwe g'hleyno/s h1e/st h1e/syo-kwe k'e:/r gwhe/rt.

ph2te:/r se/dt. tre/yes bhre/h2teres h1e/smi sth2e/h1yont. to/ybhos h2e/g't. se/nos h1e/smi me/rtis-kwe me gwm_sk'e/ti. k'lute/ me. h2e/rh3yontey to/smey h2e/g'rom h1ope/yoh2 h2o/wins ph2sn_te/y to/smey gwo:/s h1ope/yoh2 h1e/k'wons h2e/g'ontey to/smey kwe/kwlom h1ope/yoh2. bhre/h2tern_s wos me: stundte/ h1no/mn_-kwe wos mo/rtoysu h1we/su h1sye/h1t. h1e/syo we/kwos k'luwe/nt. nu h1e/y me/dhu pe/h3t le/ght-kwe.

g'e/rh2onts sh2we/ni se/dyet bhe/h2t-kwe. HeHk'u/s h1e/sm_ h2e/rh3yom h2e/g'mn_ de/h2nu h2e/g'om mo/ri neh2we/h1 ya:/m wl_/kwom gwhe/nm_. nu se/nos h1e/smi ke/h2ikos-kwe h1e/smi. ph2we/ni se/dyoh2. me/ne k'e:/r g'hleyno/m h1e/sti me/g'h2-kwe moy k'le/wos h1e/sti. me/ne h1no/mn_ gwi/h3woyh1t.

ph2te:/r k'e/yo tre/yes-kwe to/y h1e/smi si/sdont. suHnu/s bhe/h2t. kwe/smey do:/m de/deh3si? g'e/rh2onts we/rh1t. to/smey h1i/m de/deh3mi h1yo/s me h2e/leti. h2e/lyos se/kwt. wo/dr_ toy bhe/roh2. gwo:/s toy h2me/lg'mi. g'e/rh2onts to/yso:m h3e/pos wide/t me/dhyom-kwe de/yk'et. me/dhyos mle/wHt. de/mey te ne h2e/lom. do:/m toy h1e/sti.

h1yo/s se/nom ph2te/rm_ h2e/le:ti g'r_h2n_te/s do:/m to/smey h1e/seti. h1yo/s se/nom ph2te/rm_ line/kweti h2g'me/ns kwid to/smey me: h1e/st. h1yo/d suHnu/s wiHro:/y ne h1e/seti h1e/syo dhugh2tre/s ne/po:ts h2e/g'rom h1a/yetor. h1yo/d ne/po:ts h1e/smey ne h1e/seti h1e/syo bhre/h2te:r h1i/m h1a/yetor. h1widhe/wh2 g'o/nh1o:y h2e/g'rom h2le/ksetu. h1i/m kwis ne h1a/yetor.

to/yso:m ph2te:/r mr_two/s h1e/st. tre/yes bhre/h2teres sde/nt. h1e/k'wons h2e/g'onts so/ bhe/h2t. h1e/k'wo:s moy h1se/nti. h2e/rh3yonts so/ we/rh1t. dwo/h1 moy h1e/k'woh1 dh3dhi/. to/h1 me: h1ope/yo:h2. nu bhre/h2te:r h2e/lyom stune/dt h2e/lyos-kwe h1i/m stune/dt. bhuge/nt de/h2nu-kwe dr_me/nt. de/h2nu h1e/k'wom h2epo/ we/g'het. nu h1i/m we/dni wido/nt bhih2e/nt-kwe. h2o/wins ph2so/nts so/ Hre/wdHt. kwi/d dhedhh1te/s?

tre/yes bhre/h2teres kwse/pi si/sdont. me/dhyos bhe/h2t. ph2te:/r nos mr_two/s h1e/sti. gwo:/s bhine/domes h1i/ns-we: k'o/m h2le/kso:mes? h2e/rh3yonts so/ we/rh1t. h1ye/h2es wi/ h1ye/nti wl_/kwos ta:/s gwhe/nti. h2o/wins ph2so/nts so/ se/kwt. h2e/g'mn_ k'o/m h2le/kso:mes h2e/g'rom-kwe me: bhine/domes k'o/ymoy-kwe nos tk'e/yomes. me/dhyos we/wket. to/d h1sye/h1t. nu mr_two:/y g'huto/m me/dhu dh3e/nt.

me/g'h2s ta/wros h2e/g'mn_ we/dhet se/nos-kwe ta/wros h1i/h1 h1e/yt. me/g'h2s ta/wros h1e/smey we/rh1yet. se/nos h1e/si. n_sme/d h2epo/ gwh2dhi/. h2epo/ gwe/h2t. so/h2wl_ dhe/gwhet de/h2nu-kwe te/rst. h2e/g'mn_ tr_sye/t. me/g'h2s ta/wros wo/dr_ ne wide/t. h2e/lyos h2k'me/ni ste/h2t. se/kwt. wo/dr_ wo/ydh2e. h1i/d h2k'me/ni Hn_dhi/ h1e/sti. h1i/d h1i/d we/dhet ph3e/nt-kwe tr_pe/nt-kwe. h2ne:/r m_g'h2e/y ta/wro:y h1e/sti se/nos ta/wros sto/yghom wo/yde.

h1widhe/wh2 h3re/g'm_ h1ludhe/t. h3re:/g'nih2 h3re:/g'i se/dyet. h1widhe/wh2 bhe/h2t. h3re/g' h1widhe/wh2 h1e/smi me/ne-kwe po/tis mr_two/s h1e/sti. me/ne po/teys bhre/h2teres me/ne h2e/g'rom kapyo/nti. me/ne-kwe me g'o/nh1om h2le/kse. h3re:/g's h1widhe/wh2m_ spe/k'yet to/ybhos-kwe mle/wHt. kwe/smey h1e/y h1e/sti? we/wkont. h1e/y nos h1e/sti. h3re:/g's h2e/g't. h1e/y h1widhwe/h2s g'o/nh1o:y h1e/sti. h1i/m h2ew dedh3te/ h1widhwe/h2ey-kwe de/k'm_ h2o/wins h1ope/yete. h3re/g'm_ k'luwe/nt h1i/m-kwe h2ew dh3e/nt.

g'e/rh2onts k'e/yo h1e/syo-kwe pne/wmn_ h1i/m line/kwt. h1e/syo g'o/nh1o:s h1i/m h2m_bhi/ sth2e/h1yont. we/rh1yet. k'le/wsete me. tr_syo/h2 wo/dr_ moy we/g'hete. ph2te/rn_s h1e/ymi. h2e/g'mn_ k'o/m ph2ste/ bhre/h2term_-kwe wos me: stundte/. g'huto/m moy me/dhu h1a/yte dhg'hme/h1 me k'e/lete h2e/k'monm_-kwe stoh2e/yete. me/ne h1no/mn_ memn_te/. nu h2ew ne bhe/h2t HrudHe/nt-kwe.

po/tey-ge h2epo/ lelo/ykwth2e. kwi/s h2o/wins pe/h2seti kwi/s h2e/rh3ye:ti? nu h1widhe/wh2 h1e/smi me/ne-kwe k'e:/r ste/wdt. h1n_gwni/s dhe/gwheti h1e/smi ne se/dyesi. te/we sth2dhlo/m spe/k'yoh2 ku/dhe-ge h1e/si? sto/yghom spe/k'yoh2 le/wbhe wi/Hre h2ew gwm_dhi/. te/we-kwe g'o/nh1o:s HrudHe/nti. k'wo:/ te h2isk'e/ti pe/kwom toy ne h1e:/dsi. nu-kwe te dhe/g'ho:m k'e/leti.

ph2ter nos line/kwsi te/we g'o/nh1o:s h1sme/s. nu-kwe nos kwi/s pro/ we/dhe:ti? kwi/s te/we sth2dhlo/y se/deti? te/we-ge nos we/kwesh2 h2e/wsu h1e/sti. te/we-kwe nos h1no/mn_ dn_g'hwe/h2i h1e/sti. nu-kwe h2e/wso:s le/wkt ne-kwe bhe/wdhs. mo/rtoysu-kwe-ge me/g'h2s h1e/s. n_/dhgwhitom toy k'le/wos h1e/sti. nu te h2e/k'monm_ upe/r stoh2e/yomes. dhg'he/mi k'e/yso te/we we/kwos h2le/kso:mes.

bhre/h2teres mr_two/m h2use/si dhh1me/ns h1e/g'hs bhe/ront. h1i/m pl_/h2seh2h1 wose/yont. h2e/g'mn_ h1i/m se/kweto. h1i/m dre/wi loghe/yont pe/h2wr_-kwe bhore/yont. pe/h2wr_ h1i/m h1e/wset dhuh2mo/s-kwe dye:/m u/d h1e/yt. h1e/syo po/tnih2 Hre/wdHt k'wo:/-kwe Hre/wdHt. nu h1i/m dhg'hme/h1 k'e/lont. h2e/k'monm_ h1i/m upe/r stoh2e/yont mr_two:/y-kwe g'huto/m me/dhu dh3e/nt. so/h2wl_ se/ngwt. nu kwse/ps gwe/mt k'wo:/-kwe h2k'me/ni le/ght.

tre/yes bhre/h2teres h2k'me/ni sth2e/h1yont. me/dhyos h2e/g't. dye/w nos ph2ter k'le/wse. dhe/g'hom nos k'l_nudhi/. h1o/ytom wos h1ope/yomes. gwo:/s k'o/m h2le/kso:mes h2e/g'mn_ me: bhine/domes. h1e/syo nos kwis bhre/h2term_ me: stune/dt. h1yo/s k'o/m h1o/ytom Hrune/peti dye:/ws h1i/m gwhn_ye/h1t dhe/g'ho:m-kwe h1i/m me: we/g'hoyh1t.

Hyudhsmo/s le/ght h1e/syo-kwe suHnu/bhos se/kwt. Hyudhsmo/s h1e/sm_. h1o/gwhim gwhe/nm_ h3re:g'e/s-kwe gho/stins gwhe/nm_. h3re:g'e/s Hro/th2om h2e/g'om. me/g'h2 moy k'le/wos h1e/sti. nu me me/rtis gwm_sk'e/ti ne-kwe dedwo/yh2e. mo/rto:s m_re/nt deywo:/s ne m_re/nt. h1yo/d mo/rtosyo h1no/mn_ gwi/h3weti h1e/syo k'le/wos n_/dhgwhitom h1e/sti. k'le/wos wos me/g'h2 h1e/stu. nu h1e/syo pne/wmn_ h1i/m line/kwt.

swe/so:r h2k'me/ni se/dyet. h1e/syeh2s bhre/h2te:r h1ludhe/t h1i/h2-kwe h1e/smey mle/wHt. ph2te:/r nos mr_two/s h1e/sti. nu kwi/s gwo:/s pe/h2sti? we/wket. h1i/ns h2le/ksoh2. kwi/s nos me/h2term_ h2e/leti? h1i/h2m_ h2e/loh2. nu se/kwt. kwi/d swe/sor dhe/dheh1esi? h2e/g't. pe/h2wr_ h2le/kso:h2 h1e/syo-kwe we/kwesh2 meme/noh2. nu k'wo:/ gwe/mt h1ente/r-kwe le/ght.

ph2ter sto/yghoh1 h1idhi/ ph2tro:/m-kwe sto/yghoh1 h1idhi/. de/h2nu neh2we/h1 gwh2dhi/ ne/h2us h1i/m we/g'hoyh1t. h2e/wsos diwe/s dhu/gh2ter h1e/smey lukdhi/. nu so/h2wl_ h1i/m spe/k'ye dhe/g'hom me/h2ter h1i/m h1a/yso. ph2tr_bhi/s sdye/h1t g'huto/m-kwe me/dhu ph3ye/h1t. h1e/syo-kwe h1no/mn_ me: mr_ye/h1t. h1e/syo k'le/wos n_/dhgwhitom h1sye/h1t h1i/m-kwe memn_me/.

kwi/d po/tey so/h2wl_ h1e/sti? so/h2wl_ diwe/s h3o/kw h1e/sti. n_sme/ dedo/rk'e? n_sme/ gwo:/s-kwe dedo/rk'e. kwi/h1 ye/h2ti? swo/h1 kwe/kwloh1 ye/h2ti. kwi/s sh2we/ns kwe/kwlom de/wkti? HeHk'e/wes h1e/k'wo:s h1i/m duke/nti. h1yo/d h1regwesye/ti so/h2wl_ ku/ se/ngwt? mre/y se/ngwt. kwi/s-kwe h1i/d h2ew we/dheti? h2e/wso:s diwe/s dhugh2te:/r h1i/d we/dheti. dhe/g'ho:m gwhr_ne/wti? h1i/h2m_ gwhr_ne/wti. su/Hnew so/h2wl_ spe/k'ye. h1i/d ne sth2e/h1yeti. gwe/h2t. He/h2mr_ kwse/pm_ se/kwetor kwse/ps-kwe He/h2mr_ se/kwetor.

h1yo/d h2we/st po/tey kwse/ps ku/dhe lelo/ykwe? le/wbhe dhg'he/mi Hn_dhi/ lelo/ykwe. h2ste/res-kwe kwo/r lelikwe:/r? lelikwe:/r. He/h2mn_s bhe/h2os h1i/ns we/nt. kwse/ps h2ew gwe/mt? h2ew gwe/mt. h1yo/d so/h2wl_ se/ngwt kwse/ps gwe/mt. kwi/d we/dheti? swe/pnom sri/Hgos-kwe we/dheti. kwi/s bhe/wdheti? k'wo:/ poh2ime:/n-kwe bhe/wdhetes. wl_/kwos gwm_sk'e/ti. kwi/s dedwo/ye? h2o/weyes dedwiye:/r. kwi/d-kwe kwse/ps h2e/wso:s-kwe h1ste/s? dwo/yh1 swe/sorh1e h1ste/s. h1yo/d kwse/ps le/ytt h2e/wso:s gwm_ye/ti. h1yo/d h2e/wso:s le/ytt kwse/ps gwm_ye/ti.

kwi/s po/tey dye:/m teto/lh2e? me/g'h2 do/ru dye:/m teto/lh2e. do/ru ku/dhe stesto/h2e? h1wr_Huye/h2i dhg'he/mi stesto/h2e. kwi/d-kwe dhe/g'ho:m teto/lh2e? h1i/h2m_ kwid ne teto/lh2e. h1i/h2 teto/ne. dye:/ws se/ngwt? ne se/ngwt. h1e/y upe/r teto/ne. kwi/s dye:/m upe/r dhe/h1t? pr_h2wo:/s deywo:/s h1i/m upe/r dhh1e/nt. dye:/ws nos ph2te:/r h1e/sti dhe/g'ho:m-kwe nos me/h2te:r h1e/sti.

kwi/s po/tey dhg'hme/y we/dos de/deh3ti? dye:/ws dhg'hme/y we/dos de/deh3ti. h1i/d-kwe kwi/h1 de/deh3ti? h1o/gwhis kl_He/ni k'e/yo wo/dr_-kwe h1e/pt. gwo/wes tr_syo/nt? tr_syo/nt dhe/g'ho:m-kwe te/rst. nu pe/rkwunos h1o/gwhim gwhe/nt. h1o/gwhis kwo/r k'e/yor? kl_He/ni k'e/yor ne-kwe ste/h2t. nu wo/dr_ h1ne/dht h2we/rst-kwe. kwi/s wo/dr_ pe/h3t? dhe/g'ho:m h1i/d pe/h3t. h1yo/d h2we/rst dhe/g'ho:m pe/h3t h2o/weyes-kwe gwi/h3wonti g'r_h2no/m-kwe h2we/kst.

kwi/d k'lu/te g'he/ymn_ h1e/sti? g'he/ymn_ sri/Hg'esos de/h2itis h1e/sti. kwi/d g'hime/ni diwe/s gwe/mt? sni/gwhs diwe/s gwe/mt. gwo/wes g'hime/ni ku/dhe sth2e/h1yonti? gwowsth2o/y sth2e/h1yonti. pe/h2wr_-kwe k'o/ymoy dhe/gwheti. kwi/d g'hime/ni dhedhh1me/s? ph2we/ni se/dyomes g'r_h2no/m-kwe h1e/dmes. h1yo/d so/h2wl_ tewh2so/m bhu/Ht sni/gwhs tine/h1ti? tine/h1ti wo/dr_-kwe bhu/Ht. wo/dr_ kwo/r h1e/yti? wo/dr_ mo/ri h1e/yti. nu He/h2mo:r dweh2re/h2 bhu/Ht. g'he/ymn_ gwe/mt g'he/ymn_-kwe le/ytt. dhe/g'ho:m stesto/h2e.

po/tey polh1e/wes sro/wo:s mo/ri ih2e/nti. mo/ri kwi/h1 ne ple/h1t? ne ple/h1t. so/h2wl_ mre/ys we/dos pi/ph3eti. we/dos-kwe ku/ h1e/yti? dye:/m u/d h1e/yti. nu h2we/rst we/dos-kwe h1i/d h2ew gwe/h2t. mo/ri dhubhro/m h1e/sti? dhubhro/m h1wr_Hu/-kwe h1e/sti. mre/ys kwis bhudhme/nm_ ne wide/t. kwi/s k'o/d h2re/ydhet? deywo:/s h1i/d h2re/ydhont.

ph2ter h1n_gwni/s kwo/r g'eg'o/nh1e? tre/yes h1n_gwne/ys g'o/nh1o:s h1se/nti. dye/wi g'eg'o/nh1e. we/desi g'eg'o/nh1e. dre/wi g'eg'o/nh1e. h1n_gwni/m dre/wi wido/m. h1e/smo:d kwi/h1 h1ne/dht? h1yo/d pe/rkwunos do/ru gwhe/nti h1n_gwni/s h1ne/dht. kwi/d h1n_gwni/s h1e:/dti? do/ru h1e:/dti. kwi/d h1i/m more/yeti? wo/dr_ h1i/m more/yeti. kwi/s pe/h2wr_ k'o/ymoy h2le/kseti? k'o/ymosyo po/tnih2 h1i/d h2le/kseti. h1e/y nos lewbho/s h1e/sti.

gho/stipotey nos dhe/g'ho:m me/h2te:r kwi/h1 h1e/sti? dhe/g'ho:m nos h2e/leti. dhe/g'ho:m me/h2te:r h1e/k'wons gwo:/s-kwe h2e/leti. kwi/s-kwe dhe/g'ho:m h2e/leti? so/h2wl_ we/dos-kwe h1i/h2m_ h2e/letes. kwi/d dhe/g'ho:m bhe/reti? ko/lHonn_s do/ruh2 n_sme/-kwe bhe/reti. dhe/g'ho:m k'e/mh2t? ne k'e/mh2t. h2e/rh3trom dhe/g'ho:m de/reti dhe/g'ho:m-kwe nos g'r_h2no/m de/deh3ti. kwi/s dhe/g'ho:m pe/h2sti? deywo:/s h1i/h2m_ ph2se/nti. dhe/g'ho:m h1wr_He/wih2 h1e/sti dhubhre/h2-kwe h1e/sti.

kwe/yes po/tey deywo:/s h1se/nti? deywo:/s diwyo:/s h1se/nti. ku/dhe tk'iye/nti? dye/wi tk'iye/nti. polh1e/wes h1se/nti? polh1e/wes h1se/nti. kwi/s h1e/yso:m pr_h2wo/s h1e/sti? dye:/ws deywo:/m pr_h2wo/s h1e/sti. kwi/d nos de/dh3n_ti? h1we/suh2 nos de/dh3n_ti. h1use/wo:m de/h3tores h1se/nti. kwi/m wn_He/nt? h1yo/s h1so/nt mle/wHti to/m wn_He/nt. deywo:/s h2ne/res h1se/nti? ne h1se/nti. h2ne/res m_re/nt deywo:/s ne m_re/nt. su/Hnew k'le/wse. dedr_k'e:/r nos h2wise/nt-kwe nos.

kwi/d nos me/g'h2 dye:/ws de/h3t? dye:/ws nos He/h2mr_ de/h3t. kwi/d-kwe nos dhe/g'ho:m me/h2te:r de/deh3ti? g'r_h2no/m nos we/yh1onm_-kwe de/deh3ti. kwi/d-kwe nos h2e/wso:s h1ope/yeti? bhe/h2os nos h1ope/yeti. kwi/s nos swe/pnom h1a/yt? kwse/ps nos swe/pnom h1a/yt. kwi/d deywo/ybhos de/h3omes? h1e/ymos h1so/nt we/kwos dh3te/ h1e/ymos-kwe g'huto/m we/dos dh3te/. h1yo/s h1e/ymos h1a/yt deywo:/s to/smey h1a/yn_t. de/h3tis deywo/ybhos lewbhe/h2 h1e/sti.

kwi/h1 po/tey deywo/ns g'huHe/yo:mes? h1i/ns h1sn_te/h1 we/kwesh1 g'huHe/yete. h1i/ns dhro/wghoh1 me: g'huHe/yete. kwi/m deywo:/s k'le/wsonti? h1yo/s h1i/ns h1sn_te/h1 we/kwesh1 g'huHe/yeti to/m k'le/wsonti. ku/ sth2e/h1yo:mes? ph2we/ni h1e/pi sth2e/h1yete h1i/ns-kwe wewr_tte/. kwi/d h1e/ymos bhe/h2omes? k'lute/h2 deywo:/m h1no/mn_h2 bhh2te/. nu h1r_h1te/. h1i/d k'r_de/s k'luwe/nt. k'o/ymom gwme/nt sde/nt-kwe.

kwi/s po/tey dye:/ws h1e/sti? dye:/ws deywo:/m wiHro:/m-kwe ph2te:/r h1e/sti. kwi/d h1e/y dedo/rk'e? n_sme/ dedo/rk'e deywo/ns-kwe dedo/rk'e. ku/ se/dyeti? upe/r se/dyeti. dhe/g'ho:m diwe/s po/tnih2 h1e/sti? h1e/syo po/tnih2 h1e/sti h1e/y-kwe h1e/syeh2s po/tis h1e/sti. dhe/g'ho:m k'e/leti? h1i/h2m_ k'e/leti. kwe/syo dhugh2te:/r h2e/wso:s h1e/sti? h2e/wso:s diwe/s dhugh2te:/r h1e/sti. le/wbha dye:/m g'huHe/ye. dye/w nos ph2ter k'ludhi/. dye:/ws te k'luye/h1t.

kwi/s k'lu/te pe/rkwunos h1e/sti? pe/rkwunos gwhono/s h1e/sti. tewh2so/s h1e/sti. ku/ tk'e/yti? bhe/rg'hoy tk'e/yti. kwi/m gwhe/nti? pe/rkwunos h1o/gwhim wl_/kwom-kwe gwhe/nti. kwi/m-kwe ne more/yeti? h1yo/s ne dhrughye/ti to/m ne more/yeti. h1yo/d pe/rkwunos gwe/mt tre/somes? tre/somes gwo/wes-kwe tre/sonti. h1yo/s h1so/nt wo/yde so/ ne dedwo/ye. k'e/ywe me: tre/ses.

gho/stipotey g'huto/m we/dos deywo/ns kwi/h1 h1r_sk'e/ti? h1n_gwni/s h1i/d h1i/ns bhe/reti. kwi/s-kwe h1n_gwni/s h1e/sti? deywo:/m h1o/h3s h1e/sti. deywo:/s h1i/h1 h1e/dn_ti. kwi/d h1e/smey h1ope/yomes? h1e/smey we/yh1onm_ g'r_h2no/m-kwe h1ope/yomes. ku/dhe se/dyeti? k'o/ymoy se/dyeti. k'o/ymoy gho/stis h1e/sti. h1n_gwni/m g'huHe/ye.

kwi/s ph2ter h2e/wso:s h1e/sti? h2e/wso:s diwe/s dhugh2te:/r h1e/sti. h1rudhe/h1ti? h1rudhe/h1ti. kwi/d we/dheti? bhe/h2os He/h2mr_-kwe we/dheti. h2e/wsosm_ we/ydsoh2. dhu/gh2ter bhudhdhi/. h1yo/d h2we/st h1i/h2m_ wide:/si. kwi/s h1i/h2m_ kwe/k't? h1yo/s bhe/wdheti so/ h1i/h2m_ kwe/k't. bhe/wdhm_ h1i/h2m_-kwe wido/m. h1rudhe/h1t. h1yo/d h2we/st bhudhme/.

gho/stipotey dwo/h1 diwe/s suHnu/h1 k'le:/m. kwi/d dhedhh1te/s? HeHk'u/ns h1e/k'wons h2e/g'etes. kwi/h1 gwm_ye/tes? Hro/th2oh1 gwm_ye/tes. h1yo/d h2we/st gwm_ta:/m. kwi/m h2le/ksetes? h2ne/rn_s ne/h2wi h2le/ksetes. h1yo/d ne/h2us se/ngweti gwm_ta:/m. ku/ tk'ite/s? dye/wi tk'ite/s. h2le/ksoyh1ta:m te.

kwi/d me/g'h2 h1so/nt h1e/sti? h1so/nt to/d h1e/sti h1yo/d h1e/sti. kwi/d-kwe dhro/wghos h1e/sti? dhro/wghos to/d h1e/sti h1yo/d ne h1e/sti. kwi/s h1so/nt wo/yde? h1yo/s h1i/d wide/t so/ wo/yde. kwi/s h1so/nt we/rh1t? h1yo/s wo/yde so/ h1so/nt we/rh1t. kwi/h1 h1so/nt g'n_h3me/? h1so/nt h3kwbhi/s me/nesh1-kwe g'n_h3me/. h1so/nt stesto/h2e. dhro/wghos se/ngwt.

kwi/s k'lu/te bhoydhe/h2m pe/h2sti? deywo:/s h1i/h2m_ ph2se/nti. bhoydhe/h2 kwo/r tk'e/yti? k'e/rdi tk'e/yti. kwi/m pe/rkwunos gwhe/nti? h1yo/s bhoydhe/h2m Hrune/pti pe/rkwunos to/m gwhe/nti. kwi/m-kwe deywo:/s h2le/ksonti? h1yo/s bhoydhe/h2m pe/h2sti deywo:/s to/m h2le/ksonti. h2ne:/r h1i/ns dhre/wght? h1i/ns ne dhre/wght. h1e/yes h2n_re/s me/nos dedr_k'e:/r. h1so/nt we/rh1oh2. k'le/wsonti.

kwi/s gho/stipotey gho/stis h1e/sti? h1yo/s h2e/lyosyo k'o/ymom h1r_sk'e/ti so/ gho/stis h1e/sti. kwi/d ghoste/yey de/h3omes? h1i/m sode/yete h1e/smey-kwe wo/dr_ dh3te/. gho/stis ku/ se/dt? ph2we/ni h1e/pi se/dt. h1yo/d h1e/y se/h2t le/yttu. kwi/d gho/stis bhe/reti? we/kwos h2e/lyosyo k'o/ymo:d bhe/reti. kwi/s gho/stim h2le/kseti? dye:/ws h1i/m h2le/kseti. gho/stim h2epo/ me: h2e/g'es. gho/stis nos k'o/ymom dhg'hye/s h1ludhe/t. h1i/m sode/yom. le/wbhe h3reg'to/m dhe/h1s. gho/stis diwe/y lewbho/s h1e/sti.

kwi/s k'lu/te ste/h2yeti? h1yo/s h2e/lyosyo gwo:/s h1e/pt so/ ste/h2yeti. ste/h2yonts wiHro/s kwse/pi gwm_sk'e/ti? gwm_sk'e/ti bhe/h2esos-kwe bhebho/yh2e. kwi/d ste/h2yeti? h2r_g'n_to/m h2o/wins-kwe ste/h2yeti. kwi/s h1i/m wide/t? k'wo:/ h1i/m wide/t. kwi/d ste/h2yonts wiHro/s de/h3eti? h1yo/s gwo:/s ste/h2ye:ti h1i/ns dwi/s h2ew de/h3tu. h1yo/s h2e/lyosyo h2e/g'rosyo mo/rg'm_ Hyowdhe/yeti so/ ste/h2yeti? ste/h2yeti-ge. h2e/lyosyo gwo/wes swo:/s ne h1se/nti.

kwi/d po/tey h3reg'to/m h1e/sti? h3reg'to/m to/d h1e/sti h3re:/g's h1yo/d h3re/g'eti. kwi/h1 h3re/g'eti? te/tk'o: do/ru h3re/g'eti. h3re:/g's n_sme/ h3re/g'eti. kwi/s-kwe h3re/g'm_ h3rog'e/yeti? deywo:/s h1i/m h3rog'e/yonti. kwi/s h3sle/ydht? h1yo/s h3reg'to:/d h3sle/ydht so/ ne/k't. kwi/s h3reg'to/s h1e/sti? h1yo/s h1so/nt mle/wHti so/ h3reg'to/s h1e/sti. dhu/gh2ter te/we we/kwos h3rog'e/ye te/we-kwe k'e:/r h3rog'e/ye. h3reg'to/m stesto/h2e.

gho/stipotey me/ne bhre/h2term_ kwi/h1 pe/h2soh2? te/we bhre/h2term_ ph2sdhi/. h1i/m me: dhrughye/s. h1yo/d me me/ne bhre/h2te:r dhrughye:/ti h1i/m more/yo:h2? te/we bhre/h2term_ me: more/yes. h1i/m h1r_h1dhi/ h1e/syo-kwe we/kwos k'l_nudhi/. me/ne bhre/h2term_ h1re/h1oh2. h1yo/d me h1e/y gwo:/s h1re/h1eti kwi/d h1e/smey h1a/yoh2? h1e/smey h1i/ns se:mi h1a/ydhi. bhre/h2terh1e tewh2so/h1 h1ste/s. bhre/h2te:r bhre/h2trey lewbho/s h1e/sti.

kwi/s me/g'h2 g'r_h2no/m h1e:/dti? h1yo/s h2e/g'rom h2e/rh3yeti so/ g'r_h2no/m h1e:/dti. kwi/s-kwe g'r_h2no/m ne h1e:/dti? h1yo/s ne wr_g'ye/ti so/ ne h1e:/dti. kwi/s misdho/m h1a/yto? h1yo/s wr_g'ye/ti so/ misdho/m h1a/yto. kwi/s pk'u/h1 h3pe/wst? h1yo/s wr_g'ye/ti so/ pk'u/h1 h3pe/wst. kwi/s h2e/rh3trom de/wkti? ukse:/n h2e/rh3trom de/wkti. h1yo/d h2e/g'rom h2e/rh3yeti dhe/g'ho:m h1e/smey g'r_h2no/m de/deh3ti. h1yo/d ne h2e/rh3ye:ti dhe/g'ho:m kwid ne de/deh3ti.

kwi/s gho/stipotey h1we/sus h3re:/g's h1e/sti? h3re:/g's wiHro:/m poh2ime:/n h1e/sti. kwi/d poh2ime:/n dhe/dheh1ti? h1e/y h2o/wins wl_/kwo:d pe/h2sti. h1yo/d wl_/kwos h2o/wins h1ludhe/t k'wo:/ poh2ime:/n-kwe h1i/m h2epo/ h2e/g'etes. kwi/s-kwe h3re:g'e/s wl_/kwos h1e/sti? h1e/syo wl_/kwos so/ h1e/sti h1yo/s dhrughye/ti. dusme/ne:s h3re:/g's swa:/s h2o/wins gwr_h3e/ti. h2o/weyes h1e/smo:d bhuge/nt. kwi/d wl_/kwosyo Hrowpo/s h1e/sti? wl_/kwosyo Hrowpo/s h2egwno/s po/rk'os-kwe h1e/sti. h1yo/d poh2ime:/n h2o/wins pe/h2sti h2o/weyes gwi/h3wonti.

h1yo/d h2ne:/r me/rt kwi/d k'lu/te gwi/h3weti? h1e/syo h1no/mn_ gwi/h3weti h1e/syo-kwe k'le/wos gwi/h3weti. ku/dhe-kwe lelo/ykwe? lelo/ykwe. h1n_gwni/s h1i/m dhe/gwheti. h1e/syo k'le/wos dhe/gwheti? h1e/syo k'le/wos ne dhe/gwheti. kwye/h1tis h1e/smey h1e/sti? kwye/h1tis h1e/smey h1e/sti. kwi/s h1i/m memo/ne? suHne/wes h1i/m memne:/r. h1e/syo k'le/wos k'luwe/nt. le/ytt k'le/wos stesto/h2e.

kwi/s me/g'h2 k'le/wos we/nt? wiHro/s k'le/wos we/nt. h1i/d-kwe ku/ we/nt? h1i/d ko/ryoy we/nt. h1suh1e/k'wos wiHro/s kwo/r ye/h2ti? ko/ryom ye/h2ti. me/rt? ko/ryoy me/rt h1e/syo-kwe k'le/wos stesto/h2e. n_/dhgwhitom kwe/syo k'le/wos h1e/sti? h1yo/s h1o/gwhim gwhe/nt n_/dhgwhitom to/syo k'le/wos h1e/sti. wiHro:/s h1i/d ku/ h2wise/nt? h1i/d h1wr_Huye/h2i dhg'he/mi h2wise/nt. h1o/gwhim gwhe/noh2. tewh2so/s h1sdhi/ h3reg'to/m-kwe dhh1dhi/. te/we k'le/wos h1we/su h1sye/h1t.

h1yo/y m_re/nt to/y ku/dhe gwh2e/nt? dhubhro/m wo/dr_ h2e/n k'e/yor. dwo/h1 k'wo/nh1e pe/rtum ph2ste/s. kwi/h1 gwh2e/nt? neh2we/h1 gwh2e/nt. kwi/d bhe/ronti? ne kwid bhe/ronti. h2ew gwm_yo/nti? h1e/nosmo:d kwis h2ew ne gwm_ye/ti. so/h2wl_ dedr_k'e:/r? h1i/d ne dedr_k'e:/r. gho/stipotey dedwo/yh2e. le/wbhe me: tre/ses. gwih3wo:/s bhe/h2os dedr_k'e:/r.

kwi/m me/g'h2 me/rtis h1r_sk'e/ti? me/rtis gwo:/s h1r_sk'e/ti. bhre/h2tern_s h1r_sk'e/ti. n_sme/ h1r_sk'e/ti. kwi/d-kwe ne me/rt? k'le/wos ne me/rt. kwo/d k'le/wos gwi/h3weti? h1we/su k'le/wos gwi/h3weti. dusk'le/we:s wiHro/s ne/k't? ne/k't. k'e/ywe memn_dhi/. h1we/su k'le/wos stesto/h2e.

kwi/s gho/stipotey swe/pnos h1e/sti? swe/pnos mr_te/ys bhre/h2te:r h1e/sti. h2ne:/r swe/pnoy me/rt? ne me/rt bhe/wdht-kwe. h1yo/s lelo/ykwe so/ ne bhe/wdht. me/nos swe/pnoy ku/dhe gwe/h2t? me/nos h2e/n gwe/h2t h2ew-kwe gwm_ye/ti. me/nos-kwe mr_te/y kwo/r ye/h2ti? mr_te/y h2e/n ye/h2ti h2ew-kwe ne gwm_ye/ti. dhu/gh2ter swe/pno:d me: tre/ses. h1yo/d h2we/st bhe/wdhesi.

kwi/s gho/stipotey wiHro/syo h1no/mn_ dhe/h1t? ph2te:/r suHno/wey dhugh2tre/y-kwe k'luto/m h1no/mn_ dhe/h1t. h1yo/d lelo/ykwe kwi/s h1i/d bhe/reti? suHne/wes h1i/d bhe/ronti. ne/k't? h1yo/s dhrughye/ti to/syo h1no/mn_ ne/k't. h1yo/s h1so/nt mle/wHti h2ne/res to/syo k'le/wos k'luwe/nt. dhu/gh2ter te/we h1no/mn_ h2le/kse. ph2tre/s h1no/mn_ suHno/wi tk'e/yti.

kwi/d me/g'h2 g'e/nh1os h1e/sti? g'e/nh1os ph2tro:/m suHne/wes h1e/sti. h1yo/s k'luto/y g'e/nh1esi g'eg'o/nh1e so/ k'luto/m h1no/mn_ bhe/reti. kwi/d k'une/s g'o/nh1os h1e/sti? k'une/s g'o/nh1os k'wo:/ h1e/sti h1e/k'wosyo-kwe g'o/nh1os h1e/k'wos h1e/sti. h1e/syo h1ne/mn_h1 h1we/sus bhu/Ht? h1e/syo me/nesh1 h1we/sus bhu/Ht. ph2te/res ku/dhe lelikwe:/r? lelikwe:/r. h1no/mn_ h1e/ymos gwm_ye/ti k'le/wos swo/m h1e/sti.

kwi/d k'lu/te k'le/wos h1e/sti? k'le/wos to/d h1e/sti h2ne/res h1yo/d k'luwe/nt. kwi/s k'le/wos k'le/wseti? deywo:/s k'le/wos k'le/wsonti h1i/d-kwe k'l_nume/s. kwi/s wiHro/syo k'le/wos mle/wHti? h1yo/s we/kwesh2 wo/yde so/ h1i/d mle/wHti. h1yo/d kwis h1i/d ne mle/wHeti ne ye/h2ti. nu we/kwesh2 h1e/wkoh2. h1i/h2 h1ukdhi/ h1so/nt-kwe bhh2dhi/.

kwi/s we/kwos te:/tk'ti? h1yo/s wo/yde so/ h1i/d te:/tk'ti. kwi/d-kwe te/tk'o: de/meti? te/tk'o: kwe/kwlom de/meti. h1i/m dre/ws de/meti. kwo/smo:d-kwe so/ h1i/d te:/tk'ti? h1i/d k'r_de/s me/nesos-kwe te:/tk'ti. kwi/h1 h1i/h2 h2re/ydheti? h1i/h2 h1h3se/h1 h2re/ydheti. kwe/kwlos HeHk'u/s h1e/sti we/kwos-kwe HeHk'u/ h1e/sti. bhrusto/s kwe/kwlos ne ye/h2ti. bhrusto/m we/kwos ne stesto/h2e. dhu/gh2ter te/we we/kwesh2 h2re/ydhe.

kwi/s toy gho/stipotey we/kwos de/h3t? deywo:/s moy we/kwos dh3e/nt. h1i/d kwo/r dhh1e/nt? h1i/d me/ne h1e/h3si me/ne-kwe k'e/rdi dhh1e/nt. pl_h1u/bhos h1i/d h1ope/yonti? pl_h1u/bhos ne h1ope/yonti. deywo:/s h1yo/m wn_He/nt to/smey h1i/d dh3e/nt. wn_He/nt me? ne wo/ydh2e. deywo/ns g'huHe/ye k'le/wse-kwe. h1yo/d toy h1i/d dh3e/nt g'ne/h3esi. deywo:/m de/h3tis me/g'h2ih2 h1e/sti.

we/kwos ku/dhe g'eg'o/nh1e? k'e/rdi g'eg'o/nh1e me/nesi-kwe h2we/kst. h1o/h3s we/kwos ne te:/tk'ti. h1o/h3s h1i/d we/g'heti. we/kwos k'r_de/s ku/ gwe/h2t? h1o/h3s gwe/h2t h2ne/rn_s-kwe h1h3se/s u/d gwe/h2t. h1yo/d k'e/rdi ne g'eg'o/nh1e to/d dhro/wghos h1e/sti. me/ne k'e:/r h3reg'to/m kwi/h1 bhu/Heti? le/wbhe k'le/wse h1ukdhi/-kwe. h1we/su we/kwos h1use/wi k'e/rdi g'eg'o/nh1e.

me/g'h2 h1suwe/kwesm_ h2we/ysm_. kwi/d te:/tk'ti? k'le/wos te:/tk'ti. kwe/syo k'le/wos so/ te:/tk'ti? h1yo/s h1o/gwhim gwhe/nt to/syo k'le/wos te:/tk'ti. kwi/d h1a/yto? misdho/m h1a/yto. kwi/s h1i/m h1ope/yeti? h3re:/g's misdho/m h1ope/yeti. h1yo/d k'le/wos h2we/yst h3re:/g's me/wdt. kwi/d misdho/s h1e/sti? misdho/s h2r_g'n_to/m we/smn_-kwe h1e/sti. misdho/s le/ytt k'le/wos stesto/h2e.

kwi/d towdo/s ye/wgt? towdo/s Hro/th2o:y HeHk'u/ns h1e/k'wons ye/wgt. kwi/d h1e/k'wo:s duke/nti? h1e/k'wo:s Hro/th2om duke/nti. kwi/d-kwe h1suwe/kwe:s ye/wgt? we/kwesh2 ye/wgt. kwe/smey h1i/h2 ye/wgt? me/nesey h1i/h2 ye/wgt. me/nos h1e/yso:m towdo/s h1e/sti. kwi/s kwe/kwlom Hrune/pti? dusme/ne:s towdo/s kwe/kwlom Hrune/pti. k'e/ywe te/we we/kwesh2 yugdhi/ h1i/h2-kwe h2e/g'e.

we/kwesh2 kwi/h1 h1e/wkoh2? su/Hnew k'le/wse h1r_h1dhi/-kwe. kwi/m h2we/ysoh2? h1yo/s wo/yde to/m k'l_nudhi/. kwi/d h2isk'o:/h2? h1so/nt we/kwos h2isk'e/. h1i/m kwo/r h2isk'o:/h2? h1i/m h1e/syo k'o/ymoy h2isk'e/. h1yo/d h1i/h2 h2we/ysm_ kwi/d dhe/dheh1oh2? h1i/h2 memn_dhi/. h1yo/s h2k'h2owsye/ti so/ h1i/h2 h1e/wkt. h1yo/s wr_g'ye/ti so/ h1e/wkt.

h1yo/d kwis we/kwos ne h2we/yseti we/kwos gwi/h3weti? ne gwi/h3weti. h1yo/s h1i/d h2we/yst to/smi tk'e/yti. kwi/s h2k'h2owsye/ti? h1yo/s k'le/wseti so/ h2k'h2owsye/ti. h1yo/s mle/wHti. h1yo/s-kwe k'le/wt k'o/h1 k'le/wos te/tk'tes. mluHdhi/ k'le/womes-kwe.

we/kwos pr_h2wo/m h1e/sti me/ntis-we: pr_h2we/h2 h1e/sti? me/ntis pr_h2we/h2 h1e/sti. me/ntis kwo/r k'e/yor? me/nesi k'e/yor. nu we/kwos gwm_sk'e/ti. te/we h1o/h3s pro/ me: dre/mt. me/nos h1i/d h2e/g'etu. h1yo/d we/kwos me/ne h1h3se/s le/ytt kwi/d dhe/dheh1oh2? h1yo/d le/ytt to/d h2ew ne gwm_ye/ti. su/Hnew memn_dhi/. nu mluHdhi/.

me/g'h2 kwr_/mey h1yo/s h3e/sth1 h1e:/dsi mr_dhi/. kwr_/mey-ge h1yo/s h1e/sh2r_ pi/ph3esi n_k'dhi/. nu-kwe pe/h2wr_ twe/ dhe/gwhetu. nu so/h2wl_ twe/ h2sowse/yetu. nu-kwe h1pye/h1t h2e/wis twe/ sn_h1we/ns h1e/g'hs h1n_dhdhi/. h3sth1e/s h1n_dhdhi/ sne/h1wr_ me: gwr_h3e/s.

nu h3e/sth1 h1e/sh2r_ sne/h1wr_-kwe. h3e/sth1 h3sth1e/h1 yungih1to/ h1e/sh2r_ h1sh2e/nh1 yungih1to/. sne/h1wr_ sn_h1we/nh1 yungih1to/. nu-kwe h1yo/d h3e/sth1 Hrumpto/ to/d h2ew sth2e/h1yoyh1t. me/g'h2i dhe/g'hom me/h2ter h2e/ng'hos h3sth1e/s h1a/yso. h1yo/s k'e/yor ste/h2tu gwm_sk'e/tu.

nu h1e/sh2r_ sth2dhi/ nu h1e/sh2r_ h1e/g'hs me: gwe/h2s. nu h1yo/s g'hoyso/s twe/ te/rh3t so/ Hrumpih1to/. h1rudhe/h1n_t-ge kre/wh2s sn_h1we/ni sth2e/h1yoyh1t. k'ludhi/ dye/w ph2ter k'o/d we/kwos. nu h1i/m h2le/kse h1e/y-kwe Hwr_dhye/h1t.

pe/h2wr_ wo/dr_ h3sth1su/ dr_mdhi/. h1i/d me/g'h2i h3sth1i/ studye/h1t gwm_ye/tu sri/Hgos. nu gwhr_no/s bhe/wgtu swidye/tu. pe/h3tu te/rptu-kwe me: tre/soyh1t.

me/g'h2 k'e:/r me: tre/ms k'e:/r me: bhe/yh2s. h2e/ng'hos-ge k'r_de/s h1e/g'hs gwh2dhi/. h2e/wsos so/h2wl_-kwe h2e/ng'hos k'r_de/s h1l_h2to/m. nu-kwe gwm_ye/h1t swe/pnos h1e/smey. da/k'ruh2-ge tr_sye/h1t nu mudye/h1t.

su/Hnew me: me/rs gwi/h3we h2uksdhi/. te/we te me/h2te:r h2le/kseti. dye:/ws te dhe/g'ho:m-kwe ph2sih1ta:/m. nu-kwe h1e/ytu h2e/ng'hos wl_/kwom. me/g'h2s g'hwe:/r h1i/d h1e:/dtu gwh2te h1i/m dhgwhh2dhi/. nu dye/w h1use/wo:m de/h3tor h1e/smey se/g'hos dh3dhi/.

wl_/kwe wr_h1e/nm_ me: le/mbhs k'wo/n pe/k'u ph2sdhi/. po/h2imen gwo:/s k'o/ymom dukdhi/. me:-kwe-ge h1r_sk'o/yh1t g'hwe:/r h2e/nti. nu h2wl_/h1neh2 bhuHyo/yh1t k'wo/n bhe/wdhe. nu-kwe h2o/weyes pl_h1ih1e/nt.

nu h1o/gwhey l_ghdhi/ k'o/m-ge wiHro/m me: le/mbhs. h1o/gwhey-ge h1yo/s kwse/pi se/rpesi me: ste/h2s. nu toy pe/rkwune h1o/gwhim g'hoyso/h1 stunddhi/. h1o/gwhey bhugdhi/ nu h1o/gwhis h2epo/ le/yttu.

swe/pne-ge gwh2to/syo bhre/h2ter. gwm_dhi/ h1we/sus k'wo:/ le/ghyoyh1t h2o/wis le/ghyoyh1t. h2e/wis le/ghyoyh1t k'o/ymos le/ghyoyh1t. nu bho/r ne/kwti me: gwe/ms nu nos no/kwt k'e/le. nu-kwe h2use/si bhudhih1me/.

pe/h2wr_ h2n_re/s bheh2g'hu/m h1e/wset. bheh2g'hu/s studye/h1t h2linHdhi/ sme/rum bheh2g'he/wi. nu sme/rus bheh2g'hu/m me:/detu. sri/Hgos-ge bheh2g'hu/m h1r_sk'e/ bheh2g'hu/s Hwr_dhye/h1t.

pe/yh2wr_-ge me: ple/h1dhes pe/yh2wr_ ni/ gwh2dhi/. nu-kwe pe/yh2wr_ tinh1dhi/. HeHk'u/ wo/dr_ pe/yh2wr_ h2epo/ bhe/retu. nu-kwe sri/Hgos pe/yh2wr_ le/mbhtu. gwr_h3e/tu-kwe g'hwe:/r pe/yh2wr_ gwi/h3wetu-kwe.

kwi/s-ge twe/ te/rh3t kwi/s toy h3e/sth1 ske/lHt? nu g'hoyso/s h1me/ te/rh3t ske/lHt g'hoyso/s h1i/d. twe/ me:/doh2 sme/rum h2line/Hmi. h3e/sth1 bhuh2ye/h1t wi/Hre sth2dhi/.

nu h2e/wsos h1ye/h2 sh2we/ns kwe/kwlom h2e/g'esi h2usdhi/. no/kwt h2epo/ litdhi/ h2e/ng'hos te/mHesh1 bhe/wgtu. h2e/wis-ge bhudhye/h1t nu te wido/yh1t h2e/wsos.

k'm_to/m ye/h1o:r gwi/h3woyh1t gwh2to/s h1i/m dhgwhh2ye/h1t. nu g'orh2e/yoyh1ent deywo:/s h1i/m so/h2wl_ h1i/m spe/k'ye. nu-kwe swo:/s h1e/k'wo:s bhuHyo/yh1ent pk'u/h1 h3pusye/h1t.

wi/Hre h1yo/s gwo:/s h2e/g'es h1yo/s h1o/gwhim we/ns me/rs. nu h2e/g'roy k'e/yth2er swe/so:r toy Hre/wdHti. h1e/pt-kwe-ge toy h2e/ng'hos po/tnih2m. usme/nh1 te wose/yomes. nu-kwe toy k'le/wos n_/dhgwhitom.

su/Hnew h1me/ line/kws h2e/lom te tu/H moy k'e:/r. kapyo/h2 toy we/smn_ ne/kwti te Hre/wdHmi. nu dhe/g'hom me/h2ter me/ne suHnu/m h1a/yso.

bhre/h2ter gwo:/s h2e/g'es bhoh2go/m-kwe moy de/deh3s. nu-kwe toy h2le/kses swe/sorm_. nu moy kwi/s bhoh2go/m de/deh3ti? te/mHesi se/dyoh2 tu/H mr_wo/s Hre/wdHmi. h1no/mn_-ge toy h2nr_su/ k'luto/m.

ph2ter-ge nos de/wks h1e/k'wons-kwe nos de/deh3s. h2le/kses-ge nos memn_me/ te. sri/Hgos nos le/mbht se/dyomes HrudHme/s-kwe. nu nos kwi/s bhe/h2ti nu-kwe nos we/dheti?

wi/Hre h2epo/ h1e/ys h2ew ne gwe/ms. we/smn_ toy we/sh2er yugo/m-kwe toy spe/k'yoh2. nu-kwe h1regwesye/ti k'o/ymoy. h2isk'o/h2 te memo/nh2e-kwe te.

h3re/g'-ge h2n_ro:/m po/h2imen me/rs. h2le/kses nos misdho/ns nos de/deh3s. nu te se/dyomes h2m_bhi/ nu h2e/wso:s le/wkt. ne ste/h2s nu nos kwi/s h3re:/g's h1e/sti?

nu mr_/we po/h2imen h2e/g'roy k'e/yth2er. k'wo:/ toy se/dyeti Hre/wdHti. h1regwesye/ti ne h1e/dn_ti h2o/weyes. wl_/kwos h1r_sk'e/ti h1i/h2n_s kwis ne pe/h2sti. nu toy h1no/mn_ h2nr_su/ k'luto/m.

g'e/rh2ont ph2ter me/rs polh1u/h2 ye/h1o:r wide/s. nu se/no:m we/kwesh2 g'n_ne/h3s. we/dhes nos se/ds we/rh1s-kwe nos. deywo:/s te h1a/yn_to h1no/mn_ toy k'luto/m.

h1su/k'lewes-ge su/Hnew He/Hk'ew. ne g'orh2e/yeth2e dre/ms g'hoyso/s te te/rh3t. nu-kwe h1rudhe/h1n_t kre/wh2s h2e/g'roy h1e/sti. nu-kwe te ne wide/h1yomes. nu-kwe te gwh2to/s sle/h2gwt h1no/mn_ toy k'luto/m.

gho/stey-ge n_sme/ gwe/ms me/rs-kwe. nu toy kwi/s gho/stey g'e/nh1to:r? me/h2te:r-ge toy ku/dhe h1e/sti? sri/Hgos te h1e/pt dh3me/ toy HeHk'u/ wo/dr_. h1i/d te ne me:/det dhe/g'hom gho/stim h1a/yso.

h1yo/s uh3we/ h2e/g'et He/Hk'uh1 h1e/k'woh1 so/ mr_wo/s. yugo/y sth2e/h1yetes uh3we/-kwe kwis ne ye/wgt. nu-kwe h2isk'e/tes h1e/k'woh1 h1i/m. h1e/k'woh1-ge ne h1e/dtes kwe/kwlos stesto/h2e.

bhre/h2ter sn_gwo/ntm_ twe/ wido/m. nu h2e/nti stesto/h2h2e ne-kwe te h2le/ksom. bhe/rom te h2e/g'ro:d h2e/g'ros toy kre/wh2s pe/h3t. nu h2epo/ h1e/ymi memo/nh2e-kwe-ge te. k'le/wos-kwe-ge toy n_/dhgwhitom.

wi/Hre-ge ku/dhe h2epo/ gwe/h2s? dhe/g'ho:m-ge me/h2term_ ni/ gwa:/m. nu-kwe toy kwi/s gwo:/s h2e/g'eti? nu-kwe me/ne swe:k'uro/s h1i/ns h2e/g'eti. nu toy kwi/s we/kwesh2 bhe/h2ti? h3re:/g's h1i/h2 bhe/h2ti kwi/d bhe/resi? n_/dhgwhitom k'le/wos bhe/roh2 h1i/d ne mr_ye/tor.

su/Hnew me/ne we/kwos k'ludhi/. h1so/nt we/kwos h1we/su. h1so/nt we/wke. k'e/ywom me: dhre/wghs. h1yo/s h1so/nt bhe/h2ti so/ deywo/ybhos lewbho/s. h1yo/d we/kwos h1e/g'hs gwe/h2t ne gwm_ye/ti to/d h2ew. k'ludhi/ nu bhh2dhi/.

ghoste/yey wo/dr_ we/yh1onm_-kwe dh3dhi/. h1e/y diwe/s h1ludhe/t. h1i/m me: dhre/wghs. sode/ye h1i/m te/we sto/loy. h1i/m te/we k'o/ymo:d h1e/g'hs me: h1e/lh2s. h1yo/s k'o/ymos ghoste/yey de/deh3ti so/ deywo/ybhos lewbho/s. gho/stis de/h3torm_ memo/ne.

te/we bhoydhe/h2m ph2sdhi/. bhoydhe/h2 me/g'h2ih2. bhoydhe/h2m me: Hrune/ps. dye:/ws ph2te:/r dedo/rk'e. h1yo/s bhoydhe/h2m Hrune/pti stune/dti pe/rkwunos h1i/m. h1yo/s bhoydhe/h2m pe/h2sti h1e/syo h1no/mn_ k'luto/m.

su/Hnuh1 h1me/ k'luto/m. bhre/h2te:r lewbho/s. te/we bhre/h2term_ me: linkwto/m. dwo/h1 bhre/h2terh1e steh2ro/h1. k'o/m h1e/dtom k'o/m pi/ph3etom. h1yo/s h1i/m line/kwti so/ wl_/kwos.

se/nons k'le/wse. g'e/rh2onts we/ydto:r. h1yo/s gwi/h3weti so/ g'orh2e/yetor. wo/yde g'e/rh2onts polh1u/. g'r_h2n_te/y sto/lom dh3dhi/. g'e/rh2ontm_ usme/nh1 wose/ye. ph2we/ni se/dyonts g'e/rh2onts bhe/h2ti.

wo/dr_ ni/ h1e/yti pe/h2wr_ u/d h1e/yti. pe/h2wr_ wo/dr_-kwe ne k'e/ywoyh1. so/h2wl_ h1e/yti no/kwts-kwe gwm_ye/ti. k'e/leti no/kwts bho/rm_. h2e/wso:s h1i/h2m_ se/kwetor.

gwo/wes mr_yo/ntor. ph2te/res mr_yo/ntor. h2ne:/r mr_ye/tor. k'le/wos ne mr_ye/tor. mr_wo:/m k'le/wos n_/dhgwhitom. h1we/su k'le/wos h2isk'e/. k'luto/m h1no/mn_ dhh1so/.

wl_/kwos h2e/wibhos ne lewbho/s. bho:/r k'o/ymo:y ne k'e/ywos. k'wo:/ h2o/wins pe/h2sti. gwm_ye/ti wl_/kwos kwse/pi. se/rpeti bho:/r kwse/pi. kwse/pi bhe/wdhe. h1yo/s poh2ime:/n ne bhe/wdheti swe/h2es h2o/weyes lelikwe:/r. me: ste/h2yes.

wr_g'ye/. h2e/g'rom h2e/rh3ye. h1yo/s h2e/rh3yeti so/ h1e:/dti. gwo:/s h2ml_g'dhi/. de/me g'ho/rtom. h1e/k'wons yugdhi/. h1yo/s wr_g'ye/ti so/ swo/m bhoh2go/m h1a/yto.

h1so/nt h2isk'e/. we/ydto:r h1we/sus. h1yo/s polh1u/ dedo/rk'e so/ polh1u/ g'n_ne/h3ti. h1yo/s k'le/wseti h1e/wkt so/. h1so/nt me: k'e/les. h1so/nt lewbho/m.

h1yo/s gwi/h3weti so/ mr_ye/tor. mr_wo:/s h2ew ne gwm_yo/nti. h1a/yto gwh2to/s se/nom HeHk'u/m-kwe. pl_h1e/wih2n_s kwse/pn_s me: Hre/wdHs. h2e/ng'hos le/ytt so/h2wl_-kwe gwm_ye/ti.

deywo/ybhos ne/mos dh3dhi/. to/y h1use/wo:m de/h3tores. g'huto/m to/ybhos lewbho/m. to/y de/h3torm_ dedr_k'e:/r. k'le/wseti dye:/ws ph2te:/r de/h3torm_. deywo/ns me: dhrughye/s. dhe/g'ho:m me/h2te:r h2ne/rn_s bhe/reti.

k'e:/r wih1ne/h1 me/wdt. we/yh1onm_ k'e/ywoh1 k'o/m ph3dhi/. h1i/m me: dhrughye/s. dh3dhi/ h1e/smey te/we bhoh2go/m. k'wo:/ poh2imne/y k'e/ywos. dusk'le/we:s wiHro/s ne k'e/ywos.

pri/He gwegwo/mth2e. do:/m wik'dhi/ sddhi/-kwe. nu h1ddhi/ ph3dhi/-kwe. gho/stey toy me/dhu de/deh3mi. gwi/h3woyh1s.

gwe/mt no/kwts. gho/stipotey h1we/sum swe/pnom h1a/yih1th2e. dye:/ws k'o/d dhe/h1mn_ h2le/ksoyh1t.

h2e/wso:s h2we/st. bhe/wdhesi? nu bhe/wdhoh2. h1eg'h gwm_dhi/ so/h2wl_-kwe wide/.

kwi/s gho/stey tu/H? h1eg'o/h2 poh2ime:/n h1no/mn_-kwe moy wl_/kwos. wl_/kwe nos do:/m gwm_dhi/. nu gho/stis h1sdhi/.

gho/stey h1idhi/ deywo:/s-kwe te we/dhoyh1ent. ne/kwti me: h1e/ys. se/dye. nu h2use/si ye/h2esi.

po/tni nos dhe/h1mn_ h2eh2no/k'th2e. kwi/d toy h1no/mn_? h1no/mn_ moy h2e/wso:s. k'o/smey dhh1me/ney priHe/h2 h1sdhi/. de/ms toy pe/h2wr_ h1we/su h1sye/h1t.

gho/stey tu/H priHo/s. nu pe/kwto:r h1e/dnom h2re/ydhet. puHro/m wo/sh2r_-kwe h1ddhi/ pl_h1dhi/-kwe.

ku/ h1e/ysi? k'o/ymom ye/h2mi. bhre/h2term_ moy h2isk'o/h2. kwi/s toy bhre/h2te:r? h1e/y poh2ime:/n h2r_/tk'os. h1i/m g'n_ne/h3mi. h1e/y k'e/ywos wiHro/s. nu ih2dhi/ h1e/smey-kwe moy h1no/mn_ wr_h1dhi/.

k'o/ymom h2eh2no/k'th2e. wide/. nu nos suHnu/s gwegwo/me. h1i/m dedo/rk'h2e k'e:/r-kwe moy me/wdt. sddhi/ gwr_h3e/-kwe.

bhre/h2terh1e h1r_to/m. sdto/m. kwo/syo suHnu/h1 yu/? we/ h3re:g'e/s suHnu/h1. h1e/dtom me/dhu-kwe ph3to/m. yu/ moy priHo/h1.

h2ne/r kwi/h1 gwi/h3wesi? gwi/h3woh2 deywo:/s-kwe moy h1we/suh2 h1ope/yonti. nu toy po/tnih2 kwi/h1 gwi/h3weti? gwi/h3weti. dye:/ws toy h1use/wo:m de/h3to:r dhe/h1mn_ h2le/ksoyh1t.

ph2ter gwegwo/mth2e. me/h2te:r puHro/m pe/kweti. wo/dr_ bhr_h1we/ns bhe/roh2 swe/so:r-kwe moy gwo:/s h2me/lg'ti. sddhi/. dedo/rk'h2e te k'e:/r-kwe moy me/wdt.

me/g'h2 h3re/g' gwi/h3woyh1s. k'le/wos toy me/g'h2 h1sye/h1t. we/y toy gho/steyes. h3re/g' nos k'le/ytreh2m dh3dhi/.

kwi/s gho/stey tu/H? h1eg'o/h2 h2r_/tk'osyo suHnu/s h1no/mn_-kwe moy pe/rkwus. pe/rkwew ku/ h1e/ysi? h3re:g'e/s do:/m ye/h2mi. gwh2dhi/.

po/h2imen moy h2o/wins dedo/rk'th2e? h1i/h2n_s dedo/rk'h2e. ku/ h1se/nti? h2e/g'roy pr_we/ni h1e/pi h1se/nti. k'wo:/ h2o/wins h2le/kseti. me: tre/ses. po/h2imen te dye:/ws ph2sye/h1t.

po/tni moy pe/h2wr_ dh3dhi/. dhe/h1mn_ moy ste/wdt. pe/h2wr_ toy de/deh3mi. nu h1i/d h1a/yso k'o/ymom-kwe bhe/re. h1i/d me: le/h1s. do:/m toy gwhr_ye/h1t.

me/h2ter tr_syo/h2. wo/dr_ ph3dhi/. wo/dr_ ne pi/ph3oh2. nu moy me/dhu dh3dhi/. me/dhu h2nr_bho/s h1e/sti. ph3dhi/ h2uksdhi/-kwe.

kwi/d gho/stey h2isk'e/si? dhe/h1mn_ ne/kwti h2isk'o/h2. do:/m toy ku/ h1e/sti? dhe/h1mn_ moy pr_we/ni h2e/n h1e/sti. sddhi/. nu toy h1e/dnom me/dhu-kwe de/deh3mi. dhh1me/ni moy l_ghdhi/. gho/stipotey tu/H k'e/ywos.

h2e/g'rom h2e/rh3yesi? h2e/g'rom h2e/rh3yoh2. dwo/h1 ta/wroh1 yugdhi/. ta/wroh1 ye/wgm_. gwo:/s bhre/h1wr_ we/dhe. h1i/ns we/dhoh2.

ph2ter wl_/kwom k'le/wsoh2. dedwo/yth2e? dedwo/yh2e. me: tre/ses. k'wo:/ dhwo/rm_ h2le/kseti. nu l_ghdhi/ swe/pnom-kwe h1a/yso.

kwi/d h1e/k'wosyo wo/sn_? wo/sn_ ti/sres h2o/weyes. wo/sn_ me/g'h2. h1e/k'wos HeHk'u/s me/g'h2s-kwe. dwo/h1 toy ta/wroh1 h2wl_/h1neh2m-kwe h1ope/yoh2. bhoydhe/h2 h1e/stu.

kwi/d h1e/nod? h1e/nos wl_/kwos. h2e/g'rom me: h1e/ys. ne h1e/ymi. nu swe/sor do:/m ye/h2omes poh2imne/y-kwe we/rh1omes.

h2r_/tk'om wide/s? h2r_/tk'om pr_we/ni h1e/pi wido/m. ku/ gwe/h2t? pe/rkwuns gwe/h2t. h2r_/tk'om me: se/kweth2e. nu no/kwts gwm_sk'e/ti.

h1regwesye/ti. h2e/g'mn_ ku/ h1e/sti? h2e/g'mn_ h2e/g'roy h1e/sti. h2e/g'mn_ k'o/ymom h2e/g'e k'wo/nm_-kwe ge/wHe. h1i/d h2e/g'oh2.

kwi/s dye:/ws? dye:/ws deywo:/m wiHro:/m-kwe ph2te:/r. nu kwi/s dhe/g'ho:m? dhe/g'ho:m me/h2te:r. deywo:/s m_re/nt? wiHro:/s m_re/nt. deywo:/s ne m_re/nt.

dhe/h1mn_ moy gwe/mesi? gwe/moh2. nu pe/kwto:r h1e/dnom h2re/ydheti po/rk'om-kwe de/deh3mi. de/h3nom me: bhe/res. tu/H nos gho/stis.

k'wo/nm_ moy toy dh3dhi/. k'wo/nm_ toy ne de/deh3mi. h1e/y moy h2e/g'mn_ h2le/kseti. nu toy dwo/yh1 h2o/wih1 de/deh3mi. k'wo/nm_ ne h1ope/yoh2. h2wl_/h1neh2m misdho/m h1a/yso.

po/tnih2 h2e/wso:s ste/h2t wo/dr_-kwe gwhr_ne/wt. nu h2e/g't. ko/sle puHro/m bhe/re. nu ko/slos puHro/m we/g'het. po/tnih2 puHro/m pe/kwet dhe/h1mn_-kwe gwhe/rt. gwr_h3e/te.

gerh2e:/n gwo:/s k'o/ymom h2e/g'et. gwm_sk'e/t no/kwts. swe/h2 po/tnih2 gwo:/s h2me/lg't. nu gerh2e:/n h2e/g't. po/rk'o:y h1e/dnom dh3dhi/. nu h1e/smey h1e/dnom de/h3t po/rk'os-kwe gwr_h3e/t. nu gerh2e:/n se/dt.

wl_/kwos do:/m de/met. me/g'h2ih2m dhwo/rm_ dhe/dheh1t. nu ko/slosyo gho/rdhom dhe/h1mn_ pe/ri de/met. nu we/wket. dye:/ws k'o/m do:/m ph2sye/h1t.

me/h2te:r swo/m suHnu/m h2wl_/h1neh2s usme/nh1 wose/yet. h1e/y we/smn_ we/sto. we/smn_ h1we/su? h1i/d h1we/su. nu lo/wh3trom dhwo/rm_ we/g'het.

me/h2te:r h1e/dnom h2re/ydheti. ph2te:/r se/dyeti. kwi/d h1e/dmes? puHro/m wo/sh2r_-kwe h1e/dmes. nu gwr_h3o/nti.

swe/sor so/h2wl_ tr_so/m gwhr_ne/wti. dhoHne/h2m h2sowse/ye. h1i/h2m_ h2sowse/yoh2. tr_ste/h2m dhoHne/h2m dhe/h1mn_ bhe/re. nu h1i/h2m_ we/g'hoh2.

swe/sor pe/h2wr_ h2le/kse. pe/h2wr_ ko/slom dhe/gwheti h1rudhe/h1ti-kwe. h1i/d ne/kwti me: le/h1s. pe/h2wr_ de/mey priHo/m do:/m-kwe deywo/ybhos priHo/s.

pe/kwto:r twork'o/s po/rk'om pe/kwet. nu gho/stipotis ghoste/yey we/wket. gho/stey po/rk'om gwr_h3e/ me/dhu-kwe ph3dhi/. nu gho/stis gwr_h3e/t pi/ph3et-kwe. gho/stipoteys k'e:/r me/wdt.

h2e/wso:s lo/wksneh2-kwe bhre/h1wr_ gwh2ta:/m. nu h2e/wso:s h2e/g't. lo/wksna bhr_h1we/ns wo/dr_ ph3dhi/. pi/ph3oh2. nu nos me/h2te:r wo/dr_ h2isk'e/ti. nu h1i/d k'o/ymom we/g'heta:m.

kwse/ps gwe/mt. so/h2wl_ le/ght. l_ghdhi/ swe/pnom-kwe h1a/yso. ph2te:/r toy bhe/wdheti. wl_/kwos dhe/h1mn_ me: h1r_ye/h1t. nu so/h2wl_ wide:/si.

kwi/s dhwo/rm_ gwegwo/me? gho/stis gwegwo/me h1e/syo-kwe h1no/mn_ ta/wros. kwi/d bhe/h2ti? pre:/k'st. gho/stipotis de/mi h1e/n h1e/sti? we/yk'tu se/dyetu-kwe.

dwo/h1 h1e/k'woh1 tre/yes ta/wro:s septm_/ h2o/weyes po/rk'os-kwe twork'o/syo de/mi h1se/nti. k'm_to/m h2o/weyes gr_h2ne/s h2e/g'roy h1se/nti. nu twork'o/s we/wket. h2e/g'mn_ toy me/g'h2. pe/rkwunos h1i/d ph2sye/h1t.

bhre/h2ter moy h1o/lom dh3dhi/. h1o/lom toy ne h1ope/yoh2. ph2ter moy me/ne bhre/h2te:r h1o/lom ne h1ope/yeti. su/Hnuh1 me: HrudHto/m. bhre/h2ter h1e/smey h1o/lom dh3dhi/. h1i/m h1ope/yoh2 ne-kwe Hre/wdHmi.

dye:/ws dhe/g'ho:m-kwe sk'ih3wo/y k'e/yro. lewko/m kwid ne h1e/st. nu me/g'h2s dye:/ws we/rh1t. le/wks bhu/Htu. h2yuHe/nih2 de/ywih2 h1e/smo:d g'n_h1ye/to. deywo:/s dhm_bhe/nt. h1i/h2 ste/h2t wo/yk'ons-kwe lowke/yet. h1e/syeh2s bhe/h2os h3kwbho/s lewbho/m. h2ne/res h1i/h2m_ wide/h1yont mude/nt-kwe. nu ph2te:/r h1e/syeh2ey k'luto/m h1no/mn_ dhe/h1t. h1e/syeh2s h1no/mn_ h2e/wso:s. h1e/syeh2s g'hleymo/s n_/dhgwhitos.

me/g'h2s h1o/gwhis dhubhro/y h2ewlo/y tk'e/yt. so/ h2ewlo:/d se/rpet so/h2wl_-kwe gwr_h3e/t sk'ih3wo/s-kwe wo/yk'ons k'e/let. h2ne/res bhih2e/nt. nu h3e/ro: u/d gwe/h2t. h1o/gwhim h1e/pt h1i/m-kwe ske/lHt. h1o/gwhis me/rt. h1e/sh2r_ h1e/smo:d h1ne/dht. me/lh3t so/h2wl_ pro/ h2we/st-kwe. h1i/d kwk'e/nt mude/nt-kwe. h3r_ne/y de/h3nom h1ope/yont. h3r_ne/s h1no/mn_ k'luto/m.

h2e/wso:s swo/ns gwo:/s pl_th2eno/m h2e/g'rom h2e/g'et. bho:/r gwo:/s h1e/pt h1i/ns-kwe h2ewlo/m g'he/yt. nu h2e/wso:s ne le/wkt h1regwesye/t-kwe. nu priHo/s h1e/syeh2s k'wo:/ gwo:/s h2isk'e/t h1i/ns-kwe h2ewlo/y wide/t. h1i/h2m_ dre/mt. nu h2ewlo/m h1e/rt. bho:/r tre/set bhe/wgt-kwe. gwo:/s spe/rt h1i/ns-kwe lowko/m h1e/g'hs we/dhet le/wks-kwe le/h2pt.

sh2we/ns kwe/kwlos Hrumpto/. nu h1i/d we/rh1t. dye/w ph2ter me/ne kwe/kwlom kwk'dhi/. nu yemHo/h1 h1e/syo wo/kwm_ k'luta:/m gwh2ta:/m-kwe. HeHk'e/wes h1e/k'wo:s sth2e/h1yont dhm_bhe/nt-kwe. h1regwesye/t. kwe/kwlom h2re/ydheta:m HeHk'u/ns-kwe h1e/k'wons yugta:/m. h1e/y solh2wo/s bhu/Ht Hwe/rdht-kwe. h2ew gwe/h2t le/wks-kwe h1e/rt. yemHo/h1 n_sme/y k'e/ywoh1.

no/kwts h2e/wso:s-kwe swe/sorh1e h1sta:/m. no/kwts dhg'he/mi gwm_sk'e/t. nu h2e/wso:s swe/h2m swe/sorm_ se/kweto h1rudhe/h1t-kwe h2yuHe/nih2-kwe h1e/st. no/kwts gwm_yo/ntih2m h1i/h2m_ wide/t bhe/wgt-kwe. swe/sorh1e ne g'n_h3ta:/m.

dye:/ws dhe/g'ho:m-kwe k'o/m k'e/yro. dye:/ws ph2te:/r pl_th2ene/h2-kwe dhe/g'ho:m me/h2te:r. deywo:/s h1e/ymos g'n_h1yo/nto. nu deywo:/s dye:/m upe/r tn_nwe/nt dhe/g'ho:m-kwe Hn_dhi/ stoh2e/yont. nu le/wks h1ente/r gwm_sk'e/ti ni/-kwe gwm_ye/ti. pe/rkwus h1ente/r stesto/h2e pe/rkwus-kwe te/gus. h2ne/res h2e/n tk'iye/nti.

h1regwesye/t. no/kwts gwe/mt wiHro:/s-kwe l_ghe/nt. nu so/h2wl_ k'e/mh2t dhubhro/m-kwe wo/dr_ se/ngwt. swe/pnos h1i/d h1e/pt plo/wyoh1-kwe kwse/pi ye/h2t. nu h2yuHe/nih2 de/ywih2 h2e/n h1e/rt se/kwt-kwe. sth2dhi/. nu h1i/d bhe/wdht. h1i/d bhe/h2esh1 wose/yet. h2we/st.

poh2ime:/n swa:/s h2o/wins pl_th2eno/m h2e/g'rom we/dhet. nu gwe/nh2m_ wide/t. h1i/h2 lewke/h2m we/stim we/sto. nu mle/wHt. kwi/s tu/H? h1eg'o/h2 h2e/wso:s. pro/ h1e/ymi so/h2wl_-kwe h1me/ se/kwetor. bhe/yh2t g'nu/bhis-kwe se/ngwt. nu h2e/g't. me: bhe/yh2s. poh2imne/s k'e:/r me/wdt. nu h1i/h2 poh2imne/y h2e/h2usom h2wl_/h1neh2m-kwe de/h3t. h1e/syo h2o/weyes pl_h1e/wih2es.

tge/wih2 sni/gwhs pr_kwe/wi k'e/yo wl_/kwo:s-kwe wo/yk'om h1re/nt. h2ne/res ph2we/ni se/dyont. nu wo/sr_ h1ludhe/t gwhe/rt-kwe. sni/gwhs tine/h1t dre/mt-kwe. n_k'e/nt. nu wiHro:/s wo/yk'o:d linkwe/nt swe/h2-kwe dhewse/h2 pl_th2eno/m h2e/g'rom we/dhet. gwe/nh2es pe/yh2tu pe/kwont. h2e/weyes nisdo/ns pr_kwe/wi de/mont. gwhe/ros h1e/ymos h1we/su.

h3e/ro: u/d ye/h2t. so/h2wl_ swo:/ys h3kwbhi/s spe/k'yet. nu h1i/d h3r_ne/y se/kwt. kwi/s h1me/ spe/k'yeti? we/rh1t. h1eg'o/h2 twe/ spe/k'yoh2. me/ne h3o/kwih1 ne tre/setes h1eg'o/h2-kwe ne bhebho/yh2h2e. nu so/h2wl_ h3e/ronm_ g'ne/h3t h1e/smey-kwe k'luto/m h1no/mn_ dhe/h1t. h1e/y h2we/yo:m h3re:/g's.

pe/h2wr_ h2nr_bho/s ne h1e/st stude/nt-kwe. nu h2yuHe/nih2 gwe:/n so/h2wl_ u/d spe/k'yet. n_sme/y pe/h2wr_ dh3dhi/. nu to/d h1i/h2m_ k'le/wt h1i/d-kwe se/h1t. h1i/d h1e/pt h1i/d-kwe k'o/ymom bhe/ret. gwne/h2ey de/h3nom h1ope/yont h1e/smi-kwe si/sdont gwhre/nt-kwe. gwne/h2s k'e:/r teto/rpe. pe/yh2tu ph2we/ni pe/kwet h1e/dn_t-kwe. gwne/h2s k'le/wos n_/dhgwhitom.

h3re:/g's h3mi/ghleh2i gwm_sk'e/t. k'wo:/ h1i/m se/kweto h3sle/ydht-kwe. h3mi/ghleh2 tge/wih2. tre/set. nu Hre/wdHt. le/wkm_ h1a/ydhi. h2we/st h3mi/ghleh2-kwe bhe/wgt. nu le/wkm_ wide/t k'o/ymom-kwe we/yk't. h2use/y moyso/m de/h3t. h1i/h2 h1e/smey h1we/su k'le/wos h1ope/yet. nu h1e/syeh2ey k'le/ytreh2m de/met.

h2e/wso:s yugo/m sle/h2gwt HeHk'u/ns-kwe h1e/k'wons ye/wgt. so/h2wl_ Hro/th2oy se/dt h1i/ns-kwe h2e/g'et. h1e/syo kwe/kwlos h2re/wet. h1i/ns h2le/kset. nu h1e/k'wo:s bhh2e/nt. ku/dhe ih2me/s? h2e/wso:s we/rh1t. pro/ h1ite/ h2nr_bho/s-kwe le/wkm_ bhe/rete. nu gwh2e/nt ne-kwe dedwiye:/r h2we/st-kwe.

wiHro/s bhoydhe/h2m dhe/h1t. dye:/ws h1i/h2m_ k'le/wt h1i/h2m_-kwe kwe/k't. so/ swo/m bhre/h2term_ dhre/wght. nu me/rt. wl_/kwo:s h1i/m h1e/dn_t. h1yo/s bhre/h2term_ dhre/wght so/h2wl_ to/m dedo/rk'e. so/ ne gwi/h3weti.

h2e/wsos le/wka de/ywi diwe/s dhu/gh2ter. n_sme/y le/wks sk'ih3wo/s-kwe twe/d bhe/wgt. nu wiHro:/s twe/ kwk'e/nt mude/nt-kwe. tu/H h2yuHe/nih2 tu/H n_/dhgwhiteh2. tu/H kwspe/s swe/so:r te/we bhe/h2os h3kwbho/s lewbho/m. nu k'e/ywa n_sme/y h1we/su k'le/wos dh3dhi/. nu-kwe n_sme/y n_/dhgwhitom k'le/wos h1a/ydhi.

h2e/wsos k'e/ywa po/tni gwo:/s h1e/g'hs we/dhesi. lewke/h2m we/stim wose/yeth2er. h2e/weyes sth2e/nt h1yo/d gwm_ye/si. n_sme/y h2e/h2usom de/deh3si. poh2imne/y h2wl_/h1neh2m de/deh3si. nu n_sme/y suHnu/ns moyso/ns-kwe h1ope/ye. po/tni n_sme/y n_/dhgwhitom k'le/wos dh3dhi/.

so/h2wl_ diwe/s h3o/kw h1yo/d h2n_ro:/m k'e/rdh2 wo/ydth2e. nu h1yo/d HeHk'u/ns h1e/k'wons ye/wgs h1yo/d dhewse/h2 gwhr_ne/wsi. h1yo/d sni/gwhm_ tine/h1si h1yo/d dhoHne/h2m pe/kwesi. n_sme/y h2ew l_h2pdhi/ n_sme/y le/wkm_ h1a/ydhi.

sh2we/ns kwe/kwlos dye/wi ye/h2ti. nu-kwe HeHk'e/wes h1e/k'wo:s h1i/m duke/nti. wo/yk'ons lowke/yeti kwe/kwlos h1e/y me: Hrumpih1to/. nu-kwe HeHk'e/wes h1e/k'wo:s me: sth2e/h1yoyh1ent. dye/wmn_-ge h2e/nti h1r_sk'e/ti. nu bhe/h2os Hro/th2oy se/dyeti. sh2we/ns-kwe-ge le/wkos n_sme/y priHo/m h1sye/h1t.

dye/w ph2ter h1use/wo:m de/h3tor. h1yo/s h2use/y k'luto/m h1no/mn_ dhe/h1s. h1yo/s n_sme/y lewbhe/h2m le/wkm_ dhe/h1s. nu tu/H n_sme/y upe/r teto/nth2e tu/H deywo:/m h3re:/g's. nu n_sme/ k'ludhi/ we/kwesh2 nos k'ludhi/. dhewse/h2 nos pe/h2wr_-kwe h2le/kse.

dye/w dhe/g'ho:m-kwe me/g'h2ih2 me/h2te:r. nu deywo:/s usme/d g'n_h1yo/nto. yu/ pl_th2eno/h1 yu/-kwe dhubhro/h1. nu-kwe we/rsenn_s h2egwno/ns-kwe h2e/letes. nu pe/rkwus h1ente/r stesto/h2e. g'e/nh1torh1e n_sme/ k'luto/m. suHnu/ns nos k'wo/nn_s-kwe ph2sto/m.

nu le/wks pr_h2we/h2 le/wks-ge k'e/yweh2. le/wks me/g'h2m_ sk'ih3wo/m gle/wbheti. le/wks deywo:/m h3o/kw wiHro:/s lowko/y gwi/h3wonti. to/y-kwe-ge sk'ih3wo/y tr_me/nti. nu-kwe g'hleymo/s bhe/h2os-kwe luke/s suHnu/h1. nu bhe/h2os n_sme/ me: linkwye/h1t. nu nos dye/wmn_ k'le/ytreh2m h1ludho/yh1t.

no/kwt h2e/wso:s-kwe me/g'h2ih2h1 swe/sorh1e kwse/p le/yts. po/tni-kwe h1i/h2m_ se/kweth2er. h1rudhe/h1si h2e/weyes-kwe sth2e/nt. kwse/p-kwe-ge sk'ih3wo/m bhe/resi. le/wbha po/tni lewbhe/h2m le/wkm_ bhe/resi. nu-kwe swe/sorh1e n_sme/ kwse/pi ph2sto/m.

nu h2use/y h1we/suh2 we/kwesh2 bhe/h2omes. h1i/h2 gwm_ye/ti h2e/weyes sth2e/nt. nu-kwe h1e/syeh2ey de/h3nom de/deh3omes. nu h1e/syeh2s wo/kwm_ k'le/wso:mes. h2re/weti poh2ime:/n dhewse/h2 h2e/g'eti. bhebhudhme/ le/wkm_ dedr_k'me/ mudme/-kwe.

h1yo/d dye/w ph2ter h2we/rs dhoHne/h2 bhwe/h2t. polh1e/wes gwo/wes wo/dr_ ph3e/nt. nu g'hwe:/res tr_syo/nti h2e/g'roy nos h2wr_sdhi/. wo/dr_ h1ludho/yh1t dhoHne/h2 h2uksye/h1t.

h2e/wso:s pr_h2we/h2 so/h2wl_ h1i/h2m_ se/kwetor. nu h1i/h2 pro/ ye/h2ti h1i/d-kwe e/nu h1e/yti. nu h1i/h2 h2re/weti h1i/d-kwe n_sme/y priHo/m. h1yo/d-kwe h1r_sk'e/tes sth2me/-ge.

nu wo/sr_ gwegwo/me gwhe/rt-kwe. nu sni/gwhs tine/h1ti wo/dr_ dre/mt. gwe/nh2es pe/yh2tu pe/kwonti wl_/kwo:s n_k'e/nt. h2e/weyes nisdo/ns de/monti. nu lewko/m n_sme/y priHo/m h1sye/h1s. te/we le/wkesi gwi/h3woyh1me.

h2yuHe/nih2 kwi/s diwe/s gwm_ye/ti? nu h1i/h2 h2e/wso:s k'e/yweh2 de/ywih2. nu ku/dhe h1e/yti ku/dhe-kwe stesto/h2e? h1i/h2 h1e/yti h2o/weyes h1i/h2m_ se/kwontor. nu pr_h2wo/s kwi/s h1i/h2m_ wide/t? nu-kwe pr_h2wo/s poh2ime:/n h1i/h2m_ wide/t. k'e/yweh2 de/ywih2 n_sme/y lukye/h1t.

pri/Ha de/ywi n_sme/ gwm_dhi/. n_sme/y lukdhi/ sk'ih3wo/s bhugye/h1t. le/wkm_ kwk'ih1me/ me: m_rih1me/. n_sme/y h2o/yu h1we/su-kwe k'le/wos h1a/ydhi. nu-kwe nos k'o/ymos dyume/nh1 lewko/s h1sye/h1t. nu-kwe bho/rm_ wl_/kwom-kwe n_sme/d g'hidhi/. pe/h2wr_ nos k'le/ytreh2i dhe/gwhoyh1t. k'e:/r-kwe-ge nos lowko/y mudye/h1t.

h2e/wsos h2o/wins pl_th2eno/m h2e/g'rom we/dhoh2. h1i/h2n_s-kwe-ge wl_/kwo:d h2le/kse. nu wl_/kwos h1i/h2n_s me: gwm_ye/h1t. me/ne h2o/weyes pl_h1e/wih2es h1sih1e/nt. nu h2o/yu gwi/h3woyh1ent me:-kwe m_rih1e/nt. nu me/ne priHo/s k'wo:/ h1i/m kwk'ye/h1t me: kwis lelikwye/h1t. le/wbha de/ywi te/bhye h2wl_/h1neh2m de/deh3oh2.

so/h2wl_ me/ne k'o/ymo:d h1e/ymi. me/g'hye pro/ ih2dhi/ h1me/ spe/k'ye. wl_/kwos bho:/r-kwe h1me/ me: h1pih1ta:/m. me/ne g'o/nwih1 me: tr_mih1ta:/m. nu me/ne tpe:/rsneh2 me: h3slidhye/h1t me: bhebhih2ye/h1m_. k'o/ymom h1r_ye/h1m_ solh2wo/s bhuHye/h1m_.

nu dye/w ph2ter te/bhye moyso/m dedh3me/s. nu h1i/m h1a/yso n_sme/y-kwe wo/dr_ dh3dhi/. te/bhye h2wl_/h1neh2m h2e/h2usom-kwe bhe/ro:mes. nu tr_syo/mes dhewse/h2-kwe nos tr_sye/ti. nu nos h2e/g'roysu h2wr_sdhi/ dhoHne/h2 h2uksye/h1t. dhewse/h2-kwe-ge nos me: mr_ye/h1t.

nu po/tni me/ne suHnu/s k'e/yor gwhe/rt-kwe. nu me/ne suHnu/m memn_dhi/. nu h1i/m spe/k'ye h1i/m-kwe h2le/kse. nu me/ne suHnu/s sth2ye/h1t me:-kwe mr_ye/h1t. nu wo/dr_ ph3ye/h1t h1e/dih1t-kwe. po/tni te/bhye h2egwno/m de/h3oh2.

le/wkm_ dedr_k'me/ mudme/-kwe. nu le/wka n_sme/y bhe/h2os h1a/ys. nu te/bhye de/h3nom dedh3me/s. n_sme/ h2ew gwm_dhi/ tetr_pme/. nu-kwe nos h1we/suh2 we/kwesh2 k'lute/h2 h1sye/h1t. nu n_sme/y priHo/s h1sdhi/ n_sme/ me: line/kws.

h2yu/Heni k'lu/ta po/tni g'hwe:/rm_ h2isk'o/h2. nu h1me/ we/dhe h1me/-kwe spe/k'ye. nu me/g'hye h1i/m woyde/ye me:-kwe bhugye/h1t. nu me/ne h3o/kwih1 h1i/m kwk'ih1ta:/m. h1i/m h1pye/h1m_ te/bhye-kwe de/h3nom de/deh3oh2.

dye/w k'ludhi/ so/h2wl_ wide/h1ye. me/g'h2ih2m-kwe-ge bhoydhe/h2m dhe/h1m_. h1yo/d k'e/ywom dhre/wghoh2 nu me dye:/ws more/yoyh1t. h1yo/d h1i/m me: dhre/wghoh2 gwi/h3woyh1m_.

so/h2wl_ ko/ryom h1ime/s n_sme/y pro/ ih2dhi/. nu n_sme/ h2le/kse gho/stins-kwe unih1me/. polh1e/wes gho/steyes n_sme/d bhugih1e/nt. nu n_sme/ ghosti/bhos spr_dhi/. n_sme/y polh1u/ns misdho/ns h1a/ydhi. lewbho/m pe/h2wr_ h1rih1me/ me:-kwe m_rih1me/.

nu h2e/wsos me/g'hye swo/m suHnu/m h1ope/ye. h1e/y Hwr_dhye/h1t h2o/yu-kwe gwi/h3woyh1t. nu h1e/y wiHro/s h1sye/h1t nu h1e/y h1le/wdheros h1sye/h1t. nu h1e/syo h1no/mn_ k'luto/m h1sye/h1t. nu pe/yh2tu h1e/dih1t sh2ye/h1t-kwe. nu-kwe te/bhye h2egwno/m h2wl_/h1neh2m-kwe de/h3oh2.
