h1eg'o/h2 me/g'h2s k'o/ymosyo h3re:/g's. nu h2e/wsos so/h2wl_ dye/w-kwe me/ne wo/yk'om ph2ste/. nu-kwe me/ne wiHro:/s h2o/yu gwi/h3woyh1ent. h1e/yso:m h2o/weyes pl_h1e/wih2es h1sih1e/nt. bho:/r me/ne k'o/ymom me: gwm_ye/h1t. pl_h1e/wih2 dhoHne/h2 wo/yk'oy h1sye/h1t. nu-kwe me/g'hye n_/dhgwhitom k'le/wos dh3te/.

h1regwesye/ti kwse/ps gwm_ye/ti. nu so/h2wl_ wo/dr_ Hn_dhi/ se/ngws. ph2ter n_sme/ ne/kwti h2le/kse. l_ghih1me/ swe/pnos n_sme/ h1pye/h1t. k'wo:/ nos k'le/ytreh2m h2le/ksoyh1t pe/rkwus n_sme/ k'e/loyh1t. bhudhih1me/ le/wkm_-kwe h2ew dedr_k'ih1me/.

nu pl_th2eno/m me/ne h2e/g'rom h2e/rh3yoh2. dye/w h1e/smi h2wr_sdhi/ so/h2wl_ h1i/m gwhr_nudhi/. nu dhe/g'hom me/h2ter me/g'hye dhoHne/h2m dh3dhi/. nu me/ne dhoHne/h2 h2uksye/h1t h1e/dih1m_-kwe. nu-kwe h2e/weyes h1i/h2m_ me: h1e/dih1ent. me/ne yugo/m me: Hrumpih1to/. nu pe/rkwunos h1me/ me: stundye/h1t.

me/g'h2s wiHro/s HeHk'u/ns h1e/k'wons ye/wgt h1e/gwhis-kwe h2e/g'rom h1r_sk'o/nt. nu Hre/wdHt. h1o/gwhey h1n_dhdhi/. me/ne gwe/ru tr_sye/ti. nu h1o/gwhis h1i/m wide/t bhe/yh2t-kwe. nu h1e/syo gwe/ru h1o/gwhim te/rdt me/rt-kwe. nu we/nt n_/dhgwhitom k'le/wos.

bhre/h2terh1e HeHk'u/h1 ma/rkoh1 yugta:/m. nu k'o/s kwe/kwlom h2e/g'et h1e/nos-kwe g'hoyso/m kapye/t. nu dhe/g'ho:m tre/mt. nu gho/stis stne/h2t h2e/g't-kwe. ku/dhe h1ite/? nu k'le/wos h2isk'o/mes. nu h1e/nos h1i/m more/yet.

h3re:/g's wiHro:/y do/h3r_ de/h3t. nu te/bhye wl_/kwosyo k'e/rh2sonm_ bhe/ro:h2. nu no/kwtm_ h1ne/dht. nu wl_/kwos h1i/m k'le/wt h1i/m-kwe pro/ti dre/mt. nu wl_/kwom le/mbht h1e/syo-kwe k'e/rh2sonm_ ske/lHt. nu k'e/rh2sonm_ h3re/g'm_ bhore/yet. nu we/rh1yet. te/we h1no/mn_ k'luto/m.

polh1e/wes wo/yk'om ne/kwti h1ludho/nt. nu k'o/s dhwo/rm_ Hrune/pt h1e/nos-kwe pe/h2wr_ h1i/h2m_ dhe/h1t. nu h1i/d dhe/gwhet. nu h3re:/g's dhwo/ri ste/h2t se/kwt-kwe. ph2we/ns ne dedwo/yh2e h1e/ymos-kwe me: bhe/wgoh2. nu h1e/yso:m pr_h2wo/m more/yet h1e/tero:s-kwe bhuge/nt.

h2e/wso:s le/wkt sh2we/ns-kwe kwe/kwlos dhg'he/mi me/lh3t. nu wiHro/s ste/h2t h1e/k'wons-kwe yugo/m ye/wgt we/wket-kwe. He/Hk'ewes h1e/k'wo:s h1me/ bhe/rete. nu h1e/k'wo:s h1i/m h2wise/nt pro/-kwe dr_me/nt.

h2ne:/r kwis poh2imne/s gwo:/s ste/h2yet. nu poh2ime:/n wiHro:/y we/rh1t. h1e/yes lelikwe:/r. nu h1i/ns h2isk'o:/h2. nu swo/ns ma/rkons ye/wgt h1e/syo-kwe h2o/g'mom se/kwetor. nu h1i/m gwo:/s-kwe h2use/si kwe/k't. nu swo/m g'hoyso/m Hye/h1kt h1e/y-kwe se/ngwt. nu h1i/ns k'o/ymom we/dhet.

wiHro/s dhg'he/mi k'e/yor. nu h1e/syo bhre/h2te:r gwegwo/me se/dt-kwe. nu h2e/g't. bhre/h2ter me/roh2 nu me/ne k'le/wos gwi/h3we:ti. me/ne gwe/ru sl_h2gwdhi/ me:-kwe Hre/wdHs. nu supno/s h1i/m le/mbht k'luto/s-kwe bhu/Ht.

wiHro/s h2e/g'roy sth2e/h1yet deywo/ybhos-kwe we/rh1yet. dye/w ph2ter h1me/ k'l_nudhi/. me/g'hye h1we/su k'le/wos HeHk'u/ns-kwe ma/rkons dedh3dhi/. pe/rkwune h1yo/s gwe/ru h1o/gwhim Hye/h1ks. me/g'hye k'e/ywos h1sdhi/. nu deywo:/s h1i/m h2wise/nt pro/-kwe ye/h2t ne-kwe tre/set.

ma/rkos stesto/h2e. nu kwis h1i/m ne dhe/gwhh2t. nu wiHro/s h1e/rt h1e/k'wom-kwe spe/k'yet se/kwt-kwe. twe/ ye/wgoh2. h1me/ we/g'he:si te/bhye-kwe wo/dr_ de/h3oh2. nu h1e/k'wos ste/h2t wiHro/s-kwe h1i/m we/dhet. nu pro/ gwh2e/nt h1e/k'wos-kwe k'luto/s bhu/Ht.

dhh2bhro/m kwis gwm_ye/t. nu we/wket. me/g'hye gwe/ru te/tk'dhi. nu dhh2bhro/s wr_g'ye/t. nu g'hoyso/m gwhr_ne/wt h1i/m-kwe stune/dt. nu h1e/y pik'to/s h1e/y-kwe k'uh1ro/s. nu h1i/m sl_h2gwdhi/. me/g'hye do/h3r_ me: de/deh3s. te/we k'le/wos me/ne misdho/s. nu g'hoyso/m h1a/yto gwe/h2t-kwe.

tre/yes h2ne/res mo/ri gwme/nt. nu h1i/d h1wr_Hu/ h1regwesye/t-kwe. nu pr_h2wo/s h2e/g't. k'e/ywo:s me: tr_mte/. mo/ri upe/r h1e/yomes k'le/wos-kwe h2isk'o:/mes. nu lite/nt. nu h1i/d h1i/ns gwr_h3e/t nu h1e/yso:m k'le/wos n_/dhgwhitom.

h2ne:/r h2use/si gwm_ye/t. nu po/tnih2 h1i/m wide/t we/rh1yet-kwe. wi/Hre gwegwo/mth2e. ku/dhe h1e/s? nu te/bhye de/h3o:r k'le/wos-kwe bhe/roh2. po/tni sddhi/ h1ddhi/-kwe. nu me/wdt si/sdont-kwe.

bhoydhe/h2m diwe/h1 dhh1ta:/m. nu k'o/s bhoydhe/h2m pe/h2st nu h1e/nos dhrughye/t h1e/syo-kwe gwo:/s h1e/pt. nu pr_h2wo/s h1e/smey we/wket. h1yo/s bhoydhe/h2m Hrune/ps. ne gwi/h3we:si. me/ne g'hoyso/s twe/ h2isk'e:/ti. nu g'hoyso/m Hye/h1kt h1e/nos-kwe me/rt.

suHnu/s wiHro:/y g'n_h1ye/to. nu h1i/m de/wsbhis sle/h2gwt bhe/h2t-kwe. te/bhye h1no/mn_ dhe/h1oh2. te/we h1no/mn_ me/g'h2 h1sye/h1t te/we-kwe k'le/wos n_/dhgwhitom h1sye/h1t. de/ywo:s h1i/m ph2ste/. nu deywo:/s h1i/m k'le/wsont.

h2ne/rh1e h2o/g'moy h2e/nti sth2e/h1yeta:m. nu k'o/s h2e/g't. kwi/s tu/H ku/dhe-kwe h1e/ysi? nu h1e/nos we/rh1t. h1eg'o/h2 k'uh1ro/s h1eg'o/h2-kwe k'luto/s. me/ne h2o/g'mo:d litdhi/. nu me: line/kwoh2. h2o/g'mos h1wr_Hu/s. h1yo/d me/ne gwh2to/m h2isk'e/si. sth2dhi/ te/we-kwe gwe/ru sl_h2gwdhi/. nu g'hoyso/ns Hih1ke/nt h1e/nos-kwe se/ngwt.

h1e/k'woysu stesth2e:/r. nu k'o/s h1e/nosmey se/kwt. h1me/ h2wisdhi/. me/ne ma/rko:s HeHk'e/wes me/ne-kwe kwe/kwlo:s k'uh1ro:/s. mo/ri h2e/g'o:mes. h1yo/s mo/ri h1e/reti h1e/y misdho/m h1e/peti. nu te/we we/kwesh2 polh1u/h2 nu h2e/g'oh2. te/we ma/rkons yugdhi/. nu gwh2e/nt dhe/g'ho:m-kwe tre/mt. nu h1e/nos mo/ri h1r_sk'e/t misdho/m-kwe le/mbht.

polh1e/wes h2ne/res h2e/g'roy stn_h2e/nt. nu k'o/s pro/ gwe/mt Hre/wdHt-kwe. kwi/s h1me/ pro/ti gwe/meti? me/ne gwe/ru polh1u/ns h2ne/rn_s more/yet. ne kwosmo:d dedwo/yh2e. nu te/we we/kwesh2 polh1u/h2 nu me/ne gwe/ru pe/h3eti. nu h1e/nos h1i/m pro/ti h1r_sk'e/t k'o/s-kwe se/ngwt.

wiHro/s ste/h2t h1e/gwhyey-kwe Hre/wdHt. h1o/gwhey me/ne we/kwesh2 k'ludhi/. me/ne gwe/ru k'uh1ro/m me/ne-kwe k'e:/r k'uh1ro/m. gwo:/s h2o/wins-kwe gwr_h3e/si tu/H-kwe gho/stis. ml_h3dhi/ twe/-we: h1e/roh2. nu h1o/gwhis h1ne/dht we/wket-kwe. polh1e/wes me/ne de/nk'troh1 sn_gwe/nt. me/resi. nu swo/m gwe/ru Hye/h1kt h1e/gwhis-kwe k'e/rh2so: skl_Hto/. nu h1we/su k'le/wos we/nt.

h3re:/g's wiHro:/y we/rh1yet. me/g'hye te/we ma/rkons gwo:/s-kwe dh3dhi/ h1i/ns-we: sle/h2gwoh2 te/we-kwe wo/yk'om h1e/wso:h2. nu k'l_ne/wt we/wket-kwe. te/bhye me/ne h1e/k'wons me: de/h3oh2 me/ne-kwe gwo/wes twe/ ne ye/h2onti. h1yo/d h1me/ pro/ti gwm_sk'e:/si me/ne gwe/ru wide:/si. nu h3re:/g's gwm_ye/t nu h1e/y gwe/ru kwe/k't bhe/wgt-kwe.

bhre/h2terh1e se/dyeta:m pi/ph3eta:m-kwe. nu k'o/s se/kwt. h1eg'o/h2 pr_h2wo/s h1eg'o/h2-kwe stuh2ro/s. g'hoyso/m le/mbhoh2. nu tu/H pr_h2wo/s nu wl_/kwom more/yom. sth2e/h1ye te/we-kwe g'hoyso/m h1pdhi/. nu me/h2te:r h1ludhe/t mle/wHt-kwe. te/we bhre/h2term_ me: stune/ds. h2e/g'ros ne h1wr_Hu/s? nu h1i/h2m_ k'le/wsont g'hoyso/ns-kwe dhh1e/nt.

gho/stis bhe/wgt h1e/nos-kwe Hre/wdHt. ku/dhe h1e/ysi? te/we we/kwesh2 dhg'hye/s me/g'h2h2. nu te/we k'e:/r tre/seti. sth2e/h1ye te/we-kwe gwe/ru sl_h2gwdhi/. nu k'o/s we/rh1yet. me: ste/h2oh2 nu gwi/h3wo:h2. nu gwh2dhi/. h1yo/s bhe/wgeti h1e/syo h1no/mn_ ne k'luto/m.

sth2e/h1yeta:m. nu k'o/s h1e/nosmey mle/wHt. me/ne gwo:/s ste/h2yes. nu ne ste/h2yom. nu bhoydhe/h2m diwe/h1 dhh1dhi/. nu h1e/nos bhoydhe/h2m dhe/h1t we/rh1t-kwe. h1yo/d dhrughyo:/h2 dye:/ws h1me/ more/yoyh1t. nu dhrughye/t. nu dye:/ws h2we/yst h1e/nos-kwe me/rt.

wl_/kwos h2o/wins ne/kwti h1r_sk'e/t. nu k'wo:/ h1i/m k'le/wt ste/h2t-kwe bhe/h2t-kwe. poh2imne/s h2o/wins pe/h2smi. linkwdhi/ me/ne-we: de/nk'treh2 kwe/k'esi. nu tu/H poh2imne/s k'wo:/ nu h1eg'o/h2 h1le/wdheros. poh2imne/y wr_g'ye/si ne-kwe kwesmey wr_g'yo/h2. nu k'wo:/ se/kwt. poh2ime:/n h1me/ h2e/leti nu tre/msi. nu h1e/nos no/kwtm_ le/ytt.

sdta:/m. nu k'o/s h2e/g't. septm_/ wl_/kwons ne/kwti more/yom. nu h1e/gwhis k'e/rh2sonm_ ske/lHm_. h2o/wins ka/prons-kwe gwr_h3e/t. nu h3re:/g's h1i/ns h2we/yst mle/wHt-kwe. yu/ me/g'h2h1e. k'o/smey g'hoyso/m de/deh3mi h1e/nosmey ma/rkom de/deh3mi.

k'o/s h1e/syo ghoste/yey bhe/h2t. h2use/si h2e/g'roy stne/h2oh2. te/we gwru/h1 gwm_dhi/. nu h1ludho:/h2. so/h2wl_ n_sme/ wido/yh1t. nu h2e/wso:s h2we/st stesth2e:/r-kwe. nu swe/h2 gwe/ruh2 Hih1kta:/m h1e/nos-kwe se/ngwt.

h1r_ta:/m. nu kwi/s tu/H? twe/ ne g'n_ne/h3mi. nu h1e/nos we/rh1t. te/we ph2te:/r me/ne ph2tre/s k'o/ymoy gho/stis h1e/st. nu k'o/s we/rh1t. nu tu/H me/ne gho/stis twe/-kwe me: more/yo:h2. te/we g'hoyso/m dhh1dhi/ de/h3o:r-kwe de/h3omes. me/ne h1e/k'wons sl_h2gwdhi/ te/we-kwe g'hoyso/m le/mbhoh2. nu de/h3o:r h1ope/yeta:m k'luto/h1-kwe bhuHta:/m.

stuh2ro/s wiHro/s wo/yk'osyo dhwo/ri stesto/h2e. nu Hre/wdHt. h1yo/s k'a:/m dhwo/rm_ gwm_sk'e:/ti me/ne gwe/ru wide:/ti. nu h1suk'le/we:s wiHro/s gwe/mt. nu mle/wHt. dhure/s litdhi/ h1i/h2m_-we: Hrune/poh2. nu stuh2ro/s ne sth2e/h1yet. nu swo/m g'hoyso/m Hye/h1kt stuh2ro/s-kwe se/ngwt. nu h1i/h2m_ Hrune/pt wo/yk'om-kwe we/yk't.

wiHro/s h1e/syo kwe/kwloy stne/h2t po/tn_yeh2ey-kwe mle/wHt. po/tni nos k'o/ymo:d ye/h2mi k'le/wos-kwe h2isk'o:/h2. se/dye h2o/wins-kwe nos ph2sdhi/. me: tre/ses. so/h2wl_ h2ew gwm_sk'e/ti h2ew-kwe gwm_yo:/h2. dhe/g'ho:m me/h2te:r twe/ h2le/ksoyh1t. nu h2e/g'et po/tnih2-kwe h1i/m spe/k'yet.

ph2te:/r suHno/wey we/rh1t. me/ne we/kwesh2 k'le/wse. te/we ghoste/yey me: dhrughye/s g'hoyso:/d-kwe me: bhe/wgs. h1yo/d k'o/d dhe/dheh1esi te/we k'le/wos me/g'h2 h1e/seti. nu h1idhi/ deywo:/s-kwe twe/ ph2sih1e/nt. nu we/kwesh2 h2we/yst le/ytt-kwe.

me/h2te:r h2e/g't. su/Hnew gwe/ruh2 h1e/ysi me/ne-kwe k'e:/r tre/mti. k'a:/m we/stim we/so. me/ne we/kwesh2 memn_dhi/. nu me/h2ter me: tre/ses. h2ew h1e/roh2 me/ne-kwe k'le/wos k'l_ne/wesi. nu gwe/h2t h1i/h2-kwe stesto/h2e.

swe/so:r h1r_sk'e/t mle/wHt-kwe. bhre/h2ter ku/dhe ye/h2si? nu we/wket. swe/sor mo/ri upe/r h1e/ymi. nu wo/dr_ ph3dhi/ k'o/d-kwe do/h3r_ h1a/yso. te/we swe/sorm_ memn_dhi/. dye:/ws te/we h2o/g'mom h2le/ksoyh1t h1usu/h1-kwe k'le/wesh1 h2ew h1r_sk'e/. nu wo/dr_ pe/h3t do/h3r_ h1a/yto le/ytt-kwe.

h3re:/g's le/ght suHne/wes-kwe h1i/m h1ludho/nt. nu bhe/h2t. su/Hnewes me/ne usme/y gwe/ruh2 ma/rkons-kwe line/kwmi. bhre/h2teres h1ste/ bhoydhe/h2m-kwe me: Hrumpte/. h1me/ memn_te/. supno/s h1me/ gwm_sk'e/ti.

wiHro/s swo/ns h1e/k'wons h2e/g'rom de/wkt h1e/ymos-kwe we/rh1yet. He/Hk'ewes h1e/k'wo:s h1yo/y h1me/ pl_h1uye/h2su ne/kwtsu bhe/rete nu le/ghyete. g'r_h2no/m h1e/dte wo/dr_-kwe pi/ph3ete. usme/ kwis ne ye/wgeti. nu h1e/k'wo:s h1i/m spe/k'yont line/kwt-kwe.

wiHro/s ye/h2t diwe/y-kwe ph2tre/y dhg'hme/y-kwe me/h2trey bhe/h2t. de/ywo:s h1me/ h2wiste/. usme/y me/ne suHnu/m de/deh3mi. h1i/m k'o/ymoy ph2ste/. h1yo/d me/roh2 h1e/y me/ne h1no/mn_ k'le/wsoyh1t we/rh1yoyh1t-kwe. me/ne k'le/wos h1we/su.

h2ne:/r h1e/syo k'o/ymom line/kwt. nu se/dt Hre/wdHt-kwe. me/ne dhwo/r. me/ne pe/h2wr_. me/ne h2e/g're. le/ytm_ h2ew-kwe me: h1ludho:/h2. deywo:/s twe/ h2le/ksoyh1ent twe/-kwe kwis me: h1e/wsoyh1t.

po/tnih2 h1e/smey we/rh1t. linkwdhi/. pe/h2wr_ h2le/kso:h2 g'r_h2no/m-kwe h2we/kseti. h1me/ memn_dhi/. te/we gwe/ru k'uh1ro/m h1sye/h1t te/we-kwe h1e/k'wo:s HeHk'e/wes h1sih1e/nt. h1me/ h2ew gwm_ye/.

wiHro/h1 ph2we/ni ne/kwti se/dyeta:m. nu k'o/s h1e/nosmey mle/wHt. gwe/ruh2 h2use/si pe/h3eti. h1yo/d me/roh2 me/ne me/h2trey skwdhi/. ne bhe/wgt. nu h1e/nos we/wket. h1e/syeh2ey h2e/g'oh2. tu/H k'luto/s. nu pi/ph3eta:m le/ghyeta:m-kwe.

wiHro/s wo/yk'o:d stne/h2t k'une/y-kwe bhe/h2t. k'wo/n me/ne h1yo/s wo/yk'om pe/h2s. si/sde. me/ne po/tnih2m h2o/wins-kwe h2le/kse. wl_/kwos wo/yk'om me: h1r_sk'o/yh1t. ye/h2mi h2ew-kwe h1ludho:/h2. nu k'wo:/ h1i/m k'le/wt le/ght-kwe.

h2e/wso:s le/wkt swo/m-kwe g'hoyso/m kapyo/nts wiHro/s ma/rkons de/wkt. nu h1e/syo k'e/ywo:s stesth2e:/r skwe/nt-kwe. sh2we/ns kwe/kwlos pro/ gwh2ye/h1t. me/g'h2 k'le/wos wn_ye/h1s. nu nos k'o/ymom h2le/ksete. nu ma/rko:s pro/ dr_me/nt sth2e/h1yont-kwe.

suHne/wes h1e/yso:m ph2te/rm_ ph2we/ni loghe/yont. nu pe/h2wr_ dhe/gwhet bhh2e/nt-kwe. ph2ter sh2we/ns h2o/g'moh1 ih2dhi/. dhe/g'ho:m me/h2te:r twe/ h1a/yih1to. te/we k'le/wos n_/dhgwhitom. te/we h1no/mn_ meme/nomes. nu gwh2e/nt.

wl_/kwos h2egwno/m srume/ni wide/t h2e/g't. me/ne wo/dr_ pe/h3s. h2egwno/s we/rh1t. wl_/kwe te/we wo/dr_ ne pe/h3m_. nu le/mbht wl_/kwos h2egwno/m h1i/m-kwe h2e/k'h3t.

wl_kwi/h2s k'wo/nm_ wide/t mle/wHt wl_kwi/h2s. k'wo/n tu/H pleh1to/s h1eg'o/h2-kwe tr_syo/h2. k'wo:/ se/kwt. wl_/kwih2 me/ne k'o/ymom gwm_ye/. me/ne moy po/tnih2 pe/yh2tu de/deh3ti. h1eg'o/h2 yugto/s. se/h2 we/rh1yet. tu/H yugto/s. h1eg'o/h2 h1le/wdhereh2. h1eg'o/h2 tr_syo/h2 h1eg'o/h2-kwe h1le/wdhereh2. nu wl_kwi/h2s le/ytt.

wl_/kwos h2wl_/h1neh2h1 wose/yeto h2o/wins-kwe h1r_sk'e/t. nu moyso/s de/nk'treh2 wide/t. we/wket moyso/s. h2o/weyes spe/k'yete. h1e/nos ne h2o/wis. h2wl_/h1neh2 we/smn_ de/nk'trom swo/m. nu wl_/kwos bhe/yh2t bhe/wgt-kwe. h2wl_/h1neh2 wl_/kwom k'e/leti de/nk'trom-kwe h1i/m woyde/yeti.

h2e/wis pr_we/ni se/dyet. nu se/rpet h2e/ngwhis h1i/h2m_. h2e/wis se/kwt. h2e/ngwhey ku/dhe se/rpesi? so/ we/rh1t. h2isk'o/h2 te h1we/su toy we/kwos bhe/roh2. h2e/wis we/wket. h2e/ngwhey me dhrughye/si. nu h2e/wis bhe/wgt h2e/ngwhis-kwe pr_we/ni le/ght.

h2e/wis h2e/bolm_ bhe/ret. nu moyso/s h1i/h2m_ kwe/k't we/rh1yet moyso/s. te/we we/kwos h1we/su. bhh2dhi/ moy. nu h2e/wis Hre/wdHt h2e/bo:l-kwe h3sle/ydht. nu moyso/s h1i/m h2e/k'h3t. h2e/wis h2e/g't. we/kwos k'le:/m. nu h2e/bo:l lelo/ykwe.

h2e/ngwhis h2e/g'roy k'e/yo ste/wdt-kwe. nu wide/t h1e/k'wos h1i/m. h2e/ngwhis we/wket. h1e/k'we me gwhr_nudhi/. ste/wdm_. nu h1e/k'wos h2e/ngwhim gwhr_ne/wt. nu h2e/ngwhis ste/h2t mle/wHt-kwe. nu te more/yo:h2. h2e/ngwhey me dhrughye/si. h2e/ngwhis we/rh1yet. h1eg'o/h2 h2e/ngwhis. nu h1e/k'wos bhe/wgt.

dwo/h1 moyso/h1 pr_we/ni gwm_ta:/m. moyso/s h2e/g't. mo/yse h2po/ gwh2dhi/ h1eg'o/h2 pro/ ye/h2mi. h1e/nos moyso/s we/rh1t. tu/H h2po/ gwh2dhi/ h1eg'o/h2 pro/ ye/h2mi. nu stundta:/m dwo/h1 moyso/h1 srume/ni-kwe ni/ gwh2ta:/m. nu sre/wmn_ pro/ h1e/yt.

wl_kwi/h2s se/kwt. wl_/kwe ku/dhe gwm_sk'e/si? h2o/wins ye/h2mi. h1eg'o/h2 me/g'h2s. wl_kwi/h2s mle/wHt. k'wo:/ bhe/wdheti poh2ime:/n-kwe h2e/nti sth2e/h1yeti. wl_/kwe me: gwe/h2s. nu wl_/kwos wl_kwi/h2m_ ne k'le/wt gwe/h2t-kwe. nu le/mbht k'wo:/ wl_/kwom wl_/kwos-kwe bhe/wgt. wl_kwi/h2s we/wket. ne me k'le/ws.

h2o/weyes k'une/y skwe/nt. k'wo/n nos pe/h2si poh2ime:/n nos h2wl_/h1neh2m skine/dti. k'wo:/ we/rh1t. pe/h2smi wos me/ne moy po/tnih2 misdho/m de/deh3ti. h2o/weyes h2g'e/nt. te/we nos misdho/s h2wl_/h1neh2. nu k'wo:/ ne bhe/h2t.

h2e/wis tn_tlo/y h3sle/ydht. nu gwe/mt g'hwe:/r. h2e/wis we/wket. g'hwe:/r me spr_dhi/. de/h3rom toy de/h3oh2. nu g'hwe:/r tn_tlo/m ske/lHt h1i/h2m_-kwe spe/rt. nu le/ght g'hwe:/r. nu wiHro/s h1e/rt. nu h2e/wis wiHro/m kwe/k't Hre/wdHt-kwe. nu g'hwe:/r bhe/wdht bhe/wgt-kwe. h2e/wis we/rh1yet. de/h3rom toy de/h3m_.

h2e/ngwhis h2o/wim h1r_sk'e/t. se/kwt. h2o/wey toy me/ne h1o/ytom h1ope/yoh2. me: te more/yo:h2. se/h2 mle/wHt. h2e/ngwhey te/we h1o/ytos dhuh2mo/s. litdhi/. k'wo:/ bhebho/wdhe.

h2egwno/s pr_we/ni stesto/h2e h2e/wim-kwe upe/r wide/t. so/ we/rh1yet. h2e/wey u/d ye/h2si u/d-kwe h1e/yoh2. me/ne me porne/h2 we/g'heti. dhg'he/mi gwm_sk'e/. h1eg'o/h2 dye/wi ye/h2mi. nu h2egwno/s h1i/h2m_ ne k'le/wt pr_we/ns-kwe h3sle/ydht.

h1regwesye/t. nu sth2e/h1yet wl_/kwos dhwo/ri. mle/wHt. k'wo/n toy de/h3rom h1ope/yoh2. h2o/wins we/yk'oh2. k'wo:/ h2e/g't. me/ne moy po/tnih2 misdho/m de/deh3ti me/ne-kwe h1o/ytom memo/nh2e. bhe/wdhoh2. dr_mdhi/. nu wl_/kwos bhe/wgt.

wl_kwi/h2s swo/syo k'o/ymosyo dhwo/ri le/ghyet. nu gwe/mt moyso/s. se/h2 we/rh1t. mo/yse h2e/nti gwm_ye/ me/roh2-we:. polh1u/ gwe/h2mn_ te/we k'o/ymom ye/h2ti. h1e/smo:d kwid h1e/g'hs ne gwm_sk'e/ti. h2e/nti me: gwm_yo:/h2. nu moyso/s le/ytt wl_kwi/h2s-kwe le/ghyet tr_sye/t-kwe.

ghoste/yey wo/dr_ me/dhu-kwe dh3dhi/. h1yo/s ghoste/yey me/dhu h1ope/yeti so/ h1we/su k'le/wos h1e/pt. gho/stis deywo/s h1e/seti.

te/we h1o/ytom me: Hrune/ps. h1yo/s h1o/ytom Hrune/pti kwoyne/h2 h1i/m se/kwetor. deywo/s h1o/ytom k'le/wseti.

bhre/h2terh1e do/wsih1. bhre/h2te:r bhre/h2term_ h2le/kseti. h1i/m ne dhrughye/ti. swe/so:r bhre/h2trey lewbhe/h2.

so/h2wl_ ni/ h1e/yti no/kwts-kwe h1ludhe/t. no/kwts le/ytt h2e/wso:s-kwe h1ludhe/t. ne/kwti me: bhe/yh2s.

h1yo/d dhuh2mo/s h1e/sti pe/h2wr_ h1e/sti. dhuh2mo/s k'o/ymo:d u/d gwm_sk'e/ti. pe/h2wr_ ne k'e/letor.

wo/dr_ pe/rwr_ h2n_re/h1 ne te/rdt. h2yu/h1 te/rdt. srume/ns h1ukdhi/.

ph2te:/r suHno/ws bho/rom bhe/reti. suHnu/s ph2tre/s h1no/mn_ bhe/reti. h1we/sus suHnu/s ph2te/rm_ ne dhrughye/ti. me/h2te:r suHno/wey lewbhe/h2.

me/nos pro/ h1e/yti g'ho/stos-kwe se/kwetor. te/we g'ho/stos pro/ me: dr_mye/h1t.

gwo/wes m_re/nt bhre/h2teres m_re/nt h2ne:/r-kwe me/rt. n_/dhgwhitom k'le/wos ne ne/k't. k'luto/m h1no/mn_ sth2e/h1yeti.

h1yo/d k'r_de/s gwm_ye/ti to/d k'e:/r h1r_sk'e/ti. h1we/su we/kwos de/h3rom.

le/ghyonts poh2ime:/n wl_/kwom h2e/leti. bhe/wdhonts k'wo:/ h2o/wins h2le/kseti. h1yo/d k'wo:/ bhebho/wdhe. wl_/kwos h2o/wins ne h1e:/dti.

h2e/wso:s wr_g'yo/ntey h2n_re/y pe/k'os de/h3t. h2e/wso:s str_h3me/ni k'e/ym_h1no:y suHno/wey pe/k'os ne de/h3t. h2we/st. h2ne/r sth2dhi/.

polh1e/wes pe/k'esi lewbho:/s. lewbho/s h2e/ghesi g'n_nh3to/r. wi/Hre lewbho/m me: dhrughye/s.

kwe/kwlos dye/wi gwm_sk'e/ti. h1i/m kwis ne ye/wgt h1e/k'wos-kwe kwis h1i/m ne de/wkti. kwo/s kwe/kwlos h1e/y? h1e/y sh2we/ns kwe/kwlos.

kwi/d swe/sor h1e:/dti ne-kwe ple/h1dheti. kwi/d wo/dr_ pi/ph3eti me/rt-kwe. swe/so:r we/wket. pe/h2wr_ h1e:/dti ne-kwe ple/h1dheti. wo/dr_ pi/ph3eti me/rt-kwe.

kwi/s h2e/g'roy se/rpeti? so/ h2e/wim le/mbht. g'ho/stoh1 ne kapye/ti. g'n_h3dhi/. h1e/y h1o/gwhis.

gwm_ye/ti h1i/h2m_-kwe kwis ne we/dheti. h2e/g'rom k'o/ymom-kwe k'e/leti. so/h2wl_ h1e/syeh2s k'e/letor. kwi/d h1i/d? no/kwts.

kwi/s wiHro:/y we/smn_ de/deh3ti gwi/h3weti-kwe. h2e/g'ros h1i/h2m_ h2e/leti. poh2ime:/n h1i/h2m_ pe/h2sti. h2o/wis.

kwi/s h2e/g'roy se/dyeti? se/h2 u/d h1e/yti. h1i/h2m_ kwis g'ho/stoh1 ne kapye/ti. h1yo/d h2e/wso:s h1e/reti se/h2 Hre/wdHti. ph2ter g'n_h3dhi/. ph2te:/r se/kwt. h1i/h2 h2e/wis.

h1yo/d poh2ime:/n le/gheti kwi/s gho/stey bhe/wdheti? so/ poh2imne/y lewbho/s. so/ ghoste/yey dusme/ne:s. gho/stis we/rh1t. k'wo:/ bhe/wdheti.

kwi/d swe/sor gwm_sk'e/ti swo/m-kwe ste/rh3mn_ ne line/kwti. to/d dhg'hme/s gwm_ye/ti. g'hwe:/res h1i/d pi/ph3onti. kwi/d h1i/d? sre/wmn_.

h1e/y we/sni h2we/kst h1rudhe/h1ti-kwe. po/tnih2 wiHro:/y h1i/m h1ope/yeti. kwi/s h1e/y? h2e/bo:l.

kwi/s ph2we/ns g'n_h1ye/tor k'o/ymo:d-kwe u/d h1e/yti. h1i/m kwis g'ho/stoh1 ne kapye/ti. dhuh2mo/s.

h3re/g'm_ wiHro/m-kwe h1ludhe/t. dhwo:/r h1i/m ne kapye/ti. h1i/m kwis ne kwe/k't. kwi/s h1e/y? supno/s.

kwi/d ne/pot h2ne:/r de/deh3ti h1i/d-kwe kapye/ti. ne/po:ts h2e/g't. h2ne:/r we/kwos de/deh3ti h1i/d-kwe kapye/ti.

kwi/s dhwo/ri sth2e/h1yeti? h1yo/d so/h2wl_ h1e/reti se/h2 le/ytt. h2e/wso:s dhwo/ri sth2e/h1yeti.

k'wo/n h2o/wins ph2sdhi/. wr_h1e/nn_s me: line/kws. nu wl_/kwos ne/kwti gwm_sk'e/ti. k'le/wsesi? wl_/kwosyo h3o/kwih1 dedo/rk'h2e. nu k'wo:/ ste/h2t pro/-kwe dre/mt g'hwe:/r-kwe bhe/wgt.

gwo/wes gwowsth2o/m gwm_te/. nu so/h2wl_ se/ngwt no/kwts-kwe gwm_ye/ti. pi/ph3ete. nu poh2ime:/n ste/h2dhh1om do:/m we/dheti k'wo:/-kwe se/kwetor. nu gwowsth2o/y k'e/yror.

po/tni wr_h1e/nes ku/ h1se/nti? nu wr_h1e/nes h2e/g'roy h1se/nti. h1i/ns wido/m h1e/dn_t-kwe. nu suHnu/s h1i/ns h2le/kseti. po/tni gwm_dhi/ nu h1e/domes.

h2we/st. nu poh2ime:/n ste/h2dhh1om h2e/g'rom h1e/lh2t. nu ste/h2dhh1om h1e:/dt. nu se/dt ste/h2dhh1om-kwe spe/k'yet. nu wl_/kwom wide/t Hre/wdHt-kwe h1i/m-kwe h1e/g'hs g'he/yt.

swe/sor h2o/wins h2ml_g'dhi/. nu bhre/h2ter wr_h1e/nes tetr_pe:/r. h1i/h2n_s h2me/lg'mi. h2o/lut bhe/re. nu g'e/nh1to:r swesre/s wo/kwm_ h2we/yst.

g'hwe:/r ne/kwti gwe/mt. nu su/Hes bhih2e/nt h1e/k'wo:s-kwe tr_me/nt. nu ph2te:/r ste/h2t Hre/wdHt-kwe. su/Hnew wl_/kwom h1e/g'hs g'hidhi/. nu bhe/wgt.

poh2imne/s ste/h2dhh1om me/g'h2. swo/s h1e/k'wos HeHk'u/s. g'hwe:/r h1e/syo h2o/wins ne le/mbht. nu swo/m h1we/su k'le/wos h1sye/h1t swo/m-kwe n_/dhgwhitom k'le/wos h1sye/h1t.

h2o/weyes kl_He/ni h1e/dte de/h2newi-kwe pi/ph3ete. nu me: bhih2te/. k'wo:/ usme/ h2le/kseti. nu twe/h2 h2wl_/h1neh2 h2uksye/h1t two:/s-kwe wr_h1e/nes gwi/h3woyh1ent. nu usme/ do:/m we/dho:h2.

kwi/s gho/stey tu/H? nu h1eg'o/h2 poh2ime:/n. ku/dhe gwe/ms? pe/runs gwe:/m. two/m ste/h2dhh1om me/g'h2? nu me/ne ste/h2dhh1om me/g'h2. h2e/g'ros te/rst. gho/stey me/ne h2e/g'rom h1r_sk'e/ h1e/domes-kwe.

h3re/g' de/k'm_ gwo/wes lelikwe:/r. nu to/ns pe/runi h2isk'o/m. nu suHnu/s se/h2gyet to/ns-kwe de/h2newi wido/m. nu septm_/ h1e/yes gwi/h3wonti wl_/kwos-kwe tri/ns h1i/ns gwr_h3e/t.

h2we/rst. nu poh2ime:/n ste/h2dhh1om h2e/g'rom ne h1e/lh2t. nu gwowsth2o/y le/ghyont. h2Hse/h2i se/dt h2o/lut-kwe pi/ph3et. pe/h2wr_ dhe/gwhet. nu gwe:/n h2wl_/h1neh2m uh1ine/Ht. nu n_bhro/s le/ytt.

dye/w nos ph2ter ste/h2dhh1om ph2sdhi/. g'hwe:/r h2o/wins me: lm_bhye/h1t gho/stis-we: h1e/k'wons me: ste/h2yoyh1t. nu h2e/g'roy h1e/dih1t tr_pye/h1t-kwe. nu nos we/kwos k'ludhi/.

bhre/h2ter spe/k'ye. nu h1o/gwhim de/h2newi dedo/rk'h2e. h1o/gwhis se/rpeti. to/m me: kapye/s. nu k'wo:/ to/m more/ye:ti. dedwo/yth2e? ne dedwo/yh2e.

ste/h2dhh1om ko/lHonm_ h2e/g'o:mes. nu le/ymo: h2e/n h1e/sti. nu suHnu/s ste/h2dhh1om se/kwe:tor bhre/h2te:r-kwe pe/h2seti. ne/pot memn_dhi/. me: h3sle/ydhs.

h1e/k'woh1 yugte/ h2erh3wo/m h2e/rh3yete. nu h1e/k'wo:s yugo/m dukte/ wi/Hre k'm_h2dhi/. dhe/g'ho:m de/retor dhe/g'hom me/h2ter h3pusdhi/. nu-kwe k'e/ywos misdho/s h1we/sus.

h2wl_/h1neh2m uh1inHdhi/ to/nom tn_nudhi/. gwe:/n h2wl_/h1neh2m uh1ine/Hti to/nom tn_ne/wti. gwne/h2s wo/kwm_ k'le/wsete kwi/d gwe:/n te:/tk't to/nom te:/tk't.

wi/Hro:s dukte/ wi/Hro:s stundte/. nu kwe/kwlos we/ndhetor kwe/kwlos pro/ h1e/yti. nu k'e:/r stuh2ro/m me: sth2e/h1yete.

k'e/ywom h2o/lut ye/se pe/h2wr_ dhe/gwheti. ghoste/yey h2o/lut pe/kwete. gho/stis srobhe/yeti k'e/ywom-kwe k'e:/r me/wdt. nu k'e/ywe ph3dhi/ sh2dhi/-kwe.

nu dhh2bhre h1o/lom gwhr_nudhi/. h1o/lom ph2we/ni h3rog'e/ye. k'e/ywom pe/h2wr_ dhe/gwheti dhh2bhre stunddhi/. h1o/los h1we/sus dhh2bhro/syo k'le/wos me/g'h2.

gwo/wes sth2e/h1yete gwe:/n h2me/lg'ti. gwo/wes dedh3te/ wr_h1e:/n srobhe/yeti. me: bhih2te/ k'e/ywa gwe:/n muddhi/.

do:/m de/mete do:/m h2re/ydhete. h1we/sewes bhre/h2teres dukte/. nu do:/m stesto/h2e h2e/Hsh2-kwe dhe/gwheti. bhre/h2teres two/m misdho/m h1a/ydhwe.

le/wbho:s wi/Hro:s wo/dr_ de/h2nus bhe/rete. swe/sor wo/dr_ limne/s bhe/re. nu le/ymo: ste/wdt ghoste/yey h2o/lut dh3dhi/. nu me: h3slidhte/ nu we/g'hete.

nu kwe/kwlom pitse/h1 h2linHdhi/. kwe/kwlom we/ndhe pi:/ts dhe/gwheti. lewbho/s dhh2bhro/s kwe/kwlom de/met. kwe/kwlos h1we/sus Hro/th2os h1ln_gwhro/s.

lewbho/m ko/lHonm_ h1ime/s k'wo:/ pro/ h1e/yti. se/kwomesdhh2-kwe gwe/h2omes.

nu h2o/weyes sth2e/h1yete h2wl_/h1neh2m skindme/s. h2wl_/h1neh2m ph2we/ni h2sowse/yete nu yu/H nogwe/h2es.

nu wi/Hro:s pe/rwr_ gle/wbhete. nu to/d gle/wbhete swidyo/mes. nu pe/rwr_ Hrumpto/r me: h3slidhte/.

su/Hnew bhe/wdhe h1regwesye/ti. ste/h2dhh1om spe/k'ye wo/kwm_ k'le/wse nu me: le/ghs.

h1yo/d h2e/wso:s h1rudhe/h1ti n_bhro/s gwm_ye/ti. h1yo/d h2e/wso:s le/wkt ne h2we/rst. h1yo/d no/kwts ste/wdt dye:/ws le/wkt. poh2ime:/n h2e/wsosm_ dedo/rk'e.

h1yo/d ne/bhos kl_He/ni se/dyeti h2we/rst. h1yo/d h3mi/ghleh2 lime/ni k'e/yor ne h2we/rst. n_bhro/s pe/runs gwm_sk'e/ti h2erh3wo/m-kwe k'e/leti. h2e/nh1mos ne/bhos we/dheti.

h2e/nh1mos h2we:/h1ti. h1yo/d stuh2ro/s h2e/nh1mos h2we:/h1ti ne/bhesh2 bhe/wgt. h1yo/d h2e/nh1mos le/ght le/ymo: k'e/yor. h2e/nh1mosyo wo:/kws me/g'h2ih2. h2e/nh1mos n_bhro/s-kwe bhre/h2terh1e.

h1yo/d gwo/wes le/ghyonti n_bhro/s gwm_ye/ti. h1yo/d h1e/k'wo:s tr_me/nti pe/rkwunos h1r_sk'e/ti. h1yo/d su/Hes bhebhih2e:/r h2e/nh1mos h2we:/h1ti. h1yo/d wl_/kwos Hre/wdHti no/kwts ste/wdt.

h1yo/d pe/rkwunos bhe/h2ti dhe/g'ho:m wo/dr_ pi/ph3eti. pe/rkwunos pe/h2wr_ ne/bhesos Hye/h1kt. pe/rkwune ne/bhos gle/wbhe n_sme/y-kwe n_bhro/m dh3dhi/. dye/w ph2ter h2wr_sye/h1t.

h1yo/d so/h2wl_ se/ngwt h1regwesye/ti. h1yo/d so/h2wl_ ne/bhesi se/ngwt h2e/nh1mos gwm_ye/ti. te/mHos kl_He/ni k'e/yor. no/kwts ste/wdt.

n_bhro/s wo/dr_ de/h3t. wo/dr_ ste/h2dhh1om h2e/leti. ste/h2dhh1om do:/m h2e/leti. h1yo/d n_bhro/s h1r_sk'e/ti de/h2nu ple/h1dheti. h1yo/d n_bhro/s ne gwm_ye/ti le/ymo: te/rst g'hwe:/res-kwe tr_syo/nti.

h1yo/d so/h2wl_ gwhe/rt h3mi/ghleh2 tine/h1ti. so/h2wl_ h2erh3wo/m h1e/wseti g'hwe:/res-kwe swidyo/nti. h1yo/d h2e/nh1mos gwhe/rt h1o/gwhis se/rpeti.

h1yo/d n_bhro/s h1r_sk'e/ti ste/h2dhh1om gwowsth2o/m we/dhe. h1yo/d ne/bhos kl_He/ni se/dyeti su/Hn_s pe/rwr_ me: h2e/g'es. h1yo/d stuh2ro/s h2e/nh1mos h2we:/h1ti suHnu/m k'o/ymom we/dhe. h1yo/d pe/rkwunos bhe/h2ti h2Hse/h2i sddhi/.

dye:/ws ph2te:/r. dhe/g'ho:m me/h2te:r. n_bhro/s diwe/s g'e/nh1mn_. dhe/g'hom me/h2ter gwo:/s h2e/le. dye/w ph2ter wo/dr_ dh3dhi/. bho/h1 lewbho/h1.

h1yo/d n_bhro/s h1r_sk'e/ti me: h2e/rh3yes. h1yo/d ne/bhos le/ytt dhe/g'ho:m pi/ph3eti h2erh3wo/s-kwe h3pe/wst. h1yo/d n_bhro/s ne h1ludhe/t wiHro/s de/h2nu h2isk'e/ti.

n_/bhre gwm_dhi/. h2erh3wo/y h2wr_sye/h1t. h2e/nh1mos gwowsth2o/m me: Hrumpye/h1t. n_/bhre litdhi/. so/h2wl_ lukye/h1t.

kwi/s ne/bhos wo/yde? ph2te:/r ne/bhos wo/yde. kwi/s h3mi/ghleh2m wo/yde? me/h2te:r h3mi/ghleh2m wo/yde. kwi/s h2e/nh1mom wo/yde? we/ydto:r h2e/nh1mom wo/yde.

kwo/smo:d k'o/ymo:d gho/stey gwm_sk'e/si? kwelso:/d k'o/ymo:d gwm_sk'o/h2 me/g'h2-kwe de/h2nu pe/rm_. de/h2nu kwi/h1 pe/rs? poh2ime:/n h1me/ de/wkt h1i/m-kwe se/kwoh2e. gho/stey sddhi/ me/dhu-kwe ph3dhi/. deywo:/s twe/ h2le/ksoyh1ent.

gwm_to/s pe/runs ste/yks kwo/r h1e/sti? de/h2newi h2e/n h1e/sti. ne/kwti me: h1e/ys. pe/rwr_ me/g'h2 wl_/kwo:s-kwe h2e/n tk'iye/nti. h1e/ymos ne dedwo/yh2e pe/h2wr_-kwe bhe/roh2. so/h2wl_ twe/ we/dhoyh1t.

kwi/s h1e/si? gho/stis h1e/smi. kwelso/s ste/yks h1me/ de/wkt. kwi/d h2isk'e/si? pe/h2wr_ ne/kwti h2isk'o/h2. gho/stey wik'dhi/ sddhi/-kwe. h1regwesye/ti. nu te/bhye me/dhu de/h3oh2.

bhre/h2ter ku/dhe ye/h2s? pe/rwr_ gwa:/m wl_/kwom-kwe wido/m. h1e/smo:d ne bhe/yh2s? h1e/smo:d bhe/yh2m_ bhe/wgm_-kwe. gwm_dhi/. me/ne me/h2te:r twe/ h2isk'e/ti.

kwo/m h2e/g'rom ste/h2dhh1om h2e/g'esi? h1i/d h1e/nom h2e/g'rom h2e/g'oh2. ku/ h1e/ysi? k'o/ymom ye/h2mi me/ne-kwe k'e/ywom h2e/n h2isk'o/h2. te/we k'e/ywos ne h1ludhe/t. de/h2newi h1idhi/ no/kwts-kwe h1use/wih2 h1sye/h1t.

swe/sor te/we bhre/h2te:r kwo/r h1e/sti? h2e/g'rom le/ytt. kwi/d h2e/n dhe/dheh1ti? gwo:/s h2e/g'eti.

h1a/ydhi me/g'hye bhoydhe/h2m n_sdhi/-kwe. te/bhye bhoydhe/h2m de/h3m_. ne/so:h2er we/yh1onm_-kwe bhe/ro:h2. dye:/ws ph2te:/r te/we we/kwos k'le/wsoyh1t.

kwi/d te/we h1no/mn_ h1e/sti? me/ne h1no/mn_ k'luto/m h1e/sti. h3re:/g's h1e/si? h3re:/g's ne h1e/smi. poh2ime:/n h1e/smi me/ne-kwe h1no/mn_ k'luto/m h1e/sti.

de/h2nu ku/dhe pe/romes? de/h2nu me/g'h2 HeHk'u/-kwe. ne/kwti se/domes h1yo/d-kwe h2e/wso:s h1e/reti nu pe/romes.

wl_/kwe ku/ ye/h2si? h2o/wins h1e/ymi. me: ye/h2s. poh2ime:/n bhe/wdheti h2o/wins-kwe pe/h2smi.

kwi/d swe/sor bhu/Ht? me/ne bhre/h2te:r pe/rwr_ gwe/h2t ne-kwe ne/st. deywo:/s h1i/m h2le/kso:nti gwe/meti-kwe.

kwelso/m pe/rwr_ dedo/rk'th2e? h1i/d dedo/rk'h2e. h1o/gwhis h2e/n tk'e/yti. we/dhe h1me/ h2e/n te/bhye-kwe misdho/m de/h3oh2.

te/we h1e/k'wos kwo/r h1e/sti? g'hwe:/r me/ne h1e/k'wom ne/kwti gwr_h3e/t. h1ddhi/ pi/ph3e-kwe. te/bhye h1e/k'wom h1a/yoh2.

de/h2nu gwm_to/m? de/h2nu ne gwm_to/m wo/dr_-kwe HeHk'u/. pe/rtum h1idhi/. pe/rtus ku/dhe h1e/sti? pe/rtus h2e/nti h1e/sti.

nu wiHro/s me/g'h2 de/h2nu h1ludhe/t. wo/dr_ kwe/k't bhe/yh2t-kwe de/h2nu-kwe pe/rt. poh2ime:/n h2e/g't. ku/ ye/h2si? we/rh1t. ne/soh2er.

nu te/tk'o: pe/kwto:r-kwe ne/kwti litta:/m. te/tk'o: se/kwt. pe/kwtor k'e/mh2m_. se/domes. pe/kwto:r we/wket. k'o/ymos ne kwelso/s. h1e/yomes.

nu ph2te:/r pe/rwr_ u/d gwe/h2t. kwelso/m k'o/ymom pe/runs wide/h1yet h1e/syo-kwe k'e:/r me/wdt. bhe/h2t. dye/w ph2ter h1me/ de/wksi ne/so:h2er-kwe.

nu ste/h2dhh1om h2e/g'onts poh2ime:/n wl_/kwom pe/runi spe/k'yet. we/rh1yet. k'wo/n gwo:/s ph2sdhi/. k'wo:/ dre/mt wl_/kwos-kwe bhe/wgt.

nu h3re:/g's Hro/th2om pe/rwr_ h2e/g'et. h1e/syo suHnu/s h1i/m se/kweto. h3re:/g's mle/wHt. ste/yks kwelso/s h1we/sus-kwe. nu h2e/g't. ph2ter ne dedwo/yh2e.

nu po/tnih2 swe/h2m h2o/wim h2isk'e/t. h2ne/rm_ h1r_sk'e/t we/rh1t-kwe. me/ne h2o/wim wide/s? se/kwt. h1i/h2m_ dhg'hye/s kwe/k'm_. nu pe/rwr_ le/ytt swo/m-kwe k'e:/r tre/mt.

nu tre/yes wiHro:/s pe/runi sde/nt. no/kwts h1e/rt pe/h2wr_-kwe dhe/gwhet. we/wket. kwi/s bhe/wdheti? h2e/nteros h2ne:/r bhe/h2t. bhe/wdhoh2. pe/h2wr_ pe/h2smi.

nu wiHro/s HeHk'u/ns h1e/k'wons de/h2nu we/dhet. we/dn_s ne dedwo/ye. we/rh1yet. He/Hk'ewes h1e/k'wo:s h1me/ bhe/rete. h1e/k'wo:s de/h2nu pre/nt h1i/ns-kwe se/kweto.

h1yo/d h2e/wso:s le/wkt nu wiHro/h1 sth2ta:/m litta:/m-kwe. h1e/teros mle/wHt. le/wka h2e/wsos n_h3me/ dukdhi/. so/h2wl_ le/wkt.

nu gho/stis dhwo/rm_ gwe/mt h2e/g't-kwe. te/we ph2we/ni sdye/h1m_? po/tnih2 we/rh1t. gho/stis deywo/ybhos lewbho/s. nu h1e/smey me/dhu de/h3t.

nu h2ne:/r ne/kwti h3sle/ydht. pe/h2wr_ pe/runi wide/t h1i/d-kwe gwe/h2t. poh2ime:/n se/kwt. h3sle/ydhs ste/yks-kwe h2e/nti h1e/sti. h1me/ se/kweso.

kwi/d kwe/k's? me/g'h2m_ h1o/gwhim wido/m. h1o/gwhis kwo/r k'e/yo? de/h2newi k'e/yo. bhe/wgs? bhe/wgm_.

nu h2ne:/r tisr_su/ ne/kwtsu le/ytt kwetwr_te/h2i-kwe ne/kwti h2ew h1ludhe/t. po/tnih2 dhwo/ri ste/h2t we/wket-kwe. gwegwo/mth2e.

nu suHnu/s ne/st. me/h2te:r h1i/m dhwo/ri kwe/k't bhe/h2t-kwe. gwegwo/mth2e. deywo:/s twe/ h2le/ksont. we/rh1yet. me/h2ter de/h2nu pe/rwr_-kwe wido/m.

nu wiHro/s swe/h2m dhwo/rm_ pl_h1u/su ye/h1n_su h1e/rt. h1i/m kwis ne g'n_ne/h3t. k'wo:/ h1i/m g'n_ne/h3t h1i/m-kwe dre/mt. mle/wHt. k'wo/n h1me/ g'n_ne/h3si.

nu wiHro/s we/kwsperoy ne/seto. po/tnih2 dhwo/ri ste/h2t h2e/g't-kwe. gwegwo/mth2e. h1ddhi/ pi/ph3e-kwe to/d-kwe bhh2dhi/ h1yo/d kwe/k's.

nu swe/so:r swo/m bhre/h2term_ wide/t h1e/syeh2s-kwe k'e:/r me/wdt. h1i/m dre/mt we/rh1t-kwe. bhre/h2ter tu/H gwih3wo/s.

nu ph2te:/r ste/h2dhh1om we/kwsperoy h2e/g'et. po/tnih2 se/kwt. h2o/weyes gwih3we/h2es? we/wket. h1i/h2es gwih3we/h2es. wl_/kwos gwe/mt k'wo:/-kwe h1i/m h2epo/ trose/yet.

nu h3re:/g's ne/seto. h2ne/res bhh2e/nt. h3re/g' te/we k'le/wos me/g'h2. te/we n_/dhgwhitom k'le/wos h1sye/h1t.

nu bhre/h2te:r pe/runs ne/seto. we/rh1yet. swe/sor te/bhye h2wl_/h1neh2m we/yh1onm_-kwe bhe/roh2. mle/wHt. tu/H me/g'hye lewbho/s.

kwi/s h2ne/r h1e/si? me/h2ter te/we suHnu/s h1e/smi. me/ne suHnu/s pe/rwr_ gwe/h2t ne-kwe ne/st. me/h2ter ne/sm_. te/we we/kwos g'n_ne/h3mi.

nu swe/so:r ye/h1ni h1r_sk'e/t. h2e/g't kwis. swe/sor h2we/ks. te/we h1no/mn_ k'luto/m.

kwi/h1 h1ludhe/s? deywo:/s h1me/ h2le/ksont k'wo:/-kwe h1me/ we/dhet. g'hwe:re/s bhe/wgm_ ne/sm_-kwe.

nu me/h2te:r we/kwsperoy dhwo/ri ste/h2t ste/yghm_-kwe spe/k'yet. ste/yghi kwim kwe/k't swo/m-kwe k'e:/r h1i/m g'n_ne/h3t. dre/mt we/rh1t-kwe. su/Hnew.

nu suHnu/s gwegwo/me. h1e/domes pi/ph3o:mes-kwe. po/tni me/dhu bhe/re. nu ph2te:/r h1e/smey we/yh1onm_ de/h3t.

nu h2use/si de/h2newi ye/h2t k'o/ymosyo-kwe dhuh2mo/m spe/k'yet. se/kwt. dhuh2mo/s. nu swo/m k'e:/r me/wdt.

h1yo/d wl_/kwos h2o/wim gwr_h3e:/ti poh2ime:/n-kwe h1i/h2m_ ne h2le/kse:ti so/ h3re:g'e/y de/k'm_ po/rk'ons kwe/yetu. h1yo/d-kwe h1i/h2m_ h2le/kse:ti nu kwid me: kwe/yet. h1yo/d h3e/ro: po/rk'om h1e/peti nu kwid me: kwe/yet.

h1yo/s h1e/k'wom ne/kwti kle/pe:ti nu so/ bho:/r septm_/ h2o/wins de/deh3tu. h1yo/d-kwe h1i/m h2epo/ h2e/g'e:ti nu h3re:g'e/y k'm_to/m gwo:/s de/wktu. h1yo/d towdo/s bho/rm_ h2use/si wide:/ti nu h1i/m h1e/ptu h1i/m-kwe h3re/g'm_ de/wktu. h1yo/d bho:/r bhe/wgeti nu kwis h1i/m me: more/yet. k'wo:/ bhre/s lo/yseh2m h2isk'e/tu.

h1yo/s wo/yk'osyo dhe/yg'hos Hrune/peti nu so/ h1i/d h2ew de/metu. h1yo/d sni/gwhs h1i/d gle/wbhe:ti nu kwis me: kwe/yet. h1yo/d g'hwe:/r po/tn_yeh2s g'ho/rtom de/re:ti nu h1i/m de/metu. dhe/yg'hos ste/h2eti h1le/wdhero:s-kwe h1i/d pe/h2sonti.

h1yo/d k'wo:/ po/rk'om g'ho/rtoy more/ye:ti nu h1e/syo po/tnih2 suHno/wey h2o/wim h1a/ytu. h1yo/d wl_/kwom more/ye:ti nu kwid me: de/deh3t. h1yo/d k'wo:/ h2eh3me/h2m h2e/wim h1e/deti nu h1i/h2m_ k'e/letu.

swesre/s kwis h2e/g'rom me: h2e/rh3yet. h1yo/d h2ne:/r h1i/m h2e/rh3ye:ti nu dwo/yh1 to/syo yugo/yh1 h1e/ptu. h1yo/d to/syo gwo/wes h2e/g'rom we/yk'onti h1e/donti-kwe so/ h1e/syeh2ey h2wl_/h1neh2m h1ope/yetu.

h1yo/s h1le/wdherosyo gwowsth2o/m h1e/wse:ti nu h1i/m h2ew de/metu de/k'm_-kwe h2o/wins de/wktu. h1yo/d h2e/nh1mos pe/h2wr_ gwowsth2o/m bhe/re:ti nu kwid me: h1a/yt. h1le/wdheros bho/rm_ swo/h1 k'une/h1 h2isk'e/tu.

gho/stim kwis me: more/yet. h1e/y deywo/ybhos lewbho/s h1e/stu. h1e/y se/dtu po/tnih2-kwe h1i/m wose/yetu. h1yo/s h1i/m more/ye:ti nu h3re:/g's to/m h1e/g'hs h1e/lh2tu.

h1yo/s bhoydhe/h2m Hrune/peti nu to/syo h1no/mn_ g'e/nh1esos ne/k'tu to/smey-kwe kwis pe/h2wr_ wo/dr_-kwe me: h1ope/yet. h1yo/s ne dhre/wgheti bhoydhe/h2m-kwe ne Hrune/peti to/syo h1no/mn_ k'luto/m h1e/stu. dye:/ws to/smey h1we/su k'le/wos dh3ye/h1t.

h1yo/d h2ne:/r swe/sorm_ k'o/ymom we/dhe:ti nu h1e/syeh2s me/h2trey we/dhmn_ de/h3tu. h1i/h2 h1le/wdhereh2 h1e/stu. h1yo/d h1i/d ne h1a/yeti nu me/h2te:r swe/sorm_ h2ew we/dhetu.

h1yo/s h3re:g'e/y dhro/wghom we/rh1eti nu to/syo we/kwos h2ew me: k'le/wseto wo/yk'o:d-kwe h1e/g'hs h1e/ytu. h1yo/s dhro/wghom k'le/weti h3re:g'e/y-kwe h1i/m we/rh1eti misdho/s to/smey h1e/stu. h1le/wdherosyo we/kwos diwe/y lewbho/m.

h1yo/d gwe/wo:m ste/h2dhh1om h1le/wdherosyo h2e/g'rom we/yk'eti h1e/deti-kwe. poh2ime:/n h1e/smey dwo/yh1 h2o/wih1 we/dhetu. h1yo/d ste/h2dhh1om h1e/smo:d h1e/g'hs h2e/g'e:ti nu kwid me: h1a/yt. ste/h2dhh1om bhr_h1we/ni pi/ph3etu.

mo/rtos bhr_h1we/ns wo/dr_ pi/ph3etu. h1yo/s bhr_h1we/ni h3me/yg'he:ti nu h2ne/res to/m h1e/smo:d h2epo/ h1l_h2e/ntu. h1yo/s swo/m ste/h2dhh1om bhre/h1wr_ h2e/g'e:ti nu so/ g'e/nh1esey h2o/wim de/wktu. bhre/h1wr_ kwis me: k'e/let.

h1yo/d h2ne:/r towdo/syo kwe/kwlom Hrune/peti nu h1e/smey yugo/m sh2imo/nm_-kwe h1ope/yetu. h1yo/d kwe/kwlos Hrune/petor nu towdo/s h1i/m h2ew de/metu h1e/smey-kwe kwis me: kwe/yet.

h1yo/s h2e/wim tn_tlo:/d sle/h2gweti so/ bho:/r. h1yo/d h3e/ronm_ sle/h2gweti nu h3re:g'e/y h1e/k'wom de/h3tu. h1yo/s h2e/wim tn_tlo:/d h2po/ le/h1eti nu tn_tlo/m te:/tk'tu. h3e/ro: diwe/y lewbho/s.

nu so/h2wl_ h1yo/d mo/rtons dedo/rk'e we/kwos moy wo/ydth2e. nu moy k'o/d bhoydhe/h2. nu h2e/wim ne ste/h2yom h2wl_/h1neh2m-kwe ne le/mbhm_. h1yo/d te dhrughyo:/h2 pe/h2wr_ me h1e/wsoyh1t dhe/g'ho:m-kwe me gwr_h3o/yh1t. h1yo/d-kwe me: dhrughyo:/h2 h1we/su moy k'le/wos h1a/yih1s. nu toy me/g'h2 h3o/kw h2r_g'ro/m-kwe toy h1no/mn_.

ph2ter dye/w h1use/wo:m de/h3tor me/h2te:r-kwe dhe/g'ho:m. nu nos k'luto/m. nu k'a:/m bhoydhe/h2m dhh1we/. nu h1yo/s h1i/h2m_ Hrune/peti wl_/kwo:s to/m gwr_h3o/yh1ent. nu se/h2 bhoydhe/h2 sth2ye/h1t. nu-kwe h1yo/s ne dhre/wgheti so/ gwi/h3woyh1t. nu ta:/m bhoydhe/h2m memnih1we/. nu noh3 so/h2wl_ h2e/wso:s-kwe dedr_k'ih1ta:/m.

nu me n_mr_to/m pe/h2wr_ spe/k'ye. nu swo/syo bhre/h2tr_s h1e/k'wom ne kle/pom. nu h1i/m g'ho/rto:d h1e/g'hs ne h1e/lh2m_. h1yo/d h1i/m kle/pom h1e/wsoyh1s me. nu moy suHnu/s k'e:/r g'n_ne/h3ti. nu tu/H HeHk'u/ lewko/m-kwe.

nu wo/dr_ h1eg'o/h2 poh2ime:/n h3re:g'e/s po/rk'ons ne h1e:/dm_. nu wl_/kwos h1i/ns ne/kwti gwr_h3e/t. nu h1i/m kwe/k'm_. h1yo/d h1i/ns h1e:/dm_ tr_syo/yh1m_. nu me me: h1e/ys. nu moy so/ wl_/kwos ste/h2dhh1om me: gwm_ye/h1t. nu h2e/nh1mos g'hwe:/rm_ me/d h2po/ bhe/royh1t.

nu su/Hnew k'a:/m bhoydhe/h2m dhh1dhi/ wr_h1dhi/-kwe. nu wo/yk'osyo gwo:/s ne h2e/g'om. nu deywo/ns me: dhre/wghs. h1yo/d dhrughye:/si to/y te deywo:/s me: h2wisih1e/nt. nu moy k'e:/r me/wdeti. nu moy tu/H lewbho/s.

nu bhre/h2terh1e bhoydhe/h2m dhh1ta:/m. nu ste/h2dhh1om k'o/m h1e/lh2owos. h1yo/d h1o/ynos bhe/wgeti h1e/nos h1i/m h1pye/h1t. nu nos me/h2te:r k'le/wos dedr_k'ye/h1t. nu nos ste/h2dhh1om h2uksye/h1t. nu nos h1no/mn_ g'e/nh1esi me/g'h2 h1sye/h1t.

h3re/g' h1use/wo:m de/h3tor. nu te se/kwo:h2er. nu me: bhe/wgoh2. h1yo/d bhe/wgoh2 h1no/mn_ moy k'luto/m me: h1sye/h1t. h1yo/d me/roh2 k'le/wos moy n_/dhgwhitom h1sye/h1t. nu toy h1no/mn_ pe/h2soh2. nu toy k'le/wos me/g'h2.

nu k'o/syo wo/yk'osyo h2ne/res h1e/nosyo wo/yk'osyo h2nr_bho/s bhoydhe/h2m dhh1e/nt. nu wos gwo:/s h2epo/ me: h2e/g'o:mes. nu nos deywo:/s we/kwos h2wise/ntu. nu h1yo/s k'a:/m bhoydhe/h2m Hrune/peti to/syo ste/h2dhh1om n_k'ye/h1t. nu nos suHne/wes gwi/h3woyh1ent. nu dwo/h1 wo/yk'oh1 k'e/ywoh1 h1sta:/m. nu nos dye:/ws upe/r h2wisye/h1t.

nu toy wi/Hre h2wl_/h1neh2m h2le/ksom. nu ghoste/yey h1i/h2m_ ne h1ope/yom. nu moy k'e:/r h1we/su. h1yo/d dhrughyo:/h2 dhe/g'ho:m me gwr_h3o/yh1t. nu toy h1eg'o/h2 lewbho/s.

nu moy gho/stey tu/H lewbho/s. nu toy pe/h2wr_ wo/dr_-kwe de/h3oh2. nu te swo/y k'o/ymoy h2le/kso:h2. nu te h2epo/ me: h1e/lh2oh2. nu me h2epo/ me: h2e/g'es. nu nos dye:/ws h2le/ksoyh1t. nu gwi/h3woyh1s.

nu wo/dr_ h1e/ymi. nu moy de/ywo:s k'e:/r dedr_k'e/. nu moy we/kwos ne dhro/wghos. h1yo/d dhrughyo:/h2 h1o/gwhis me gwr_h3o/yh1t. h1yo/d me: dhrughyo:/h2 to/d me wo/dr_ h1e/g'hs bhe/royh1t. nu ne dedwo/yh2e.

nu k'o/d dhe/yg'hos h2e/g'roy de/mome. nu h1yo/s h1i/d Hrune/peti swo:/s gwo/wes bhugih1e/nt. nu to/d dhe/yg'hos sth2ye/h1t. nu so/h2wl_ h1i/d dedr_k'ye/h1t. nu g'hwe:/r h1i/d me: de/royh1t. nu to/d dhe/yg'hos albho/m me/g'h2-kwe h1sye/h1t.

nu h3re/g' h1eg'o/h2 ne bho:/r. nu h1e/k'wons ne le/mbhm_. nu h2e/g'roy h1e/sm_. nu h1e/yes n_k'e/nt. nu moy k'wo:/ to/m bho/rm_ kwe/k't. h1yo/d dhrughyo:/h2 wl_/kwos moy k'e:/r h1e/dih1t.

nu gwe/mt poh2ime:/n h3re/g'm_ h2e/g't-kwe. nu moy po/rk'o:s ne/kwti n_k'e/nt. nu moy bhre/h2te:r h1i/ns ste/h2yet. nu h1e/nos we/wket. nu h1i/ns ne ste/h2yom. nu wl_/kwos h1i/ns gwr_h3e/t. nu so/ h3re:/g's se/kwt. kwi/s to/m wl_/kwom wide/t? nu kwis h1i/m ne wide/t. nu bhre/h2ter h1e/smey misdho/m kwe/ye.

nu h1ludhe/ta:m dwo/h1 suHnu/h1 swo/m ph2te/rm_. nu h1o/ynos mle/wHt. nu moy k'o/s h2e/g'ros h1e/sti. nu h1e/nos h2e/g't. nu to/syo we/kwos dhro/wghos. nu moy h1i/m h1a/ys. nu so/ ph2te:/r we/wket. kwi/s h1i/m h2e/rh3yet? nu h1o/ynos se/kwt. nu h1i/m h2e/rh3yom. nu so/ ph2te:/r bhe/h2t. nu h1yo/s h1i/m h2e/rh3yet so/ h2e/g'ros to/smey h1e/stu. nu h1e/nos h2po/ gwe/h2t.

nu towdo/s bho/rm_ h2use/si h1e/pt h1i/m-kwe wiHro/m de/wkt. nu we/rh1yet. nu moy k'o/s tn_tlo/m ste/h2yet. nu h1i/m ne/kwti wido/m. nu so/ wiHro/s to/smey bhre/y mle/wHt. to/m tn_tlo/m ste/h2yes? nu so/ bho:/r h2e/g't. nu h1i/m ste/h2yom. nu bhe/h2t. nu h1e/smey sh2imo/nm_ dedh3dhi/ h1e/g'hs-kwe h1idhi/.

nu g'hwe:/r tri/ns po/rk'ons ne/kwti gwr_h3e/t. nu poh2ime:/n wiHro/m gwe/mt dedwo/ye-kwe. nu se/kwt. nu moy to/ns po/rk'ons k'une/h1 h2le/ksom. nu moy k'wo:/ h1i/m wide/t. nu so/ wiHro/s bhe/h2t. nu so/ k'wo:/ ne mle/wHti. nu k'o/s we/rh1yet. nu h1eg'o/h2 ne bho:/r. nu mle/wHt. nu tu/H ne bho:/r. nu h1i/ns ne h2le/kses. nu deywo/ybhos h1o/ynom h1e/k'wom h1ope/ye. nu k'o/s se/dt.

nu dhe/h1t mo/rtos bhoydhe/h2m h3re:/g'i. nu moy bhre/h2tr_s h2wl_/h1neh2m ne le/mbhm_. nu so/ bhre/h2te:r h2e/g't. nu dhre/wghs. nu te ne/kwti wido/m. nu toy se/h2 h2wl_/h1neh2 k'o/ymoy h1e/sti. nu so/ mo/rtos we/wket. nu moy deywo:/s k'e:/r dedr_k'e:/r. nu so/ h3re:/g's se/kwt. nu h1e/yes h1i/d dedr_k'e:/r. nu dhre/wghs. nu h1e/smey misdho/m kwe/ye. nu so/ to/m misdho/m de/deh3t to/syo-kwe h1no/mn_ ne k'luto/m.

nu h1ludhe/t towdo/s swesre/s ph2te/rm_ bhe/h2t-kwe. nu we/dhmn_ de/h3m_. nu moy toy swe/sorm_ h1a/ydhi. nu so/ ph2te:/r we/rh1yet. nu moy to/d we/dhmn_ ne de/deh3s. nu me/h2te:r mle/wHt. nu de/k'm_ h2o/wins g'ho/rtoy wido/m. nu so/ ph2te:/r we/rh1t. nu h1i/h2es we/dhmn_. nu toy h1i/h2m_ k'o/ymom we/dhe. nu me/h2tr_s k'e:/r me/wdt.

nu h1e/wseto h1le/wdherosyo gwowsth2o/s ne/kwti. nu h1e/y towdo:/y we/wket. nu toy moy pe/h2wr_ gwowsth2o/m h1e/wset. nu so/ towdo/s se/kwt. nu h1i/m ne h1e/wsom. nu h2e/nh1mos h1i/d to/m gwowsth2o/m bhe/ret. nu po/tnih2 bhe/h2t. nu kwid me: h1ope/yet. nu h1i/m h2ew k'o/m de/mo:mes. nu so/ towdo/s h1le/wdheros-kwe to/m gwowsth2o/m h2ew de/meta:m.

nu h2ne:/r poh2ime/nm_ h3re/g'm_ we/dhet we/rh1yet-kwe. nu moy h2wl_/h1neh2m le/mbht misdho/m-kwe ne de/h3t. nu so/ poh2ime:/n mle/wHt. nu to/m misdho/m kwe/yom. nu moy swe/so:r h1i/m wide/t. nu swe/sor wr_h1dhi/. nu h1i/h2 h2e/g't. nu h1e/smey to/m misdho/m h2e/n h1a/yt. nu so/ h3re:/g's we/wket. nu to/syo we/kwos dhro/wghos. nu so/ h1e/smey h1e/k'wom de/deh3tu. nu swesre/s h1no/mn_ k'luto/m.

nu gho/stis mo/rtosyo k'o/ymom ne/kwti gwe/mt. nu so/ mo/rtos h1i/m h2epo/ h1e/lh2t. nu k'o/s h3re:g'e/y se/kwt. nu moy pe/h2wr_ wo/dr_-kwe ne h1ope/yet. nu me h2epo/ h2e/g'et. nu so/ mo/rtos bhe/h2t. nu h1i/m ne g'ne/h3m_. nu so/ h3re:/g's we/rh1yet. nu so/ gho/stis deywo/ybhos lewbho/s. nu h1e/smey to/d pe/h2wr_ to/d-kwe wo/dr_ dh3dhi/ h1e/y-kwe toy k'o/ymoy tk'e/ytu. nu h1e/y se/dt po/tnih2-kwe h1i/m wose/yet.

nu suHnu/s gwe/wo:m lo/yseh2m se/kweto wiHro/syo-kwe k'o/ymom h1ludhe/t. nu h1i/ns g'ho/rtoy kwe/k't. nu h3re/g'm_ gwe/mt mle/wHt-kwe. nu moy gwo/wes to/syo g'ho/rtoy h1se/nti. nu se/h2 lo/yseh2 to/m h1e/yti. nu so/ h3re:/g's to/smey wiHro:/y we/rh1t. h1e/yes toy g'ho/rtom kwi/h1 gwme/nt? nu so/ wiHro/s we/wket. nu ne g'n_ne/h3mi. nu h2e/g't. nu tu/H bho:/r. nu to/smey suHno/wey h1i/ns h2ew dukdhi/. nu so/ suHnu/s h1i/ns to/smo:d g'ho/rto:d h1e/g'hs h2e/g'et.

nu gho/stis wo/yk'oy me/rt. nu to/syo bhre/h2te:r ph2te/rm_ gwe/mt bhe/h2t-kwe. nu moy k'o/syo wo/yk'osyo h2ne/res bhre/h2term_ more/yont. nu h1e/yes we/rh1yont. nu g'hwe:/r h1i/m h1e:/dt. nu we/wket. nu h1e/yes bhoydhe/h2m dhh1e/ntu. nu h1yo/s h1i/h2m_ ne dhe/h1eti h1e/yes to/m h1e/g'hs h1l_h2e/ntu. nu h1e/yes ta:/m bhoydhe/h2m dhh1e/nt. nu dye:/ws ta:/m bhoydhe/h2m dedo/rk'e.

nu gwm_ta:/m dwo/h1 mo/rtoh1 h3re/g'm_. nu h1o/ynos h2e/g't. nu moy h1e/k'wos h1e/sti. nu h1e/nos we/wket. nu dhrughye/ti. nu moy so/ h1e/k'wos h1e/sti. nu so/ h3re:/g's se/kwt. nu h2e/g'roy sth2to/m HrudHto/m-kwe. nu h1i/m le/h1oh2. nu h1e/y h1e/nom gwe/h2t. nu h1e/smey se/kwt. nu toy h1e/y h1e/sti. nu h1o/ynos h1i/m h2epo/ de/wkt.

nu gho/stis me/h2trey we/rh1yet. nu moy poh2imne/s po/tnih2 h2wl_/h1neh2m kle/pet. nu se/h2 me/h2te:r mle/wHt. kwi/d po/tni we/rh1yesi? nu h1i/h2 h2e/g't. nu moy se/h2 h2wl_/h1neh2 h1e/sti. nu moy h2o/wis h1i/h2m_ de/deh3t. nu bhe/h2t. nu we/kwesh2 k'le:/m. nu gho/stey h1e/syeh2ey po/rk'om h1ope/ye. nu h1e/syeh2s k'e:/r me/wdt.

kwr_/mey h3sth1e/s h1e/g'hs gwh2dhi/ h1sh2e/ns-kwe h1e/g'hs gwh2dhi/. h1e/sh2r_ me: gwr_h3e/s h1e/lk'os me: srobhe/yes. nu kwr_/mis mr_to/s h1e/lk'os-kwe lelo/ykwe. h1i/d Hwe/rdhtu k'e/ywom-kwe h1e/sh2r_ h3sth1i/ h1e/stu. deywo/s te h2le/ksoyh1t h2e/wso:s-kwe te ph2sye/h1t.

deywo/s h1e/g'hs gwe/h2t h1e/k'wosyo h3e/sth1 Hrumpto/. h1e/y k'e/ywom we/kwos tri/s we/wket. nu dhe/g'hom me/h2ter h3e/sth1 h3sth1e/h1 k'o/m yungdhi/. lewbho/m kre/wh2s h1sh2e/nh1 yungdhi/. h3e/sth1 Hwe/rdht h1e/sh2r_-kwe yungto/. k'o/syo wiHro/syo k'e/ywom h3e/sth1 Hwr_dhye/h1t.

kre/wh2s sth2e/h1ye h1e/g'hs-kwe me: le/yts. sh2imo/nm_ h1e/lk'esi pe/ri loghe/yoh2. sh2we/ns h3o/kw h1i/d spe/k'yoyh1t so/h2wl_ kre/wh2s h2sowse/yoyh1t. nu dhwo:/r Hwr_to/ h1e/sh2r_ h1n_do/m se/dyeti. nu te/bhye we/kwos tri/s we/wkom.

h1yo/d lewbho/y k'e/rdi dhe/gwheti to/d studye/h1t. wo/dr_ h2ne/rm_ h1e/syo-kwe h3e/sth1h2 gwm_dhi/. nu le/ghtu nu-kwe swidye/tu. lewbho/m pe/h2wr_ h2epo/ litye/h1t. nu no/kwts h1i/d h2epo/ bhe/re:ti. nu h2e/wso:s le/wkt gwi/h3weti-kwe.

se/rpont He/Hk'ew h2e/ngwhey lo/yseh2i l_ghdhi/. nu h2o/wins poh2ime/nm_-kwe me: h1r_sk'e/s. nu k'wo:/ bhe/wdheti nu twe/ kwe/k't. h2epo/ se/rpe nu bhudhme/nm_ ni/ h1idhi/. wih1ro/s kwis g'hwe:/r-kwe me: n_k'ye/h1t.

wl_/kwe poh2imne/s lewbha:/s h2o/wins me: h1e/ps. nu poh2ime:/n k'wo:/-kwe h1i/h2n_s h2le/ksetes. nu-kwe g'hwe:/rn_s h2epo/ h1idhi/. nu pe/rkwune h2o/wins ph2sdhi/. wl_/kwos lewbhe/h2s dhure/s h2e/g'ro:d-kwe bhugye/h1t. nu k'wo:/ bhe/wdheti wl_/kwos-kwe me: h1r_ye/h1t.

nu no/kwts gwe/mt wik'po/tis-kwe le/ght. bho/r wik'po/teys lewbhe/h2m dhwo/rm_ me: gwm_ye/s. te/we h3o/kwih1 me: wido/yh1ta:m. nu bho/r te/we do/ws k'r_mh2ye/h1t. k'wo:/ bhe/wdheti deywo/s-kwe k'o/ymom pe/h2sti. h1yo/s gwo:/s ste/h2yeti so/ mr_ye/h1t.

nu su/Hnew kwse/pi me: tre/ses. te/we me/h2te:r h2e/nti h1e/sti. h1we/sus deywo/s h2e/nti h1e/sti. twe/ h2le/kseti h1we/sus-kwe k'wo:/ bhe/wdheti. h1we/su su/pniyom twe/ h1r_sk'o/yh1t. nu-kwe kwse/ps bhe/wgt nu-kwe h2ew h2we/st.

dye/w ph2ter dhoHne/h2i h2wr_sye/h1t. dhe/g'hom me/h2ter h1i/h2m_ bhe/re. nu gwo:/s yugte/ lo/yseh2m-kwe h2e/rh3yo:mes. nu dhoHne/h2 lo/yseh2i h3pusye/h1t. h1we/su so/h2wl_ h1i/h2m_ pe/kwoyh1t. h2o/weyes sph1re/h2es h1sih1e/nt. h3e/pos r_h1dhye/h1t yugo/m-kwe me: Hrumpih1to/.

nu gwo/wes swo/m k'o/ymom gwm_te/. poh2ime:/n usme/ we/dheti. nu wih1ro/s e/nu se/kwetor. h1regwesye/ti me: h3slidhte/ me: kwis n_k'ye/h1t. nu po/tnih2 gwo:/s dhwo/ri h2me/lg'ti. nu lewko/s h2e/ydhos se/desi dhe/gwheti.

nu dye/w ph2ter h2wr_sye/h1t h2wr_sye/h1t. nu pe/rkwune pe/rkwum stunddhi/. nu wo/dr_ diwe/s ni/ h1ludhe/t. nu dhe/g'ho:m tr_sye/ti pe/h3tu ple/h1tu-kwe. nu dhoHne/h2 h2we/kseti k'o/d do/h3r_ me/g'h2.

polh1u/ pe/h2wr_ wo/rmons h2wl_/h1neh2i h1e/wse. kwr_/mins h1e/wse h2o:wye/h2 h1e/wse. nu h2wl_/h1neh2m me: h1e/wses se/dos me: h1e/wses. h2e/wis h2wl_/h1na lewke/h2 bhuHdhi/. nu-kwe wih1ro/s h1i/h2m_ m_dhu/h1 h2line/Hti. nu wo/rmo:s h1e/smi me/ronti.

nu sh2imo/nm_ te/we k'e/rdi pe/ri loghe/yoh2. te/we k'e:/r h1me/ pro/ h1r_sk'e/tu. te/we me/nos h1me/ se/kwoyh1to. nu-kwe pe/rkwus h1e/syo wr_h2de/h1 stesto/h2e. nu-kwe te/we we/nh1os sh2imne/h1 stesto/h2tu. sh2imo:/ kwse/pi me: Hrumpih1to/.

nu pe/rkwunos wih1ro:/m k'e/rdh2 dedo/rk'e. nu h1yo/s gho/stim dhrughye/ti nu so/ n_k'ye/h1t. bhoydhe/h2 sth2e/h1yetu h1i/h2 me/g'h2ih2. nu h1yo/s bhoydhe/h2m pe/h2sti nu so/ gwi/h3we:ti. deywo:/s bhoydhe/h2s we/kwos k'luwe/nt. gho/stim se/dos h1n_do/ we/dhe.

po/tnih2 me:/dontm_ h2ne/rm_ gwe/h2t se/kwt-kwe. me/ne wiHro/s le/ghyeti h1e/lk'os-kwe h1e/syo de/wsi h1rudhe/h1ti. we/wket. kwi/d h1e:/dt? kwi/d pi/ph3et? h1i/h2 h2e/g't. ne kwid h1e:/dt wo/dr_ pi/ph3et. h1i/d m_dhu/h1 h2linHdhi/ h1i/d-kwe h2wl_/h1neh2h1 k'e/le. h1yo/d so/h2wl_ le/wketi h1i/d h2sowse/yetor.

wl_/kwos h2o/wins gwe/mt. poh2ime:/n sth2e/h1yet h1i/m-kwe h2epo/ h1e/lh2t g'hwe:/r h1i/m te/rh3t. h1e/syo do/ws kruh2se/h1 h1rudhe/h1t. h1e/syo po/tnih2 h1i/m wide/t Hre/wdHt-kwe. h2wl_/h1neh2m h1e/pt do/ws k'e/let h1i/d-kwe m_dhu/h1 h2line/Ht. ti/srn_s no/kwtn_s k'o/ymoy le/ghyet. nu ste/h2t. g'hwe:/r h2ew ne gwe/mt.

h1yo/d kwesyo h3e/sth1 Hrune/petor do/ws h3rog'e/ye. h1i/d h2wl_/h1neh2h1 k'e/le sh2imo/nm_-kwe pe/ri loghe/ye. h1e/smey me/dhu h1ope/ye pi/ph3etu-kwe. me: sth2e/h1yet le/ghyetu. h1yo/d h1ne/wn_ no/kwtes le/ytonti ste/h2tu. h1yo/d h3e/sth1 ne Hwe/rdheti me: bhe/yh2s. to/m h2isk'e/ h1yo/s wo/yde.

suHno/ws k'e:/r dhe/gwhet. h1e/syo me/h2te:r h1e/pi se/dyet wo/dr_-kwe bhe/ret. h2e/g't. dye/w h1me/ k'ludhi/. me/ne suHnu/m me:/de. h1e/smey we/yHm_ dh3dhi/. nu swidye/t pe/h2wr_-kwe h1e/smo:d ne/k't. h1i/h2m_ h2use/si wide/t mle/wHt-kwe. me/h2ter tr_syo/h2. h1e/smey me/dhu de/h3t.

h2e/wsos le/wka de/ywi n_sme/ k'ludhi/ h1ye/h2 no/kwtm_ ske/lHs h1ye/h2 so/h2wl_ we/dhesi. k'o/syo wiHro/syo h3kwbho/s bhe/h2os dh3dhi/ h1e/syo-kwe h1e/lk'os me:/de. sh2we/ns kwe/kwlos h2e/ng'hos h2epo/ bhe/royh1t. kre/wh2s h2sowse/ye bhe/wdhtu-kwe. gwi/h3woyh1t te/we-kwe bhe/h2os h2ew kwk'ye/h1t.

h1e/k'wos h2e/g'roy k'e/yo ne-kwe h1e:/dt. wiHro/s h1i/m wide/t h3e/sth1 Hrumpto/. h3e/sth1 h3rog'e/yet h1i/d-kwe k'e/let. dhoHne/h2m bhe/ret h1e/smey-kwe h1i/h2m_ de/deh3t. nu h1e/k'wos sth2e/h1yet h1e:/dt-kwe.

tr_mo/nts gwo:/ws dhwo/ri sth2e/h1yet. kwr_/meyes h1e/syo h1e/lk'esi h1se/nt kre/wh2s-kwe gwr_h3o/nt. po/tnih2 wo/dr_ gwhr_ne/wt h1i/d-kwe ye/set. poh2ime:/n kwr_/mins h1e/pt h1i/ns-kwe pe/h2wr_ Hye/h1kt. nu h1i/d m_dhu/h1 h2line/Ht. nu gwo:/ws h1e:/dt ne-kwe dedwo/ye.

h3re:/g's k'e/yo me:/donts-kwe h2ne:/r h1e/pi se/dyet. gwi/h3wo:h2? h3re/g' gwi/h3we:si h1yo/d le/ghye:si pi/ph3e:si-kwe. kwi/d te/bhye de/h3oh2? me: moy kwid de/h3s. diwe/y do/h3r_ h1a/ydhi. nu h3re:/g's h1e/smey do/h3r_ h1a/yt bhe/wdht-kwe.

h1yo/d wiHro/s h2ne/rm_ te/rh3eti h1i/m me:/detu h1e/smey-kwe misdho/m de/h3tu. h1yo/d h1e/y me/reti k'm_to/m de/h3o:r h1a/ytu. h1yo/d kwis h2ne/rm_ me:/de:ti so/ h1e/smey misdho/m de/deh3tu.

ph2te:/r swo:/y suHno/wey we/wket. gho/stim spe/k'ye. h1e/syo we/kwesh2 k'le/wse. h1i/m me: line/kws me:-kwe k'e/mh2s. h1we/su k'le/wos to/m h1r_sk'e/ti h1yo/s h1e/pi se/dyeti. pe/h2wr_ kwr_/mim h1e/wseti sh2imo:/ do/ws yune/gti. ph2tre/s we/kwesh2 k'le/wt.

h2we/st h2e/g'rom gwa:/m pr_kwe/ws-kwe wre/h2dm_ h2isk'o/m. h1i/h2m_ wido/m h1i/h2m_-kwe u/d de/rom. h1i/h2m_ we/dni dhe/dheh1m_ h1i/h2m_-kwe gwhr_ne:/m. ghoste/yey h1i/h2m_ h1a:/m pe/h3t-kwe. dhe/g'ho:m me/h2te:r me/g'hye wre/h2dm_ h1a/yt h1i/m-kwe spe/rt.

h1e/k'wo:s h2e/g'roy k'e/yro ne-kwe stesth2e:/r. po/tnih2 h1e/pi se/dyet pe/h2wr_-kwe dhe/gwhet. nu h2we/st. h1i/ns spe/k'yet h2e/g't-kwe. sth2te/ me:-kwe bhih2te/. nu sth2e/nt pro/-kwe gwh2e/nt.

bhre/h2te:r mr_ye/to h1e/syo-kwe swe/so:r h1e/pi se/dyet. h2e/ng'hos h1i/h2m_ gwr_h3e/t. h2e/g't. de/ywe me/ne bhre/h2term_ me: h1e/ps. gwi/h3woyh1t. no/kwts le/ytt. bhe/h2os h2use/si wide/t swesre/s-kwe k'e:/r me/wdt.

nu su/Hnew swo:/d k'o/ymo:d dhg'hye/s le/yts. ne h1ludhe/s te/we ph2te:/r twe/ h2isk'e/ti. nu g'hoyso/s twe/ h2e/g'roy h3wa/th2t. te/we h1e/sh2r_ dhe/g'ho:m h1e/yti. nu te/we h3e/sth1h2 h2e/n k'e/yor. kwi/s te/we Hro/th2om h2e/g'e:ti? nu-kwe k'luto/m te/we h1no/mn_ me: mr_ye/h1t. nu twe/ kwse/pi tri/s Hre/wdHmi.

nu h2e/k'ruh2 me/ne h3kwsu/ h1e/sti. nu me/ne suHnu/s lelo/ykwe. wl_/kwos h1i/m ne h1e/pt g'hoyso/s h1i/m ne te/rh3t. nu kwis h1i/m sle/h2gwt h1i/m-kwe we/ydsoh2. nu-kwe h2e/ng'hos me/ne gudo/m gwr_h3e/ti. kwi/s me/g'hye h1i/m h1ope/ye:ti?

h2we/st bhre/h2ter-kwe ne gwm_sk'e/si. h2e/g'rom spe/k'yoh2 twe/ ne kwe/k'm_. te/we h1e/k'wo:s h1ludho/nt tu/H-kwe ne h1ludhe/s. nu-kwe kwi/s me/g'hye bhe/h2eti? nu me/ne h3kwo:/m bhe/h2os h1e/s twe/ memo/nh2e.

me/ne wiHro/s pr_kwe/wi Hn_dhi/ k'e/yor. nu kwis h1e/syo se/desi ne si/sdeti. h2e/ng'hesos we/strom we/sh2er nu kwi/s yugo/m ye/wgeti? nu-kwe kwi/s me/ne lo/yseh2m h2e/rh3ye:ti? nu me/ne se/dos h2ew wik'dhi/. mr_to:/s-kwe-ge ne k'l_nwe/nti. h1we/su te/we k'le/wos h2uksye/h1t.

h3re:/g's me/rt h1e/syo k'le/wos me/g'h2. nu h1e/syo k'le/wos n_/dhgwhitom h1e/seti. nu-kwe h1e/syo we/yHs me/g'h2ih2 h1e/st. nu wik'po/teyes stesth2e:/r HrudHe/nti-kwe. Hro/th2os stesto/h2e h2eg'o/s-kwe ne h1e/sti. nu-kwe k'luto/m h1e/syo h1no/mn_ me/g'h2 h1sye/h1t.

nu me/ne k'wo:/ mr_to/s me/ne k'e/ywos h1e/st. nu-kwe h1me/ lo/yseh2i e/nu se/kweto. h1yo/d-kwe wl_/kwos h1e/rt nu k'e/ywos h1i/m u/d g'he/yt. nu h1e/syo h3o/kwih1 lewko/yh1 h1sta:/m. nu Hn_dhi/ h2e/n le/ghyeti k'e/ywe l_ghdhi/. nu-kwe kwi/s kwse/pi bhe/wdhe:ti?

gho/steyes h1re/nt h2e/ydhos-kwe k'o/ymom gwr_h3e/t. nu-kwe Hrowpo/m h1e/g'hs bhore/yont. nu-kwe me/ne bhre/h2teres ku/dhe h1se/nti? nu-kwe me/ne swe/sores ku/dhe h1se/nti? nu-kwe kwi/s k'o/ymom de/me:ti? nu pe/rkwune k'o/d dedo/rk'th2e?

nu bhe/h2os se/ngwt h2ew-kwe h1ludhe/t. nu dhoHne/h2 ni/ se/ngwt nu-kwe h2we/kst. h1yo/d nos bhe/h2os se/ngweti h2ew ne gwm_sk'o/mes. g'n_h1yo/mesdhh2 mr_yo/mesdhh2. k'le/wos ne me/rt n_/dhgwhitom k'le/wos h1e/sti.

nu g'hoyso/s h1me/ ghbhe/li te/rh3t. nu me/ne h1e/sh2r_ h1e/g'hs h1e/yti. ni/ k'e/yh2er h1regwesye/ti-kwe. nu kwr_/meyes me/ne h3e/sth1h2 gwr_h3o:/nti. nu me/ne swesre/y mluHdhi/ nu g'hoyso/h1 se/ngwt. nu me: Hre/wdHs me/ne h1no/mn_ k'luto/m h1e/seti.

nu ne h2we/rst nu lo/yseh2 te/rst dhoHne/h2 ne bhwe/h2t. g'hwe:/res tr_syo/nti tr_syo/mes. nu upo/ spe/k'yomes n_sme/ me: line/kws. nu pe/rkwune ne h2we/rseti? k'm_h2me/ we/yHs-kwe nos lelo/ykwe.

nu mr_to/s me/ne swo/s ph2te:/r. nu n_k'e/wih2 me/ne swe/so:r me/ne se/dos ste/wdt. nu kwi/s h1me/ pe/h2seti kwi/s h1me/ we/dhe:ti? nu-kwe kwi/s h1me/ wose/ye:ti? nu pro/ h1e/yoh2 nu-kwe Hre/wdHoh2.

nu dhe/g'hom me/ne bhre/h2term_ h1a/yso h1i/m h2le/kse. nu-kwe bhre/h2ter pr_kwe/wi Hn_dhi/ l_ghdhi/. pr_kwe/ws wre/h2des te/we h3e/sth1h2 te/rdonti. nu pe/ruti h2we/ks nu Hn_dhi/ le/ghyesi. nu-kwe pe/rkwunos te/we h1no/mn_ widye/h1t. nu te/we h1no/mn_ me: n_k'ye/h1t.

wi/h1re HeHk'e/wes h1e/k'wo:s dhwo/ri stesth2e:/r. nu tr_me/nti h2eg'o/s-kwe se/ngwt. te/we g'hoyso/s se/ngwt te/we Hro/th2os Hrumpto/. kwi/s h1i/ns ye/wgeti kwi/s Hro/th2om h2e/g'e:ti? nu wi/h1re l_ghdhi/ te/we h1no/mn_ me/g'h2.

ph2te:/r moy ku/dhe h1e/sti? ph2te:/r toy ko/lHonm_ gwe/h2t se/kweto-kwe k'wo:/ h1i/m. h1yo/d so/h2wl_ se/ngweti h1e/reti k'o/m-kwe h1e/domes. tr_syo/h2. h1ope/ye moy h2e/kweh2m. He/Hk'ew ph3dhi/ Hwr_dhdhi/-kwe. pe/h3m_ sa:/m-kwe. nu ko/lHonm_ spe/k'ye. ph2te/rm_ toy dedo/rk'th2e? h1i/m dedo/rk'h2e. gwm_sk'e/. twe/ k'le:/m h1r_sk'o/h2-kwe. dre/mt k'wo:/ pro/ h2o/wins-kwe h2e/g'oh2. k'e/mh2s? te/rs? k'e/mh2m_ h2e/kweh2m-kwe srume/ns pe/h3m_.

kwi/s gho/stey tu/H kwo/syo-kwe suHnu/s tu/H? h1eg'o/h2 poh2imne/s suHnu/s. ph2te:/r moy h2o/wins pe/h2sti. h1no/mn_ moy k'luto/m h1eg'o/h2-kwe h1le/wdheros. kwi/d moy k'o/ymoy h2isk'e/si? po/tnih2m h2isk'o/h2. de/k'm_ gwe/wo:m h1we/dmn_ de/h3oh2 h2r_g'n_to/m-kwe h2wl_/h1neh2m-kwe bhe/ro:h2. k'le/wos toy h1me/ h2eh2no/k'e. sddhi/ h2o/lut-kwe srobhe/ye nu we/kwesh2 we/rh1omes twe/-kwe k'le/wso:h2. se/h2 h1n_do/m ne h1e/sti. de/h2nu gwe/h2t. h2e/n se/dyo:h2. wr_g'ye/si? h2e/rh3yesi? wr_g'yo/h2 h2e/rh3yoh2-kwe.

bhre/h2ter bhudhdhi/ h2e/wso:s h2we/st. bhe/wdhoh2 tre/soh2-kwe. h1o/gwhim swo/pniyoy wido/m. k'o/ymom nos pro/ti se/rpet. bhre/h2ter me: bhe/yh2s. swo/pniyom ne more/yeti. bhebho/ydhh2e toy k'e:/r-kwe moy tre/mti. sth2dhi/ h2wl_/h1neh2m-kwe toy we/so. sre/wmn_ h1e/yowos pe/h3owos-kwe. h1yo/d h1o/gwhis gwm_ye:/ti h1i/m we/noh2. bhre/h2ter Hwe/rdhs ne-kwe dedwo/yth2e. h1o/gwhis se/h2gyeti. ko/lHonm_ h1e/yowos spe/k'yo:wos-kwe.

g'm_/re h1ludhe/s. wik'dhi/ moy k'o/ymom sddhi/-kwe. swe/k'ure toy we/yh1onm_ we/g'hoh2 po/tn_yeh2ey-kwe toy h2wl_/h1neh2m h1ope/yoh2. we/yh1o: nos lewbho/s. tu/H moy g'm_ro/s me/wdt-kwe moy k'e:/r. po/tnih2 toy ku/dhe h1e/sti? wr_h1e/nm_ pe/kweti we/kwesh2-kwe toy k'le/wt. h1e/k'woh1 toy ku/dhe h1ste/s? gho/rtoy sth2e/h1yetes wo/dr_-kwe ph3ta:/m. pi/ph3o:mes me/wdomes-kwe. no/kwts ste/wdt. kwi/s k'o/s? k'o/s moy bhre/h2te:r. kwi/s h1e/nos? h1e/nos moy swe/k'uros.

snu/se gwm_sk'e/. h1eg'o/h2 toy wiHro/syo me/h2te:r. bhebho/yh2h2e. snu/se me: bhe/yh2s. k'o/s toy k'o/ymos lewbho/s twe/-kwe pe/h2soh2. wiHro/s toy nos k'e/ywos h1e/y-kwe twe/ ste/rge:ti. ste/wdm_. twe/ h2wl_/h1neh2h1 wose/yo:h2. h1a/yso k'o/m poyk'o/m to/m-kwe we/so. we/kwesh2 toy h2we/ysm_ me/wdm_-kwe. twe/ wn_h1sk'o:/h2. h1e/nom h2e/g'rom h1e/nom-kwe ko/lHonm_ spe/k'ye. wiHro/s toy h2e/rh3yeti. wr_g'yo:/h2. kwi/s h1e/nos? h1e/nos toy dayh2we:/r.

ku/dhe h1e/s? twe/ h2isk'o/m. de/h2newi h1e/sm_ g'hwe:/rm_-kwe se/lesi kwe/k'm_. dedwo/yth2e? ne dedwo/yh2e. g'hwe:/r bhe/wgt. se/los h2ew me: gwe/h2s. me/h2te:r twe/ Hre/wdHt. gwm_ye/ k'o/ymom ye/h2owos. me/h2te:r wo/dr_ gwhr_ne/wti. ph3dhi/ tr_pdhi/-kwe. bhre/h2ter teto/rph2e twe/-kwe wn_h1sk'o/h2 nu gwe/mt me/h2te:r h2e/g't-kwe. gwi/h3wetes. gwm_sk'e/tom h1e/dtom-kwe.

yugdhi/ He/Hk'ew gwo:/s h2e/rh3trom-kwe bhe/re. kwi/h1 h2e/rh3yo:h2? h2e/g'rom h2e/rh3ye h2e/g'ros-kwe n_sme/ h2e/le:ti. po/ntoh1m_ h3rog'e/ye h2e/rh3trom-kwe dukdhi/. wr_g'yo/h2. h2e/g'rom spe/k'ye. h3e/pos toy h1we/su. nu do/ru skinddhi/ to/d-kwe gle/wbhe. to/d skine/doh2 nu h2e/wibhos gho/rtom de/mo:h2. wr_g'ye/si. dre/wi si/sde wo/dr_-kwe srobhe/ye.

swe:/k'ure toy swe/sorm_ we/dhom. h1i/h2m_ pe/h2soh2 h1i/h2m_-kwe ste/rgo:h2. h1i/h2 moy k'e:/r. h1yo/d h1i/h2m_ dhrughye:/si twe/ more/yo:h2. we/kwesh2 wos h2we/ysm_. h1sdhi/ nos k'e/ywos. h2le/ksoyh1t pe/rkwunos n_h3me/. gwm_ye/ we/yh1onm_ pi/ph3e. k'a:/s toy h2o/wins h1ope/yoh2. ta:/s spe/k'ye. ta:/s wide/h1yoh2 k'e:/r-kwe moy me/wdt. swe:/k'ure wr_h1e/nm_ gwr_h3e/ sh2dhi/-kwe. kwi/s k'o/s? k'o/s moy swe/k'uros h1e/nos-kwe moy bhre/h2te:r.

kwe/h2m He/Hk'ew wn_h1sk'e/si? ta:/m wn_h1sk'o/h2 h1ye/h2 de/h2newi tk'e/yti. to/syeh2s ph2te:/r poh2ime:/n. twe/ wn_h1sk'e/ti? ne wo/ydh2e. nu h1i/h2m_ se/h2gye bhh2dhi/-kwe. k'le/wse:ti. de/h2nu h1e/yoh2 to/syeh2s-kwe ph2tre/y we/rh1oh2. h1a/yso h1we/sum wr_h1e/nm_ h2r_g'n_to/m-kwe. h1no/mn_ toy mluHdhi/ to/d-kwe h2we/yseti. h1no/mn_ toy k'luto/m. dedwo/yh2e. me: tre/ms. to/syeh2ey poyk'o/m h1ope/yo:h2.

de/k'm_ gwo:/s de/deh3mi k'm_to/m h2o/wins h1ope/yoh2 h2r_g'n_to/m-kwe bhe/roh2. k'o/d h1we/dmn_ me/g'h2. to/d h1a/yh2e k'a:/m-kwe toy po/tnih2m de/h3m_. h2wise/ntu nos deywo:/s bhoydhe/h2m dye:/ws-kwe ta:/m dedo/rk'tu. h1yo/d twe/ dhrughyo:/h2 wl_/kwos h1me/ lm_bhye/h1t. h1yo/d h1me/ dhrughye:/si lm_bhye/h1t wl_/kwos twe/. h3re:/g's nos bhoydhe/h2m k'l_ne/wti. nu h1i/h2 me/g'h2ih2. bhoydhe/h2 sth2e/h1yetu h1e/stu-kwe h1we/dmn_ h1we/su. pe/kwto:r wr_h1e/nm_ dhe/dheh1tu we/yh1onm_-kwe pi/ph3o:mes. ke/h2nmn_ h1e/stu.

gho/steyes gwm_sk'o/nti. gho/rtom h2re/ydhe wr_h1e/nm_-kwe more/yo:h2. pe/kwetu pe/kwto:r to/m. h1regwesye/ti pe/h2wr_ dhe/gwhetu. kwi/s gwm_ye/ti? h3re:/g's gwm_ye/ti h3re:/g'nih2-kwe k'o/m h1e/sti. nu h1e/ymos we/yh1onm_ h2o/lut-kwe de/h3omes ke/h2nmn_-kwe h1we/su h1e/stu. si/sde. h3e/pos re/h1dht. k'e/mh2m_ me/wdm_-kwe. to/y se/h2onti. snu/se we/yh1onm_ we/g'he we/g'he g'm_/re h2o/lut. kwi/s k'e/h2? k'e/h2 moy snuso/s h1e/nos-kwe moy g'm_ro/s.

no/kwts gwm_ye/ti tre/mmi-kwe. me: bhe/yh2s. k'wo:/ gho/rtom h2le/kseti. l_ghdhi/ k'l_nudhi/-kwe moy ke/h2nmn_. kwi/d sth2e/h1yeti? do/ru sth2e/h1yeti. kwi/d dre/mt? sre/wmn_ dre/mt. kwi/d dhe/gwheti? pe/h2wr_ dhe/gwheti. kwi/d h2we/kst? Hrughi/s h2we/kst. kwi/s me/rt? mo/rtos me/rt. ne me/rt deywo/s. k'le/wsoh2. h1o/gwhis h2e/nti h1e/sti? h1o/gwhis ne se/rpeti. h1we/su toy swo/pniyom gwm_ye/h1t. h2e/wso:s h2we/seti so/h2wl_-kwe kwe/k'esi. h2e/nh2ts h2e/kweh2i k'e/yor h1o/gwhis-kwe k'e/yor g'hwe:/r-kwe h2po/ h1e/sti.

wi/Hre no/kwts ste/wdt. k'e:/r moy h2e/nti gwm_sk'e/. twe/ h2wl_/h1neh2h1 wose/yo:h2. h1me/ ste/rgesi? twe/ ste/rgoh2. tu/H moy wiHro/s. twe/ pe/h2soh2. swo/m k'o/ymom de/mo:wos h2e/g'rom-kwe h2e/rh3yo:wos. h2e/g'ros pl_th2u/s h1sye/h1t. h1sye/h1t nos h1no/mn_ k'luto/m dwo/h1-kwe nos suHnu/h1 h1sih1ta:/m. gwi/h3woyh1we.

kwi/s dhe/g'ho:m me/h2te:r? dhe/g'ho:m mo/rto:m me/h2te:r. h1i/h2 to/ns h2e/leti dye:/ws-kwe to/yso:m ph2te:/r. dye:/ws ku/dhe h1e/sti? dye:/ws upe/r h1e/sti dhe/g'ho:m-kwe Hn_dhi/ h1e/sti. dye:/ws n_sme/ k'le/wseti? k'le/wseti dedo/rk'e-kwe. bhe/h2oh2. bhh2dhi/ h2we/yseti-kwe. kwi/s pe/rkwunos? pe/rkwunos deywo/s. h1yo/d pe/rkwunos gwm_ye:/ti nu h2we/rseti dhe/g'ho:m-kwe h1werse/h2. so/h2wl_ deywo/s? so/h2wl_ le/wkm_ bhe/reti dhe/g'ho:m-kwe gwhr_ne/wti.

suHnu/s kwse/pi g'n_h1ye/to nu h2we/st h2e/wso:s nu spe/k'yet me/h2te:r to/m me/wdt-kwe nu we/wket ph2te:/r h1e/syeh2ey. suHnu/s toy g'eg'o/nh1e. tu/H moy lewbho/s nu dhe/h1t ph2te:/r to/smey h1no/mn_. h1sye/h1t k'o/d h1no/mn_ k'luto/m n_/dhgwhitom-kwe k'le/wos h1e/smey h1sye/h1t. h1sye/h1t h1e/syo ste/h2mn_ me/g'h2 h1e/syo-kwe we/yHs h2uksye/h1t. bhuh2ye/h1t Hwr_dhye/h1t-kwe ph2sye/h1t-kwe h2e/wso:s h1i/m nu gwe/mt swe/so:r nu to/m wide/t se/kwt-kwe. h1e/y moy lewbho/s. h1i/m ste/rgo:h2 nu loghe/yet ph2te:/r to/m.

h1yo/d toy gho/stis gwe/meti h1i/m sode/ye h1ope/ye-kwe h1e/smey h2e/kweh2m h2o/lut-kwe. gho/stim me: dhrughye/s. gho/stis deywo/ybhos lewbho/s. twe/ k'le:/m memo/nh2e-kwe. h1yo/d so/ po/yk'os h1e/seti? to/smey k'e/yweh2 we/kwesh2 wr_h1dhi/. deywo:/s to/m dedr_k'e:/r. gho/stim sode/yo:h2. h1we/sus wiHro/s h1e/sesi h1no/mn_-kwe toy k'luto/m h1e/seti.

swe/sorh1e ph2we/ni se/dyeta:m h2we/rst-kwe. bhebho/yh2h2e. me: bhe/yh2s. me/h2te:r h2e/nti h1e/sti k'wo:/-kwe k'e/yor. le/ghowos nu l_ghta:/m gwe/mt-kwe swo/pniyom te/h2bhos nu h2we/st h2e/wso:s sth2ta:/m-kwe nu sre/wmn_ h1ita:/m wo/dr_-kwe we/g'heta:m nu gwe/mt me/h2te:r swe/sorh1e-kwe h2wl_/h1neh2h1 k'e/let. le/ghyetom nu gwe/mt bhre/h2te:r h2e/g't-kwe. ku/dhe h1sto/m? srume/ni h1swe/. h2e/kweh2m bhore/yowe.

suHnu/h1 nos ku/dhe h1ste/s? de/h2nu gwh2ta:/m h2o/wins-kwe h1l_h2ta:/m. h1yo/d so/h2wl_ se/ngweti h1e/retes. me: bhe/yh2s. wl_/kwos gho/rtom ne h1r_sk'e/ti. to/h1 kl_He/ni wide/h1yoh2 wo/dr_-kwe bhore/yetes. to/ybhos h2o/lut h2re/ydho:mes nu h1ludhe/ta:m ph2ter me/h2ter. tr_syo/wos. pi/ph3etom h1e/dtom-kwe. g'hwe:/rm_ se/lesi kwk'we/ ne-kwe dedwiwe/. to/m wn_we/. Hwr_dhto/m k'le/wos-kwe wos me/g'h2.

me/h2te:r po/ntoh1i se/dyet spe/k'yet-kwe. suHnu/s moy gwm_sk'e:/ti? se/ngwt so/h2wl_ no/kwts-kwe gwm_ye/t h2eh2o/ghe nu h2ne/rm_ po/ntoh1i wide/t nu to/m g'n_ne/h3t Hre/wdHt-kwe pro/-kwe dre/mt. h1ludhe/s nu me/wdt. ku/dhe h1e/s? h2o/wins ko/lHonm_ h1e/lh2m_ se/rpet-kwe g'hwe:/r n_sme/ pro/ti nu to/m more/yom. sddhi/ h2o/lut-kwe srobhe/ye wr_h1dhi/-kwe moy. h3re/g'm_ wido/m h2r_g'n_to/m-kwe moy de/h3t.

dwo/h1 bhre/h2terh1e h2o/wins ko/lHonm_ h1l_h2ta:/m nu wl_/kwom wide/ta:m se/rpet-kwe h1e/y to/h1 pro/ti. ph2sdhi/ bhre/h2ter ta:/s. wl_/kwom we/noh2 nu wl_/kwom more/yeta:m mudta:/m-kwe nu le/ghyet wl_/kwosyo kre/pos h2e/g'roy nu k'o/ymom dr_mta:/m nu de/h3t me/h2te:r to/ybhos wo/dr_ nu se/kwt ph2te:/r. k'le/wos wos me/g'h2. usme/y misdho/m de/deh3oh2 nu sdta:/m bhre/h2terh1e wr_h1e/nm_-kwe h1e/dta:m.

h2e/wso:s h2we/st nu skine/dt ph2te:/r do/ru gle/wbhet-kwe bhre/h2te:r to/d nu gwhr_ne/wt me/h2te:r wo/dr_ swe/so:r-kwe h2wl_/h1neh2m h2sowse/yet nu de/wkt h1e/k'wos h2e/rh3trom ph3e/nt-kwe gwo/wes srume/ni. ku/dhe ye/h2si? h2e/g'rom ye/h2mi. He/h2mr_ gwhe/rt nu Hre/wdHt ph2te:/r. gwm_ye/te h1e/dte-kwe nu sde/nt.

k'l_nute/ moy we/kwesh2. memo/nh2e. me/h2term_ wos de/h2newi kwe/k'm_. h1i/h2m_ g'ne/h3m_. h1i/h2 moy ko/h2mos h1e/st h1i/h2m_-kwe ste/rgom nu k'm_to/m h2o/wins de/k'm_ gwo:/s de/h3m_ nu moy de/h3t me/ne swe/k'uros h1i/h2m_. k'o/m gwi/h3wowos g'n_h1ye/dhwe-kwe. me/h2te:r wos h1use/wih2. wn_h1sk'e/t me/h2te:r twe/? h1me/ wn_h1sk'e/t h1i/h2m_-kwe wn_h1sk'o/h2. h1e/syeh2ey bhebho/ydhh2e bhebho/ydhe-kwe moy. k'o/m k'o/ymom de/mom.

h2le/kse toy swe/sorm_ h1i/h2m_-kwe ste/rge. k'wo/nm_ k'o/m sl_h2gwso/. k'wo:/ k'e/ywos h1e/y-kwe HeHk'u/s. h1i/h2m_ h2le/ksoh2. me/ne moy swe/so:r k'e/yweh2. twe/ wn_h1sk'o/h2. bhre/h2ter wl_/kwo:d dedwo/yh2e. wl_/kwos uh3we/ me: lm_bhye/h1t. ph2sye/h1t dye:/ws uh3we/. Hwr_dhih1to/m nu h2e/g't me/h2te:r. yu/ moy k'e:/r. uh3we/ ste/rgoh2.

ph2te:/r deywo/ybhos g'huto/m we/yh1onm_ de/h3t. de/ywo:s ph3te/ we/kwesh2 nos h2wiste/ nu bhre/h2trey wo/dr_ h1a/yt nu pe/h3t se/h2t-kwe nu we/rh1t me/h2te:r h1erkwo/m. dye/w ph2ter. k'l_nudhi/ nos ke/h2nmn_ n_sme/-kwe h2le/kse. h2we/yst dye:/ws dhe/g'ho:m-kwe h2we/yst. nu we/wket ph2te:/r. deywo/ns memn_te/ nu mle/wHt bhre/h2te:r ke/h2nmn_.

tre/yes suHne/wes ph2tre/y h1se/nt nu we/wket. kwo/s gwo:/s h2le/kse:ti? h1i/ns h2le/kso:h2. h2e/g'rom h2e/rh3yo:h2. gwe/h2oh2 po/tnih2m-kwe se/h2gyo:h2 nu me/wdt ph2te:/r nu to/ybhos k'wo/nm_ h2e/rh3trom h2r_g'n_to/m-kwe h1a/yt. yu/H bhre/h2teres. k'o/m sth2te/ yugo/m-kwe me: Hrumpte/. gwh2te/ h2ew-kwe gwm_te/.

swe/so:r bhre/h2trey lewbhe/h2 bhre/h2te:r-kwe swesre/y lewbho/s. h1yo/s swo/syo ph2tre/s we/kwos k'le/weti so/ k'luto/s. bhre/h2te:r ne gho/stis. h3e/pos h1we/su. h3re:/g's mo/rtos deywo/s-kwe n_mr_to/s. kre/wh2s h1rudhe/h1ti. mo/rtos me/rt. k'le/wos n_/dhgwhitom. we/nh1os me/g'h2. po/tnih2 k'o/ymosyo k'e:/r. dre/mt sre/wmn_ do/ru sth2e/h1yeti ko/lHo:-kwe stesto/h2e. g'hwe:/r bhe/wgt h1o/gwhis-kwe se/rpeti.

gwh2dhi/ deywo:/s te we/dhoyh1ent. me: h3sle/ydhs. h1no/mn_ toy memn_dhi/ memn_dhi/ ph2te/rm_ me/h2term_-kwe. h2wl_/h1neh2m we/so h2r_g'n_to/m-kwe h1a/yso. ne/kwti me: h1e/ys. h1e/yoh2 h2ew-kwe gwm_yo:/h2. we/kwesh2 wos meme/noh2. h1sye/h1t toy po/ntoh1s pl_th2u/s. wl_/kwos twe/ me: lm_bhye/h1t. lukye/h1t toy He/h2mr_ nu we/wket swe/so:r. memn_dhi/ toy bhre/h2ter swe/sorm_. Hwe/rdht moy kre/pos k'e:/r-kwe moy teto/rpe.

dye/w ph2ter dhe/g'hom me/h2ter nu nos h2wisto/m. dh3to/m k'o/syeh2ey po/tn_yeh2ey k'le/wos. gwi/h3woyh1t g'n_h1yo/yh1ro suHne/wes h1e/syeh2ey. g'm_/re toy h1i/h2m_ de/deh3mi. swe/k'ure-ge h1i/h2m_ wn_h1sk'o:/h2. nu pe/h2wr_ h2e/kweh2-kwe h1i/h2m_ h2le/ksoyh1ta:m. h2uksye/h1t Hrughi/s do/ru sth2e/h1yoyh1t. nu dr_mye/h1t sre/wmn_ ko/lHo:-kwe stesth2ye/h1t. nu tr_sye/h1t se/los h1o/gwhis-kwe me: se/rpoyh1t. bhugye/h1t g'hwe:/r sprongho/s wos Hrughi/m me: h2k'h3ye/h1t. lukye/h1t He/h2mr_ no/kwts-kwe gwhr_ye/h1t. h1sye/h1t wos we/nh1os ko/h2mos-kwe wos me/g'h2s h1sye/h1t. Hwr_dhye/h1t toy kre/pos h2yuHn_te/h2 toy lukye/h1t. h1sye/h1t wos k'woyto/s bhudhto/h1 h1sih1to/m. we/yHs toy h2uksye/h1t dh3ye/h1t wos de/h3to:r misdho/m. h1erkwo/s wos k'luwih1to/ k'luwih1to/ wos ke/h2nmn_.

nu h2e/wso:s h2we/st nu ye/wgt ph2te:/r dwo/h1 h1e/k'woh1. wose/yet lewbhe/h2 me/h2te:r sne/wbhontih2m ta:/m. we/dhet ph2te:/r ta:/m h1e/g'hs bhre/h2te:r se/kweto. nu swidye/t se/h2 tre/set-kwe gwe/h2t. Hre/wdHt me/h2te:r h1me/ memn_dhi/. h2ew-kwe gwm_sk'e/ k'le/wesh1 h1idhi/ me: bhe/yh2s. we/wket ph2te:/r h2le/ksoyh1t te dye:/ws. nu-kwe dr_mta:/m HeHk'u/h1 h1e/k'woh1 po/ntoh1i. nu stesto/h2e me/h2te:r spe/k'yet.

dye/w ph2ter h1use/wo:m de/h3tor. k'ludhi/ nos we/kwesh2 ye/wgs h1yo/s HeHk'u/ns h1e/k'wons. h1yo/s sh2we/ns kwe/kwlom g'he/ys dhe/h1s-ge h1yo/s so/h2wl_. h1yo/s h1o/gwhim we/ns spe/rs h1yo/s h2e/kweh2m. h1yo/s-kwe dhe/g'ho:m stoh2e/yes nu k'l_nudhi/. ph2ter to/ybhos h2r_g'n_to/m gwo:/s-kwe dh3dhi/. nu sh2we/ns kwe/kwlos pro/ h1iye/h1t. bhe/royh1ta:m-ge dwo/h1 h1e/k'woh1 to/h1. h2e/wsos gwm_sk'e/ le/wkm_ bhe/re. h2e/wsos to/ybhos h2usdhi/ pe/h2wr_ me: n_k'ye/h1t. nu h1sye/h1t to/yso:m h1no/mn_ k'luto/m. me/g'h2-kwe k'le/wos to/ybhos h1sye/h1t. ph2ter to/ybhos n_/dhgwhitom k'le/wos dh3dhi/. we/nh1os dh3dhi/ h3re:/g's h3re:/g'nih2-kwe gwi/h3woyh1ta:m. h1sye/h1t h1we/sus poh2ime:/n bhudhto/s.

gwm_me/ nu nos bhre/h2trey po/tnih2m se/h2gyomes. kwi/s wos bhre/h2te:r nu h1e/y we/rse:n? h1e/y k'm_to/gwus h1e/y h1le/wdheros. nu gho/steyes wik'te/ sdte/-kwe. h1yo/d h1we/dmn_ de/h3ete nu ta:/m de/h3omes. de/k'm_ gwo:/s k'm_to/m h2o/wins de/deh3omes. h2r_g'n_to/m-kwe wos h2wl_/h1neh2m-kwe bhe/romes. gho/steyes te/h2 loghe/yete. se/h2 nos k'e/yweh2 se/h2-kwe nos k'e:/r. nu ta:/m ph2ste/ se/h2-kwe usme/ h2e/le:ti. se/h2 h1ne/dhtu spe/k'yete se/h2 le/wks. nu h1e/stu k'e/h2 bhoydhe/h2 n_sme/y. kwe/k't poh2ime:/n ta:/m de/h2newi. kwe/yes k'o/y polh1e/wes h2ne/res? k'o/y-ge moy k'e/ywo:s bhre/h2teres.

gho/stins k'le/wsonts ph2te:/r to/ns h1n_do/ we/dhet. nu dhe/dheh1t me/h2te:r h2o/lut sde/nt. nu h2e/g't ph2te:/r kwi/d h2isk'e/te? bhre/h2trey nos po/tnih2m h2isk'o/mes. h1we/dmn_ dh3te/ ta:/m-kwe h1a/ydhwe. h1we/dmn_ dh3e/nt h1a/yto ph2te:/r to/d. nu bhe/ret se/h2 h2wl_/h1neh2m h2r_g'n_to/m yugo/m. bhe/ret h2e/rh3trom dwo/h1 h1e/k'woh1 de/k'm_ gwo:/s. h2e/g't me/h2te:r ta:/m ph2ste/ ta:/m me: dhrughye/te. nu h1we/su h2o/lut ph3e/nt sh2e/nt-kwe. k'e/ywe swe/k'ure g'm_/re-kwe.

nu HeHk'u/h1 dwo/h1 h1e/k'woh1 gwm_ta:/m. nu wide/t wiHro/syo me/h2te:r snuso/m me/wdt. snu/se n_sme/ h1n_do/ gwm_sk'e/. pe/h2wr_-ge nos toy lewbho/m h1sye/h1t. nu bhe/ret dayh2we:/r we/yh1onm_. nu snu/se sddhi/ to/m-kwe pi/ph3e. de/h3t wiHro/syo me/h2te:r snuso:/y poyk'o/m. nu to/m h1a/yso to/m-kwe we/so. se/kwt snuso/s h2wl_/h1neh2m h2r_g'n_to/m-kwe bhe/roh2. h1eg'o/h2 toy snuso/s twe/ ste/rgo:h2. snu/se. da/yh2wer.

h1eg'o/h2 twe/ we/dhoh2 h1me/ h1a/yth2e? te/bhye sne/wbhoh2 twe/-kwe h1a/yh2e. k'o/m gwi/h3wo:wos h1e/dowos-kwe. nu we/ we/yh1onm_ pi/ph3o:wos. twe/ pe/h2soh2. twe/ ste/rgo:h2 nu pe/h2wr_ pe/ri h1e/yowos. pe/h2wr_ n_h3me/ k'luye/h1t wi/ me: litih1we/. nu nos sh2we/ns kwe/kwlos bhoydhe/h2m dedo/rk'tu. k'l_ne/wtu dhe/g'ho:m me/h2te:r ta:/m. nu h2e/g't snuso/syo bhre/h2te:r h1eg'o/h2 h2we/ysm_. nu h2e/g't dayh2we:/r h1eg'o/h2 kwe/k'm_. nu h2e/g't me/h2te:r h1eg'o/h2 te/rpm_. nu snu/se g'm_/re da/yh2wer swe:/k'ure-kwe. yu/H-ge nos bhoydhe/h2m k'l_nute/.

h1regwesye/t gwm_sk'o/nt gho/steyes pe/kwto:r pe/kwet. nu h1ludhe/ta:m h3re:/g's h3re:/g'nih2-kwe to/h1-kwe sdta:/m. nu kwi/s k'o/s h2ne:/r kwi/s-kwe h1e/nos? k'o/s dayh2we:/r h1e/nos swe:k'uro/s. de/h3t ph2te:/r to/ybhos we/yh1onm_. wr_h1e/nm_ gwr_h3o/nt we/yh1onm_ srobhe/yont. h2o/lut-kwe srobhe/yont ke/h2nmn_-kwe mluHe/nt. nu te/rpt pe/kwto:r we/rh1t poh2ime:/n h1erkwo/m. nu we/wket h3re:/g's nu moy k'o/s k'o/ymos k'e/ywos. g'm_/re toy k'le/wos me/g'h2 h1sye/h1t. nu bhh2ta:/m bhre/h2terh1e h1erkwo/m. nu ste/h2t snuso/syo bhre/h2te:r h2e/g't-kwe tr_pte/. nu ste/wdt no/kwts nu h1n_do/ l_ghe/nt.

pl_/th2ewi nos dhe/g'hom me/h2ter. h1ye/h2 gwo:/s mo/rtons-kwe h2e/lesi. dh3dhi/ k'o/syeh2ey polh1u/ns suHnu/ns Hwr_dhye/h1t. h1ye/h2 Hrughi/m do/ru h2e/lesi k'ludhi/ nos. nu wl_/kwos h2o/wins me: lm_bhye/h1t. nu nos me: wik'ye/h1t g'hwe:/r gho/rtom. sprongho/s-ge Hrughi/m me: h2k'h3ye/h1t. me: kwis ste/h2yoyh1t wr_h1e/nm_ me:-kwe mr_ye/h1t. me:-ge Hrumpye/h1t h1e/k'wos yugo/m. h2e/kweh2 me: ple/h1dhoyh1t gwi/h3woyh1t-kwe.

tu/H k'o/ymosyo po/tnih2 h1eg'o/h2-kwe wiHro/s. wik'dhi/. h1eg'o/h2 we/yk'm_ twe/ h1n_do/m we/g'ho:h2 me: h3sle/ydhs. ne dedwo/yh2e kre/pos moy Hwe/rdht. me/g'h2-kwe moy k'e:/r teto/rpe. pe/h2wr_ toy moy pe/h2wr_ h1no/mn_-kwe toy moy h1no/mn_. nu toy we/kwesh2 k'le:/m tu/H-ge moy lewbho/s. nu ste/h2t se/h2 u/d le/wkt-kwe pe/h2wr_. nu h2e/g't wiHro/syo me/h2te:r k'o/s moy bhre/h2te:r. k'e/h2-kwe moy me/h2te:r. nu swe:/k'ure swe/k'ure-kwe.

h1eg'o/h2 toy dayh2we:/r bhre/h2te:r-ge toy h1e/soh2. da/yh2wer-ge h1eg'o/h2 teto/rph2e. nu moy wiHro/s ku/dhe h1e/sti? h1e/k'wons we/dheti gwm_ye:/ti. nu sdta:/m dayh2we:/r swe:k'uro/s-kwe. spe/k'ye k'o/m h2e/g'rom h1e/nom-kwe ko/lHonm_. nu snu/se n_sme/y h1e/wkesi. k'o/s moy swe:k'uro/s h1e/nos moy swe/k'uros. bhre/h2ter gwm_sk'e/.

h1e/k'wo:s HeHk'e/wes so/ we/rse:n se/h2-kwe le/wks. to/yso:m dye/wmn_ n_/dhgwhitom we/nh1os me/g'h2. nu lewbhe/h2 h2e/wso:s n_/dhgwhitom-ge k'le/wos. dayh2we:/r mle/wHti swe:k'uro/s mle/wHti. swe/k'uros mle/wHti g'm_ro/s mle/wHti.

we/dhes nu toy ph2sdhi/ po/tnih2m. ste/rge-kwe h1i/h2m_ me: dhrughye/s. nu toy de/h3t te/we swe/k'uros h1i/h2m_. h2e/g'rom h2e/rh3ye gwo:/s h2le/kse. nu twe/ k'le/wsoh2 memo/nh2e-kwe. nu toy h1sdhi/ swe:k'uro:/y k'e/ywos. h1e/y-kwe toy k'e/ywos h1e/seti. nu dh3ye/h1t dhe/g'ho:m usme/y polh1u/ns suHnu/ns. h2e/g't swe/k'uros tu/H moy g'm_ro/s. h1eg'o/h2-kwe toy swe/k'uros k'o/m pi/ph3o:mes. swe/k'ure g'm_/re swe:/k'ure. n_sme/ k'l_nute/.

h1o/gwhis pe/runi k'e/yo. h2e/kwa:s Hwe/rt dhe/g'ho:m-kwe h2susye/t. h1le/wdheyes tr_syo/nt deywo/m-kwe g'huHe/yont. nu pe/rkwunos h2e/k'monm_ h1e/pt h2e/n-kwe upo/ gwe/h2t. gwhe/nt h1o/gwhim. h1e/syo h3o/kwih1 h1regwesye/ta:m h1e/syo-kwe dn_g'hwe/h2s h1h3se/s pe/th1t. h1i/m h2k'm_ne/h1 ke/lh2t h1e/syo-kwe k'e/rh2sonm_ bhe/ydt. we/do:r spe/rt mo/ri-kwe sre/wont. nu tewh2te/h2 deywo/m gwr_ne/Ht.

h1e/st h2yuHn_k'o/s h2ne:/r h1suk'le/we:s. k'o/ymoy swe/h2h1 me/h2tr_h1 k'o/m gwi/h3wet h1e/k'wons-kwe ye/wgt. dusme/ne:s g'hwe:/r de/h2nuh2 h2ewlo/y Hwe/rt. wo/dr_ pk'e/wey ne h1e/st. h1suk'le/we:s g'hoyso/m h1e/pt h2ewlo/m-kwe h1r_sk'e/t. h1i/m kwe/k't ghre/met-kwe. h2yuHn_k'o/s ne tre/set Hye/h1kt g'hoyso/m. g'hoyso/s h1i/m h2we/gt g'hwe:/r-kwe me/rt. nu wiHro/s h2ewlo/m Hrune/pt. we/do:r me/lh3t pe/k'u-kwe pe/h3t. me/g'h2 k'le/wos h1suk'le/wesm_ gwe/mt.

pe/rkwune te k'u/h1re de/ywe gwr_nHme/s. dusme/nesm_-ge h1o/gwhim pe/runi gwhe/ns. h1yo/s h2e/kwa:s spe/rs n_sme/ gwm_dhi/. h1yo/s sokso/m bhe/yds te/we nos we/yHm_ dh3dhi/. k'le/wos toy h2nr_su/ n_/dhgwhitom. nu toy so/g'hos h2r_Hmo/s lewko/m-kwe toy h3o/kw. h1erkwo/m nos k'ludhi/ n_sme/y-kwe priHo/s h1sdhi/.

te/k'nom h2e/wh2om pre:/k'st. kwo/s h1o/gwhim gwhe/nt. g'e/rh2onts se/kwt. ghro/mosyo dhe/h1s tewh2so/s h1i/m gwhe/nt. h1i/m h2k'm_ne/h1 diwe/s gwe/lh1t. kwi/d h1i/m gwe/lh1t. we/do:r Hwe/rt h2ne/res-kwe tr_syo/nt. h1e/syo de/nk'treh2 dl_h1ghe/h2 h1e/st h1e/syo-kwe h3o/kwih1 h1rudhro/yh1 h1sta:/m. nu we/do:r sre/weti pi/ph3omes-kwe. te/k'nom sme:/yt do:/m-kwe dre/mt.

h1yo/d h1o/gwhis me/rt sro/wo:s pe/runs ni/ te/kwont. pl_th2eno/ns h2e/g'rons pl_nh1e/nt ghre/h1mn_-kwe g'helh3wo/m bhu/Ht. gwo/wes gwowsth2o/ybhos ml_h3e/nt we/dos-kwe pi/ph3ont. poh2ime/nes me/wdont te/k'neh2-kwe we/dni lo/yget. gwe/nh2es sro/wom ni/ h1ye/nt gwe/Hyont-kwe. h1e/syo ne/k'u kre/pos ghr_h1me/ni le/ghet h2e/weyes-kwe h1e/syo me:/ms h1e/dn_t.

bha/ge te h1use/wo:m de/h3tor gwr_nHme/s. nu nos we/do:r de/h2nuh2-kwe se/lg's. gwo/wes nos we/dos pi/ph3onti. te/k'neh2-ge nos gwi/h3weti lo/ygeti-kwe. i/sh1re-ge te/bhye h1erkwo/m gwe/Hyomes. h1we/sew nos k'e/rdh2 twe/d mudre/h2. n_sme/y priHo/s h1sdhi/ h1we/suh2-kwe nos dh3dhi/.

dusme/ne:s po/yk'os dhg'he/mi Hn_dhe/r gwi/h3wet. h1ne/dht sro/wons-kwe gwe/rh3t. h2e/g'ro:s tr_se/nt h2o/weyes-kwe tr_syo/nt. gwe/nh2es ke/nkont g'e/rh2ontes-kwe k'e/h2desi l_ghe/nt. tewh2te/h2 deywo/ns Hre/wdHt. bha/gos h1e/syeh2s wo/kwm_ k'le/wt. HeHk'u/ns h1e/k'wons ye/wgt h1i/ns po/yk'om h2e/g'et. h1e/syo kre/pos h2k'm_ne/h1 bhine/dt sro/wo:s-kwe h1e/smo:d h1e/g'hs sre/wont. nu h2o/weyes pi/ph3ont tewh2te/h2-kwe me/wdet.

kwr_/mis ni/ se/ngwt. h1e/syo h1e/sh2r_ mo/ri h1n_do/ sre/wet. h1e/syo h3e/sth1h2 we/dni le/ghet. deywo/s h2e/n ste/h2t stonh2e/yet-kwe. nu wo/dr_ h1le/wdherom h1e/st. h2e/g'rons upe/r te/kwet so/h2wl_-kwe le/wkt. poh2ime/nes ne/k'um kwr_/mim kwk'e/nt tr_me/nt-kwe. nu me/wdont deywo/m-kwe sepelyo/nt. poh2ime/nes h1e/syo kre/pos ph2we/nh1 dhe/gwhont h1e/syo-kwe h3e/sth1h2 h2epo/ Hih1ke/nt.

k'uh1ro/s deywo/s k'uh1ro/s-kwe h1e/syo h2r_Hmo/s. gwr_h2u/s h1e/syo h2e/k'mo: h2ek'ro/s-kwe g'hoyso/s. h1e/syo-ge h1e/k'wo:s HeHk'e/wes. nu sweh2du/ me/dhu pe/h3t Hwe/rdht-kwe. nu dusme/nesm_ g'hwe:/rm_ we/nt. h1i/m kwis kh3te/wi ne te/rh2ut. stuh2ro/syo te/wh2os gwe/Hyomes.

ph2te:/r dye:/ws swo:/y suHno/wey h2e/k'monm_ te:/tk't. h2e/k'mo: gwr_h2u/s lewko/s-kwe h1e/st. suHno/wey h1i/m de/h3t h2e/g't-kwe. k'o/m h1pdhi/ h1o/gwhim-kwe kl_h2dhi/. suHnu/s h1i/m g'ho/stoy h1e/pt bhe/rg'hontm_-kwe pe/rkwum ste/yghet. h1i/m h2e/n po/yk'om pro/ti Hye/h1kt. h1e/syo k'e/rh2sonm_ dhe/wght h1i/m-kwe bhe/ydt. nu h1e/y h2ew deywo/syo g'ho/stom h1n_do/ gwe/h2t.

h1o/gwhis h2e/kweh2su pe/ri k'e/yo. deywo:/s h1i/m wido/nt h1e/smo:d-kwe tre/sont. dye:/m upo/ bhuge/nt n_bhro/ysu-kwe Hn_dhi/ k'e/lonto. ghro/mosyo somHo/s dhe/h1s dhe/rst. h1e/ymos we/wket me: tre/sete. h1o/gwhim more/yo:h2. h2e/n h1r_sk'e/t h1i/m-kwe h2k'm_ne/h1 tri/s ke/lh2t. h1e/syo h1orse/h2 tre/mt nu me/rt. deywo:/s h2ew ni/ gwme/nt n_k'e/wi-kwe pe/ri sth2e/nt. h1i/m gwr_nHe/nt h1yo/s dhe/rst.

te/bhye ne/wom h1erkwo/m gwe/Hyoh2. h1o/gwhim-ge h2k'm_ne/h1 more/yes. nu dhg'hme/s h1ludhi/bhos we/do:r spe/rs. pe/rkwune moy ke/h2nmn_ k'ludhi/. nu nos pe/k'u HeHk'u/ns-kwe h1e/k'wons dh3dhi/. nu nos do/mn_s ne/kwti ph2sdhi/.

dhugh2te:/r me/h2term_ ne/kwti pre:/k'st spe/lom moy wr_h1dhi/. no/kwts dl_h1ghe/h2 h1e/st dhugh2te:/r-kwe k'l_ne/wt. me/h2te:r h1e/gwhis spe/lom we/rh1t. h1o/gwhis h2e/kweh2su k'e/yo h1i/h2n_s-kwe gwr_h3e/t. h2ne/res tr_syo/nt gwo/wes-kwe m_re/nt. nu wiHro/s h1wesuk'le/we:s h1ludhe/t. h1e/syo k'e/rh2sonm_ h2k'm_ne/h1 te/wkt. so/ me/rt wo/dr_-kwe me/lh3t. dhugh2te:/r mle/wHt h1e/smo:d ne bhebho/yh2h2e. h1e/syeh2s me/h2te:r sme:/yt mle/wHt-kwe h1e/y ne/k'us. supdhi/.

h2nr_me/ne:s po/yk'om pro/ti h1e/yt. h1i/m g'hoyso/h1 pe/rt ne-kwe me/rt. h1i/m dwi/s pe/rt ghre/met-kwe. h1i/m tri/s pe/rt h1e/syo-kwe k'e/rh2so: bhindto/. h1e/syo h1e/sh2r_ kr_sno/m h1e/st. h1e/sh2r_ h1e/smo:d h1e/g'hs te/kwet we/desh2-kwe h1sh2e/nh1 k'o/m te/kwet. sro/wo:s k'o/rka:s le/wh3ont do/ruh2-kwe pi/ph3et. h2nr_me/ne:s ste/h2t swo/m-kwe g'hoyso/m tl_ne/h2t. tewh2te/h2 h1i/m kwe/k't k'luto/m-kwe h1e/syo h1no/mn_ gwr_ne/Ht.

h1o/gwhim-ge te/wkt we/do:r-kwe spe/rt. nu sokso/m gwr_h2u/h1 h2k'm_ne/h1 bhe/ydt. gwo:/s pe/runs h2ewlo:/d h1e/g'hs we/dhet. so/h2wl_ h2ew dhg'he/mi upe/r le/wkt. nu to/m gwr_nHme/s nu to/m g'huHe/yomes. nu h1e/smey stuh2ro:/y se/ngwho:mes.

h1o/gwhis deywo:/y we/wket kwo/s tu/H. we/do:r dhore/yoh2 te/h2-kwe kwis me/d ne sle/h2gweti. h2e/g't h1eg'o/h2 pe/rkwunos. k'o/h1 te h2k'm_ne/h1 ke/lh2oh2. ghre/met do/ru-kwe wih1to/. nu deywo/s h2e/k'monm_ Hye/h1kt h1o/gwhis-kwe se/ngwt. do/ruh2 tre/mt h2e/weyes-kwe pth2e/nt. h1e/syo dn_g'hwe/h2s dl_h1ghe/h2 h1e/st h1e/syo-kwe h3do/ntes h2ek'ro:/s h1se/nt. deywo/s sme:/yt. h1e/syo we/kwesh2 dhro/wgho:s h1e/st.

po/yk'os gwo:/s h2epo/ h2e/g'et h1i/ns-kwe pe/runi we/rg't. gwoldho/h1 h1e/smi h1n_do/m HrudHe/nt. bha/gos h1e/yso:m wo/kwn_s k'le/wt. so/ h1n_gwni/s-kwe k'o/m h2e/n gwh2ta:/m. h1n_gwni/s po/yk'om h1e/wset ish1ro/s-kwe h1i/d swo/h1 h2k'm_ne/h1 ske/lHt. po/yk'os bhe/wgt h1n_gwni/s h1i/m h1r_h1pye/t. le/wkm_ u/d h1n_dhe/nt. poh2ime/nes h1i/ns h2ml_g'e/nt h2me/lg'os-kwe sweh2du/ h1e/st.

pe/rkwune h2e/ngwhim h2k'm_ne/h1 te/wks. i/sh1re nos po/yk'ons tukdhi/. nu nos gwo:/s te/k'neh2-kwe ph2sdhi/. h1use/wo:m-ge nos de/h3tor k'le/wos dh3dhi/. h2e/k'mo: toy h1i/ns pro/ti pe/th2oyh1t. kh2n_mn_/bhis te gwr_ne/Homes.

h1yo/d deywo/s h1o/gwhim more/yet h1le/wdheyes dh2pno/m h2re/ydhont. pe/h2wr_ h2idhe/nt me:/ms kwe/roy pe/kwont me/dhu-kwe pe/h3tleh2 h1n_do/ g'hwe/nt. h2yuHn_k'o:/s h2ne/res g'hwe:re/s h1erkwo/m se/ngwhont. deywo/s gwe/mt dh2pno/y-kwe se/dt. g'e/rh2ontes spe/leh2 sokwe/yont. me/dhu pe/h3t me/wdt-kwe. h1e/ymos de/h3reh2 gwo:/s h2o/wins-kwe de/h3t. h2roh1dhe/yont h1use/wo:m de/h3tor n_sme/y priHo/s h1sdhi/.

dwo/h1 bhre/h2terh1e h1o/gwhim pro/ti ste/ygheta:m. h1o/ynos bhe/yh2t h1e/teros dhe/rst. h1yo/s dhe/rst so/ h1i/m g'hoyso/h1 h1ne/g'ht. g'hwe:/r me/rt we/do:r-kwe sokso:/d h1e/g'hs h1e/yt. bhre/h2te:r do:/m dre/mt h1yo/s bhe/yh2t h1ludhi/bhos-kwe we/wket. bhre/h2te:r moy g'hwe:/rm_ we/nt h1i/m sepelye/te. h1e/yso:m g'e/rh2onts ph2te:/r mo/wdoh1 sme:/yt. h1le/wdheyes bhre/h2term_ sepelyo/nt h1e/smey-kwe kwe/kwlom yugo/m-kwe dh3e/nt.

nu h1le/wdhereh2es h2e/kweh2es sre/wete. nu wos ish1ro/s h2n_gwhe/ys spe/rt. gwo:/s nos h2o/wins-kwe h2e/lete. h2e/kweh2es nos me/h2teres h1we/suh2 dh3te/. h2e/g'rons-ge nos wos we/desh1 pl_nh1te/. nu-kwe yu/H sweh2de/wih2es lewke/h2es-kwe.

suHnu/s ghro/mom k'le/wt bhe/yh2t-kwe. ph2te/rm_ pre:/k'st kwi/d dye:/ws stonh2e/yeti. ph2te:/r we/wket pe/rkwunos h1o/gwhim h2k'm_ne/h1 gwhn_sk'e/ti. we/do:r h2ew gwm_sk'e/ti deywo/s-kwe h1i/m h2ew gwhn_sk'e/ti. me: tre/ses po/s h2we/rseti. nu h2we/rst suHnu/s-kwe k'lush1ye/t. h2e/g'ro:s g'helh3wo:/s h2e/yeri h1se/nt. ne bhe/yh2t swe/pt-kwe.

h1sn_tyo/k'lewe:s h2yuHn_k'o/s h2ne:/r h1e/st. deywo:/m me/dhu pe/h3t Hwe/rdht-kwe. h2we/kst h1e/syo-kwe h2r_Hmo/h1 gwr_h2u/h1 bhuHta:/m. mo/ri gwe/h2t h2e/ngwhis h1yo/smi k'e/yo. h1i/m wih1to/ wiHro/s bhe/wgt. h1e/syo k'e/rh2sonm_ h1e/pt h1i/m-kwe skine/dt. mo/ri h1e/syo h1sh2e/nh1 h1rudhe/h1t. k'e/rh2sonm_ do:/m bhe/ret tewh2te/h2-kwe me/wdet.

n_/dhgwhitom deywo/syo k'le/wos. me/g'h2 h1e/syo h1no/mn_ h1ludhe/y. nu h1o/gwhim gwhe/nt we/do:r-kwe spe/rt. h2ne/res k'luto/m h1e/syo h1no/mn_ gwe/Hyete. h1e/syo-ge we/kwesh2 h1sn_tye/h2. nu h1e/syo k'le/wos me/g'h2 h1sye/h1t.

h2e/ngwhis dhubhro/y h2ewlo/y dhg'he/mi Hn_dhi/ k'e/yo. le/wks h2e/n ne h1e/st. de/h2n_wo:m sro/wons h3dn_tsu/ dhore/yet. deywo/k'lewe:s h2ewlo/m h1n_do/ ni/ h1e/yt. pe/h2wr_ swo/y g'ho/stoy bhe/ret. pe/h2wr_ kwe/k't bhe/yh2t-kwe. sro/wons le/h1t bhe/wgt-kwe. wiHro/s h1i/m se/kweto h1i/m-kwe kr_sno/y we/desi more/yet. pl_h1uye/h2o:m h2e/wyo:m h3e/sth1h2 h2ewlo/y le/ghet. de/h2nuh2 u/d h2e/g'rons upe/r sre/wet. nu wiHro/s so/h2wl_ upo/ h1ludhe/t. h1e/syo me/h2te:r h1i/m kwe/k't mo/wdoh1-kwe Hre/wdHt.

deywo/s h2e/yeri h1e/g'hs gwe/h2t. h1o/gwhim bhr_g'he/nti pe/runi wide/t. h1i/m gwhe/nt h1i/m h2k'm_ne/h1 gwe/lh1t. h1rudhro/m h1e/syo h1e/sh2r_ sokso/ybhos ni/ te/kwet. we/do:r luh1e/to dhe/g'ho:m-kwe h1i/h2 pe/h3t. me/g'h2 k'le/wos n_/dhgwhitom k'le/wos deywo/m gwe/mt. h2yuHn_k'o:/s h1i/m dh2pno/y se/ngwhont g'e/rh2ontes-kwe k'l_nwe/nt.

nu toy we/y gwe/nh2es gwe/Hyomes. bha/ge nos h2e/ngwhim more/yes. te/k'neh2 nos we/dos pi/ph3eti. pe/k'u nos h2me/lg'os de/deh3ti. h2o/wis nos h2wl_/h1neh2m de/deh3ti. nu te/wh2se ke/h2ilos h1sdhi/. n_sme/y h1we/sus priHo/s-kwe h1sye/h1s.

g'e/rh2onts h2yuHn_k'o/ybhos h2nr_bho/s h2roh1dhe/yet. h1yo/d deywo/s h1o/gwhim gwhe/nt te/k'nom h1e/sm_. ghro/mom pe/h2wr_-kwe memo/nh2e. dye:/ws kr_sno/s h1e/st h2e/nh1mos-kwe ghre/met. do/mes tr_me/nt do/ruh2-kwe pe/th1t. nu sokso/ybhos gwm_sk'o/nth2 we/do:r wido/m. de/h2nu te/kwome ph3me/-kwe me/h2teres-kwe nos sme/yn_t. h2yu/Hn_k'o:s wos te/k'noybhos k'o/d spe/lom wr_h1te/.

h2e/ngwhis dhe/h1sm_ dhre/wght. sh2we/ni le/ghet sme:/yt-kwe. se/kwt we/dos me/g'hye ne h1e/sti h2e/kweh2es h2epo/ lelikwe:/r. deywo/s h2epo/ gwe/h2t. h2e/g'ro:s h2sowso:/s h1se/nt tewh2te/h2-kwe Hre/wdHt. dhe/h1s dhro/wghom kwe/k't ghre/met-kwe. h2ew gwe/mt h1i/m-kwe gwr_h2u/h1 h2k'm_ne/h1 dhe/wght. h1e/syo h3e/sth1h2 Hrune/pt wo/dr_-kwe h1e/g'hs te/kwet. h1e/syo dhro/wghos h1i/m ne spe/rt. h1e/syo kre/pos k'o/rkeh2su le/ghet tewh2te/h2-kwe wo/dr_ pi/ph3et.
