kwo/s dusme/nesm_ h1o/gwhim pe/runi gwhe/nt? pe/rkwunos tewh2so/s h1i/m h2k'm_ne/h1 ke/lh2t. kwo/s-ge we/do:r de/h2nuh2-kwe spe/rt? h1use/wo:m-ge de/h3to:r h1i/h2 spe/rt. h1e/syo h1no/mn_ h1ludhe/y k'luto/m. nu h1i/m h1erkwo/h1 gwr_ne/Homes.

ph2te:/r dye:/ws g'hwe:/rm_ dhubhro/y mre/y kwe/k't. n_bhro/ns gwe/rh3t dhg'he/mi-kwe ne h2we/rst. nu me/g'h2m_ h2e/k'monm_ ni/ Hye/h1kt. mo/ri h1n_do/ se/ngwt wo/dr_-kwe ye/set. g'hwe:/rm_ gwe/lh1t h1yo/s h2e/n k'e/yo. h1e/syo kre/pos de/rt n_bhro:/s-kwe h1n_dhe/nt. nu h2we/rst sro/wo:s-kwe pl_nh1n_to/. ghre/h1mn_ h2we/kst h2o/weyes-kwe h1i/d h1e/dn_t. h2ne/res h2e/k'monm_ po/s wido/nt. h1i/m dhe/h1si stoh2e/yont h1i/m-kwe sepelyo/nt.

dusme/ne:s mre/y le/ghet h1e/syo-kwe we/dos pe/h2st. h1i/m kwis ne dhe/gwhh2t. nu h1n_gwni/s ish1ro:/y dhh1se/y mle/wHt we/dos h2sowse/yo:h2. h1n_gwni/s dhe/gwhet mo/ri-kwe ye/set. dusme/ne:s h1e/smo:d h1e/g'hs u/d h1ne/dht. ish1ro/s swo/h1 h2k'm_ne/h1 sth2e/h1yet. h1i/m gwe/lh1t so/-kwe ne/k'us se/ngwt. h1n_gwni/s h1e/syo kre/pos dhe/gwhet. h1n_gwni/s ish1ro/s-kwe k'o/m me/wdeta:m. tewh2te/h2 dwo/h1 to/h1 se/ngwheti.

h2e/wso:s-ge diwe/s dhugh2te:/r gwegwo/me. h1le/wdherons sro/wons h2e/g'rons-kwe spe/k'yeti. nu dusme/ne:s dhg'he/mi k'e/yor. bha/gom-ge gwr_nHte/ h1yo/s h1i/m more/yet. nu k'uh1ro:/y ke/h2nmn_ gwe/Hyete. nu nos h2e/wso:s h1we/suh2 bhe/royh1t.

dhugh2te:/r me/h2term_ we/kwsperoy pre:/k'st nu ku/dhe h1o/gwhis k'e/yor. sokwe/yet bha/gos h1i/m sh2imn_/bhis mre/y Hn_dhi/ bhe/ndhet. h2ew gwe/meti. h1yo/d gwe/meti h1i/m h2ew te/wketi. bhe/wdheti h2le/kseti-kwe nos. me: trome/yes ssdhi/. dhugh2te:/r h2e/nh1mom k'le/wt. h2ste/res de/mi upe/r luke/nt. dhugh2te:/r le/ght swe/pt-kwe.

h1suh1e/k'wos h1e/k'wons de/h2nu h2e/g'et. h2e/ngwhis we/dn_s h1e/g'hs h1ne/dht wiHro/m-kwe wih1to/. h1e/k'wo:s h2epo/ dr_h2e/nt. wiHro/s bha/gom g'huHe/yet stu/h2re me spr_dhi/. bha/gos h1i/m k'le/wt h2e/k'monm_-kwe diwe/s Hye/h1kt. kwr_/mim dhe/wght kwr_/mis-kwe h1i/m le/h1t. h1suh1e/k'wos bhe/wgt h1i/m-kwe swo/h1 g'hoyso/h1 h2we/gt. h1e/syo h1e/k'wo:s po/tim h2ew h1ludho/nt. dhh1se/y de/h3rom lewko/m h1e/k'wom po/s de/h3t.

h1eg'o/h2 poh2ime:/n dhe/h1s-kwe te sepelyo/h2. h2o/weyes de/h2nus pi/ph3onti. h2e/ngwhis-ge de/h2nu dhore/yet tu/H h1i/d se/lg's. nu moy te/wh2se h2o/wins wl_/kwo:d h2le/kse. nu toy sweh2du/ h2me/lg'os de/h3oh2.

deywo:/s k'o/m sde/nt. h1o/ynos h2e/g't kwo/s h1o/gwhim more/ye:ti. ne kwis we/wket h1e/yso:m k'e/rdh2 h2e/ng'hesh1 tre/mt. nu pe/rkwunos ste/h2t we/wket-kwe h1e/yoh2. swo/ns HeHk'u/ns h1e/k'wons ye/wgt. deywo:/s h1i/m wl_h1e/nt h1e/smey-kwe h2e/k'monm_ dh3e/nt. ph2te:/r dye:/ws h2e/g't su/Hnew moy h1idhi/ pr_dhi/-kwe. h1e/yt h1i/m-kwe pe/rt me/rt-kwe. nu h1i/m sepelyo/nt.

h2e/kweh2es sokso:/d h1n_do/m HrudHe/nt wi/Hre nos spr_dhi/. h1wesuk'le/we:s h1i/h2so:m wo/kwm_ k'le/wt pre:/k'st-kwe kwo/s wos dhore/yeti. dusme/ne:s nos h1o/gwhis sokso/y dhore/yeti. h1i/d Hrune/pt h1n_do/-kwe we/yk't. h2ewlo/s kr_sno/s h1n_do/m h1e/st. sso/ntm_ h1i/m h2e/n wide/t. h1e/syo k'e/rh2sonm_ skine/dt so/-kwe ne bhe/wdht. u/d h1n_dhe/nt we/wkont-kwe spe/rs nos k'le/wos toy me/g'h2 h1e/seti.

pe/rkwune te i/sh1re g'huHe/yomes. dh2pno/m-ge nos gwm_dhi/ h1yo/s h1o/gwhim ke/lh2s. sweh2du/-ge me/dhu ph3dhi/ h1yo/d toy g'hume/. we/yHm_-ge nos me/g'h2-kwe k'le/wos dh3dhi/. po/yk'ons te/we h2k'm_ne/h1 gwl_h1dhi/.

h1yo/d we/do:r spr_to/ h1le/wdheyes bhoydhe/h2m dhh1e/nt. we/wkont deywo:/y k'o/m h1erkwo/m ne/woy ye/h1ni kh2no:/mes. h1e/smey nos gwe/wo:m pr_h2wo/ns de/h3omes. h1e/yso:m suHne/wes h1i/m se/ngwhonti h1e/yso:m-kwe ne/potes h1i/m se/ngwhonti. me/h2teres swe/h2bhos dhugh2tr_bho/s h1i/m ne/kwti sokwe/yonti. spe/lom h1ludhi/su gwi/h3weti. g'e/rh2ontes h1i/m dh2pno/y se/ngwhonti. te/k'neh2 h1i/d k'lute/ deywo/m-kwe memn_te/ h1yo/s we/do:r spe/rt.

mo/ghew me/ne we/kwesh2 k'le/wse. h1yo/d h2we/seti sth2dhi/ dwo/h1-kwe ugwse/nh1e yugdhi/. yugo/m to/yso:m h2me/su dhh1dhi/. nu ye/wgos h2e/g'rom we/dhe. to/h1 h2e/rh3trom dukte/s h1i/d-kwe h3rog'e/yesi. to/h1 me: stune/ds to/ybhos h2roh1dhe/ye. h1yo/m stune/dsi so/ ugwse:/n mok's k'e/mh2t. h1yo/d so/h2wl_ upe/r sth2e/h1ye:ti tr_syo/ntbhos to/ybhos wo/dr_ dh3dhi/. h1yo/s swo/m ye/wgos pe/h2sti so/ g'r_h2no/m h1e:/dti.

k'le/wse su/Hnew pl_h1e/wih2n_s g'he/yomn_s dedo/rk'h2e. h1yo/d dhe/g'ho:m tine/h1eti nu h2e/rh3ye. h2susyo/ntm_ h2e/g'rom me: h2e/rh3yes. h2e/rh3trom h2susyo/ntyeh2i dhg'he/mi Hrumpto/r ye/wgos-kwe swidye/ti. h2e/rh3trom ke/h2ilom h2e/g'rom pe/r tri/s dukdhi/. nu se/h1mn_ h1e/smi le/gheti bhuHye/ti-kwe. h2erh3to/m dhe/wghti polh1u/-kwe g'r_h2no/m de/deh3ti. h1yo/d h2e/rh3trom kapye:/si me: we/rteth2e pro/ spe/k'ye. su/Hnew k'e/h2 we/kwesh2 memn_dhi/.

spore/h2m h2erh3to/m mok's bhe/re. h1i/d pe/r ste/yghe spore/h2m-kwe Hih1kdhi/. h1i/h2m_ ste/ge. h1yo/d spore/h2 h1e/smi h1e/pi le/ghe:ti g'hwe:/res wo/rmeyes-kwe h1i/h2m_ gwr_h3o/nti. h1yo/d h2we/rseti se/h1ye. wo/dr_ se/h1mn_ h2e/leti so/h2wl_-kwe g'r_h2no/m pe/kweti. h1yo/ti se/h1mn_h2 se/h1ye:si to/ti h2e/k'esh2 bhuHye/ti. se/h1mn_ ghre/month1 k'r_de/h1 me: Hye/h1ks. nu gwo:/s h2epo/ h2e/g'e.

dhu/gh2ter mok's sth2dhi/ no/kwts line/kwti. h2e/Hsh2m_ gwh2dhi/ pe/h2wr_-kwe spe/k'ye. h1yo/d h2e/ydhos ne/k'eti h2susyo/ntn_s h3o/sdons bhe/re. h1i/ns h1e/syeh2i loghe/ye h2e/ydho:y-kwe pne/wmn_ dh3dhi/. nu Hwe/rdht h3o/sdons le/yg'hti dhe/gwheti-kwe. pe/h2wr_ k'o/ymom gwhr_ne/wti kwse/pm_-kwe h2epo/ h2e/g'eti. pe/h2wr_ no/kwtm_ pe/r ph2sdhi/. n_/h2eydhos k'o/ymos ste/wdt.

dhu/gh2ter wo/dr_ h1n_do/m bhe/re. dhoHne/h2m h1e/smi dhh1dhi/ h1i/d-kwe ph2we/ni stn_h2dhi/. h1yo/d h1i/d ye/se:ti dhoHne/h2 pe/kwetor. h1yo/d h1i/h2 h1e/wse:tor te/we ph2te:/r ghre/meti. h2e/Hsh2m_ me: line/kws si/sde bhe/wdhe. h1yo/d h1i/h2 swe/h2deti h2nr_bho/s h1i/h2m_ h1ope/ye. ke/nkontes tr_syo/ntes-kwe to/y h2e/g'ro:d gwm_sk'o/nti. h1yo/s h2e/k'h3t so/ wr_g'ye/ti.

h2e/wso:s le/wkt h2nr_me/ne:s-kwe bhe/wdht. dwo/h1 ugwse/nh1e ye/wgt to/h1-kwe h2e/g'rom we/dhet. h1e/syo suHnu/s spore/h2m bhore/yet. ye/wgos h2e/rh3trom dhe/g'ho:m pe/r de/wkt. h2nr_me/ne:s h2e/rh3yet suHnu/s-kwe se/h1yet. h1yo/d so/h2wl_ upe/r h1e/st pr_kwe/wi Hn_dhi/ sdta:/m. puHro/m h1e/dta:m ph3ta:/m-kwe. nu h2ew wr_g'ye/ta:m. h1yo/d h1regwesye/t litta:/m.

h2e/g'ros h2susye/t se/h1mn_-kwe ne bhuHye/t. gwo/wes tr_syo/nt k'wo/nes-kwe le/ghyont. h1suk'le/we:s wik'po/tis dye:/m spe/k'yet h2roh1dhe/yet-kwe. dye/w ph2ter n_sme/y wo/dr_ dh3dhi/. ne/kwti h2we/rst. dhe/g'ho:m mok's tine/h1t se/h1mn_-kwe h2we/kst. gwo/wes k'wo/nes-kwe pi/ph3ont. h1suk'le/we:s me/wdt ph2te/rm_-kwe dye:/m gwr_ne/Ht.

dhu/gh2ter g'he/yo:m gwm_sk'e/ti. h2wl_/h1neh2m h1n_do/m bhe/re h1i/h2m_-kwe wr_mi/bhos h2le/kse. te/we bhre/h2te:r h3o/sdons pr_kwu/bhos we/g'heti. to/ns h2Hse/h2i loghe/ye h2e/ydhos to/ns h2sowse/yeti. h1yo/d ste/wdeti h2wl_/h1neh2h1 wose/yeso. gwe/wo:m k'le/ytreh2m pe/wk'eh2s h3o/sdo:ys ste/ge. po:/lHom k'le/ytreh2m we/dhe wl_/kwos po:/lHons g'hye/mi h1r_h1pye/ti. wl_/kwos k'o/ymom ne we/yk't pe/h2wr_ h1yo/smi dhe/gwheti.

pri/He me k'ludhi/ ke/h2ilom me: h2e/k'h3s. sh1me/ney h1we/su g'r_h2no/m dhh1dhi/. h1i/d wr_mi/bhos k'e/le. h1yo/s se/h1mn_ h1e:/dti so/ ne se/h1yeti. se/h1mn_ k'o/ymo:y h2e/h2usom. k'o/d memn_dhi/ te/we-kwe g'e/nh1mn_h2 gwi/h3woyh1t.

so/h2wl_ puHro/m h2e/g'roy pe/kwet. h2e/k'esh2 ple/h1dhet. wiHro:/s mok's h1e/g'hs gwh2e/nt. h1i/h2 skinde/nt h1i/h2-kwe h1n_do/m we/g'hont. po/tnih2es g'r_h2no/m h2sowse/yont. gwe/Hyont. wiHro:/s swidyo/nt dhugh2te/res-kwe to/ybhos wo/dr_ bhe/ront. h1yo/d h1regwesye/t wik'po/tis to/ybhos misdho/m de/h3t. nu h1e/dn_t sh2e/nt-kwe.

mo/ghew k'le/wse h1eg'o/h2 h1idhe/ pe/kwto:r. h2e/Hsh2m_ h2use/si h2re/ydhe. puHro/m ph2we/ni stne/h2mi h1i/m-kwe gwhr_ne/wmi. pe/h2wr_ pe/kweti pe/kwto:r bhe/wdheti. h1yo/s ne bhe/wdheti so/ h1i/m h1e/wseti. h1e/y suHro/s? h1yo/d h1e/y suHro/s h1e/seti h1e/y ne h1we/sus. h1yo/d h1e/y swe/h2deti wik'po/teyey h1i/m we/dhe. h1yo/d se/h2eti nu h1ddhi/.

dhu/gh2ter gwo/wes HrudHe/nti. h1e/g'hs gwh2dhi/ h1i/ns-kwe h2ml_g'dhi/. h1e/ymos se/ngwhe sth2e/h1yonti-kwe. tre/sonts kwid so/ ne de/deh3ti. nu h1i/ns h2e/g'rom h2e/g'e k'wo:/-kwe h1i/ns pe/h2sti. h1yo/d h1regwesye:/ti wl_/kwos gwm_sk'e/ti. nu gwo:/s k'le/ytreh2m h2ew we/dhe. po:/lHos swe/h2m me/h2term_ k'le/ytreh2i se/kwetor.

ugwse:/n h2e/g'roy le/ghyet ne-kwe ste/h2t. mo/ghus h1i/m stune/dt ne-kwe sth2e/h1yet. nu h1sume/ne:s gwe/mt. se/kwt h1i/m me: stune/ds. tr_sye/ti. mo/ghus h1e/smey wo/dr_ h1ope/yet pe/h3t-kwe. nu ste/h2t h2e/rh3trom-kwe de/wkt. h1sume/ne:s we/wket tr_syo/nt ye/wgos ne wr_g'ye/ti. ye/wgos lubhye/ h1i/d-kwe te lubhye/ti. mo/ghus-kwe h1e/wkt.

sh2we/ns kwe/kwlos we/rtetor h3e/pos-kwe nos h1i/h1 k'o/m we/rtetor. h1yo/d dhe/g'ho:m tine/h1eti h2e/rh3ye. h1yo/d gwhe/reti se/h1ye. h1yo/d g'r_h2no/m pe/kwe:tor h2e/k'esh2 skinddhi/. h1yo/d g'he/yo:m gwe/meti h2Hse/h2i sddhi/ h1ddhi/-kwe. nu h2e/rh3trom h3rog'e/ye se/h1mn_-kwe ph2sdhi/. k'o/d h1le/wdherosyo wiHro/syo we/rg'om.

h1yo/d h1regwesye/t gho/stis k'o/ymom gwe/mt. po/tnih2 h1i/m h2e/Hsh2m_ we/dhet. we/wket pri/He gho/stey sddhi/ ke/h2ilos-kwe h1sdhi/. h1e/syeh2s dhugh2te:/r h1e/smey dhoHne/h2m h1ope/yet. gho/stis gwr_h3e/t tr_pto/-kwe. po/tnih2m gwr_ne/Ht. deywo:/s toy h1we/suh2 dh3ih1e/nt. h1eg'o/h2 deywo/g'enh1e:s. h2use/si le/ytt.

h2e/rh3trom pr_kwe/ws wr_g'dhi/. pe/wk'eh2 Hrumpto/r pe/rkwus sth2e/h1yeti. h1i/m bhiddhi/ h1i/m-kwe h3rog'e/ye. h2e/rh3trom yugo/m-kwe yungdhi/. h1yo/d h2e/rh3trom h2e/g'roy Hrune/petor we/rg'om le/gheti. nu swo/m h2e/rh3trom ne/kwti h2use/si-kwe spe/k'ye. h2e/rh3trom h2me/si tl_nh2dhi/ h1i/d-kwe h1e/g'hs bhe/re.

h1yo/d de/ywog'enh1es g'r_h2no/m pe/kwe:si h1n_gwne/yey kwid dh3dhi/. h1n_gwni/s h1use/wo:m de/h3to:r. n_sme/ gwhr_ne/wti n_sme/-kwe h2e/leti. h1e/smey k'e/ywo:ys we/kwesbhis h2roh1dhe/ye. h1n_/gwney k'a:/m h2e/Hsh2m_ h2le/kse n_sme/y-kwe h1sume/ne:s h1sye/h1s. me/ne moy me/h2te:r k'e/h2 we/wket.

h1regwesye/t h2o/weyes-kwe h2e/g'roy le/ghyont. poh2ime:/n h2e/ydhoy si/sdet bhe/wdhet-kwe. h2ste/res upe/r luke/nt. wl_/kwos h2o/wins pro/ti se/rpet. k'wo:/ h1i/m k'le/wt Hre/wdHt-kwe. poh2ime:/n ste/h2t dhe/gwhontm_-kwe h3o/sdom h1e/pt h1i/m-kwe h2epo/ h2e/g'et. bhe/yh2t bhe/wgt-kwe. poh2ime:/n-kwe h2o/wins kwse/pm_ pe/r pe/h2st.

h1yo/d h2ne/res h2e/k'esh2 h1n_do/m we/g'ho:nti h1i/h2 sh2we/ni loghe/ye h1i/h2-kwe h2sowse/yeti. h1yo/d h2we/rseti h1i/h2 k'le/ytreh2m sl_h2gwdhi/. kwi/d h1i/h2 h2sowse/yomes? tinh1o/nt g'r_h2no/m suHro/m bhu/Ht. nu h1i/h2 h1n_do/m dhh1dhi/. nu g'r_h2no/m g'hye/mi h1e/sti g'e/nh1mn_h2-kwe ne ke/nketi.

h1e/st mo/ghus h1yo/s we/rg'om ne lubhye/t. h1yo/d wik'po/tis h2e/rh3yet n_/werg'os pr_kwe/wi Hn_dhi/ le/ghyet. h1yo/d h2e/k'esh2 skine/dt n_/werg'os k'e/yo. gwe/mt g'he/yo:m. g'r_h2no/m h1e/syo k'o/ymoy k'e/yo. n_/werg'os ke/nket. wik'po/tim gwe/mt h2isk'e/t-kwe kwid. wik'po/tis we/rh1t h1yo/s ne h2e/rh3yeti so/ ke/nketi.

me/ne bhre/h2tr_s su/Hnew dye:/m spe/k'ye. h1yo/d dye:/ws h1rudhe/h1eti h2we/rseti se/h1mn_ me: Hye/h1ks. h1yo/d h2e/nh1mos ghre/me:ti pe/rkwunos gwm_sk'e/ti. nu h2o/weyes trome/yonti h1e/k'wo:s-kwe yug'He/nt. h2o/wins po:/lHons-kwe k'o/m g'hidhi/. h1yo/d h2ste/res ne/kwti le/wkonti mok's h2we/seti. te/we ph2te:/r k'e/h2 wo/yde nu tu/H h1i/h2 wo/ydth2e. h1yo/s h2e/nh1mom wo/yde so/ ne h3sle/ydht.

no/kwts gwegwo/me pe/h2wr_-kwe dhe/gwheti. me/h2te:r g'e/nh1mn_h2 h1e/smi loghe/yeti. h1i/h2 h2wl_/h1neh2h1 ste/geti kh2ne/ti-kwe. g'e/nh1mn_h2 l_ghte/ k'wo:/ wos h2le/kseti. ph2te:/r wos h2ew gwegwo/me h1e/k'wo:s stesth2e:/r. dhoHne/h2 h1n_do/m k'e/yor ne kwis ke/nketi. wl_/kwos h2e/n h1e/sti. nu kh2ne/t h1i/h2-kwe swope/yet.

h1wesuk'le/we:s spore/h2m h1e/pt swo:/y-kwe suHno/wey h1suh1e/k'wo:y h1i/h2m_ de/yk'et. kwi/d k'o/syeh2i spore/h2i h1e/sti we/wket h1suh1e/k'wos. h1wesuk'le/we:s we/wket puHro/s polh1u/h2-kwe se/h1mn_h2 h1e/syeh2i h1se/nti. h1i/h2m_ dhg'he/mi k'e/le nu wide:/si. h1suh1e/k'wos spore/h2m h2erh3to/m Hwe/rt. spore/h2 h1ne/dht h2we/kst-kwe. h1suh1e/k'wos h1i/h2m_ wide/t dhe/mbht-kwe. h1e/syo ph2te:/r we/wket nu se/h1mn_ g'r_h2no/m bhwe/h2t g'r_h2no/m-kwe se/h1mn_ bhwe/h2t.

pri/He me/g'hye wr_g'ye/si misdho/m toy de/deh3mi. misdho/m toy dhl_gho/h2 we/rg'om moy dhl_ghe/si. h2use/si bhudhdhi/ h1e/g'hs-kwe ye/wgesh1 k'o/m h1idhi/. h2e/rh3troy me: k'l_nith2e/ wr_g'ye/. h1yo/d h1regwesye:/ti h2ew gwm_dhi/. nu sede/h1ye pi/ph3e-kwe. h1yo/s wr_g'ye/ti so/ h1we/sum misdho/m h1a/yto. h1yo/s me/d ste/h2yeti to/m h2epo/ h2e/g'oh2.

h1suh1e/k'weh2 po/tnih2 bhe/wdht moghu/bhos-kwe h2roh1dhe/yet. tu/H h2wl_/h1neh2m tn_nudhi/. tu/H ph2we/ney h3o/sdons bhinddhi/. tu/H-kwe h2o/wins h2e/g'rom h1l_h2dhi/. tu/H kwe/kwlom h2re/ydhe. h1eg'o/h2 pe/kwoh2. h1e/g'hs gwh2e/nt we/rg'om-kwe wr_g'e/nt. h1yo/d h1ludho/nt h1suh1e/k'weh2 to/ybhos g'r_h2no/m de/h3t.

h1yo/d h2we/st h2ne:/r h1e/g'hs gwe/h2t. ugwse/nn_s ye/wgt h2roh1dhe/yet-kwe. me/h2ter dhe/g'hom se/h1mn_ h1a/yso h1i/d-kwe h2e/le. n_sme/y polh1u/ g'r_h2no/m dh3dhi/ gwr_ne/Homes-kwe te. h2e/rh3trom h2e/g'rom te/rdt se/h1mn_-kwe h1i/m se/ngwt. h1e/syo po/tnih2 se/ngwhet.

de/ywog'enh1es k'e/h2 ph2we/ns we/kwesh2 k'ludhi/. wo/dr_ ph2we/ni me: Hye/h1ks. pe/h2wr_ so/h2wl_-kwe pro/ti me: h3me/yg'hes. h1n_gwni/s deywo/s. dhe/h1leh2 nos dedo/rk'e. h1i/m h2susyo/ntyeh2h1 pe/wk'eh2h1 h2e/le h2le/kse:ti-kwe te. pe/h2wr_ ne/kwti ste/ge h2use/si bhe/wdht.

ph2te:/r h2e/g'roy wr_g'ye/t. me/h2te:r dhoHne/h2m gwhr_ne/wt g'n_h1mn_/bhos-kwe we/wket. h1e/smey k'o/d bhe/retom ke/nketi. litta:/m. to/y wide/t me/wdt-kwe. h2e/k'h3t h1e/smi-kwe h1e/pi si/sdeta:m. nu h2ew wr_g'ye/t g'e/nh1m_nih1-kwe kh2ne/ta:m.

pri/He se/h1mn_ g'r_h2no/syo ph2te:/r. h2erh3to/m h1e/syo me/h2te:r. dye:/ws h1i/d gwhr_ne/wti h1i/d-kwe h2e/leti. h1yo/s h2e/rh3yeti so/-kwe h2e/g'rosyo poh2ime:/n. se/h1mn_ h2e/g'rom-kwe ph2sdhi/. h1yo/s k'o/d wo/yde so/ h3pe/wst. te/we-kwe k'o/ymos g'r_h2no/h1 pl_nh1ih1to/.

wiHro:/s pe/wk'a:s gwh2e/nt h1i/h2n_s-kwe skinde/nt. h1i/h2n_s h2e/n loghe/yont. h1yo/d tr_se/nt h1i/h2n_s h1e/wsont. pe/h2wr_ dhe/gwhet g'hwe:/res-kwe bhuge/nt. h1o/gwhis ph2we/ns h1e/g'hs se/rpet. nu h2e/rh3trom to/m h2e/g'rom pe/r duke/nt puHro/m-kwe Hih1ke/nt. g'r_h2no/h1 h3pe/wst.

h1yo/d gho/stis h1e/reti h1i/m pe/h2wr_ we/dhe. h1i/m ph2we/ni sode/ye h1e/smey-kwe dhoHne/h2m dh3dhi/. h1yo/d h2e/k'h3eti nu h1e/syo h1no/mn_ mle/wHeti. gho/stis deywo/ybhos priHo/s. h1yo/s gho/stim h2epo/ h2e/g'eti deywo:/s to/m gwhne/nti. h1e/smey h2wl_/h1neh2m loghe/ye le/gheti-kwe. h1yo/d le/yteti h1e/smey h2e/h2usom dh3dhi/.

swo:/y ye/wgesey h1no/mn_h2 dhh1dhi/. h1yo/s h1no/mn_ wo/yde so/ te k'lush1ye/ti. h1yo/d to/h1 ye/wgesi to/yso:m h1no/mn_h2 h2roh1dhe/ye. h1yo/d pe/rkwum h1ludhe:/tes to/h1 h2ew wodhe/ye. to/ybhos me: ghre/mes. to/ybhos se/ngwhe h2e/rh3trom-kwe dukte/s.

wiHro:/s swo/m we/rg'om r_h1dhe/nt. kwe/kwlo:s h2e/k'esh2 k'le/ytreh2m we/g'hont. h1suh1e/k'weh2 po/tnih2 k'le/ytreh2m puHro/h1 pl_ne/h1t. pe/kwto:r h1i/m h2re/ydhet gwr_h3o/nt-kwe. pi/ph3ont gwe/Hyont-kwe deywo/ns-kwe gwr_nHe/nt. de/ywo:s h1use/wo:m de/h3tores n_sme/y g'r_h2no/m h2ew dh3te/. gho/steyes pe/kwtorm_ gwr_nHe/nt.

h1yo/d dhu/gh2ter te/we wiHro/m gwe/h2esi h1e/syo me/h2term_ ste/rge. h1e/syo swe/sorm_ bhre/h2term_-kwe lubhye/. h1e/syo pe/h2wr_ ph2sdhi/. mok's sth2dhi/ we/rg'om-kwe wr_g'dhi/. h1ye/h2 bhe/wdheti se/h2 po/tnih2 k'o/ymom h1usu/bhis pl_ne/h1ti. h1e/syeh2s-kwe h1no/mn_ k'luto/m. k'e/h2 we/kwesh2 memn_dhi/.

pe/rkwunos upe/r ghre/met. h1e/k'wo:s trome/yont po:/lHo:s-kwe l_ghe/nt. h2e/nh1mos h2e/k'esh2 Hrune/pt. h1wesuk'le/we:s h1e/g'hs gwe/h2t h2e/g'rom-kwe wide/t. bhe/yh2t g'r_h2no/m ne/k't. nu h1e/syo po/tnih2 sokwe/yet me: bhe/yh2s. puHro/s n_sme/y h1n_do/m h1e/sti. h2ew se/h1yo:mes g'r_h2no/m-kwe bhuHye:/ti.

pri/He k'wo:/ h2e/g'roy te/we bhre/h2te:r. h1i/m h2e/le. h1yo/d le/ghye:si h2o/wins h2le/kseti. h2k'h2owsye/ti kwse/pi k'lush1ye/ti. h1yo/d wl_/kwos gwm_sk'e:/ti Hre/wdHti. h1i/m me: stune/ds h1i/m ph2we/ns me: h2e/g'es. h1yo/d lele/ykweti wl_/kwos h1i/h2n_s gwr_h3e/ti.

bhre/h2ter h2e/ydhos ne dhe/gwheti h3o/sdons bhe/re. ku/dhe h1e/yes h1se/nti? k'le/ytreh2i k'e/yror. bhre/h2te:r h1i/ns we/g'het h1i/ns-kwe ph2we/ni loghe/yet. h2e/ydhos Hwe/rdht swe/so:r-kwe dhoHne/h2m gwhr_ne/wt. ke/nkesi bhre/h2ter? ke/nkoh2. nu sddhi/ h1ddhi/-kwe. h2e/ydhoy-kwe sdta:/m dhoHne/h2m-kwe h2k'h3ta:/m.

h2e/rh3tro:y h1e/k'wons me: ye/wgs. h1e/k'wos HeHk'u/s dre/mt k'e/mh2t-kwe. ugwse:/n ne dre/mt ne-kwe k'e/mh2t. kwe/kwlo:y h1e/k'wons yugdhi/ h2e/rh3tro:y ugwse/nn_s yugdhi/. wiHro/s h1e/k'wons g'he/yt wik'po/tis h2e/rh3trom h1e/lh2t. ugwse:/n k'e/mh2t h1e/k'wos gwr_h3e/ti. swo/m we/rg'om wr_g'dhi/.

deywo/k'lewe:s we/rg'om h3re:g'e/s h2e/g'roy we/rg't. h2e/rh3trom de/wkt se/h1mn_ Hye/h1kt h2e/k'esh2 skine/dt. h1yo/d g'r_h2no/m h1n_do/m h1e/st h3re:/g's h1e/smey misdho/m puHro/syo me/h1tim de/h3t. deywo/k'lewe:s swe/h2ey me/h2trey h1i/m we/dhet. h1i/m wide/t me/wdt-kwe. sokwe/yet me/ne su/Hnew h1we/su we/rg'om we/rg's. te/we k'le/wos n_/dhgwhitom h1sye/h1t.

k'ludhi/ k'e/h2 we/kwesh2. h2e/g'ros h2ne/rm_ h2e/leti h2ne:/r h1i/m we/rg't. h2e/rh3trom dhe/g'ho:m de/reti se/h1mn_ h1i/h2m_ pl_ne/h1ti. g'r_h2no/m we/rg'o:d gwm_sk'e/ti h1i/h1-kwe gwi/h3womes. h1yo/s h2use/si le/ghyeti so/ kwid g'hye/mi ne wide/t. deywo:/s de/dh3n_ti wiHro/s-kwe k'e/mh2t. deywo:/s toy polh1u/ g'r_h2no/m me/g'h2-kwe k'le/wos dh3ih1e/nt.

h1su/menes ghoste/yo:m gwo:/s h2epo/ h2e/g'o:mes? h1i/ns me: h2e/g'es. h1e/yso:m ko/ryos me/g'h2s. nu kwi/d dhe/dheh1omes? g'r_h2n_tbhi/s k'o/m se/domes bhe/h2omes-kwe. kwye/h1tis h1ludhe/yey h1use/wih2.

kwi/d g'e/rh2ont kwye/h1tis? poh2ime/nes kwih1te/y ne/kwti le/ghyonti h1e/yso:m-kwe kwis h2o/wins ne h1r_h1pye/ti. kwi/d-kwe ko/ryos? h2ne/res ko/ryoy m_re/nt me/h2teres-kwe HrudHe/nti. su/Hnew h1i/h2m_ ph2sdhi/.

ku/dhe h1we/suk'lewes gwo:/s g'hime/ni h2e/g'o:mes? h1i/ns ko/lHonm_ h2e/g'e. h2e/g'ros h2e/n g'helh3wo/s? h1e/y g'helh3wo/s wl_/kwo:s-kwe ni/ ne gwm_yo/nti. nu h1i/ns h2use/si we/dho:h2. gwh2dhi/ deywo:/s-kwe te ph2sih1e/nt.

swe/sor poh2imne/s dhugh2te/rm_ kl_He/ni h1e/pi wido/m. h1i/h2m_ lubhyo/h2 po/tnih2 moy bhuHye/h1t. kwo/d h1we/dmn_ h1e/syeh2s ph2te:/r h2isk'e/ti? de/k'm_ gwo:/s h2isk'e/ti. to/d polh1u/. h1use/wih2 po/tnih2 de/mey priHe/h2 h1we/dmn_ de/h3omes.

kwi/h1 mle/wHmi? g'e/rh2ontes k'o/ymoy k'o/m se/dyonti. g'e/rh2ontes k'o/ymoy k'o/m se/dyonti. ko/ryosyo we/kwesh2 bhh2e/nti. gwm_dhi/ k'le/wse-kwe.

bhre/h2ter moy gho/stis suHnu/m gwhe/nt. h1i/m moy g'hoyso/h1 more/yo:h2. h1i/m me: more/yes. nu te h1e/syo bhre/h2teres more/yo:nti. nu kwi/d dhe/dheh1oh2? h1e/syo nos ph2te:/r misdho/m dh3ye/h1t kwye/h1tis-kwe nos h1sye/h1t.

kwi/d g'r_/h2enti ph2we/ni h1e/pi se/dyesi? h1eg'o/h2 g'r_h2e/ntih2 pe/h2wr_-kwe gwhre/h1yeti. h1idhe/ sddhi/ lewbhe/h2-kwe toy we/kwesh2 we/wko:h2. h1i/h2 moy we/wke. h1yo/s k'le/wseti so/ polh1u/h2 wo/yde. h1yo/s h2roh1dhe/yeti so/ kwid ne k'le/wt.

h3re/g' dusme/neses n_sme/ pro/ti gwm_sk'o/nti. h2ne/rn_s h1e/g'hs wodhe/ye. ko/ryos gwowsth2o/ysu h2e/nti sth2e/h1yoyh1t. h1i/ns pro/ti h1e/yomes? h1yo/d nos h2o/wins h1e/ponti h1i/ns more/yete. h1yo/d kwye/h1tim bhe/ro:nti h1e/ybhi sdte/.

h1su/k'lewes ko/ryom memo/nth2e? h1i/m memo/nh2e bhre/h2te:r moy h1e/smi se/ngwt. kwi/s h1i/m gwhe/nt? gho/stis h1i/m g'hoyso/h1 gwhe/nt. nu moy k'e:/r kwih1te/y h1e/sti. deywo:/s toy h1we/suh2 dh3ih1e/nt.

h2nr_/menes h1e/nos h1le/wdhis kwye/h1tim h2isk'e/ti. dhrughyo/nti nos? ne wo/ydh2e. bhoydhe/h2m nos de/deh3onti. nu h1i/h2m_ h1a/yomesdhh2 gwowsth2o/ns-kwe pe/h2somes. h1yo/s bhebho/wdhe so/ ne bhebho/yh2e.

kwi/h1 me/h2ter g'r_h2n_tbhi/s h2roh1dhe/yo:h2? k'l_nudhi/ po/s-kwe h2roh1dhe/ye. me: Hre/wdHs. h1yo/d-kwe me ne k'le/wonti? nu toy wo/kwm_ tl_nh2dhi/. h1use/ws wiHro/syo we/kwos k'luto/m.

bhre/h2ter h1e/nosyo h1ludhe/ys h1e/k'wo:s HeHk'e/wes. h1i/ns ne/kwti ste/h2yo:mes. h1e/yso:m k'wo/nes ne/kwti bhe/wdhonti h2ne/res-kwe nos h1r_h1pyo:/nti. dedwo/yth2e? ne dedwo/yh2e me/h2term_ nos memo/nh2e. kwi/s h1i/h2m_ h2e/le:ti h1yo/d me/romesdhh2.

kwi/d h1we/suk'lewes tewh2te/h2? tewh2te/h2 polh1e/wes do/mes. h1e/syeh2s h1le/wdhis sm_g'e/nh1e:s. kwi/d-kwe nos tewh2te/h2s h1no/mn_? po/tnih2 toy h1i/d wo/yde h1eg'o/h2 g'e/rh2onts.

h3re/g' tewh2te/h2 ko/ryo:d k'e/mh2t. h1i/d g'n_ne/h3mi. dusme/neses nos h1e/k'wons pe/ruti h1pe/nt. h1i/ns h2ew dh3ih1e/nt kwye/h1tis-kwe h1sye/h1t. pri/He toy wo:/kws h1use/wih2. h1i/ns h2use/si h1r_sk'o:/h2.

kwo/ti g'he/ymn_h2 h1su/k'lewes dedo/rk'th2e? polh1u/h2 g'he/ymn_h2 dedo/rk'h2e. tri/ns ko/ryons septm_/-kwe h3re/g'n_s dedo/rk'h2e. kwo/m lubhye/s? h1yo/s h1ludhe/yey kwye/h1tim de/h3t to/m lubhyo/m.

wi/Hre nos h2nr_me/ne:s dhugh2te/rm_ lubhye/ti. h1e/y h1suk'le/we:s? h1e/y h1suk'le/we:s h1e/syo-kwe h1e/k'wo:s HeHk'e/wes. dhugh2te:/r nos moy priHe/h2. h1e/syo do:/m we/yk'eti suHne/wes-kwe h1e/syeh2ey g'n_h1yo:/ntor. nu h1e/smey h1i/h2m_ de/h3omes.

de/ywok'lewes moy wr_h1dhi/ kwi/d wiHro/s dhe/h1t. h1o/gwhim gwhe/nt. h1e/syo k'e/rh2sonm_ swo/h1 g'hoyso/h1 bhe/ydt we/do:r-kwe me/lh3t. bhe/yh2t? ne bhe/yh2t h1e/syo k'le/wos n_/dhgwhitom.

h2ne/r h2e/g'ros h1idhe/ h2susye/ti k'o/ymom line/kwomes. ku/dhe h1e/yomes? dhe/g'ho:m h2e/n g'helh3we/h2. dusme/neses h2e/n tk'iye/nti. h1yo/d nos gwo:/s h1i/ns h2e/g'o:mes ko/ryos h1e/seti. nu de/h3no:ys h1i/ns pro/ti gwe/h2omes we/rh1omes-kwe.

me/h2ter moy wr_h1dhi/ kwi/h1 wiHro/m g'n_ne/h3oh2 h1yo/s dhrughye/ti. h1e/syo g'ho/stoh1 spe/k'ye. h1yo/s dhrughye/ti to/syo g'ho/stoh1 tr_mte/s. h1yo/d-kwe h1e/syo g'ho/stoh1 ne tre/metes? nu dhu/gh2ter h1i/m me: se/kweth2e.

ph2ter ko/ryos h1e/g'hs h1e/yti h1i/m se/kwo:h2er. ne h1eh1lo/wdhth2e. Hwe/rdhm_ g'hoyso/s-kwe moy HeHk'u/s. me/h2tr_h1 toy k'o/smi g'hime/ni sddhi/. h1eg'o/h2 te po/s we/dho:h2.

kwi/h1 k'e/ywe sokwe/yoh2? wl_/kwos moy dwo/yh1 h2o/wih1 de/ret. wl_/kwos moy dwo/yh1 h2o/wih1 de/ret. k'wo:/ toy ne/kwti ne bhe/wdhet? ph2we/ni h1e/pi le/ghet. k'e/ym_h1nos k'wo:/ gwowsth2o/m ne pe/h2sti.

swe/sor h1e/nos h1le/wdhis k'o/d we/rh1yeti. h1e/k'wons nos wos dh3te/. h1i/ns ne de/h3omes. h1e/yes nos h2e/wgmn_. nu ko/ryoh1 gwm_yo:/nti. gwm_yo/yh1ent. wiHro:/s nos ne dedwiye:/r.

kwi/d de/ywog'enh1es bhoydhe/h2? bhoydhe/h2 we/kwos deywo:/s h1yo/d k'lush1yo/nti. h1yo/s bhoydhe/h2m Hrune/pti deywo:/s to/m gwhne/nti. h3re:/g's nos bhoydhe/h2m Hrune/pt? h1i/h2m_ ne Hrune/pt so/ h1we/sus.

kwi/s ko/ryom dusme/nesn_s we/dhe:ti? h2nr_me/ne:s h1i/m we/dhoyh1t so/ HeHk'u/s. h2nr_me/ne:s kwim ne k'l_ne/wti. nu h1suh1e/k'wos h1i/m we/dhoyh1t h2nr_me/ne:s-kwe h1i/m se/kwoyh1to. tewh2te/h2ey h1i/d we/rh1omes.

po/tni ke/h2ileh2 h1sdhi/. gho/stey gwm_dhi/ priHo/s-kwe nos h1sdhi/. kwi/s tu/H kwo/syeh2s-kwe tewh2te/h2s h1e/si. h1eg'o/h2 kl_Hno:/m tewh2te/h2s poh2ime:/n. ph2we/ni h1e/pi sddhi/ h1ddhi/-kwe ph3dhi/-kwe. tu/H moy k'e/yweh2.

swe/sor pe/h2wr_ kl_He/ni h1e/pi kwe/k'm_. dusme/neses h1i/d dhe/gwhonti h1e/yso:m ko/ryos gwm_sk'e/ti. h2ne/rn_s dre/moh2? mok's gwh2dhi/ h1e/ymos-kwe we/wke gwm_sk'o/nti. k'wn_bhi/s h1idhe/ sth2e/h1yo:h2.

g'r_/h2enti moy bhre/h2te:r ghre/meti. kwi/d ghre/meti? h1e/syo h1e/k'wom h1e/pm_ h1i/m-kwe h2e/g'rom h1e/lh2m_. h1e/smey h1i/m h2ew wodhe/ye k'e/yweh2-kwe we/kwesh2 mluHdhi/. ghre/montes suHne/wes do:/m h1e/wsonti.

pri/He h1e/nos h3re:/g's swo:/y suHno/wey po/tnih2m h2isk'e/ti. h1e/smey toy dhugh2te/rm_ dh3dhi/ kwye/h1tis-kwe h1e/seti. dhugh2te:/r moy nos k'o/ymosyo poh2ime/nm_ lubhye/ti. so/ swo/m do:/m h2e/leti kwye/h1tis ke/h2ileh2m tewh2te/h2m h2e/leti. te/h2eh1 h2roh1dhe/yo:h2.

kwi/d swe/sor k'le/wos? h1i/d to/d h1le/wdhis h1yo/d k'lush1ye/ti. h2ne:/r me/rt h1e/syo h1no/mn_ ne me/rt. kwi/h1 me/g'h2 k'le/wos we/noh2? h1le/wdhim h2le/kse ghosti/bhos-kwe dedh3dhi/.

h3re/g' nos h1le/wdhis twe/ kwih1ti/h1 gwm_sk'e/ti. wiHro:/s wos nos poh2ime/nm_ gwhne/nt. h1e/smey de/h3neh2 de/deh3omes. bhoydhe/h2m deywo/ysu h2e/nti dhe/h1ete? h1i/h2m_ ph2te/ri dye/wi h2e/nti dhe/h1omes. nu kwye/h1tis n_sme/y h1sye/h1t.

k'e/ywe moy wr_h1dhi/ ku/dhe tk'e/ysi. we/dni h1e/pi tk'e/ymi. h1eg'o/h2 moy po/tnih2-kwe h2e/g'rom h2e/n h2e/rh3yowos. ku/dhe-kwe tu/H tk'e/ysi? kl_Hno:/m k'o/ymoy tk'e/ymi.

de/ywog'enh1es nos tewh2te/h2s poh2ime:/n gwo:/m ste/h2yet. kwo/smo:d h1i/m ste/h2yet? h1i/m swo:/d bhre/h2tr_s ste/h2yet. h1i/m h2e/wih1 h2ew dh3ye/h1t to/h1-kwe k'o/m pi/ph3oyh1ta:m. h1e/smey k'o/d we/wko:h2.

kwi/h1 g'e/rh2ont k'e/h2 h1e/wks? k'le/wsom me/nos-kwe moy bhe/wdhet. polh1u/ns k'o/ymons pe/r ste/yghom h1e/yso:m-kwe deywo/ns wido/m. nu h1i/ns pe/r ste/ygho:h2. h2uksdhi/ po/s-kwe gwh2dhi/.

h1su/h1ek'we tewh2te/h2 ke/nketi. h1e/k'wons more/yo:mes? h1i/ns me: more/yete. n_/h1ek'weh2 tewh2te/h2 ne/k't. nu kwi/d h1e/domes? gwl_/h2nons h1e/dte h2o/wins-kwe pe/kwete.

g'e/rh2ont ke/h2ilos h1sdhi/. bhe/wdhesi? bhe/wdhoh2 g'e/rh2ontes ne/kwti bhe/wdhonti. ku/dhe mok's ye/h2si? h2e/g'rom ye/h2mi h2e/rh3yo:h2. gwh2dhi/ dhe/g'ho:m-kwe toy me/h2te:r h1sume/ne:s h1sye/h1t.

swe/sor nos do/mn_s kl_He/ni de/mo:mes. kwi/d h1i/ns h2e/n de/mo:mes? h1yo/s gwm_sk'e/ti to/m upe/r spe/k'yomes. wo/dr_ h2e/n ne h1e/sti. wo/dr_ upo/ bhe/ro:mes. nu tewh2te/h2h1 h2roh1dhe/yo:mes.

kwi/d toy pri/He h1no/mn_? h1eg'o/h2 deywo/k'lewe:s. ph2te:/r moy me/g'hye to/d h1no/mn_ dhe/h1t. k'le/wos toy me/g'h2? k'le/wos moy ne me/g'h2 k'wo/nes moy polh1e/wes. kwi/d-kwe toy h1no/mn_? h1eg'o/h2 h1suh1e/k'wos.

k'e/ywe de/k'm_ gwo:/s ghosti/bhos lm_bhme/. kwi/h1 h1i/ns de/ro:mes? h1i/ns dwi/s de/ro:mes g'r_h2n_tbho/s-kwe h1i/ns se:mi de/h3omes. kwi/d to/ybhos h1i/ns de/h3omes? h1i/ns ne h1l_h2e/nt. g'e/rh2ontes nos h1e/yso:m h2e/g'rom de/yk'ont.

ph2ter ko/ryoh1 h2use/si h1e/ymi. su/Hnew gwh2dhi/ n_sme/-kwe h2ew gwm_dhi/. me: bhe/wgs. pe/rkwunos te ph2sye/h1t k'le/wos-kwe toy n_/dhgwhitom h1sye/h1t. we/kwesh2 toy memo/nh2e.

g'e/rh2ont kwye/h1tim h2isk'o:/mes ko/ryom-we: h1e/yomes. kwo/terom h1le/wdhim h2e/leti? kwye/h1tis h1le/wdhim h2e/leti. nu h1i/h2m_ h2isk'e/te g'hoyso/ns-kwe wos me: linkwte/. h1ludhe/ys me/ntins wo/ydth2e.

poh2ime:/n h2nr_me/ne:s k'o/ymoy tk'e/yt. pl_h1e/wih2n_s h2o/wins h2e/g'roy pe/h2st. swe/pnos h1i/m ne/kwti g'ho/rtoy h2e/nti h1e/pt. nu su/pniyom wide/t. me/g'h2s wl_/kwos swe/pnoy kl_Hne/s h1ne/dht. moyso/m le/mbht h1i/m-kwe h2epo/ bhe/ret. nu tr_mo/nts h2nr_me/ne:s h1ge/rt. h2o/wins spe/k'yet g'ho/rtoy k'e/yro. we/rh1t kwi/d bhebho/yh2h2e? su/pniyom me dhre/wght. ne le/ght kwse/pi n_/supnos h1e/st. wl_/kwos-ge mok's h1e/rt. poh2ime:/n bhe/wdhet h1i/m-kwe k'wn_bhi/s k'o/m h2epo/ h2e/g'et. su/pniyom moyso/m spe/rt. h2o/wins-kwe ste/rget ne/kwts-kwe ne bhe/yh2t.

h3re:/g's se/dyet n_/supnos-kwe h1e/st. dhe/g'ho:m h2susye/t ne-kwe h2we/rst. gwo/wes tr_syo/nt. nu dye:/ws ph2te:/r h1i/m swope/yet h1e/smey-kwe su/pniyom de/h3t. deywo/s su/pniyoy kl_He/ni sth2e/h1yet mle/wHt-kwe. de/h3nom moy h1idhe/ h1a/ydhi. nu toy h2we/rseti. bhe/wdht we/kwos h1e/syo k'e/rdi h1e/st. u/d h1r_sk'e/t h1e/smey-kwe pe/h3tlom me/lit-kwe de/h3t. nu tisr_su/ ne/kwtsu h2we/rst. ph3e/nt sh2e/nt-kwe. dye:/m-kwe gwr_ne/Ht. h3pe/wst-kwe.

dwo/h1 bhre/h2terh1e gwo:/s h2e/g'roy ph2sta:/m. h1yo/d h1regwesye/t l_ghta:/m supno/y-kwe h1sta:/m. gho/stis kwse/pi h1ludhe/t h1i/ns-kwe h2epo/ h1e/lh2t. h1yo/d h2we/st bhre/h2terh1e bhudhta:/m. h1suk'le/we:s se/kwt bhre/h2ter nos gwo/wes lelikwe:/r. deywo/g'enh1e:s h2e/g't to/ns h2isk'o:/wos. h1i/ns wo/dr_ se/h2gyeta:m. h1i/ns supno/y-kwe k'e/ym_h1nom gho/stim h2e/n kwk'ta:/m. to/ns k'o/ymom h2e/g'eta:m. ne h1ge/rt. swe/pnos gwo:/s h1e/pt swe/pnos-kwe h1i/ns h2ew de/h3t. h1i/ns Hwr_ta:/m n_/supnoh1 po/s h1sta:/m.

Hyudhsmo/syo po/tnih2 swo/pniyom ne/kwti wide/h1yet. h1o/gwhis swo/y de/mi le/ghet. bhe/yh2t h1ye/h2-kwe wo/yde ta:/m h1r_sk'e/t. mle/wHt. me/h2ter h1o/gwhim swe/pnoy me/ne de/mi wido/m. kwi/d swo/pniyom we/rh1yeti? h1sume/ne:s h1i/d de/yk'et. dhu/gh2ter me: bhe/yh2s. h1o/gwhis k'o/ymosyo pe/h2to:r. suHnu/s toy g'n_h1ye:/tor h1i/m-kwe h2le/kse:ti. h1e/syeh2ey-kwe g'n_h1ye/to. h1o/gwhis ph2we/ni h2e/nti k'e/yo h2we/kst-kwe ke/h2ilos-kwe h1e/st. po/tnih2 h1sume/nesey h2l_h1we/ns me/dos de/h3t.

dwo/yh1 dhugh2te/rh1e ne/kwti trome/yeta:m. sokwe/yeta:m. kwi/d me/h2ter no/kwts gwm_sk'e/ti? tre/sowos. se/kwt me/ne pri/Heh2h1 me: tr_mto/m. no/kwts h2use/s swe/so:r. dhe/g'ho:m wose/yeti h2o/weyes-kwe swe/pnoy k'e/yror. nu h2e/wso:s diwe/s dhugh2te:/r me/lh3t bhudhe/nt-kwe. ne dhrughye/ti h1yo/y sse/nti to/ns h2le/kseti. nu le/ghyeta:m swe/pnos-kwe h1i/h2n_s sle/h2gwt.

Hyudhsmo/s ph2we/ni h1e/pi le/ghet. tre/set dusme/neses polh1e/wes h1se/nt. nu swe/pnos h1i/m gwe/mt pe/rkwunos-kwe su/pniyoy h1i/m upe/r ste/h2t. k'e/ntrom g'hese/ri kapye/t. we/rh1t kwi/d Hyu/dhsme dedwo/yth2e? h1o/gwhim gwhe/nm_ h2le/kso:h2 te. mok's sth2dhi/ h1i/ns-kwe we/nesi. bhe/wdht ne-kwe tre/mt. h1e/syo k'e:/r Hwe/rdht. h2use/si ste/h2t dusme/nesn_s-kwe we/nt. me/g'h2 k'le/wos h1i/m h1e/rt pe/rkwunom-kwe gwr_ne/Ht.

h1suh1e/k'wos h2e/g'rom h1r_sk'e/t ko/sloy-kwe Hn_dhe/r le/ght. me/g'h2s swe/pnos h1i/m le/mbht. swe/pnoy pl_h1uye/h2su ne/kwtsu le/ghet. pe/th2leh2 se/ngwt pe/th2leh2-kwe h2ew h2we/kst. nu bhe/wdht k'o/ymom-kwe gwe/h2t. h2ne:/r de/mi sth2e/h1yet. h1suh1e/k'wos h1i/m ne g'n_ne/h3t sokwe/yet-kwe kwi/s tu/H h1e/si? we/rh1t h1eg'o/h2 h1suh1e/k'wosyo suHnu/s. ph2te:/r moy le/ytt ne/k't-kwe. nu dhe/mbht h2e/g't-kwe su/Hnew toy h1eg'o/h2 ph2te:/r. me/wdt h1i/m-kwe ph2we/ni h1e/pi sode/yet. se/kwt h1eh1lo/wdhth2e supno/y-kwe h1e/sm_.

po/tnih2 supno/y h1e/st. kwse/pi kwim h2we/yst. bhe/wdht se/dyontm_-kwe gho/stim ph2we/ni h2e/nti kwe/k't. tre/mt sokwe/yet-kwe kwi/s tu/H h1e/si? mle/wHt ke/nkoh2 tr_syo/h2-kwe. h1e/smey pe/yskm_ me/lit-kwe h1a/yt h1e:/dt-kwe pi/ph3et-kwe. nu we/rh1yet po/tni tu/H h1use/wih2. deywo:/s toy h1we/suh2 dh3ih1e/nt. h2ew le/ght swe/pnos-kwe h1i/h2m_ sle/h2gwt. mok's ne/k't. pe/h3tlom h2e/n sth2e/h1yet m_lite/h1-kwe ple/h1dheto. gho/stis deywo/s h1e/st. h1e/smo:d srobhe/yet me/lit-kwe swe/h2dt.

polh1uk'le/we:s swo/m dhe/gwhontm_ do:/m su/pniyoy wide/h1yet. pe/h2wr_ h1i/m le/yg'ht. h1ge/rt swidye/t-kwe. h1ye/h2 wo/yde ta:/m h2use/si gwe/h2t. to/syeh2ey su/pniyom h2roh1dhe/yet. se/kwt pe/h2wr_ dh2pno/m. polh1e/wes toy gho/steyes do:/m gwm_yo:/nti. pe/h3tleh2 me/lit-kwe h2re/ydhe. h1i/h2 h2re/ydhet ne/kwti-kwe h1ludho/nt. h1e/dn_t pi/ph3ont-kwe gwhre/h1yet-kwe. polh1uk'le/wesos-kwe k'le/wos h2we/kst. h1e/syo h1no/mn_ se/ngwhont.

k'wo:/ poh2imne/y h1e/st. ne/kwti swe/pnoy h1e/st k'wo:/ n_/supnos h1e/st. k'l_ne/wt h2k'h2owsye/t-kwe. se/rponts wl_/kwos h2o/wins pro/ti gwm_sk'e/t. Hre/wdHt h1i/m-kwe pro/ti dre/mt. poh2ime:/n bhe/wdht k'e/ntrom-kwe sle/h2gwt. wl_/kwos h2epo/ bhe/wgt. h1e/smey we/wket k'e/ywe k'wo/n tu/H me/ne pe/h2to:r. h1yo/d se/smi bhe/wdhesi. h1e/smey sme/rum pe/yskm_-kwe de/h3t k'wo:/-kwe h1e/syo g'he/sorm_ le/yg'ht.

h3re:g'e/s suHnu/s h1sug'e/nh1e:s no/kwtm_ we/ydset. h1e/syo me/h2te:r we/rh1yet l_ghdhi/. ne le/ghet kl_He/ni se/dyet. dye:/m spe/k'yet we/rteto. g'hwe:/r se/rpet h1sug'e/nh1e:s trome/yet ne bhe/wgt. nu h2we/st so/h2wl_-kwe me/lh3t. ni/ gwe/mt we/rh1yet-kwe. no/kwtm_ h2e/wsosm_-kwe dedo/rk'h2e. me/g'h2ih2 h1e/st priHe/h2 h1e/st. nu swe/pnos h1i/m h1e/pt.

no/kwts dhg'he/mi upe/r k'e/yo. wiHro:/s gwo/wes-kwe swe/pnoy k'o/ymoy h1se/nt. nu h2e/wso:s diwe/s dhugh2te:/r HeHk'u/ns h1e/k'wons ye/wgt. Hro/th2om g'he/yt no/kwts-kwe bhe/wgt. h2we/st wiHro:/s-kwe bhudhe/nt. poh2ime/nes gwo:/s h2e/g'rons h2e/g'ont. po/tnih2es h1i/ns h2ml_g'e/nt. wiHro:/s wr_g'yo/nt h2o/k'etm_-kwe duke/nt. gwe/Hyont h2e/wsosm_-kwe gwr_nHe/nt. diwe/s dhu/gh2ter n_sme/ h2ew gwm_dhi/.

deywo/k'lewesos ph2te:/r mr_to/. deywo/k'lewe:s h1i/m ne/kwti su/pniyoy wide/h1yet. ne/h2wi se/dyet. se/kwt su/Hnew tr_syo/h2. memn_dhi/ me. g'huto/m moy wo/dr_ me/lit-kwe dh3dhi/. ne/h2us line/kwt deywo/k'lewe:s-kwe bhe/wdht. h1e/syo k'e:/r tre/set. mok's h1e/g'hs gwe/h2t h1e/smey-kwe g'huto/m me/lit de/h3t. su/pniyoy gwe/mt we/wket-kwe. pe/h3m_ teto/rph2e. nu h2ew ne gwe/mt. nu deywo/k'lewe:s h1i/m dh2pno/y memo/ne.

swe/pnos mr_te/ys bhre/h2te:r. swe/pnos h2ne/rn_s ne/kwti gwm_sk'e/ti. h1i/ns kapye/ti h1i/ns-kwe mok's le/h1t. me/rtis h1i/ns h1r_h1pye/ti ne-kwe se/h1t. polh1uh1e/k'wos supno/y h2e/g'roy h1e/st. swe/pnos me/rtis-kwe h1i/m gwm_sk'e/ta:m. me/rtis bhe/h2t bhre/h2ter k'o/m sle/h2gwoh2. swe/pnos h2e/g't h1i/m me: sle/h2gws h1i/m teto/lh2h2e. h1yo/d h2we/st polh1uh1e/k'wos h1ge/rt h2epo/-kwe h1r_sk'e/t. me/rtis le/ytt h2ew gwm_ye:/ti. h1e/y h1use/wo:m de/h3to:r. h2ne/res h1i/m gwr_nHte/ le/h1t wos.

HeHk'e/wes h1e/k'wo:s polh1uh1e/k'wo:y h1se/nt. gho/stis ma/rkom kwse/pi g'ho/rto:d ste/h2yet. polh1uh1e/k'wos swe/pnoy h1e/st to/d-kwe su/pniyoy wide/h1yet. bhe/wdht g'ho/rtom-kwe h1r_sk'e/t ma/rkom ne wide/t. h1i/m ne/kwti se/h2gyet. h1i/m mok's kwe/k't. pi/ph3et ma/rkos-kwe pe/th2leh2 h1e:/dt. polh1uh1e/k'wos Hre/wdHt gho/stey me/ne ma/rkom ste/h2yes. bhe/yh2t wo/dr_-kwe h1n_do/ bhe/wgt h2epo/-kwe sne/h2t. swo/m-kwe ma/rkom k'o/ymom wodhe/yet. h1i/m sm_ru/h1 h2line/Ht.

h1sude/h3nos bhe/wdhet kreto/s h1e/syo k'e/rdi h1e/st. n_/supnos tri/s h1e/st. ph2tr_bho/s bhe/h2t kwi/d swe/pnos h1me/ ne gwm_sk'e/ti? h2g'e/nt h1ye/h2 wo/yde ta:/m h1idhi/. ye/h2t h1wesume/ne:s-kwe h1e/smey sokwe/yet. g'hwe:/r toy k'e/rdi le/gheti so/ kreto/s. ne/kwtey de/h3nom h1a/ydhi g'hwe:/r-kwe bhe/wgeti. h2wl_/h1neh2m me/lit-kwe kwse/pi kl_He/ni dhe/h1t. h1e/syeh2ey bhoydhe/h2m dhe/h1t nu swe/pnos h1i/m h1e/rt. h1yo/d h2we/st bhe/wdht me/wdt-kwe.

swe/so:r swo/m bhre/h2term_ su/pniyoy wide/h1yet. kl_He/ni k'e/yo k'e/mh2t-kwe. Hre/wdHt swe/sor h1me/ gwm_dhi/. bhe/wdht wo/dr_-kwe sle/h2gwt upo/-kwe ste/yghet. h1i/m h2e/n wide/t h1i/m-kwe tl_ne/h2t. h3sle/ydht tr_sye/t-kwe. h1e/smey h1i/d de/h3t pe/h3t-kwe gwi/h3wet-kwe. se/kwt kwi/h1 swe/sor g'ne/h3s? we/rh1t su/pniyom me de/wkt. nu h1e/syo swe/sorm_ ste/rgeti.

dye:/ws h3re:g'e/y sh2we/ns kwe/kwlom swo/pniyoy woyde/yet. kwe/kwlos dhg'he/mi upe/r we/rteto h1e/wseto-kwe. bhe/wdht dhe/mbht-kwe. h1e/syo po/tn_yeh2ey sokwe/yet kwi/d moy deywo/s woyde/yet? h1ye/h2 wo/yde ta:/m ih2ta:/m. h1i/d de/yk'et sh2we/ns toy kwe/kwlos k'le/wos. pro/ h1e/yeti n_/dhgwhitom-kwe h1e/seti. h1e/syeh2ey moyso/m h2wl_/h1neh2m-kwe de/h3t. k'le/wesh1 h3enHosto/s h1e/st.

so/h2wl_ gwhre/h1yet poh2ime:/n-kwe h1sume/ne:s ko/sloy Hn_dhe/r le/ght. swe/pnos h1i/m kapye/t h2o/weyes-kwe h3slidhe/nt. h1yo/d bhe/wdht h1i/h2n_s ne kwe/k't. trome/yet dusme/neses h1i/h2n_s ste/h2yo:nti. h1i/h2n_s kl_He/ni we/dni-kwe h2isk'e/t. h1e/syo k'wo:/ h1i/m se/kweto. k'wo:/ h2o/wins se/h2gyet h1i/h2n_s-kwe wide/t. h1sume/ne:s ta:/s g'ho/rtom h1n_do/ h1e/lh2t. mle/wHt ko/sloy Hn_dhe/r h2ew ne se/soh2.

po/tnih2 septm_/ h2e/boln_s swo/pniyoy wide/t h1yo/y swe/h2h1 g'he/sorh1e h1n_do/ sn_gwe/nt. h1wesume/nesey h1i/d sokwe/yet. h1wesume/ne:s h1i/d de/yk'et septm_/ h2e/boles septm_/ suHne/wes. septm_/ toy suHne/wes g'n_h1yo:/ntor h1e/yso:m-kwe h1no/mn_ k'luto/m h1e/seti. h1e/syeh2ey-kwe g'n_h1yo/nto. h2ukse/nt Hyudhsmo:/s-kwe bhuHe/nt. septm_/-kwe Hyudhsmo:/m me/h2term_ gwr_nHe/nt.

dhugh2te:/r h1e/syeh2s m_ro/ntm_ bhre/h2term_ su/pniyoy wide/h1yet. h1ge/rt Hre/wdHt-kwe. swe/h2ey me/h2trey mle/wHt bhre/h2te:r moy mr_to/. me/h2te:r h2e/g't su/pniyom k'e:/r dhrughye/ti. su/pniyom ne bhebho/ydhh2e. nu bhre/h2te:r k'o/ymom h1ludhe/t. ke/h2ilos h1e/st. h1e/syo swe/so:r me/wdt we/rh1yet-kwe h1i/d me dhre/wght. h2roh1dhe/yet swe/sor h2e/g'roy h1e/sm_. wl_/kwom kwe/k'm_ h1i/m-kwe more/yom. wl_/kwos mr_to/ h1eg'o/h2 ne mr_h2e/.

dusme/ne:s deywo/s h3re/g'm_ pro/ti ghre/met. h1e/smey su/pniyom ne/kwti h1ope/yet. su/pniyom h1e/smey bhe/h2t sth2dhi/. ma/rkons toy yugdhi/ dusme/nesn_s-kwe h1l_h2dhi/. h1i/ns we/nesi. bhe/wdht ma/rkons ye/wgt h1i/ns-kwe pro/ti h1e/lh2t. dusme/neses polh1e/wes h1se/nt h1i/m-kwe une/nt. bhe/wgt se/kwt-kwe su/pniyoh1 me dhre/wght. h2ne/res su/pniyeh2 me: se/kwedhwe.

Hyudhsmo/s do:/m ne/kwti h1e/yt. wl_/kwons k'le/wt bhe/yh2t-kwe. ko/lHonm_ ste/yghet. pe/h2wr_ h2e/n dhe/h1t h1e/smi-kwe h1e/pi se/dt. wl_/kwo:s dhgwhh2e/nt. h1e/smo:d bhih2e/nt upo/-kwe ne gwme/nt. n_/supnos h1e/st h1n_gwne/yey se/ngwhet. h1n_/gwney me h2le/kse dhe/gwhe-kwe. dhe/gwhet h2we/st lite/nt-kwe. ni/ gwe/h2t h1ludhe/t-kwe. h1e/syo kreto/s k're/mh2t.

so/h2wl_ dhe/gwhet wiHro/s-kwe h2e/g'roy le/ghyet. swo/pniyoy pe/h3tlo:d pi/ph3et. ne te/rpt. pe/h3t tr_sye/t-kwe. h1ge/rt h2susye/t. bhe/h2t su/pniyom wo/dr_ de/deh3ti tr_syo/nts h2ne:/r h1i/d ne pi/ph3eti. ste/h2t h1e/g'hs-kwe h1r_sk'e/t pe/h3t-kwe. nu h2ew le/ght swo/pniyom-kwe ne gwm_sk'e/t.

Hyudhsmo:/s de/mi supno/y k'e/yro. pe/h2to:r bhe/wdhet. kwse/pi k'le/wset. se/rpontn_s dusme/nesn_s k'le/wt. Hre/wdHt Hyu/dhsmo:s bhudhte/. dusme/neses gwegwme:/r. h1gre/nt swe/h2-kwe k'e/ntreh2 h1pe/nt. h1i/ns h1e/g'hs h2e/g'ont. h3re:/g's ph2tre/y we/rh1t n_/supnos h1e/s n_sme/ spe/rs. k'le/wos toy me/g'h2 h1sye/h1t. h1e/smey-kwe misdho/m ma/rkom de/h3t.

h3re:/g's h1wesuk'le/wesm_ g'ho/rtoy kapye/t. pe/h2to:r h1i/m ne/kwti spe/k'yet. h1wesuk'le/we:s pe/rkwuno:y kh2ne/t pe/rkwune me spr_dhi/. nu pe/rkwunos pe/h2torm_ swope/yet. swe/pnos h1i/m sle/h2gwt dhg'he/mi-kwe le/ghet. h1wesuk'le/we:s g'ho/rtom Hrune/pt bhe/wgt-kwe. pe/h2to:r h2use/si bhe/wdht gho/stis-kwe ne/k't. h3re:/g's ghre/met pe/h2to:r sokwe/yet. deywo/s me swope/yet. deywo/m kwis pro/ti ne memo/ghe. nu h3re:/g's h1i/m le/h1t.

h2ne:/r ke/nket le/ghyet-kwe. dh2pno/m su/pniyoy kwe/k't. pe/kwto:r pe/yskm_ pe/kwet h2o:wye/h2-kwe ye/set. h1e:/dt pi/ph3et-kwe se/h2t-kwe. nu bhe/wdht h2ew-kwe ke/nket. we/wket su/pniyom me h2e/let me/ne g'ho/rneh2 ne ple/h1t. nu wo/dr_ h1r_sk'e/t pe/yskm_-kwe tn_tlo/h1 h1e/pt. h1i/m pe/kwet h2e/k'h3t-kwe te/rpt-kwe.

pe/rkwunos swe/pnoy kl_He/ni h1e/st. me/g'h2s h1o/gwhis se/rpet we/dn_su-kwe h1e/pi le/ght. we/do:r te/rst gwo/wes-kwe tr_syo/nt. poh2ime/nes HrudHe/nt pe/rkwune h1gr_dhi/. h1i/h2 kapye/ti. h1i/ns k'le/wt bhe/wdht-kwe. h1o/gwhim gwhe/nt h1i/h2-kwe spe/rt. gwo/wes ph3e/nt h1le/wdheyes-kwe pe/rkwunom gwr_nHe/nt. pe/rkwune h1o/gwhim gwhe/ns. nu dhe/g'ho:m dhedho/wghe.

h3re:g'e/s dhugh2te:/r h1sug'e/nh1e:s supno/y le/ghyet. h2e/wso:s h1e/syeh2ey wiHro/m swo/pniyoy woyde/yet. HeHk'uh1e/k'wos h1e/st Hro/th2om h2e/g'et. bhe/wdht h1e/syeh2s-kwe me/h2trey h1i/d h2roh1dhe/yet. me/h2te:r mle/wHt so/ toy wiHro/s h1e/seti. h2ne:/r HeHk'u/bhis h1e/k'wo:ys mok's h1ludhe/t. so/ su/pniyosyo wiHro/s h1e/st. se/kwt h3re/g' moy toy dhugh2te/rm_ dh3dhi/. h1i/h2m_ de/h3t h1sug'e/nh1e:s-kwe h1i/m we/nHt h1i/h2m_ lubhye/t. suHnu/s h1e/ymos g'n_h1ye/to h1e/smey-kwe k'luto/m h1no/mn_ dhh1e/nt.

me/h2te:r swe/h2m dhugh2te/rm_ dhg'he/mi loghe/yet swe/pnos-kwe h1i/h2m_ kapye/t. h1e/syeh2i-kwe h2e/nti le/ghyet. h1o/gwhis ne/kwti h1i/h2m_ pro/ti se/rpet. g'he:/r bhe/wdhet h1i/m-kwe kwe/k't. g'he:/r h1i/m pro/ti dre/mt h1i/m-kwe gwhe/nt. me/h2te:r mok's bhe/wdht le/ghontm_-kwe h1o/gwhim h2e/n wide/t. tre/set g'he/rm_-kwe gwr_ne/Ht. pri/He g'hwe:/r h1i/h2m_ h2le/kses. ke/h2ilos h1sye/h1s. me/lit h1e/smi h2e/nti stne/h2t.

h1suwe/kwe:s h2e/g'rom h2e/rh3yet. k'e/mh2t le/ght-kwe no/kwts-kwe h1i/m swope/yet. bhe/wdht h2e/boles h1rudhe/h1n_t pe/kwonto-kwe. yugo/m Hrumpto/ swo:/s-kwe gwo/wes h3slidhe/nt. k'o/ymom ye/h2t h1i/m-kwe ne g'n_nh3e/nt. wr_h1e/nt kwi/s k'o/s h1e/sti? h1e/syo po/tnih2 h1ne/dht h1i/m-kwe g'ne/h3t. h2e/g't ku/dhe wi/Hre h1e/s? h2e/boles dwi/s pe/kwonto. dhe/mbht me/g'h2s swe/pnos.

poh2ime:/n h1wesuk'le/we:s wl_/kwom su/pniyoy wide/t. h1e/syo gwo:/s h2e/k'h3t. h1ye/h2 wo/yde ta:/m gwe/h2t h1e/syeh2ey-kwe su/pniyom we/rh1yet. mle/wHt wl_/kwos gwoldho/s gwoldho/s gwm_ye:/ti. h2le/h1wr_ h1n_do/m dhh1dhi/. h2le/h1wr_ h1n_do/m dhe/h1t. nu dhe/g'ho:m te/rst gwoldho/s-kwe k'o/ymom h1e/rt. ke/nkont h1wesuk'le/we:s h1e/ymos h2le/h1wr_ de/h3t. h1e/syo k'le/wos me/g'h2 bhu/Ht.

h1suwe/kwe:s we/rh1yet ku/dhe ph2ter so/h2wl_ ne/kwti h1e/yti? de/yk'et dhe/g'ho:m Hn_dhe/r se/ngwt. sh2we/ns kwe/kwlos kwse/pi h2e/n we/rtetor. h2e/n se/sti? ne se/sti. h1e/yti mok's-kwe h2ew me/lh3t. h2ne/res swe/pnoy k'e/yror so/h2wl_ bhebho/wdhe. dye:/ws nos spe/k'yeti. nu le/ght ne-kwe tre/set. nu sh2we/ns kwe/kwlom we/ydseti.

g'hwe:/r k'o/ymom gwm_sk'e/t h2o/wins-kwe gwr_h3e/t. wiHro:/s tre/sont h1e/g'hs-kwe kwis ne h1e/yt. nu Hyudhsmo/s deywo/g'enh1e:s mle/wHt k'o/syeh2i kwse/pi ne se/soh2. tn_tlo/h1 h2e/wisu h2e/nti se/dyet bhe/wdhet-kwe. se/rponts g'hwe:/r ne/kwti gwe/mt. tn_tlo/m h1e/smi upe/r Hye/h1kt h1i/m-kwe gwhe/nt. h1yo/d h2we/st wiHro:/s g'hwe:/rm_ kwk'e/nt Hyudhsmo/m-kwe gwr_nHe/nt. n_/supnos h1e/s nu-kwe ssme/s. h1i/m de/ront.

poh2ime:/n ghosti/bhos spe/lom ph2we/ni h1e/pi sokwe/yet. we/rh1yet spe/lom moy k'lute/. ph2te:/r moy ma/rkons h2e/g'roy pe/h2st. deywo/s h1i/m ne/kwti su/pniyoy gwe/mt h2e/g't-kwe. po/h2imen h1e/k'wo:s bhe/wgonti h1i/ns ph2sdhi/. bhe/wdht kwse/pi-kwe h1e/ysu h1e/pi sth2e/h1yet. dye:/ws ghre/met ma/rko:s-kwe tr_me/nt. h1i/ns kapye/t ne-kwe bhuge/nt. h1yo/d h2we/st h1i/m gwr_ne/Ht. k'l_nwe/nt n_/supno:s-kwe h1se/nt.

h1suk'le/we:s ne/h2wm_ h2re/ydhet. sn_gwn_ti/h2m ne/h2wm_ ne/kwti swo/pniyoy wide/h1yet. h1wesume/nesm_ h1r_sk'e/t h1e/syeh2ey-kwe se/kwt. h2e/g't we/dni h1e/pi me: h1e/ys. ne/h2wm_ gwr_h3e:/ti. se/dt. h2ne/res h1e/syo ne/h2wm_ sl_h2gwe/nt h1e/syeh2i-kwe h1e/pi h1ye/nt. pe/rkwunos ghre/met ne/h2us-kwe se/ngwt. sn_h2e/nt h1n_dhe/nt-kwe. h1suk'le/we:s h1wesume/nesm_ gwr_ne/Ht we/kwos toy me spe/rt. h1e/syeh2ey de/h3nom we/dhont.

h1wesuk'le/we:s kwse/pi h1e/yt ste/wdt-kwe. pe/h2wr_ kwe/k't do:/m-kwe h1e/smi h2e/nti wide/t. h1sude/h3neh2 h1e/smo:d h1ne/dht we/wket-kwe. gho/stey wik'dhi/ h1idhe/-kwe sddhi/. h1i/m h2wl_/h1neh2h1 wose/yet h1e/smey-kwe me/lit h1a/yt h1e:/dt gwhe/rt-kwe. nu h1i/m loghe/yet supno/y-kwe h1e/st. mok's h1ge/rt kl_He/ni k'e/yo. do:/m ne/k't pe/h2wr_-kwe ste/wdt. h1e/syo k'e:/r gwhre/h1yet ne-kwe ke/nket. se/kwt k'o/d swo/pniyom h1e/st deywo/syo-we: do:/m h1e/st. nu h1e/ymn_ h1e/yt.

ph2te:/r swo/s-kwe suHnu/s h1sug'e/nh1e:s h1e/k'wons ne/kwti ph2sta:/m. sokwe/yet su/Hnew k'e/mh2m_. h1i/ns spe/k'ye le/gho:h2. h1sug'e/nh1e:s bhe/h2t ph2ter le/ghe h1eg'o/h2 bhebho/wdhh2e. ph2te:/r supno/y le/ghyet h1sug'e/nh1e:s se/dyet h1e/k'wons-kwe spe/k'yet. swe/pnos h1i/m pro/ti se/rpet. ste/h2t h1e/k'wons-kwe pe/ri ste/yghet gwe/Hyet-kwe. h2use/si h1ge/rt h1su/g'enh1es se/s? ne se/sm_ ste/yghom gwe/Hyom-kwe h1e/k'wo:s-kwe h1idhe/ h1se/nti. h1i/m-kwe ste/rget.

h1wesume/ne:s kl_He/ni tisr_su/ ne/kwtsu le/ghet. ne h1e:/dt ne-kwe pi/ph3et. nu dye:/ws h1e/syeh2ey sh2we/ns h1e/ymn_ woyde/yet. h2n_ro:/m swo/pniyeh2 wide/t. ni/ gwe/mt nu-kwe su/pniyeh2 wo/yde. h1i/h2m_ gwm_sk'o/nti su/pniyeh2-kwe we/rh1yonti. h2roh1dhe/yeti h1i/h2m_-kwe k'lush1yo/nti. deywo:/s h1yo/m woyde/yonti so/ wo/yde. h1e/syeh2s h1no/mn_ k'luto/m.

k'wo:/ g'ho/rtoy h1e/pi k'e/yo. swe/pnoy Hre/wdHt tre/mt-kwe. dhugh2te:/r mle/wHt kwi/d ph2ter k'wo:/ wide/h1yeti? we/rh1t g'hwe:/rm_ h2e/g'roy wide/h1yeti. h2e/n te/kweti h1i/m-kwe kapye/ti. h1yo/s se/sti so/ su/pniyeh2 wide/h1yeti. nu k'wo:/ bhe/wdht ste/h2t-kwe. h2m_bhi/ spe/k'yet h1i/m-kwe h2isk'e/t. se/h2 h1e/smey sme/rum de/h3t se/dt-kwe.

nu te h1n_/gwney ne/mesi g'huHe/yomes. tu/H-ge h1use/wo:m de/h3to:r wiHro:/m gho/stis. h1yo/s dre/ws g'n_h1ye/th2e g'huto/m dye:/m bhe/resi. nu toy dn_g'hwe/h2s h3e/ngwn_ le/yg'hti. k'ludhi/ nos n_/mr_te h1erkwo/m. gwo:/s nos HeHk'u/ns h1e/k'wons suHnu/ns-kwe dh3dhi/. k'le/wos-ge nos n_/dhgwhitom h1sye/h1t.

h2e/ydhe dye/wi dhg'he/mi-kwe h1ente/r h1e/ysi. deywo/ybhos we/kwos bhe/resi. to/ns-ge nos ne/mos we/dhesi. h1yo/s deywo:/m sto/yghons wo/ydth2e do:/m nos ph2sdhi/. nu we/dhe le/wke to/ns h1idhe/. to/ns-kwe nos h2Hse/h2i sode/ye.

nu h1n_gwni/m h2yuHn_k'o/m deywo/m gwr_nHme/s. h2sowso/y dre/wi g'n_h1ye/to ne/kwti-kwe h2we/kst. nu dye:/m bhe/rg'ht h1e/syo bhe/h2os wiHro/ybhos le/wkt. n_mr_to:/s wido/nt mude/nt-kwe. dl_h1gho/m h2o/yu de/mi dhe/gwhoyh1t.

nu nos n_/mr_to:s de/ywo:s dh2pno/m gwm_te/. se/desh2 str_h3me/ sweh2du/ me/dhu g'hume/. sdte/ g'e/wsete me/wdete-kwe. h1yo/s wos g'he/wdeti to/smey h1we/suh2 dh3te/.

dye/w nos ph2ter h1erkwo/m k'ludhi/. te/bhye-ge me/dhu le/yHomes. te/bhye-ge se/lpos h1e/wso:mes. ph2ter wiHro:/m dhe/h1leh2 upe/r spe/k'yesi. pl_th2ene/h2i dhg'he/mi h2wr_sdhi/. g'r_h2no/m nos h2uksye/h1t pe/k'u-kwe nos h2uksye/h1t.

h2e/wso:s diwe/s dhugh2te:/r gwe/mt. pe/h2wr_-kwe h2e/yeri h2idhme/. lewko/m pe/h2wr_ h2use/h1 k'o/m bhe/wdht. h2e/wso:s h1n_gwni/s-kwe dhe/g'ho:m lowke/yetes. h2e/wso:s nos h1n_gwni/s-kwe h1le/wdhins h2le/ksoyh1ta:m.

sweh2du/ me/dhu dhh1so:/m swe/h2dos. kwe/roy m_lite/s g'n_h1ye/th2e. priHo/m te deywo:/s wiHro:/s-kwe pi/ph3onti. tu/H me/g'h2s n_mr_to:/m mo/wdos. nu deywo/ybhos pr_h2wo/m bhoh2go/m se/ykwomes. nu te pl_h1no/ybhos pe/h3tloybhos pe/h3omes.

nu pe/rkwunom k'uh1ro/m deywo/m gwe/Hyomes. me/dhu-kwe m_g'h2e/s pe/h3tlo:d pe/h3t. to/syo k'e:/r me/wdt k'uh1ro/s-kwe bhu/Ht. nu gwhe/nt h1o/gwhim we/do:r-kwe spe/rt. we/do:r-kwe dhe/g'ho:m ni/ sre/wet. pe/rkwunos k'o/d me/dhu ph3ye/h1t. h1ludhe/yo:m nos po/yk'ons gwhn_ye/h1t.

nu nos h2Hse/h2s h1n_/gwney de/mi sede/h1yesi. h2r_/g're g'e/rh2ontn_s te/k'neh2-kwe gwhr_ne/wsi. ma:/dsom nos pe/kwesi ne/kwti nos pe/h2si. h3e/ngwn_ toy h2sowso/m-kwe do/ru dedh3me/s. me: me/rs le/wke h2e/ydhe h2r_/g're.

ne/bhtew h2e/kweh2s po/tey k'ludhi/. te/bhye gwhermo/m h2me/lg'os sikwme/. h1yo/s de/h2newi tk'e/ysi do/h3r_ nos h1a/yso. pe/k'u nos toy h2e/kweh2m pi/ph3oyh1t. suHnu/ns nos de/h2newi me: h1e/ps.

h2e/ydhosyo-ge dn_g'hwe/h2s h1rudhre/h2. se/lpos le/yg'hti me:/ms gwr_h3e/ti. to/syo kr_sno/s dhuh2mo/s u/d bhe/rg'ht. deywo:/s dhuh2mo/m h3e/donti gwm_yo/nti-kwe. h1n_gwni/s n_mr_to/ybhos dhe/gwhoyh1t.

n_mr_to/ybhos ne/wom h1erkwo/m se/ngwhomes. ke/h2nmn_ nos sweh2du/ priHo/m-kwe. de/ywo:s nos h1h3se/s we/kwos k'lute/. h1no/mn_-ge wos k'luto/m k'le/wos wos me/g'h2. gwe/Hyontey wiHro:/y h1we/su k'le/wos dh3te/.

nu bha/ge h1ludhi/bhos bhoh2go/ns de/deh3si. deywo/ybhos pr_h2wo/m bhoh2go/m de/h3s. bha/ge toy dh2pno/m h2re/ydhomes. h2Hse/h2i nos sddhi/ me/g'h2 nos do/h3r_ dh3dhi/. h1use/wo:m te de/h3tor h1erkwo/h1 gwr_nHme/s.

we/kwsperos gwe/mt so/h2wl_-kwe se/ngwt. nu-kwe h2r_/g're do:/m ne/kwti lowke/yesi. kr_sne/h2m nos no/kwtm_ pe/r ph2sdhi/. wl_/kwom-kwe nos h2e/wibhos h2epo/ h2e/g'e. h3e/ngwn_ h2e/yeri g'he/womes.

dhe/g'hom me/h2ter ph2te:/r-kwe dye:/ws. g'r_h2no/m gwo:/s h2o/wins-kwe dh3to/m. usme/y me/dhu liHme/ k'o/d g'huto/m h1m_to/m. nu nos priHo/h1 h1sto/m te/k'neh2-kwe nos ph2sto/m.

dhe/h1s k'o/d sweh2du/ me/dhu ph3dhi/. h1i/d toy pl_h1no:/d kwe/ro:d g'hume/. nu muddhi/ priHo/s-kwe nos h1sdhi/.

h2e/ydhe dhe/gwhe le/wke dhe/gwhe. do:/m nos ph2sdhi/ h1i/m me: h1e/wses. h2sowso/m do/ru h1e/wse lukdhi/.

nu nos bha/ge gwo:/s wl_/kwo:d h2epo/ h2le/kse. gwo/wes-kwe nos polh1u/ h2me/lg'os de/dh3ih1ent. wl_/kwos-kwe nos h2o/wins me: h1pye/h1t. te/bhye k'o/d se/lpos dedh3me/s.

nu moy h2e/wsos suHnu/m dh3dhi/. k'uh1ro/s g'n_h3ro/s k'luto/s-kwe h1sye/h1t. h1e/syo-kwe k'le/wos me/g'h2 h1sye/h1t. nu te/bhye sweh2du/ me/dhu g'he:/m.

bha/ge ne/mesh1 tetr_pdhi/. te/bhye-ge se/lpos h1e/wsome. te/bhye ne/wom me/dhu sikwme/. nu ne/mos h1a/yso h1we/suh2-kwe nos dh3dhi/.

dhe/h1s nos dl_h1gho/m h2o/yu dh3dhi/. gwi/h3woyh1me so/h2wl_ wido/yh1me. te/k'neh2 polh1u/ns dino/ns wido/yh1t.

dhh1se/y pr_h2wo/m bhoh2go/m g'he/wdoh2. me/dhu pl_h1no:/d pe/h3tlo:d pe/h3oh2. me/dhu-kwe nos h1h3su/ sweh2du/ h1sye/h1t.

nu nos de/ywo:s we/kwos k'lute/. nu wos h1erkwo/h1 g'huHe/yomes. ne/mos gwm_te/ ph2we/ni sdte/. mudte/ h1sume/neses nos h1ste/.

nu wo/dr_ h2e/g'roysu le/yHm_. pe/rkwune h2e/g'roysu h2wr_sdhi/. de/h2nuh2 sre/woyh1t g'r_h2no/m h2uksye/h1t.

nu ph2we/ni h1e/pi le/ghm_. nu me le/wke ne/kwti ph2sdhi/. po/yk'os nos k'o/ymom me: gwm_ye/h1t. h2r_/g're te h2e/yeri h2e/ydhoh2.

nu nos h2e/ydhe po/yk'ons h1e/wse. to/yso:m do:/m dhe/gwhe to/yso:m h2e/g'rons dhe/gwhe. me: nos wido/yh1ent nu nos h1le/wdhins h2le/kse.

nu toy h2r_/g're h2me/lg'os g'hume/. le/wke gwhermo/m h2me/lg'os ph3dhi/. gwo/wes polh1e/wes ke/h2ilo:s-kwe h1sih1e/nt.

pri/He gho/stey se/desi sddhi/. gho/stey me:/ms h1ddhi/ me/dhu pi/ph3e. deywo:/s toy h1we/suh2 dh3ih1e/nt priHo/s nos h1sdhi/.

h2e/yeri sth2dhi/ swo/h1-kwe g'ho/stoh1 le/wh3e. h2sowso/m do/ru h2Hse/h2i dhh1dhi/. pe/h2wr_ h2idhdhi/ h1i/d-kwe gwr_nHdhi/. h1yo/d le/wkt h1erkwo/m gwe/Hye. nu h3e/ngwn_ pe/h2wr_ h1n_do/ g'hudhi/.

se/lpos pe/h2wr_ h1n_do/ tri/s g'huddhi/. nu pe/rkwuno:y me/dhu liHdhi/ mr_to/ybhos-kwe wo/dr_ liHdhi/. h2e/lbhit g'r_h2no/m-kwe ph2we/ni h1e/pi dhh1dhi/. dhuh2mo/s u/d bhr_g'hye/h1t.

moyso/m gwoh2mo/m we/dhe. h1i/m k'e/stroh1 skl_Hdhi/. h1e/syo h1e/sh2r_ dhg'he/mi sikwdhi/. me:/ms gwru/su pe/kwe. dhh1se/y pr_h2wo/m bhoh2go/m dh3dhi/ se/lpos-kwe ph2we/ni h1e/wse. nu h1le/wdheyes me:/ms h1e/donti.

me/lit wo/dr_-kwe kwe/roy dhh1dhi/. kwe/rom ph2we/ni gwhr_nudhi/. tri/ns dino/ns ye/se:ti. nu me/dhu g'huddhi/. pr_h2wo/m pe/h3tlom me: pi/ph3es dhh1se/y to/d dh3dhi/.

h1yo/s g'he/wdeti so/ deywo/ybhos pr_h2wo/m pe/h3tlom de/h3eti. nu g'e/rh2onts pi/ph3e:ti nu h2yuHn_k'o:/s srobhe/yo:nti. h1yo/s ne se/ykweti pe/rkwunos to/m me: k'le/wsoyh1t.

se/desh2 ph2we/ni pe/ri str_h3dhi/. deywo:/s h2e/n sede/h1yonti. to/yso:m se/desu me: se/ds. pe/h3tleh2 se/desu h2e/nti stn_h2dhi/. te/h2 h2me/lg'esh1 pl_nh1dhi/.

swo/h1 g'ho/stoh1 nigwdhi/ nu g'huto/m g'huddhi/. so/h2wl_ pro/ti me: h3me/yg'hes de/h2nu-we: h1n_do/ me: h3me/yg'hes. h2e/Hsh2m_ upe/r me: gwe/ms. h1yo/s k'e/h2 dhe/h1leh2 dhe/dheh1eti pe/rkwunos to/smey ghre/meti.

gwo:/m h2e/yeri h2ml_g'dhi/. h2me/lg'os ph2we/ni gwhr_nudhi/. gwhermo/m h1i/d pe/h2wr_ h1n_do/ liHdhi/. k'une/y loype/h2m dh3dhi/.

h1yo/d deywo:/s h2k'h3e/nt me:/ms h1e/domes. me/dhu pi/ph3o:mes ke/h2nmn_h2-kwe se/ngwho:mes. g'e/rh2ontes swo:/m ph2tro:/m spe/leh2 we/rh1onti. h1yo/s lug'ro/s h1e/sti so/ me/wdeti.

h1yo/d gho/stis ne/mos gwm_ye:/ti to/smey se/dos bhoh2go/m-kwe dh3dhi/. h1e/smey ma:/dsom kwe/ro:d dedh3dhi/. h1yo/s gho/stim h2epo/ h2e/g'e:ti bha/gos to/m me: k'luye/h1t.

h2Hse/h2s pe/h2wr_ h2le/kse. ne/kwti me: mr_ye/h1t. h1i/d we/kwsperoy ste/ge. ne/wom do/ru h2e/yeri h1e/smi h1e/pi dhh1dhi/. h1yo/d me/reti ne/wom pe/h2wr_ h3re:g'e/s k'o/ymoy h2isk'e/. h1i/d k'o/ymom kwe/roh1 bhe/re.

h1yo/d gho/steyes h2k'h3e/nt mr_to/ybhos loype/h2m sikwdhi/. to/ns kh2n_me/nh1 gwr_nHdhi/. gwoh2mo/m linkwdhi/ k'o/ymom h1idhi/ me/wde-kwe.

kwi/d g'e/nh1tri k'o/d? h1su/h1ek'wa se/h2 h2wl_/h1neh2. h2o/weyes h1i/h2m_ we/sn_tor wose/yeti-kwe nos. gwo/wes h2wl_/h1neh2m we/sn_tor? h1i/h2m_ ne we/sn_tor. h1i/ns h2ml_g'me/s. nu h1i/ns k'o/m h2me/lg'omes. tu/H lewbhe/h2 dhugh2te:/r.

kwo/ti gwo/wes g'e/rh2ont h2e/g'roy h1se/nti? ne/pot h1i/ns spe/k'ye wr_h1dhi/-kwe moy. septm_/ gwo:/s dwo/h1-kwe h1e/k'woh1 kwe/k'm_. ok'to:/w h1e/yes h1se/nti. h1e/nos sokso/y h1e/pi k'e/yor h1i/m-kwe ne wide/s. nu h1i/m wido/m. ok'to:/w gwo/wes dwo/h1-kwe h1e/k'woh1. h1e/wks.

kwi/h1 g'e/nh1tri gwo:/m h2me/lg'oh2? k'o/m sddhi/. h1i/h2m_ me: Hyowdhe/yes. h1i/h2m_ ghbhesti/bhis h2ml_g'dhi/. spe/k'ye. h2me/lg'mi. mok's h1e/wks. h1i/h2-ge me/wdt.

ku/dhe ph2ter gwo:/s h2e/g'esi? su/Hnew h1i/ns h2e/g'rom h2e/g'oh2. h2e/n h1e/dn_ti wo/dr_-kwe pi/ph3onti. h1i/ns h2e/g'o:h2. k'o/d k'e/ntrom kapye/ h1i/ns-kwe se/kweso. h1i/ns me: stune/ds. h1i/ns ne stune/doh2 h1i/ns we/dho:h2.

kwi/s me/h2ter dye:/ws? dye:/ws me/g'h2s h3re:/g's. dye:/ws ph2te:/r n_sme/ dedo/rk'e. dedo/rk'e me? dedo/rk'e te. me: kwim dhrughye/s. ne kwim dhrughyo:/h2. nu te dhu/gh2ter h1e/y ph2sye/h1t.

kwi/d swe/sor dhg'hme/y de/h3neh2 dedh3me/s? dhe/g'ho:m nos me/h2te:r. h1i/h2 n_sme/ h2e/leti. pe/th2leh2 h1e/syeh2s h1e/g'hs bhuHye/ti. n_sme/ k'lush1ye/ti? k'lush1ye/ti. h1e/syeh2ey lewbhe/h2 we/kwesh2 wr_h1dhi/.

kwi/d ph2ter pe/h2wr_? h1n_gwni/s deywo/s. de/mi nos tk'e/yti ne/kwti-kwe nos gwhr_ne/wti. kwi/d moy ghe/bhestim h1e/wset? h1i/d h1e/ps. su/Hnew pe/h2wr_ me: h1e/ps. h1i/d ne h1e/poh2.

g'e/nh1tri moy gwe/Hye. k'le/wse. h2e/wso:s diwe/s dhugh2te:/r gwegwo/me. le/wkos nos bhe/reti. nu to/d k'o/m gwe/Hyo:mes. h2e/wso:s diwe/s dhugh2te:/r gwegwo/me. gwr_ne/Hmi te to/d h1e/wks.

ph2ter nos mo/ghus su/Hm_ ste/h2yet. h1yo/s ste/h2yeti so/ dusme/ne:s. h1i/m kwis wide/t? deywo:/s h1i/m wido/nt ghre/monti-kwe. su/Hnew me: ste/h2yes. ne ste/h2yo:h2.

gwm_sk'e/ti kwis. kwi/h1 h1e/smey bhe/h2oh2? h1e/smey wr_h1dhi/. pri/He gho/stey gwm_dhi/. pri/He gho/stey gwm_dhi/. nu h1e/smey wo/dr_ dh3dhi/ h1i/m-kwe ph2we/ni h1e/pi sode/ye. nu kwi/d we/wko:h2? we/wke. n_sme/y priHo/s h1sdhi/.

kwi/d po/h2imen h1e/nod dhewso/m? mo/ghew h1e/nos wl_/kwos. h1e/y k'wo:/? h1e/y ne k'wo:/. k'wo/nes k'o/ymom ph2se/nti wl_/kwo:s h2o/wins gwhne/nti. to/smo:d dedwo/yh2e. me: tre/ses h2le/ksoh2 te.

kwi/h1 me/h2trey mle/wHoh2? ke/nkoh2. h1e/syeh2ey skwdhi/. me/h2ter ke/nkoh2. nu toy kwid h1i/h2 de/h3eti. bhre/h2ter tu/H ke/nkesi? ne ke/nkoh2 h2e/k'h3m_.

kwi/d g'e/nh1tri so/h2wl_ n_sme/ ne/kwti line/kwti? sh2we/ns kwe/kwlos dhg'he/mi upe/r we/rtetor. ne/kwti h1e/syeh2i Hn_dhe/r h1e/yti h2use/si-kwe gwm_ye/ti. kwi/s kwe/kwlom de/wkti? HeHk'e/wes h1e/k'wo:s h1i/m duke/nti. h1i/ns we/ydsoh2. ne/pot kwis h1i/ns ne dedo/rk'e.

h1yo/d g'e/rh2onts h1ludhe/t kwi/d ste/h2s? so/ pl_h1e/wih2n_s g'he/yomn_s dedo/rk'e. polh1u/h2 wo/yde h1i/m-kwe gwr_nHme/s. nu ste/h2oh2. mo/ghew ke/h2ilos h1sye/h1s.

kwi/d h1e/nod? se/h2 ne/h2us. h2ne/res wo/dr_ h1e/syeh2i h1e/n pe/r ih2e/nti. h1e/syeh2i h1e/n se/doh2? sddhi/ me:-kwe ste/h2s. h1i/h2 we/rte:tor. se/dm_ ne-kwe sth2e/h1yoh2. bhebhidhdhi/.

g'e/rh2ont moy we/wke. kwi/s h1o/gwhim gwhe/nt? pe/rkwunos h1o/gwhim gwhe/nt. h1o/gwhis h2e/kwa:s k'e/let dhe/g'ho:m-kwe h2susye/t. nu pe/rkwunos h1i/m stune/dt h1i/h2n_s-kwe spe/rt. pe/rkwunos me/g'h2s? h1e/y me/g'h2s. h1yo/d ghre/meti h2e/k'mones tr_me/nti.

kwi/d moy me/h2ter k'wo:/ ghe/bhestim le/yg'hti? dhu/gh2ter te ste/rgeti. h1e/y nos priHo/s. kwi/d h1e/syo h1no/mn_? h1e/smey k'luto/m h1no/mn_ dhh1dhi/ twe/-kwe gwm_ye:/ti.

kwo/ti po/h2imen pe/h2si? de/ywok'lewes k'm_to/m h2o/wins pe/h2smi. to/d polh1u/. h1i/h2so:m h1no/mn_h2 g'n_ne/h3si? h1i/h2n_s g'n_ne/h3mi we/kwesh2-kwe moy k'l_nwe/nti. h1i/h2n_s k'o/m pe/h2somes. h2use/si gwm_dhi/ h1i/h2n_s-kwe wide:/si.

kwe/h2 we/kwesh2 swe/sor deywo/ybhos we/rh1yomes? we/rh1yomes. de/ywo:s nos h1we/suh2 dh3te/. de/dh3n_ti? de/dh3n_ti h1yo/d h1i/ns gwr_nHme/s. de/ywo:s nos h1we/suh2 dh3te/. nu te k'luwe/nt.

ku/dhe g'e/rh2onts h1e/sti? h1i/m ne dedo/rk'h2e. dhu/gh2ter h1e/y mr_to/. ph2te/rn_s gwe/h2t. gwe/meti? ne gwe/meti h1i/m-kwe memn_me/. h1e/syo k'le/wos n_/dhgwhitom.

kwi/d mok's bhe/wdhs? h2use/si h2ml_g'me/s. su/Hnew bhudhdhi/ se/kweso-kwe me. le/ghesi le/ghoh2 ste/wdt. sth2dhi/. pe/h2wr_ do:/m gwhr_ne/wti. sta:/m.

kwi/d k'o/syo h1no/mn_? se/h2 toy ghe/bhestis. kwi/d k'o/d? so/ toy h2erHmo/s. kwi/d k'o/d? h2yu/Hn_k'e toy so/ g'he/dos. ghe/bhestis h2erHmo/s g'he/dos-kwe. tri/h2 we/kwesh2 h1e/wks.

me/ne me swe/so:r stune/dt. kwi/d te stune/dt? h1e/syeh2s h2wl_/h1neh2m h1e/pm_. to/d ne h1we/su. h1e/syeh2ey ta:/m dh3dhi/. h1e/syeh2ey ta:/m de/h3oh2. nu h1i/h2 ne ghre/me:ti.

kwi/d g'e/nh1tri h1e/nos upe/r? so/ n_bhro/s mok's h2we/rseti. h2o/wins do:/m h1n_do/ we/dhe. h1i/h2es n_bhro:/d dedwiye:/r? h1e/smo:d ne dedwiye:/r h2yuHn_k'e/h2es de stude/nt. nu h1i/h2n_s we/dhoh2.

bhre/h2ter gho/stis gwegwo/me. kwi/d h1i/m ph2we/ni h1e/pi sode/yomes? h1yo/s gho/stim h2epo/ h2e/g'eti to/syo k'le/wos ne/k't. nu h1e/smey wo/dr_ de/h3oh2. to/d dhh1dhi/ do:/m-kwe nos gwr_ne/Heti.

gho/stis moy de/h3nom de/h3t. kwi/h1 h1e/smey bhe/h2oh2? h1e/smey we/wke. tu/H h1use/wo:m de/h3to:r. tu/H h1use/wo:m de/h3to:r. nu h1e/y me/wdeti h1e/smey-kwe priHo/s h1e/sesi.

swe/sor h3re:/g's gwm_sk'e/ti. kwi/d h1e/smey sokwe/yo:h2? sokwe/ye. h3re/g' ke/h2ilos h1sdhi/. h3re/g' ke/h2ilos h1sdhi/. nu se/kweti. mo/ghew ke/h2ilos h1sdhi/.

kwi/s h1e/nos wiHro/s? so/ toy swesre/s wiHro/s h1e/y moy g'm_ro/s. kwi/s moy swe/so:r h1e/syo me/h2trey? h1i/h2 h1e/syeh2ey snuso/s. g'm_ro/s snuso/s-kwe. nu k'e/h2 we/kwesh2 h1e/wkm_. h1su/menes h1i/h2 h1e/wks.

ku/dhe g'n_h1yo/h2e? k'o/smi de/mi g'hime/ni g'n_h1ye/th2e. ph2te:/r toy twe/ tl_ne/h2t h1no/mn_-kwe toy dhe/h1t. kwo/d h1no/mn_ dhe/h1t? tu/H h1suk'le/we:s. k'le/wos toy me/g'h2 h1sye/h1t.

kwi/d h1we/suk'lewes tu/H se/nos? g'he/yomes wiHro/ns g'orh2e/yonti. h2yu/Hn_k'e-ge se/nos bhu/Hesi. nu polh1u/h2 we/ydoh2? polh1u/h2 we/ydesi h1yo/d g'e/rh2ontn_s k'le/wsesi.

kwi/h1 bhre/h2ter sne/h2si? we/dni h1e/pi l_ghdhi/ ghe/bhestih1-kwe toy Hyowdhe/ye. dedwo/yh2e. me: tre/ses teto/lh2h2e te. spe/k'ye sne/h2mi. mok's h1e/wks.

kwi/d g'e/nh1tri pe/kwesi? su/Hm_ pe/kwoh2. kwi/s h1i/m more/yet? bhre/h2te:r toy h1i/m dhg'hye/s more/yet. h1i/m k'o/syeh2i ne/kwti ghosti/bhis k'o/m h1e/domes. kwo/m h1e/y pe/kwe:tor? h1yo/d h1regwesye/ti.

kwi/d k'o/s swo/nh2os ne/kwti? k'wo/nes HrudHe/nti wl_/kwom wido/nt. h1e/y do:/m we/yk'eti? ne we/yk'eti. k'wo/nes h2ne/res-kwe n_sme/ h2le/ksonti. h1su/h1ek'wa l_ghdhi/ swope/yo:h2-kwe te.

h1yo/d g'e/rh2ont tu/H h2yuHn_k'o/s h1e/s kwi/d dhe/dheh1s? k'o/ymosyo h2o/wins pe/h2sm_. nu wl_/kwos gwe/mt h1i/m-kwe sokso:/ys h2epo/ g'he:/m. bhe/yh2s? ne bhe/yh2m_ h2yuHn_k'o/s HeHk'u/s-kwe h1e/sm_.

kwi/d h2e/g'roy dhe/dheh1si? h2e/g'rom h2e/rh3yoh2. kwi/d h2e/rh3yesi? dhe/g'ho:m dhe/wgheti h1e/domes-kwe. k'o/m h2e/rh3yo:mes. su/Hnew tu/H h2ye/wHo:. h1yo/d h2we/ksesi h2e/rh3ye:si.

wose/ye me ste/wdt. de/ywog'enh1es wose/yeso. nu tu/H me/g'h2ih2. h2wl_/h1neh2 me gwhr_ne/wti. spe/k'ye wose/yoh2e. gwr_ne/Hmi te.

bhre/h2ter kwo/terom h1e/k'wom ye/wgoh2? se/nom yugdhi/. h2ye/wHo: dedwo/ye. kwi/d dedwo/ye? yugo/m ne wo/yde. h1i/m yugo/m we/dhe h1e/wketi-kwe.

kwo/ti h1e/k'wo:s n_sme/y h1se/nti? h1ne/wn_ h1e/k'wo:s kwetwo/res-kwe po:/lHo:s n_sme/y h1se/nti. kwo/ti su/Hes n_sme/y h1se/nti? tre/yes su/Hes n_sme/y h1se/nti. to/y po:/lHo:s h2yuHn_k'o:/s? h1e/yes h2yuHn_k'o:/s pe/ruti g'n_h1yo/nto. dhewse/h2 nos wo/ydth2e.

kwi/d h3re/g' bhoydhe/h2? bhoydhe/h2 we/kwos h2ne:/r diwe/y h1yo/d de/deh3ti. h1yo/s swe/h2m bhoydhe/h2m Hrune/pti deywo:/s to/smey ghre/monti. we/kwos moy ne Hrune/poh2. ne/pot to/d memn_dhi/.

poh2imne/s we/kwesh2 h1e/wkm_. h1i/h2 moy gwe/Hye. wl_/kwos gwm_sk'e/ti h2ne/res h1i/m se/kwontor. poh2ime:/n h1i/m swo/h1 g'hoyso/h1 gwhe/nti. te/h2 h1we/suh2. nu toy bhre/h2trey h1i/h2 gwe/Hye.

h1suk'le/we:s Hyudhsmo/s kwse/pi ste/h2t. dwo/h1 HeHk'u/h1 h1e/k'woh1 ye/wgt de/h2nu-kwe pe/rt ghoste/ys-kwe gwowsth2o/m h1ludhe/t. poh2ime/nes h2e/n sse/nt. wl_/kwo:s kr_sno/ysu kl_Hn_su/ HrudHe/nt. nu pe/k'u u/d g'he/yt. k'wo:/ h1i/m k'le/wt Hre/wdHt-kwe poh2ime/nes-kwe bhudhe/nt. we/rh1t to/ybhos. po/h2imenes me: tr_mte/. h1e/sh2r_ wos ne we/k'mi. h1esh2o/syo wos gwo:/s we/k'mi. h1i/m wre/gont h1i/m ne dhgwhh2e/nt h1e/syo h1e/k'woh1 HeHk'u/h1 h1sta:/m. nu lo/wksneh2 sto/yghom lowke/yet h1i/d-kwe k'o/ymom h1e/lh2t. h1e/syo k'e:/r mudro/m h2ewse/r h1e/st. h1e/syo me/h2te:r h1i/d wide/h1yet we/rh1t-kwe. me/ne te te/k'nom deywo:/s lubhyo/nti. diwe/y bhoh2go/m h1ope/ye.

dwo/h1 Hyudhsmo/h1 pr_we/ni sth2e/h1yeta:m. pre:/k'st h2nr_me/ne:s. kwi/s tu/H? h1eg'o/h2 h1sn_tyo/k'lewe:s h3re:g'e/s suHnu/s. k'o/ns gwo:/s te/we gho/rto:d we/dhom h1i/ns-kwe toy ne de/h3oh2. nu h2nr_me/ne:s mle/wHt. h1idhe/ wiko:/wos. ph2te:/r nos dye:/ws spe/k'yoyh1t. h1sn_tyo/k'lewe:s we/wket. kwi/d we/dni me/rowos? de/k'm_ toy ma/rkons h1e/ybhi h2m_bhi/ de/h3oh2. nu h2nr_me/ne:s ma/rkons h1a/yto g'ho/stons yungta:/m h1o/ytom-kwe h3m_nh3ta:/m. nu wi/ ih2ta:/m gwo/wes-kwe h1sn_tyo/k'lewesm_ se/kwonto. h1sn_tyo/k'lewe:s wo/dr_ spe/k'yet sme:/yt-kwe. gwi/h3woh2 ma/rko:s-kwe h2ew g'n_h1yo/ntor.

wiHro:/s gwo:/s wik'po/teys dhh1me/ni bhe/h2gont. wik'po/tis pr_h2wo/m bhoh2go/m h1a/yto moghu/bhos-kwe ukse/nn_s de/h3t. h1yo/s gho/rtom pe/h2st to/smey dwo/yh1 h2o/wih1 dh3e/nt. nu mo/ghewes h1i/m gwr_nHe/nt. h1use/wo:m de/h3tor te/we k'le/wos n_/dhgwhitom h1sye/h1t. mle/wHt. gwo/wes h2n_ro:/m k'le/wos. h1yo/s h1i/ns ghoste/ys h2e/g'eti so/ bhoh2go/m bhgih1to/. me:/ms ne/kwti h1e/dn_t me/dhu-kwe ph3e/nt g'e/rh2onts-kwe ke/h2nmn_ kh2ne/t. nu swe/pnos mo/ghuns gwe/mt pe/h2wr_-kwe ste/wdt.

h2ye/wHo: poh2ime:/n gho/stipoteys do:/m h2ewse/r dre/mt. gho/stipotey bho/res ne/kwti h1ludho/nt pe/k'u-kwe nos h2epo/ h2e/g'ont. bhe/wgm_ twe/-kwe h1e/rm_. gho/stipotis pre:/k'st. kwe/yes bho/res? ku/dhe h1i/d duke/nt? poh2ime:/n bhe/h2t. to/y dusk'le/wesos h2ne/res h1se/nt h1i/d bhe/rg'hons pro/ti duke/nt. nu gho/stipotis gwe/ru kapye/t g'n_h1yo/m-kwe g'huHe/yet. h1i/ns HeHk'u/bhis h1e/k'wo:ys wre/go:mes. g'n_h1yo/m ma/rkons ye/wgt poh2ime:/n-kwe h1e/ymos sto/yghom de/yk'et. sto/yghom e/nu te/kwont k'wo/nes-kwe pro/ te/kwont. h1e/yso:m gwe/ruh2 sh2we/ni le/h2pt. kr_sne/h2es h2e/weyes sto/yghoy upe/r HrudHe/nt.

h1wesuk'le/wesos Hyudhsmo:/s ste/h2dhh1osyo lo/ysa:s se/kwonto. h2e/g'rons pe/r te/kwont. ko/nHim we/kwsperoy bhe/rg'hoy kwk'e/nt. nu h2roh1dhe/yet h1wesuk'le/we:s. pri/Ho:s bho/res pe/h2uro:s we/y-kwe polh1e/wes. h1e/ymos me: bhebhe/yh2omes. nu bhe/rg'hom ste/yghont po/yk'o:s-kwe bhuge/nt. pe/h2wr_ h2e/n dhh1e/nt me:/ms-kwe pe/kwont. nu ste/h2dhh1om k'o/ymom h1l_h2e/nt mude/nt-kwe. h1wesuk'le/we:s Hre/wdHt. ne kwid lelo/ykwe. h1e/syo priHo:/s sme/yn_t h1i/m-kwe gwr_nHe/nt. to/syeh2i ne/kwti h2str_su/ Hn_dhi/ sse/nt.

wiHro:/s sweh2du/ me/dhu dh2pno/y ph3e/nt. nu deywo/g'enh1e:s ste/h2t bhe/h2t-kwe. pri/Ho:s me k'lute/. k'm_to/m h2o/wins ghoste/yo:m gho/rtoybhos g'he:/m. tri/ns h2ne/rn_s pr_we/ni gwhe/nm_. kwi/s k'o/d k'le/wos me/d sle/h2gweti? me/ne h1no/mn_ k'luto/m me/ne-kwe k'le/wos me/g'h2. g'e/rh2onts sme:/yt sokwe/yet-kwe. we/kwesh2 gwo:/s h2epo/ ne bhe/reti g'hoyso:/s h1i/ns h2epo/ bhe/ronti. nu Hyudhsmo:/s sme/yn_t deywo/g'enh1e:s-kwe se/dt. me/dhu sweh2du/ we/kwos h2eh3mo/m.

h1sug'e/nh1e:s dusk'le/wesos ste/gos h1r_sk'e/t se/kwt-kwe. te/we h2ne/res me/ne bhre/h2term_ gwhne/nt. k'm_to/m moy gwo:/s h1i/h1 h2m_bhi/ dh3dhi/ gwru/bhis-we: gwm_yo:/mes. dusk'le/we:s we/wket. ne toy kwid dhl_gho/h2. te/we bhre/h2te:r bho:/r me/ne h2e/g'rom we/yk't. me/ne pe/k'u ste/h2yet me/rt-kwe. h1sug'e/nh1e:s h2roh1dhe/yet. nu deywo:/s h1so/nt wide/h1yo:nti. swe/h2m tewte/h2m le/ytt dusk'le/we:s-kwe k'e/rdi tre/mt. h1e/syo tewte/h2 k'm_to/m h2o/wins h1sug'e/nh1esos ste/gos h2e/yeri we/dhet. h1sug'e/nh1e:s h1i/h2n_s h1a/yto gwru/bhis-kwe h2ew ne gwe/mt. g'n_h3ro/s wiHro/s h2o/wins h1a/yto h1e/sh2r_ ne h1a/yto.

dwo/yh1 h2e/g'm_nih1 kl_He/ni sth2e/h1yeta:m. h1suh1e/k'wos h2ek'ro/m g'hoyso/m Hye/h1kt. so/ po/yk'o:m h1esh2o/m te/rh3t. dhe/g'ho:m pe/th1t h1e/syo-kwe kre/wh2s ghr_h1me/ni h1rudhe/h1t. nu h1e/syo Hyudhsmo:/s bhuge/nt pe/k'u-kwe h2e/n linkwe/nt. h1suh1e/k'wos priHo/ybhos Hre/wdHt. h1i/ns me: more/yete. h1i/d k'o/ymom h1l_h2te/. nu h3e/rones n_k'u/su h1e/pi sde/nt. h2yuHn_k'o:/s h1suh1e/k'wosyo ke/h2nmn_ kwse/pi gwe/Hyont. dweh2ro:/s h1le/wdheyes h1e/syo h1no/mn_ k'luwe/nt.

dusme/ne:s dhg'he/mi k'e/yo h1sn_tyo/k'lewe:s-kwe h1e/smi upe/r sth2e/h1yet. we/wket dusme/ne:s. h1sn_/tyok'lewes me me: gwhe/ns. te/we toy gwo:/s de/k'm_-kwe ma/rkons de/h3oh2. h1sn_tyo/k'lewe:s mle/wHt. h1o/ytom h3m_nh3dhi/. nu dusme/ne:s swo/m g'ho/stom dye:/m pro/ti tl_ne/h2t. dye/w me ph2ter k'ludhi/. h1yo/d h1i/m dhrughyo:/h2 kl_h2dhi/ me. nu wiHro/h1 h3m_nh3ta:/m ste/h2dhh1om-kwe ne/seto. dusme/ne:s h1e/smey de/k'm_ ma/rkons h2ewse/r we/dhet h1i/m-kwe ne dhre/wght. h1o/ytos sth2e/h1yet h2ew-kwe ne wike/ta:m.

ph2te:/r suHno/wey h2roh1dhe/yet. me/ne su/Hnew Hyudhsmo:/ys k'o/m gwe/h2esi. ghoste/yo:m gwru/bhos me: tre/ses te/we-kwe priHo/ns me: line/kws. h1yo/s swo/ns priHo/ns line/kwti k'le/wos to/smey ne h1e/sti. nu h1e/smey h2e/yesos g'hoyso/m de/h3t. nu suHnu/s to:/ys k'o/m h1e/yt trite/h2i-kwe kwse/pi ne/st. polh1u/ns gwo:/s h2e/lgwht. h1e/syo g'hoyso/s kruh2se/h1 h1rudhe/h1t. me/h2te:r me/wdet bhe/h2t-kwe. me/ne su/Hnew tu/H ke/h2ilos. nu te/we h1no/mn_ k'luto/m h1ludhi/su h1ente/r h1e/seti. po/tis h1e/syeh2s we/kwesh2 k'le/wt sme:/yt-kwe.

h1yo/d bho:/r ste/h2dhh1om h2epo/ h1e/lh2t sno/ygwhos h2e/g'roysu k'e/yo. ste/h2dhh1om pe/da:s sno/ygwhoy line/kwt. poh2ime:/n h1i/h2n_s h2ewse/r kwe/k't h1esh2o/m-kwe g'huHe/yet. h1e/sh2e nos sno/ygwhos h1e/syo sto/yghom de/yk'eti. nu pe/da:s de/h2nu se/kwonto gwo:/s-kwe sso/ntm_-kwe bho/rm_ h2e/n wide/ta:m. h1i/m sh2imne/h1 bhondhe/yeta:m h1i/ns-kwe dhe/h1mn_ wodhe/yeta:m. sno/ygwhos bhro:/m po/yk'os. h1esh2o/s h1i/m ne more/yet so/ h1e/syo poh2ime:/n bhu/Ht. sno/ygwhos we/kwsperoy tine/h1t.

me/g'h2s k'wo:/ g'r_h2n_te/s pe/k'u pe/h2st. tre/yes gho/steyes dhe/yg'hos ne/kwti tr_h2e/nt. k'wo:/ ste/h2t pr_h2wo/m-kwe h1e/pt. gho/steyes HrudHe/nt g'e/rh2onts-kwe bhe/wdht. gwe/ru le/mbht ste/gesos-kwe h1e/g'hs gwe/mt. to/ybhos mle/wHt. gho/steyes bhugte/. deywo:/s me/ne pe/k'u h2le/ksonti. gho/steyes kwse/pm_ h1n_do/ bhuge/nt k'wo:/-kwe gwowsth2o/y k'o/m h2ew le/ght. g'e/rh2onts h1e/smey me:/ms de/h3t. h1i/d h1e:/dt swe/pt-kwe. nu k'une/y se/kwt. k'wo/n tu/H tewh2so/s. g'e/rh2onts tri/ns g'hoyso/ns h2e/yeri dhe/yg'hesi h1e/pi wide/t.

wik'po/teys wiHro:/s h2ye/wHonm_ bho/rm_ h1r_h1pyo/nt h1i/m-kwe sh2imn_bhi/s bhe/ndhont. h1e/syo ph2te:/r to/m h1ludhe/t h2roh1dhe/yet-kwe. wi/k'potey me/ne suHnu/m sl_g'dhi/. so/ h2ye/wHo: te/we-kwe we/yHm_ ne wo/yde. pre:/k'st wik'po/tis. kwi/d moy h1i/h1 h2m_bhi/ de/h3esi? h2e/h2usom toy tri/ns-kwe ma/rkons de/h3oh2. nu wik'po/tis h1i/m se/lg't ph2te:/r-kwe suHnu/h1 k'o/m ne/seto. mo/ghus sokwe/yet. h2ew ne kle/po:h2. nu h1e/smey de/k'm_ h2o/wins de/h3t h1i/h2n_s-kwe pe/h2st. h1e/syo me/h2te:r me/wdet.

h2yuHn_k'o:/s Hyudhsmo:/s ph2we/ni pe/ri se/dyont. pe/h2wr_ gwhre/h1yet. g'e/rh2onts to/ybhos we/wket. mo/ghewes me/ne we/kwesh2 k'lute/. gwo/wes m_re/nt g'n_h1yo/m me/rt k'le/wos ne me/rt. h1use/ws wiHro/syo k'le/wos n_/dhgwhitom. ghoste/ys pe/k'u me: kle/pete. h1yo/s gho/stim dhrughye/ti deywo:/s to/m dedr_k'e:/r. po/yk'o:m ma/rkons h2e/g'ete bhoh2go/ns-kwe wos k'o/ymom bhe/rete. k'l_nwe/nt h1e/syo-kwe we/kwesh2 h1e/yso:m k'r_dsu/ dhe/dhh1n_t. polh1u/ns ma/rkons po/s h2l_gwhe/nt g'r_h2n_te/y-kwe h1we/sum bhoh2go/m dh3e/nt.

h1suk'le/we:s me/g'h2 de/h2nu h2nr_bhi/s k'o/m h1ludhe/t. h1e/syo wo/dr_ dhubhro/m h1e/st. gwo/wes tr_me/nt wo/dr_-kwe h1n_do/ ne ih2e/nt. nu stuh2ro/m ugwse/nm_ wo/dr_ h1n_do/ we/dhet ste/h2dhh1om-kwe to/m se/kweto. h1i/d tr_h2e/nt pe/h2uros-kwe gwo:/ws se/ngwt. wiHro:/s we/rh1yont. de/h2nu swo/m bhoh2go/m h1a/yto. gwo/wes ghre/h1mn_ h2e/n h1e/dn_t l_ghe/nt-kwe. nu me/dhu wo/dr_ h1n_do/ g'he/wt.

dwo/h1 h3re/g'h1e tr_me/ni g'n_h3n_to/. pr_h2wo/s h3re:/g's we/rh1t. k'o/d nos de/h2nu te/rmn_. h1yo/s gwo:/s h1i/d pe/r h2e/g'eti so/ de/k'm_ ugwse/nn_s dh3ye/h1t. h1e/teros h3re:/g's mle/wHt. te/we we/kwos h1we/su. ph2tre/y diwe/y h1o/ytom h3m_ne/h3owos. nu h3m_nh3ta:/m h2e/k'monm_-kwe de/h2newi h1e/pi dhh1ta:/m. h1e/nos h2e/k'mo: h2e/n stesto/h2e. h1yo/s h1i/m me/yth2eti pe/rkwunos to/m kl_h2ye/h1t. nu h3re/g'h1e wi/ gwh2ta:/m de/h2nu-kwe to/ysu h1ente/r te/kwet.

dusme/ne:s dhe/yg'hesi h1e/pi sth2e/h1yet wre/gontbhos-kwe wiHro/ybhos Hre/wdHt. gwo/wes wos me/ne gho/rtoy h1se/nti me/ne-kwe ghre/h1mn_ h1e/dn_ti. gwm_te/ h1i/ns-kwe h1a/ydhwe h1yo/d dhe/rsete. h1wesuk'le/we:s h1e/smey we/rh1yet. du/smenes te/we we/kwesh2 h2enh1mos. te/we h1e/sh2r_ we/kwsperoy dhg'he/mi h1rudhe/h1eti k'wo/nes-kwe h1i/d le/yg'honti. nu g'hoyso/m Hye/h1kt so/-kwe dusme/nesos h1o/h3s ke/lh2t. dhe/yg'hesos pe/th1t h1e/syo-kwe h2ne/res bhuge/nt. h1wesuk'le/we:s dhe/yg'hos Hrune/pt h1i/ns-kwe k'o/ymom h1e/lh2t.

dusk'le/wesos wiHro:/s mo/ghum h1suh1e/k'weh2s bhre/h2term_ h1pe/nt. somHe/h2 swe/so:r ghoste/ys dhe/h1mn_ gwe/h2t. h2e/h2usom albhe/h2m-kwe h2wl_/h1neh2m bhe/ret. dusk'le/wesey se/kwt. k'o/d h2e/h2usom h1a/yso me/ne-kwe moy bhre/h2term_ dh3dhi/. dusk'le/we:s bhe/h2t. te/we bhre/h2tr_s wo/sn_ k'm_to/m gwo/wes. swe/so:r sokwe/yet. me/ne ph2tre/s ma/rkons pe/ruti h2epo/ h1e/lh2s. nu dusk'le/we:s th2ke/h1yet h1i/m-kwe le/h1t swe/so:r-kwe h1i/m h2epo/ we/dhet. bhre/h2te:r sokwe/yet. swe/sor te/we k'e:/r tewh2so/m. h1le/wdheyes h1i/h2m_ gwr_nHe/nt. h1e/syeh2s h1no/mn_ k'luto/m.

h1o/gwhis deywo:/m gwo:/s pe/runi ke/wdht. no/kwts dhg'he/mi k'e/yo h2e/wso:s-kwe ne gwe/mt. nu wiHro/s deywo/g'enh1e:s kh2n_me/nh1 h1ludhe/t. pe/runey gwe/Hyet h1i/d-kwe bhidto/. h1o/gwhim gwhe/nt h1i/ns-kwe u/d h2e/g'et. h1e/gwhis kre/wh2s kr_sno/m h1e/st. nu h2e/wso:s diwe/s dhugh2te:/r le/wkt so/h2wl_-kwe h2we/st. h1e/yes h2e/g'rons u/d h1ye/nt dhe/g'ho:m-kwe gwhe/rt. deywo:/s wiHro/m gwr_nHe/nt. de/ywog'enh1es te/we k'le/wos n_/dhgwhitom. h1le/wdheyes to/d ke/h2nmn_ h2ewse/r gwe/Hyonti.

h1o/gwhis h2n_ro:/m gwo:/s ste/h2yet. tre/yes k'e/rh2sones h1e/smey h1se/nt. h1i/ns pe/rwr_ h1n_do/ de/wkt. nu trito/s h2ye/wHo: Hyudhsmo/s gwe/ru kapye/t. pe/rwr_ ste/yghet Hre/wdHt-kwe. h1o/gwhey moy h1i/ns dh3dhi/. h1o/gwhis ghre/met trito/s tri/ns h1e/syo k'e/rh2sonn_s ke/lh2t. h1o/gwhim gwhe/nt h1i/ns-kwe pe/runs h1e/g'hs h1e/lh2t. nu pe/rwr_ tre/mt. h2ne/res h1e/smey pr_h2wo/m ste/h2dhh1osyo bhoh2go/m dh3e/nt. trito/s we/wket. gwne/h2bhos h1i/m dh3te/ h1no/mn_ we/k'mi.

h1sume/nesos wiHro:/s dusme/nesos h2ne/rn_s pr_we/ni une/nt. nu dusme/ne:s n_/g'hoysos h1sume/nesm_ h1ludhe/t bhe/h2t-kwe. ste/h2dhh1om bhe/h2go:wos. tu/H ukse/nn_s h1a/yso h1eg'o/h2-kwe ugwse/nn_s bha/goh2er. h1sume/ne:s we/rh1t. kwi/d me/ne gwo:/s bhe/h2go:h2? gwi/h3we:si ne/seso me/ne-kwe h2e/g'rons h2ew me: gwe/ms. nu dusme/ne:s le/ytt h1sume/ne:s-kwe h1i/d h2epo/ g'he/yt. k'e/h2dos dusme/nesos k'e/rdi h1e/st. h1sume/nesos tewh2te/h2 h1i/m gwr_ne/Ht.

dwo/h1 mo/ghuh1 ste/h2dhh1om kl_He/ni ph2sta:/m. pe/h2wr_ kwse/pi h2e/n kwk'ta:/m. pr_h2wo/s pre:/k'st. kwe/yes h1e/noy h2ne/res? h1e/teros mle/wHt. to/y bho/res pe/k'u nos h2ewse/r gwm_yo:/nti. k'o/ymom dr_mdhi/ wiHro/ns-kwe g'huHe/ye. pr_h2wo/s dre/mt wiHro:/s-kwe ne/kwti sth2e/nt. h1e/noy h2e/yeri h1ludho/nt gwe/ruh2 wido/nt pe/k'u ne wido/nt. ke/nkont bhuge/nt-kwe. nu mo/ghuh1 h1i/d spr_ta:/m. wik'po/tis to/ybhos dwo/h1 ma/rkoh1 h1ope/yet. nu h1e/dta:m supta:/m-kwe.

h1suh1e/k'wos HeHk'u/h1 h1e/k'woh1 kwe/kwloy ye/wgt. Hro/th2oy ste/h2t ye/wgtreh2-kwe le/mbht. h1e/k'woh1 h2e/g'rons pe/r te/kweta:m kwe/kwlo:s ghre/mont swidye/ta:m-kwe. bho/rn_s pr_we/ni dhe/gwhh2t. bho/res sth2te/. me/ne h1e/k'woh1 HeHk'u/h1 po/des-kwe wos k'm_h2e/nt. nu gwo:/s linkwe/nt pe/rwr_-kwe bhuge/nt. h1i/ns ne wre/get ste/h2dhh1om k'o/ymom de/wkt. nu wo/dr_ ph3ta:/m to/h1-kwe gwr_ne/Ht.

po/yk'o:s gwo:/s dru/su h1ente/r kudhe/nt. Hyudhsmo:/s h1i/ns h2isk'o/nt h1i/ns ne wide/h1yont. ukse:/n we/kwsperoy dru/su h1ente/r ghre/met. h1e/syo k'r_h2no:/s dl_h1gho:/s h1se/nt. nu sme/yn_t. so/ nos g'huHe/yeti. do/ruh2 h1n_do/ gwh2e/nt h1i/ns-kwe u/d g'hye/nt. po/yk'o:s bhuge/nt. bho:/r gwo:/s ke/wdheti gwo/wes ne ke/wdhontor. gwe/Hyontes wiHro:/s h1i/ns k'o/ymom duke/nt.

wiHro:/s ghoste/ys pk'u/h1 k'o/m n_se/nt. gwe/nh2es te/k'neh2-kwe dhh1mn_/bhos h1e/g'hs gwm_yo/nt. h1suk'le/wesos po/tnih2 swo/m po/tim kwe/k't pre:/k'st-kwe. ku/dhe me/ne bhre/h2te:r h1e/sti? po/tis we/rh1yet. so/ pr_we/ni k'e/yor h1e/syo k'le/wos gwi/h3weti. nu ugwse/nm_ more/yont me:/ms-kwe kwe/roy pe/kwont. te/k'neh2 me:/ms h1e:/dt lo/yget-kwe. h2e/nHos bhre/h2tr_s spe/lom se/ngwhet. po/tnih2 ph2we/ni h1e/pi se/dt th2ke/h1yet-kwe. me:/ms sweh2du/ h1e/st me/dhu-kwe tewh2so/m h1e/st.

dwo/h1 wiHro/h1 albho/m ukse/nm_ uk'ta:/m. pr_h2wo/s we/wket. h1eg'o/h2 ghoste/ys gho/rtoy pr_h2wo/s h1e/sm_. h1e/teros sokwe/yet. h1eg'o/h2 h1i/m me/ne g'ho/stoh1 h2epo/ we/dhom. nu gho/stipotis to/ybhos h2roh1dhe/yet. ukse:/n swo/m po/tim wl_h1ye/h1t. h1i/m wiHro/ysu h1ente/r dhe/h1t. h1e/teros h1e/smey wo/dr_ de/h3t. nu ukse:/n to/m gwe/h2t pr_h2wo/s-kwe th2ke/h1yet. wiHro:/s we/rh1yonti. ugwse:/n to/m g'n_ne/h3ti h1yo/s h1e/smey wo/dr_ de/deh3ti.

gho/stis h1wesuk'le/wesos do:/m h1ludhe/t. h1wesuk'le/we:s h1e/smey me:/ms me/dhu-kwe de/h3t. gho/stis h1e:/dt pe/h3t-kwe. se/st ne/kwti ste/h2t albho/m-kwe gwo:/m h2epo/ h1e/lh2t. h1esh2o/s h1i/m h2e/yeri se/kweto h1i/m-kwe de/h2newi wide/t. kwi/d me gho/stey dhre/wghs? me:/ms toy me/dhu-kwe de/h3m_. gho/stis mle/wHt. h1eg'o/h2 ne te/we gho/stis h1eg'o/h2 te/we po/yk'os. nu h2e/n wike/ta:m h1wesuk'le/we:s-kwe gho/stim gwhe/nt. h1e/syo kre/pos h2e/n k'e/yo. h1e/y ne/seto. nu h1esh2o/s ghosti/bhos ne bhebho/ydhe.

h1sn_tyo/k'lewe:s mo/ghum gho/stins se/lg't. mo/ghus h1e/yso:m ste/gesi h2e/nti ste/h2t swo/syo-kwe h1esh2o/syo we/kwesh2 bhe/h2t. gwo:/s nos dh3te/ dhe/h1mn_h2-we: wos h1e/wso:mes. ph2we/nh1 g'hoyso:/ys-kwe gwm_yo:/mes. gho/steyes we/rh1yont. ph2we/ns wos ne dedwime/. dhe/yg'hesh2 nos dhr_g'hte/h2 gwe/ruh2-kwe nos h2ek're/h2. nu so/ h1e/yt h1e/syo-kwe h1esh2o:/y k'e/h2 we/kwesh2 we/rh1yet. h1esh2o/s k'l_ne/wt th2ke/h1yet-kwe nu we/wket. kwse/pi gwm_yo:/mes h1e/yso:m-kwe dhe/yg'hesh2 h1e/wse:tor.

h2yuHn_k'o:/s wl_/kwo:m ske/wHtins we/sn_to. kl_Hn_su/ tk'iye/nt h2eh3mo/m-kwe me:/ms h1e/dn_t. h1e/yso:m h3do/ntes h2ek'ro:/s h1se/nt. sno/ygwhos kl_Hn_su/ dhubhro/s h1e/st. h1e/yso:m me/h2teres h1i/ns ne g'n_nh3e/nt. ghoste/yo:m gwo:/s ye/h1n_s tr_me/ni g'hye/nt. swo/ybhos ph2tr_bho/s h1i/ns h2l_gwhe/nt. ph2te/res sokwe/yont. nu yu/H wiHro:/s yu/H ne wl_/kwo:s. k'le/wos wos me/g'h2 h1e/seti. nu h2yuHn_k'o:/s sme/yn_t h1e/yso:m-kwe me/h2teres me/wdont.

dusk'le/we:s h1suk'le/wesey bhoydhe/h2m h3m_ne/h3t. h1suk'le/wesos pe/k'u kwse/pi h2ew h1e/lh2t. h1suk'le/we:s dye:/m pro/ti Hre/wdHt. dye/w ph2ter bhoydhe/h2m wo/ydth2e dhro/wghom-kwe wo/ydth2e. nu ghro/mos kl_Hn_su/ upe/r ghre/met pe/rkwunos-kwe dusk'le/wesos gwowsth2o/m ke/lh2t. gwowsth2o/s h1e/wseto pe/k'u-kwe h1e/smo:d h1e/g'hs bhe/wgt. h1e/syo po/tim te/kwet. nu h2we/rst. h1yo/s swo/m priHo/m dhrughye/ti pe/rkwunos to/m ke/lh2t. nu dusk'le/wesos tewh2te/h2 pe/rkwuno:d bhebho/yh2e.

wiHro:/s n_se/nt g'e/rh2onts-kwe h3re:/g's to/ns pre:/k'st. kwo/ti gwo:/s h2e/g'ete? to/ti h2e/g'ome h1yo/ti h2ste/res dye/wi h1se/nti. h3re:/g's sme:/yt. h2ste/res polh1e/wes yu/H pe/h2uro:s. me: me dhrughye/te. kwo/ti h2e/g'ete? mo/ghus se/kwt. k'm_to/m gwo:/s de/k'm_ ukse/nn_s albho/m-kwe h1e/k'wom h2e/g'ome. h3re:/g's we/rh1t. te/we we/kwos h1so/nt. h1i/m moy we/dhete. mo/ghus h1i/m h3re/g'm_ wodhe/yet h3re:/g's-kwe h1e/smey h2e/h2usom h1ope/yet.

deywo/k'lewesos h1e/k'wo:m gwriHwe/h2es dl_h1ghe/h2es h1se/nt. deywo/k'lewe:s to/ybhos h2roh1dhe/yet. He/Hk'uh1 me h1e/k'woh1 ghoste/yo:m gho/rtons bhe/retom gwi/h3wontm_-kwe h1me/ k'o/ymom we/dhetom. h1yo/d me/roh2 k'wn_bho/s me/ne kre/pos me: linkwih1to/m. nu Hro/th2oy ste/h2t. nu h1e/k'woh1 swo/ns k'e/rh2sonn_s nuta:/m gho/stins-kwe pro/ti te/kweta:m. gwi/h3wontm_ h1i/m k'o/ymom we/kwsperoy bhe/reta:m to/ybhos-kwe wo/dr_ ghre/h1mn_-kwe h1ope/yet.

HeHk'uh1e/k'wos m_dhe/ws pe/h3tlom de/h2newi h1e/pi dhe/h1t deywo/ns-kwe g'huHe/yet. de/ywo:s me h1use/wo:m de/h3tores k'lute/. ghoste/ys moy gwo:/s dh3te/ pr_h2wo/m-kwe wos ukse/nm_ de/h3oh2. me/ne h1e/k'wo:s HeHk'e/wes h1sih1e/nt me/ne-kwe gwe/ru me: Hrumpih1to/. nu de/h2nu h2nr_bhi/s k'o/m te/rh2t. h2ewse/r k'm_to/m ugwsn_bhi/s k'o/m ne/st deywo/ybhos-kwe ukse/nm_ de/h3t. h1e/syo wiHro:/s h1i/m gwr_nHe/nt h1i/ns-kwe bhe/h2gont.

dwo/h1 bhre/h2terh1e gwru/bhis u/d gwh2ta:/m. pr_h2wo/s ste/h2dhh1om wodhe/yet h1e/teros sto/yghom pe/h2st. po/yk'o:s sto/yghoy h1ludho/nt g'hoyso/s-kwe h1e/terom ke/lh2t. pe/th1t me/rt-kwe. pr_h2wo/s h1e/syo kre/pos do:/m bhe/ret me/h2trey-kwe se/kwt. gwo:/s toy bhe/roh2 te/we suHnu/m ne bhe/roh2. me/h2te:r th2ke/h1yet nu we/wket. gwo/wes me/g'hye h1se/nti suHnu/s me/g'hye ne h1e/sti. nu h1e/syo kre/pesi h1e/pi se/dt. h1e/syeh2s h2e/ng'hos me/g'h2 h1e/st.

h1sume/ne:s deywo/k'lewesos dhugh2te/rm_ h1e/pt deywo/k'lewe:s-kwe h1sume/nesos suHnu/m le/mbht. dwo/h1 ph2te/rh1e pr_we/ni g'n_h3n_to/. h2roh1dhe/yet h1sume/ne:s. me/ne moy suHnu/m dh3dhi/ te/we-kwe toy dhugh2te/rm_ de/h3oh2. deywo/k'lewe:s we/rh1yet. te/we suHnu/s k'uh1ro/s me/ne-kwe dhugh2te:/r pe/h2ureh2 de/k'm_ moy h2o/wins e/ti dh3dhi/. h1sume/ne:s sokwe/yet. h2o/wim toy ne de/h3oh2. nu deywo/k'lewe:s sme:/yt ph2te/rh1e-kwe swe/h2 te/k'neh2 mith2ta:/m. kwye/h1tis pr_we/ni h1e/st. dhugh2te:/r dhe/h1mn_ dre/mt.

albho/s dl_h1gho/k'r_h2nos ukse:/n h1suk'le/wesey h1e/st. h1le/wdheyes h1i/m g'n_nh3e/nt. h1suk'le/we:s h1i/m gwowsth2o/y h2e/yeri ne wide/t. h1e/syo lo/ysa:s h1suh1e/k'wosyo dhe/h1mn_ se/kweto. h1su/h1ek'we me/ne ukse:/n te/we gho/rtoy h1e/sti. h1suh1e/k'wos mle/wHt. deywo:/s moy h1i/m sl_g'e/nt. h1suk'le/we:s we/rh1t. nu moy deywo:/s te/we ukse/nm_ se/lg'onti. h1suh1e/k'wos sme:/yt h1e/smey-kwe dl_h1gho/k'r_h2nom de/h3t. nu to/h1 priHo/h1.

Hyudhsmo/s polh1u/ns ugwse/nn_s h2e/g'ontm_ poh2ime/nm_ sto/yghoy g'ne/h3t. kwi/s k'o/yso:m ugwsno:/m po/tis? poh2ime:/n we/rh1yet. h1e/yso:m po/tis k'o/syeh2s tewte/h2s h3re:/g's. ku/dhe h1i/ns h2e/g'esi? h1i/ns de/h2nu h2e/g'oh2 tr_syo/nti. Hyudhsmo/s we/wket. de/k'm_ moy ugwse/nn_s dh3dhi/ gwe/h2esi-kwe. poh2ime:/n sokwe/yet. h3re:g'e/s wiHro:/s sto/yghoy gwm_sk'o/nti. nu Hyudhsmo/s to/ns kwe/k't le/ytt-kwe. poh2ime:/n sme:/yt h1i/ns-kwe wo/dr_ wodhe/yet.

de/k'm_ po/yk'o:s h1sug'e/nh1esos gho/rtom h1ludho/nt. h1sug'e/nh1e:s somHo/s h1e/st h1e/syo bhre/h2teres dh2pno/y h1se/nt. gwru/h1 dhn_we/nh1-kwe dhwo/ri h2e/nti ste/h2t. h1yo/d po/yk'o:s k'o/ns gwo:/s uk'te/ h1i/ns me/ne g'ho/stoybhos h1a/ydhwe. po/yk'o:s h1e/syo dhn_we/ns tre/sont. tri/ns h2ne/rn_s gwhe/nt h2e/lyo:s h1i/m kl_h2e/nt. dhwo/ri h2e/nti pe/th1t gwo/wes-kwe h1e/smi pe/ri sth2e/h1yont. h1e/syo bhre/h2teres we/kwsperoy gwm_yo/nt h1i/m-kwe wido/nt. h1e/syo kre/pos dhg'he/mi dhh1e/nt h1e/syo-kwe k'le/wos se/ngwhont. h1sug'e/nh1esos k'le/wos n_/dhgwhitom.

wiHro:/s ne/kwti ph2we/ni pe/ri se/dyont h1e/yso:m-kwe k'e/rdh2 mudre/h2 h1e/st. h2yuHn_k'o/s ne/wom ke/h2nmn_ gwe/Hyet. pe/rwr_ kwse/pi pr_me/. ghoste/ys gwo:/s g'hime/ gho/stis-kwe se/st. nu nos h2me/lg'os de/dh3n_ti h2me/lg'os-kwe sweh2du/. wiHro:/s sme/yn_t ph3e/nt-kwe. g'e/rh2onts h2roh1dhe/yet. th2ke/h1yete. h2o:/wsih1 ghoste/yey h1ste/s. h2yuHn_k'o/s th2ke/h1yet wiHro:/s-kwe l_ghe/nt. h2ste/res luke/nt.

dwo/h1 gho/stipotih1 dh2pno/y k'o/m sdta:/m. h1sn_tyo/k'lewe:s h2nr_me/nesey m_dhe/ws pe/h3tlom de/h3t. gho/stey ph3dhi/. priHo/s nos h1sdhi/. h2nr_me/ne:s pe/h3t we/wket-kwe. deywo:/s toy h1we/suh2 dh3ih1e/nt. tri/ns k'o/ns ma/rkons me/d h1a/yso. ste/h2dhh1oyh1 nos ghre/h1mn_ tr_me/ni k'o/m h1e/dih1ta:m. to/yso:m te/k'neh2 ghr_h1me/ni lo/yget. nu wiHro/h1 priHo/h1 bhuHta:/m. to/yso:m po/tnih2es h2wl_/h1neh2m k'o/m tkse/nt.

h2ne/r ke/h2ilos h1sdhi/. sni/gwhm_ pe/r gwegwo/mh2e. pri/He gho/stey wik'dhi/ ph2we/ni-kwe sddhi/. sri/Hgos me/g'h2 tre/mmi-kwe. gwhr_nuso/. h1i/d h1idhe/ dhe/gwheti po/tnih2-kwe moy pe/kweti.

kwi/d ph2ter sno/ygwhos? dhu/gh2ter moy sno/ygwhos wo/dr_. h1i/d g'hime/ni ste/wdt diwe/s-kwe se/ngwt. ku/dhe-kwe sno/ygwhos we/sni h1e/yti? we/sni tine/h1ti h2e/g'rons-kwe h2e/leti.

pri/He toy dhg'hye/s h2we/rst? h2we/rst bhre/h1wo:r-kwe pl_nh1to/. dhe/g'ho:m polh1u/ pe/h3t. h2e/g'roysu moy ne h2we/rst. h2we/rseti. h2e/wso:s h1rudhe/h1t.

ku/dhe po/h2imen gwo/wes g'hime/ni sth2e/h1yonti? g'hime/ni h1n_do/m sth2e/h1yonti sno/ygwhos-kwe h2e/g'rons ste/geti. kwi/s to/ns ne/kwti h2le/kseti? k'wo/nes to/ns h2le/ksonti h1eg'o/h2-kwe bhe/wdhoh2.

kwo/m swe/sor wo/sr_ gwm_sk'e/ti? h1yo/d so/h2wl_ gwhe/reti wo/sr_ gwm_sk'e/ti. nu kwi/d gwm_ye/ti? nu gwm_ye/ti gwhe/ros g'e/nh1mn_h2-kwe h2we/kst. yo/h1r_ we/rtetor g'he/ymn_-kwe h1idhe/ h1e/sti.

po/h2imen moy we/wke kwo/m h2o/wins h2e/g'rom we/dho:h2? h1yo/d sno/ygwhos tine/h1eti ta:/s h1e/g'hs we/dhe. ta:/s sri/Hg'esi h1e/g'hs me: we/dhes. te/h2so:m g'e/nh1mn_h2? g'e/nh1mn_h2 we/sni g'n_h1ye/tor.

pri/He moy bhre/h2tr_s k'o/ymom gwe/h2oh2? gho/stey me: gwe/h2s. pl_h1e/wih2 sni/gwhs dhg'he/mi k'e/yor wl_/kwo:s-kwe h2isk'o/nti. kwo/m ye/h2oh2? h1yo/d sno/ygwhos tine/h1eti ih2dhi/. nu moy ph2we/ni se/dye.

swe/sor swidyo/h2. gwhe/ros me/g'h2. k'olHto/m wo/dr_ bhr_h1we/ns ph3dhi/. nu sne/h2omes. me/h2te:r nos ghre/me:ti. ne nos wide:/ti swo/y le/ghesi le/gheti.

me/h2ter le/wkt? ne le/wkt. h2e/wso:s ne gwegwo/me. l_ghdhi/. ste/wdm_-ge. su/Hnew h2wl_/h1neh2h1 ste/geso.

ph2ter moy wr_h1dhi/ kwi/d h2r_/tk'os g'hime/ni dhe/dheh1ti? h2r_/tk'os g'hime/ni swo/y le/ghesi k'e/yor ne-kwe h1e:/dti. h1yo/d wo/sr_ gwe/meti bhe/wdht ke/nketi-kwe. h2o/wins h1e:/dti? ta:/s h1e:/dti h1yo/d poh2ime:/n ta:/s ne pe/h2seti.

ku/dhe h1su/menes g'hh2e/nses g'hime/ni h1ye/nti? h2epo/ so/h2wl_ pro/ti h1ye/nti. h2e/n gwhre/h1yeti. h2ew h1ludho:/nti? we/sni h1ludho:/nti g'hh2e/nsn_s-kwe k'le/wesi.

bhre/h2ter memo/nth2e? pe/ruti sne/ygwhet sri/Hgos-kwe nos h2o/wins more/yet. memo/nh2e. k'o/d g'he/ymn_ n_sme/y h1we/su h1sye/h1t. deywo:/s nos gwhe/ros dh3ih1e/nt. sri/Hgos-kwe nos ukse/nn_s me: more/yoyh1t.
