k'e/ywe h1we/sus h1sye/h1s. wo/sr_ gwegwo/me. gwegwo/me sno/ygwhos-kwe lelo/ykwe. tri/h2 moy g'e/nh1mn_h2 gwe/wbhos g'n_h1ye/to. deywo:/s toy h1we/sewes. gwm_dhi/ g'e/nh1mn_h2-kwe wide/.

kwo/m ph2ter h2e/g'rons h2e/rh3yo:mes? h1yo/d dhe/g'ho:m gwhe/reti h2e/rh3yo:mes. nu h1i/h2 k'olHte/h2? h1i/h2 k'olHte/h2. sri/Hgos ne/kwti h1e/syeh2i h1e/pi k'e/yor. nu ukse/nn_s ye/wgomes.

me/h2ter dedwo/yh2e. dye:/ws ghre/meti. su/Hnew me: bhe/yh2s. pe/rkwunos ghre/meti. nu h2we/rseti h2e/g'ro:s-kwe pe/h3onti. gwhe/neti nos? ne nos gwhe/nti. h1o/gwhim gwhe/nt.

de/ywok'lewes wl_/kwom sno/ygwhoy wide/s? to/m wido/m h2o/wins se/kweto. nu ta:/s ne/kwti pe/h2somes. ta:/s pe/h2somes k'wo/nn_s-kwe we/dho:h2.

me/h2ter gho/stis sni/gwhm_ pe/r gwm_sk'e/ti. kwi/h1 to/smey bhe/h2oh2? to/smey wr_h1dhi/. gho/stey ke/h2ilos h1sdhi/. pe/h2wr_ gwm_dhi/. kwi/d-kwe to/smey de/h3oh2? to/smey gwhr_no/m dh3dhi/.

me/h2ter ste/wdt. dhu/gh2ter h2wl_/h1neh2h1 wose/yeso. ku/dhe h2wl_/h1neh2 h1e/sti? h1i/h2 toy le/ghesi le/gheti. nu toy swesre/h1 k'o/m se/dye.

k'o/ymom neh2we/h1 gwe/h2omes? g'hime/ni ne gwe/h2omes. wo/dr_ ste/wdt. h1i/d we/sni gwhermo/m h1e/seti. h1e/k'wo:s nos ne we/g'ho:nti? to/y nos we/g'ho:nti h1yo/d sno/ygwhos lele/ykweti.

ku/dhe h2ne/r kwe/kwloh1 h1e/ysi? bhre/h2tr_s moy k'o/ymom h1e/ymi. suHnu/s to/smey g'eg'o/nh1e. deywo:/s toy h1we/suh2 dh3ih1e/nt. sno/ygwhos lelo/ykwe h1e/k'wo:s-kwe moy HeHk'e/wes.

kwo/ti g'he/yomn_s dedo/rk'th2e? pl_h1e/wih2n_s g'he/yomn_s dedo/rk'h2e. kwo/ti dedo/rk'th2e? h1ne/wn_ g'he/yomn_s dedo/rk'h2e. h1yo/d de/k'm_ g'he/yomes we/rto:ntor ukse/nn_s k'o/m h2e/g'o:mes.

bhre/h2ter sne/ygwheti. h1e/g'hs ye/h2omes. ph2te:/r nos we/wket. sri/Hg'esi h1e/g'hs me: ih2te/. so/ poh2ime/nn_s le/ytt. nu h1e/g'hs ye/h2omes mok's-kwe h2ew gwe/momes.

wi/Hre dhe/g'ho:m h2susye/ti gwo/wes-kwe tr_syo/nti. pe/rkwunos ne ghre/met. to/m gwr_ne/Homes bhre/h1wo:r-kwe nos pl_nh1ye/h1t. bhre/h1wr_ h2susye/ti? ne h2susye/ti.

po/tni no/kwts k'olHte/h2. ph2we/ni le/ghomes. ku/dhe nos suHnu/s h1e/sti? so/ k'wn_bhi/s k'o/m le/gheti to/m-kwe gwhr_nwe/nti. deywo:/s to/m h2le/ksoyh1ent.

mluHdhi/ moy kwi/d wl_/kwos k'o/ymom g'hime/ni gwm_sk'e/ti? ke/nketi. g'hwe:/res sni/gwhi k'e/lontor. kwi/d h2isk'e/ti? g'hh2e/nsn_s nos h2isk'e/ti k'wo/nes-kwe wl_/kwom h2epo/ h2e/g'onti.

swe/sor k'a:/m h2wl_/h1neh2m h1a/yso. g'he/ymn_ gwm_ye/ti. tu/H moy k'e/yweh2. h2e/nkuleh2m toy de/h3oh2. k'o/ymos toy gwhermo/s? h1e/y gwhermo/s. wiHro/s moy ph2we/ni se/dyeti.

ph2ter sh2we/ni sddhi/. gwhe/rt. dhu/gh2ter me so/h2wl_ gwhr_ne/wti. g'hime/ni sri/Hg'esi tre:/m. nu gwo:/s h2me/lg'si? ta:/s mok's h2me/lg'mi.

su/Hnew h1regwesye/ti. gwo:/s k'o/ymom h2e/g'e. to/ns h2e/g'oh2. ukse:/n lelo/ykwe. to/m bhr_h1we/ni h2isk'e/. to/m dedo/rk'h2e. h2e/n stesto/h2e.

kwi/d sri/Hgos? dhe/g'ho:m ne/kwti ste/wdt sri/Hgos-kwe dhe/yg'hesh2 h2use/si ste/geti. sri/Hgos tine/h1ti? tine/h1ti h1yo/d so/h2wl_ le/wketi.

po/tni moy sokwe/ye gwo:/s toy g'hye/mi h2me/lg'si? ta:/s g'hye/mi ne h2me/lg'mi. h2susyo/nti. g'e/nh1mn_h2 te/h2bhos we/sni g'n_h1ye:/tor ta:/s-kwe h2me/lg'oh2. kwo/ti h2o/wins pe/h2si? septm_/ h2o/wins pe/h2smi.

dhu/gh2ter bhre/h1wr_ gwh2dhi/ wo/dr_-kwe bhe/re. h1i/d k'olHto/m. k'olHto/m wo/dr_ gwhe/resi h1we/su. nu polh1u/ bhe/ro:h2.

sno/ygwhos tine/h1t ye/h2oh2-kwe. gho/stey ke/h2ilos h1sdhi/. deywo:/s te h2le/ksoyh1ent. h1use/wo:m moy de/h3tor tu/H priHo/s. gwhe/resi h2ew gwm_dhi/.

bhre/h2ter h2ew gwegwo/mth2e. skwdhi/ moy ku/dhe g'hime/ni h1e/s? h3re:g'e/s poh2imn_bhi/s k'o/m h1e/sm_. polh1u/s sno/ygwhos h2e/n k'e/yo ne-kwe gwa:/m. h3re:/g's toy misdho/m de/h3t? h1e/k'wom moy de/h3t.

kwi/d h1su/menes h2e/wso:s h1rudhe/h1ti? h1yo/d h2e/wso:s h1rudhe/h1eti h2we/rseti. me/h2te:r moy me/g'hye k'o/d se/kwt. sne/ygwhe:ti? ne sne/ygwhe:ti sri/Hgos lelo/ykwe.

bhre/h2ter tre/msi. snigwhe/s dedwo/yth2e? to/syeh2s ne dedwo/yh2e. sri/Hgos me h1e/pt. nu pe/h2wr_ gwm_dhi/. gwm_sk'o/h2. ph2tre/y-kwe nos me: we/wkes.

swe/sor moy wo/sr_ gwr_nHdhi/. wo/sr_ dhe/g'ho:m gwegwo/me. sno/ygwhos bhe/wgt. ukse/nes h1e/g'hs h1ye/nti g'hh2e/nses-kwe gwm_yo/nti. h2ew gwe/Hye. k'o/m gwe/Hyo:mes.

h1n_do/m kwis h1e/sti? h1eg'o/h2 gho/stis h1regwesye/ti-kwe. kwi/s tu/H? kwi/d toy h1no/mn_? h1eg'o/h2 h1suk'le/we:s. wl_/kwo:s me sni/gwhm_ pe/r se/kwonto. h1su/k'lewes wik'dhi/ n_sme/y-kwe priHo/s h1sdhi/.

pri/He h3re:g'e/y h1e/k'wom dhl_gho/h2. h2roh1dhe/ye moy kwo/m to/smey h1i/m we/dho:h2? h2use/si mok's litdhi/. h1e/k'wos gwhe/resi swidye:/ti. h3re:/g's me/g'hye h1sume/ne:s h1sye/h1t.

k'o/s ukse:/n wetso/s? so/ wetso/s pe/ruti g'n_h1ye/to. h1yo/d h2we/kseti kwe/kwlom de/wketi. nu to/m h2e/g'o:h2? su/Hnew to/m h2e/g'e:si h1eg'o/h2-kwe spe/k'yo:h2.

kwi/d h2nr_/menes so/h2wl_ g'hime/ni gwr_nHme/s? yo/h1r_ g'hime/ni we/rtetor le/wks-kwe nos h2ew gwm_sk'e/ti. nu sri/Hgos bhe/wgt. sno/ygwhos bhe/wgt? we/sni bhe/wgt.

h1so/nt k'uh1ro/m ne-kwe me/rt dhro/wghos ml_du/s. h1yo/s dhrughye/ti so/ bhe/h2esos bhebho/yh2e. dhro/wghosyo po/dh1e mr_g'hu/h1.

k'e:/r h1yo/d mn_ye/tor dn_g'hwe/h2s to/d we/rh1yeti. g'n_h3ro/syo h1o/h3s h1e/syo k'e/rdi h1e/sti. h2egho/syo k'e:/r h1e/syo h1e/h3si h1e/sti.

su/Hnew toy ph2te/rm_ k'le/wse. polh1u/h2 k'lush1ye/ pe/h2ureh2-kwe mluHdhi/. mo/rtoybhos h1so/nt wr_h1dhi/. priHo/m toy me: dhre/wghs. dhro/wghos h1o/gwhis. to/m gwr_h3e/ti h1yo/s h1i/m bhe/reti.

h1e/wseti pe/h2wr_ g'ho/stom h2egho/m we/kwos k'e:/r h1e/wseti. dhuh2mo/s pe/h2wr_ se/kwetor k'e/h2dos-kwe dhro/wghom se/kwetor. g'ho/stos ke/h2ilos h2ew bhuHye/ti k'e:/r ke/h2ilom ne bhuHye/ti.

m_re/nt gwo/wes m_re/nt bhre/h2teres me/rt-kwe mo/rtos. h1we/su k'le/wos ne me/rt. n_/dhgwhitom h2n_re/s k'le/wos gwi/h3weti.

kwi/d ph2ter h1e/h3si sweh2du/? dhro/wghos. kwi/d k'e/rdi pik'ro/m? somHo/s dhro/wghos. su/Hnew h1so/nt wr_h1dhi/. h1so/nt h1e/h3si pik'ro/m h1i/d-kwe k'e/rdi dluku/.

dhro/wghos bho:/r. gwo:/s ne ste/h2yeti mo/rto:m me/nesh2 ste/h2yeti. ne/kwti ne gwm_sk'e/ti dino/y gwm_sk'e/ti. me/nos toy dino/y ne/kwti-kwe ph2sdhi/.

mo/ghewes g'e/rh2ontm_ k'le/wsete. g'e/rh2onts polh1u/h2 dedo/rk'e. sweh2duye/h2ey dn_g'huh2e/y me: k'reddhh1te/. mo/ghuns wl_/kwom we/dheti. h3reg'to:/y we/kwesey k'reddhh1te/.

polh1u/h2 we/kwesh2 pe/h2ureh2 dhe/h1leh2. dusme/ne:s bhe/h2ti h1we/sus wr_g'ye/ti. dhe/h1leh2 wiHro/m de/yk'eti.

kwi/s g'e/rh2ont priHo/s? h1yo/s toy h1so/nt we/rh1yeti so/ priHo/s. h1yo/s te sweh2du/bhis we/kwesbhis gwr_ne/Hti so/ ne priHo/s. h1e/y wl_/kwos.

k'e:/r polh1u/h2 we/k'ti me/nos pe/h2ureh2 we/lh1t. h1yo/syo me/nos h3reg'to/m so/ h3reg'to/y sto/yghoy ste/ygheti. h1e/syo po/dh1e ne h3slidhta:/m.

dhu/gh2ter polh1e/wes wiHro:/s sweh2duwe/kweses h1e/yes-kwe ne h1sn_tyo:/s. wiHro/syo dhe/h1leh2 spe/k'ye h1e/syo we/kwesh2 me: spe/k'yes. dhe/h1leh2 h1sn_tye/h2.

wl_/kwos h2o/wins gwhe/nti h2egho/m we/kwos h2n_re/s h1no/mn_ gwhe/nti. wl_/kwom wide/h1yesi dhro/wghom ne wide/h1yesi. k'wo:/ h2o/wins h2le/kseti deywo:/s h1no/mn_ h2le/ksonti.

h1we/sus de/deh3ti h2egho/s h1a/yto. wiHro/s h1yo/d de/deh3ti to/d h1a/yto. h1we/su h1sume/nesm_ gwm_sk'e/ti.

ku/dhe ph2ter h1so/nt tk'e/yti? h1so/nt h3reg'to/y k'e/rdi tk'e/yti. ku/dhe dhro/wghos tk'e/yti? dusme/nesi tk'e/yti.

h1o/h3s k'r_de/s dhwo:/r. dhwo/rm_ ph2sdhi/. h1yo/s th2ke/h1yeti so/ ne h3sle/ydht. dhro/wghos polh1uwe/kwesm_ se/kwetor.

mo/ghew toy me/nos yugdhi/. wiHro/syo me/nos HeHk'u/s h1e/k'wos. n_/yugom me/nos sto/ygho:d h3sle/ydht. h1yo/s swo/m me/nos ye/wgt so/ swo/m do:/m h1r_sk'e/ti.

h3re:g'e/s we/kwos stesto/h2e. se/ngwt dhrughyo/nts h3re:/g's. deywo:/s h1e/syo bhoydhe/h2m k'lush1yo/nti. swe/h2m bhoydhe/h2m me: Hrune/pt.

su/Hnew h1eg'o/h2 g'e/rh2onts. we/kwesh2 moy k'ludhi/. gho/stim me: ghrome/yes bhoydhe/h2m me: Hrune/ps. deywo/ns sepelye/ me/h2term_-kwe toy ste/rge. h1we/su te k'le/wos se/kwoyh1to.

dedo/rk'e h3o/kw k'lush1ye/ti h2o:/ws wo/yde-kwe g'ne/h3meh2. h3o/kw h1yo/d dedo/rk'e to/d h1sn_tyo/m. h2o:/ws h1yo/d k'lush1ye/ti to/d h2e/nh1mos.

me/h2tr_s k'e:/r ne dhrughye/ti. me/h2te:r swo/m suHnu/m wo/yde. te/k'nom kwid swe/h2s me/h2tr_s ne ke/wdheti.

te/k'nom sddhi/ k'le/wse-kwe. pl_h1e/wih2n_s g'he/yomn_s dedo/rk'h2e. tine/h1ti sno/ygwhos sh2we/ni tine/h1ti dhro/wghos bhe/h2esi. h1so/nt h2e/k'mo: ne tine/h1ti.

dwo/h1 bhre/h2terh1e wike/ta:m. h1suk'le/we:s we/rh1t we/kwos moy h1sn_tyo/m. deywo/g'enh1e:s se/kwt we/kwos toy ne h1sn_tyo/m. nu ph2te:/r h2e/g't. su/Hnuh1 h1so/nt sm_sto/yghom dhro/wghos polh1usto/yghos. to/m sto/yghom k'o/m h2isk'e/tom. nu bhre/h2terh1e th2ke/h1yeta:m.

se/rpeti h1o/gwhis h2k'me/ni Hn_dhi/ dhro/wghos sweh2du/su we/kwesu Hn_dhi/ se/rpeti. h2e/k'monm_ tl_nh2dhi/ h1o/gwhim-kwe wine/dsi.

h2yuHn_k'o/syo h2r_Hmo/h1 k'uh1ro/h1 g'r_h2n_te/s me/nos g'n_h3ro/m. h2yuHn_k'o/s bhe/h2ti g'e/rh2onts th2ke/h1yeti wo/yde-kwe. h2yuHn_k'o/syo g'ho/stos g'n_h3ro/syo-kwe me/nos do:/m de/metes.

ne/k't h2e/h2usom me/g'h2 k'le/wos ne ne/k't. bho:/r h2e/h2usom kle/peti k'le/wos kwis ne kle/peti. h1i/d n_/dhrowghosyo misdho/s.

h3e/sth1 dn_g'hwe/h2i ne h1e/sti h1i/h2-kwe h3e/sth1h2 Hrune/pti. mo/ghew h1i/h2m_ ph2sdhi/. h1yo/d h1e/g'hs h1e/yti to/d kwis we/kwos ne kapye/ti.

h3re:/g's g'n_h3ro/m pre:/k'st kwi/d k'uh1ro/m. g'n_h3ro/s h2e/g't me/dhu k'uh1ro/m. h3re:/g's k'uh1ro/s gwe:/n-kwe k'uh1re/h2. h1so/nt h1i/ns se/g'heti. nu h3re:/g's h1i/m gwr_ne/Ht.

k'e:/r h1yo/d we/k'ti g'ho/stos to/d de/deh3ti. dusme/nesos do/h3r_ wiso/s. pe/h2urom k'e/ywosyo priHo/syo do/h3r_ me/g'h2.

te/k'nom dwi/s k'lush1ye/ nu bhh2dhi/. h3sle/ydht HeHk'e/wih2 dn_g'hwe/h2s. me/nos pro/ h1e/ytu wo:/kws-kwe e/nu h1e/ytu.

bhe/reti dhe/h1leh2 k'le/wos bhe/reti we/kwesh2 h2e/nh1mom. h1sudhe/h1los wiHro/s h1suk'le/we:s.

kwi/d me/h2ter wiHro:/s dhrughyo/nti? su/Hnew h1yo/s dhrughye/ti so/ bhebho/yh2e h1yo/s-kwe bhebho/yh2e so/ dhrughye/ti. h1yo/s ne bhebho/yh2e so/ h1so/nt sokwe/yeti.

pe/h2urom pe/h2wr_ me/g'h2m_ do:/m h1e/wseti. pe/h2uros dhro/wghos me/g'h2 h1no/mn_ h1e/wseti. pe/h2wr_ toy h1e/h3si me: bhe/res.

ghoste/yey h1so/nt we/rh1ye. gho/stim me: dhre/wghs. ph2te:/r dye:/ws gho/stim h2le/kseti. h1yo/s gho/stim dhre/wght so/ deywo/ns dhre/wght.

dhro/wghos te/mHos lubhye/ti h1so/nt bhe/h2os lubhye/ti. bho:/r ne/kwti h1e/yti h1sn_tyo/s wiHro/s dino/y h1e/yti. so/h2wl_ mo/rto:m dhe/h1leh2 dedo/rk'e.

dhu/gh2ter dn_g'hwe/h2m g'ho/stoh1 k'e:/r-kwe ph2sdhi/. te/rh3t dn_g'hwe/h2s ste/h2yeti g'ho/stos k'e:/r-kwe h2eghe/h2 we/k'ti. h1ye/h2 k'e/h2 pe/h2sti se/h2 h1use/wih2 gwe:/n.

h1so/nt m_dhe/wi h1e/sti. h1yo/s me/dhu pi/ph3eti so/ kwid ne ke/wdheti. ne/pot pi/ph3e me/nos-kwe toy ph2sdhi/.

wl_/kwos h2o/wins gwe/mt we/rh1t-kwe h1eg'o/h2 h2o/wis. k'wo:/ h1e/syo h3do/ntn_s wide/t se/kwt-kwe. wo:/kws toy h2e/wis wo:/kws h3do/ntes toy wl_/kwosyo h3do/ntes. wo:/kws sweh2de/wih2 h3do/ntes h2ek'ro:/s. nu k'wo:/ h1i/m h2epo/ h2e/g'et.

h1yo/d ghre/mesi th2ke/h1ye. h1yo/d toy me/nos h3reg'to/m nu bhh2dhi/. ghre/montos wiHro/syo we/kwos g'hoyso/s. priHo/m te/rh3t.

wiHro/s h1yo/d ke/wdheti deywo:/s to/d dedr_k'e:/r. deywo/ns kwis ne dhre/wght. ph2te:/r dye:/ws mo/rtons spe/k'yeti.

kwi/d h2nr_/menes tre/msi? ne tre/mmi. h1eg'o/h2 me/g'h2s. dhe/g'ho:m trome/yeti. dhe/g'ho:m ne trome/yeti. mu/Hs toy ko/lsoy si/sdeti. pri/He h1i/m me/d h2epo/ sl_h2gwdhi/. h1i/m h1pdhi/.

po/h2imen toy k'wo:/ h2Hse/h2i h2e/nti le/gheti wl_/kwos-kwe toy h2o/wins h2epo/ h2e/g'eti. k'wo:/ moy wl_/kwos-kwe priHo/h1. h1i/h2n_s k'o/m h1e/dtes. nu kwi/s h2o/wins pe/h2sti? gwe:/n moy h1i/h2n_s pe/h2sti. wl_/kwos h1e/syeh2s dedwo/ye.

bhre/h2ter po:/lHom dhg'hye/s tl_na:/m. h1e/k'wom tl_na:/m. dwo/h1 h1e/k'woh1 kwe/kwlom-kwe tl_na:/m. dhe/g'ho:m tl_na:/m. nu ku/dhe sth2e/h1yes? bhre/h2ter toy ko/lsoy h1e/pi sth2e/h1yom. ne wide/s?

h1su/k'lewes toy h1e/k'wos HeHk'u/s. HeHk'u/s moy h1e/k'wos HeHk'u/s-kwe h1eg'o/h2. h1e/k'wos toy wl_/kwo:d dhg'hye/s bhe/wgt tu/H-kwe h1e/smi h1e/pi si/sdes. ne bhe/wgm_. wl_/kwom k'o/ymo:d h2epo/ we/dhom. se/kweto te. se/kweto-ge me.

le/ymonm_ dhg'hye/s pe/h3m_. to/smi lime/ni dhg'hye/s h3me/yg'hom. nu wo/dr_ swe/h2dt. k'e/ywe tu/H ph3to/s.

h3re/g' moy misdho/m ne de/h3s. nu twe/ pro/ti kh2no:/h2 gwo/wes-kwe toy me/ronti. gho/stey kh2ne/. h1e/dn_ti ne te k'le/wsonti. nu toy gwe/nh2m_ pro/ti se/ngwho:h2. se/ngwhe. ne me k'le/wseti.

ph2te:/r moy h1o/gwhim gwhe/nt. ph2te:/r moy dwo/h1 h1o/gwhih1 g'hwe:/rm_-kwe gwhe/nt. ph2te:/r moy k'm_to/m h1o/gwhins swo/h1 gwru/h1 gwhe/nt. to/d bhebho/ydhh2e. me/h2te:r toy moy we/wket. muHse/s dedwo/ye.

pri/He gho/stey ke/nkesi? ne ke/nkoh2. nu moy kwi/d moyso/m h2e/k'h3s? tr_syo/m ne ke/nkom. nu wo/dr_ pi/ph3e. wo/dr_ ne pi/ph3oh2. h1eg'o/h2 gho/stis.

su/Hnew h2we/st. sth2dhi/. ph2ter memo/nh2e. kwi/d memo/nth2e? kwi/s h2e/g'rom h2e/rh3ye:ti? tu/H h1i/m h2e/rh3ye:si. nu h2ew le/ghoh2.

kwi/d po/tey dhwo/rm_ kapye/si? po/tni dhe/g'ho:m we/rtetor. dhe/g'ho:m ne we/rtetor. tu/H ph3to/s. nu kwi/d dwo/yh1 po/tnih2h1 dedo/rk'h2e? sme/ytes.

g'e/rh2ont moy we/wke. k'o/smi dino/y h2we/rseti? mo/ghew h2we/rseti. kwi/h1 wo/ydth2e? wo/ydh2e. po/tnih2 moy h2wl_/h1neh2m h2sowse/yeti. h1yo/d h2wl_/h1neh2m h2sowse/yeti h2we/rst.

wi/Hre gwo:/m h2me/lg's? h1su/menes h1i/h2m_ h2me/lg'm_. nu kwi/d h1e/syeh2s h1o/wHdhr_ ple/h1dheti? nu kwi/d h2me/lg'm_? ka/prom h2me/lg's. ghre/meti. ne ghre/meti sme:/yti.

kwi/h1 me/h2ter ph2tre/y we/wko:h2? kwi/d su/Hnew h1e/smey we/wke:si? po:/lHos yugo/m Hrune/pt. h1e/smey wr_h1dhi/. ph2ter toy po:/lHos Hwe/rdht. yugo/m Hrune/pt. ne ghre/me:ti? nu h1e/smey wr_h1dhi/. gwe:/n toy to/d h2e/g't.

g'e/nh1tri moy h2wl_pe/ys spe/lom we/wke. h2wl_/pis k'o/ymom ne/kwti gwe/mt. h2o/wins wide/t. nu kwi/d dhe/h1t? h1i/h2n_s gwr_h3e/t. nu l_ghdhi/. h2wl_pe/ys dedwo/yh2e. me: bhe/yh2s. h2wl_/pis medte/h2.

gho/stey tri/s h2we/st. pri/He me tri/s h2e/les. tu/H h1use/wo:m de/h3to:r. gwo/wes moy lelikwe:/r dhra:/ks-kwe lelo/ykwe. nu toy h2Hse/h2i h1e/pi si/sdo:h2. nu kwid toy ne h1e/sti priHo/s-kwe toy h1e/sti.

h2ne/r k'lush1ye/si? bho:/r h1n_do/m h1e/sti. de/ywog'enh1es h1i/m k'lush1yo/h2. nu sth2dhi/ h1i/m-kwe h1pdhi/. bho:/r me k'lush1ye/ti. dedwo/ye h1eg'o/h2-kwe dedwo/yh2e. nu h1eg'o/h2 h1i/m h1e/poh2. nu bhe/wgt.

kwi/s wi/Hre dh2pno/m h2e/k'h3t? k'e/ywa mu/Hs to/d h2e/k'h3t. mu/Hs me/g'h2 dh2pno/m gwr_h3e/t? nu bhindto/. bhindto/-ge. nu str_h3me/ni k'e/yor. tu/H so/ mu/Hs.

kwi/s po/tey pe/rdt? po/tni k'wo:/ pe/rdt. h2e/g'roy h2e/wibhis k'o/m h1e/sti. nu h2e/g'ro:d pe/rdetor. so/ me/g'h2s k'wo:/. tu/H me/g'h2s k'wo:/.

se/ne h1eg'o/h2 deywo/s. gwo:/s moy toy dh3dhi/. nu h1i/ns sl_h2gwdhi/. k'wo:/ moy deywo/s. h1i/ns pe/h2sti. so/ ne deywo/s. nu tu/H ne deywo/s. h2epo/ gwh2dhi/.

po/tey moy dayh2we:/r wl_/kwos. kwi/d le/wbha to/d bhe/h2si? h2o/wins nos h1e:/dti ne/kwti-kwe Hre/wdHti. ne Hre/wdHti gwe/Hyeti. nu kwi/d gho/steyes bhuge/nt?

dhugh2te:/r h3re:g'e/y h1e/st ne-kwe sme:/yt. h3re:/g's se/kwt. h1yo/d moy dhugh2te:/r sme/yeti to/smey wiHro:/y h1i/h2m_ de/h3oh2. polh1e/wes h2ne/res h1ludho/nt h1i/h2-kwe ne sme:/yt. nu h2yuHn_k'o/s poh2imne/s suHnu/s gwe/mt. ka/proy h1e/pi si/sdet. ka/pros pe/rdt. nu dhugh2te:/r sme:/yt. h3re:/g's poh2imne/s suHno/wey h1i/h2m_ de/h3t.

h2wl_/pis spe/rm_ h1e/pt. spe:/r h2e/g't. h2wl_/pey deywo/ns gwr_nHdhi/ nu-kwe me h1ddhi/. h2wl_/pis h1i/ns gwr_ne/Ht. spe:/r h1e/syeh2s sth3me/ns bhe/wgt. nu h2wl_/pis mle/wHt. h1i/ns h2ew ne gwr_ne/Hoh2.

h2r_/tk'os h2e/g'roy k'e/yo. mu/Hs h1i/m upe/r ste/yghet. h2r_/tk'os bhe/wdht mu/Hsm_-kwe h1e/pt. mu/Hs tre/mt. h2r_/tk'os sme:/yt h1i/m-kwe le/h1t. mu/Hs swo/ns bhre/h2tern_s dre/mt se/kwt-kwe. bhre/h2teres h2r_/tk'os me/d tre/mt.

dwo/h1 bho/rh1e gwo:/m ne/kwti kle/peta:m. h2we/st to/h1-kwe ghre/meta:m. se/nos h2e/g't. h1eg'o/h2 gwo:/m kle/pom. h2ye/wHo: se/kwt. h1eg'o/h2 h1i/h2m_ kle/pom. h1regwesye/t. h1i/h2 swo/m k'o/ymom gwe/h2t poh2ime:/n-kwe h1i/h2m_ h2me/lg't.

ph3to/s h2ne:/r lo/wksneh2m lime/ni wide/t. lo/wksneh2 le/ymonm_ se/ngwt h2e/g't. le/ymonm_ pe/h3oh2 lo/wksneh2m-kwe spe/roh2. no/kwtm_ pe/r pi/ph3et. h2we/st lo/wksneh2-kwe ne/k't. k'o/ymom gwe/h2t we/wket-kwe. po/tni lo/wksneh2m pe/h3m_. nu h1eg'o/h2 medto/s.

h2wl_/pis wl_kwih2e/y we/rh1t. h1yo/d gwe/Hyesi h2o/weyes trome/yonti. gwh2dhi/ k'o/ymoy-kwe h2e/nti gwe/Hye. wl_kwi/h2s gwe/h2t gwe/Hyet-kwe. poh2ime/nes h1i/h2m_ k'luwe/nt dr_me/nt-kwe. h2epo/ dre/mt. h2wl_/pis moyso/m h2e/n h1e/pt. nu h2wl_/pis h2e/g't. wl_/kwih2 h2ew gwe/Hye. moyso/s swe/h2dt.

bho:/r se/neh2s dhwo/rm_ ne/kwti gwe/mt. h1i/h2 h1i/m k'le/wt. Hre/wdHt. po/tey sth2dhi/. gwe/ru bhe/re. da/yh2weres gwm_te/. bho:/r h2epo/ dre/mt. se/neh2 gwe:/n mu/Hs-kwe h2e/n tk'ita:/m.

ka/pros kwe/kwloy h1e/pi sth2e/h1yet. h2r_/tk'os h1ludhe/t ka/pros-kwe sme:/yt. h2r_/tk'e h1idhe/ ne memo/ghth2e. nu kwe/kwlos we/rteto. ka/prom h1i/m pro/ti we/g'het. so/ mle/wHt. pri/He gho/stey gwm_dhi/.

g'e/rh2onts pe/rkwunom dh2pno/y gwr_ne/Ht. nu pe/rdt. h2yuHn_k'o:/s sme/yn_t. g'e/rh2onts sokwe/yet. me: sme/yte. pe/rkwunos ghre/met. k'le/wt me. nu h2yuHn_k'o:/s pe/rkwunom gwr_nHe/nt.

ne/kwti kwis moy h2Hse/h2i h2e/nti ghre/meti. so/ pe/rkwunos? ne h2we/rst. so/ moy str_h3me/ni k'e/ym_h1nos po/tis.

h1e:/dti ne-kwe wr_g'ye/ti. to/d moy ne dayh2we:/r. h1yo/d wo/dr_ pi/ph3e:ti me/rt. dayh2we:/r moy wo/dr_ ne pi/ph3eti. to/d pe/h2wr_.

h1i/m kwis ne we/dheti dh2pno/m-kwe gwm_sk'e/ti. h3re:/g'i h2e/nti h1e:/dti. wiHro:/s h1e/smo:d tre/sonti gwe/nh2es-kwe HrudHe/nti. so/ mu/Hs.

kwi/s dwo/yh1 lo/wksneh2h1 dye/wi dedo/rk'e? kwi/s steh2uro:/y h2roh1dhe/yeti? kwi/s dhwo/ri h2e/nti le/gheti h1e/syo-kwe gwe:/n h1i/m upe/r ste/ygheti? ph3to/s h2ne:/r.

g'r_h2n_te/s g'n_h1yo/h2er. ste/h3mn_ moy ne h1e/sti bhe/h2mi-kwe. gho/steyes me k'lush1yo/nti lite/nt-kwe. ne kwis me gwr_ne/Hti. g'e/rh2onts pe/rdt.

bho:/r h1e/syeh2s bhebho/yh2e h1e/syeh2s po/tis h1e/syeh2s bhebho/yh2e. h1yo/d mu/Hsm_ wide:/ti Hre/wdHti kwe/kwlom-kwe h1e/pi ste/ygheti. kwi/s se/h2? se/h2 po/tnih2.

h2nr_/menes toy k'le/wos me/g'h2. HeHk'u/m-ge mu/Hsm_ h2Hse/h2h1 k'o/m gwhe/ns. nu mu/Hs le/ghet tu/H-kwe-ge tre/ms. h2nr_/menes h1i/m gwru/h1 tri/s stune/ds. wi/Hre toy k'le/wos n_/dhgwhitom. bhre/h2te:r toy swe/so:r-kwe sme/ytes.

gho/stey h1yo/s moyso/ns gwr_h3e/si. h1yo/s-kwe nos dhra:/ghm_ pi/ph3esi. h1yo/s nos str_h3me/ni le/ghesi k'ludhi/ nos. h2e/n gwh2dhi/ deywo:/s toy h1we/suh2 h2e/n dh3ih1e/nt.

nu ph3to/m h2ne/rm_ se/ngwho:mes. le/ymonm_ pe/h3t srowe/h2m-kwe pe/h3t. nu h2use/si h2ew tr_sye/ti. dhwo/ri-kwe h2e/nti gwe/Hyeti. nu gwi/h3woyh1t dhra:/ks-kwe h1e/smo:d me: lelikwye/h1t.

mu/Hses bhre/h2teres HrudHte/. se/nos nos gho/stis k'o/ymom gwe/mt. dh2pno/m h2e/k'h3t h2e/Hsh2m_-kwe le/yg'ht. nu-kwe kwid n_sme/y ne h1e/sti. ke/nkomes h2e/g'rom bhe/wgomes.

nu moy suHnu/m wiHro/m kh2no:/h2. h2e/g'roy kwid ne h2e/rh3yeti. ne kwid tl_ne/h2ti ne-kwe kwid bhe/reti. moyso/m dh2pno/y h1e:/dti pe/h3t-kwe. nu h1e/syo k'le/wos me/g'h2 h1sye/h1t.

we/kwesh2 pine/k'si? we/kwesh2 pine/k'mi h1i/h2-kwe le/g'oh2. ku/dhe pine/k'si? de/yk'troy toy pine/k'mi le/g'esi-kwe.

h2re/h1g'tor toy h2enterowe/kwos h1we/su. gwr_Hti/ns toy de/deh3mi. te/we mo/wdos me/ne mo/wdos. ne/wom prek'no/m pre/k'sdhi.

sme/ymn_ moy we/wke. kwi/s bhe/h2ti dn_g'hwe/h2s-kwe h1e/smey ne h1e/sti? h1eg'o/h2. sme:/ymi. to/d sme/ymn_ h1we/su.

h1eg'o/h2 h1e/wkto:r. se/neh2 we/kwesh2 le/g'oh2. le/g'e dok'e/yo:h2-kwe te. kwi/d do/k'tor k'o/d we/kwos? h1i/d se/nom we/kwos ph2te/res-kwe toy h1i/d bhh2e/nt.

h2enterowe/kwos ne we/wkes. to/d ne we/k'm_. nu pre/k'sdhi. ku/dhe moy ph2tre/s h1e/k'wos h1e/sti? ne wo/ydh2e. h1e/k'wom ne dedo/rk'h2e.

kwi/h1 mle/wHmi? sne/ygwheti. sne/ygwheti. swesre/y toy we/wke. sni/gwhs po/ntoh1i k'e/yor. g'he/yo:m gwegwo/me. sto/yghoy me: h3sle/ydhs.

kwi/h1 k'o/d we/wko:h2? tr_syo/h2. tr_syo/h2. nu wo/dr_ pre/k'sdhi. h2e/kweh2m moy dh3dhi/. mok's h1e/wks.

k'wo:/ moy h2Hse/h2i se/sti. g'e/rh2onts h1e/sti? g'e/rh2onts h1e/sti. h2o/wins nos pe/h2st. lewbho/s k'wo:/. ssye/h1t. wl_/kwons swo/y swe/pnoy wide/h1yeti. h2o/wins toy h2e/n pe/h2sti.

h1regwesye/ti. do:/m h1e/ymi. sto/yghos toy h1we/sus h1sye/h1t. h2ew bhe/h2omes. kwse/pi ssdhi/ h2ew-kwe gwm_dhi/.

suHnu/s moy swesre/y g'n_h1ye/to. to/d me/g'h2s mo/wdos. kwi/d h1e/syo h1no/mn_? h1suk'le/we:s h1e/syo h1no/mn_. h1we/su h1no/mn_. h2uksye/h1t Hwr_dhye/h1t-kwe. me/h2te:r moy me/wdt.

priHo/s moy po:/lHom de/h3t. h1e/y h1use/wo:m de/h3to:r. to/m po:/lHom lubhye/si? h1i/m lubhyo/h2. me/g'h2m_ moy mo/wdom de/deh3ti. h1i/m ph2sdhi/ h2we/kseti-kwe.

kwi/d udre/h2? udre/h2 udro/s g'hwe:/r. we/dni sne/h2ti dhg'hu/Hn_s-kwe h2e/k'h3t. udre/h2 me/g'h2ih2 h1e/sti? h1i/h2 me/g'h2ih2 ne h1e/sti. h1i/h2 we/desi HeHk'e/wih2.

bhre/h2te:r moy k'o/smi dino/y sme:/yt. kwi/d sme:/yt? wo/dr_ h1n_do/ se/ngwm_. ste/wds? ste/wdm_ ne/somedhh2-kwe. k'o/m sme/yih1tom.

wih1ro/m dhg'hye/s g'ne/h3m_. kwi/s h1e/y? h1e/y k'o/ymo:d poh2ime:/n. h1e/y h1we/sus wih1ro/s? h1e/y moy k'e/ywos. wr_h1e/nm_ moy de/h3t. nu h1e/y priHo/s.

ku/dhe se/neh2m dn_g'hwe/h2m h1e/wks? h1i/h2m_ se/noybhos we/kwesbhos h1e/wkm_. polh1u/h2 we/kwesh2 wo/ydth2e? polh1u/h2 we/kwesh2 wo/ydh2e. ne/weh2 ne wo/ydh2e. nu te ne/weh2 dok'e/yo:h2. dok'e/ye me k'l_ne/woh2-kwe.

le/g'esi? le/g'oh2 pine/k'mi-kwe. ke/h2nmn_ moy pink'dhi/. no/kwts gwm_ye/ti. wl_/kwos ste/ygheti h2o/weyes-kwe sse/nti. k'o/d ke/h2nmn_ we/nHm_.

po/tnih2m moy lubhyo/h2. h1i/h2 te lubhye/ti? lubhye/ti me. me:/ms moy pe/kweti. nu toy po/tn_yeh2ey se/ngwhe. h1e/syeh2ey we/kwsperoy se/ngwho:h2. deywo:/s wos h1we/suh2 dh3ih1e/nt.

nu moy ph2te:/r g'e/rh2onts. se/nons dino/ns memo/ne? memo/ne. h1e/k'wons h3re/g'm_-kwe mle/wHti. h1i/m k'l_nudhi/ h1e/syo-kwe we/kwesh2 memn_dhi/. h1i/h2 meme/noh2.

kwi/h1 k'o/d we/rh1oh2? me/h2term_ moy ste/rgoh2. me/h2term_ moy ste/rgoh2. h1e/syeh2ey h2Hse/h2i skwdhi/. swe/h2desh1 me/wdeti. me/h2tr_s k'e:/r gwhermo/m.

k'o/smi dino/y line/kwmi. h1e/nom k'o/ymom h1e/ymi. k'e/ywa te gwe:/n deywo:/s po/ntoh1i ph2sih1e/nt. n_sdhi/. ke/h2ilos h1sdhi/. g'hye/mi h2ew gwe/moh2. gwm_dhi/ swe/sorm_-kwe toy we/dhe.

pri/He ke/h2ilos h1sdhi/. gho/stey ke/h2ilos h1sdhi/. do:/m moy wik'dhi/ h2Hse/h2i-kwe sddhi/. do:/m toy h1we/sus. n_sme/y priHo/s h1sdhi/.

dhwo/rm_ toy gwegwo/mh2e. gwr_/Hte gho/stey. me/h3yesi. h1ddhi/ ph3dhi/-kwe. tu/H moy k'e/ywos. tu/H deywo/ybhos keh2ro/s.

nu do:/m h1e/ymi. pri/He moy ke/h2ilos h1sdhi/. deywo:/s te ph2sih1e/nt. do:/m-kwe toy ph2sih1e/nt. h2ew gwm_dhi/.

we/wke moy kwi/s toy de/mi tk'e/yti? ph2te:/r moy me/h2te:r-kwe h1idhe/ tk'ite/s. dwo/h1 moy bhre/h2terh1e swe/so:r-kwe h1idhe/ tk'iye/nti. po/tnih2 toy h2e/n h1e/sti? h2e/n h1e/sti h2wl_/h1neh2h1-kwe wr_g'ye/ti.

h1e/k'woy toy h1e/pi re/ydho:h2? re/ydhe me:-kwe se/ngws. h1e/k'wos we/rtetor. kwi/d dhe/dheh1oh2? h1e/smey bhh2dhi/. twe/d ne bhebho/yh2e. re/ydhoh2 mo/wdos-kwe moy k'e/rdi h1e/sti.

g'r_h2no/m toy h2we/kst. h2we/kst h2e/k'esh2-kwe pl_h1to/. h1i/d mok's skine/domes. deywo/ybhos g'r_h2no/m de/deh3si? deywo/ybhos g'r_h2no/m dedh3me/s h1e/yes-kwe nos n_bhro/m de/dh3n_ti.

kwi/h1 k'o/d bhe/h2mi? po/ntoh1i k'e/mh2m_. po/ntoh1i k'e/mh2m_. h1i/d h2g'dhi/ wo/dr_-kwe toy de/h3oh2. k'e/mh2m_. nu ph3dhi/ l_ghdhi/-kwe.

gho/stipotey ke/h2ilos h1sdhi/. h1n_do/m gwm_dhi/ sddhi/-kwe. h1ludho/m k'e/mh2m_-kwe. me:/ms h1ddhi/ me/dhu-kwe ph3dhi/. h1i/d swe/h2dt me/dhu-kwe sweh2du/. gho/stey nos priHo/s h1sdhi/.

pri/He wo/dr_ h2isk'o/h2. h2ne/r ke/h2ilos h1sdhi/. h1e/sti wo/dr_ h1idhe/. h1n_do/ gwm_ye/ swe/so:r moy pe/kweti. tu/H h1use/wo:m de/h3to:r. sddhi/.

nu moy pri/He k'o/ymom h1e/ymi. me: h1e/ys. h1regwesye/ti. h1idhe/ l_ghdhi/. kwi/d h1e/dte? me:/ms h3n_gwe/nh1 h1e/dmes. nu se/doh2.

kwi/s tu/H? h1eg'o/h2 h1suk'le/we:s. nu kwi/s tu/H? h1eg'o/h2 h2nr_me/ne:s gho/stis. gwr_/Hte gho/stey. me/ne ph2te/ri h1e/pi sddhi/ ph3dhi/-kwe. deywo:/s toy h1we/suh2 dh3ih1e/nt.

k'o/d me/lit dluku/? h1i/d swe/h2dyos. bhre/h2te:r moy h1i/d dhg'hye/s wide/t. h1i/d moy h1ope/ye. h1i/d h1a/yso h1i/d-kwe se/lpesh1 gwr_h3e/. h1i/d-ge sweh2du/.

kwi/d h2use/si dhe/dheh1si? gwo:/s h2use/si h2me/lg'mi. nu moy bhre/h2teres h1i/ns h2e/g'rom we/dhonti. kwi/d toy swe/so:r dhe/dheh1ti? h2o/wins h2me/lg'ti h3e/ngwn_-kwe h2re/ydheti. we/kwsperoy ph2we/ni h1e/pi si/sdomes gwe/Hyomes-kwe.

se/h2lm_ h3e/ngwn_ h1n_do/ dhedhh1te/? h1i/m h1n_do/ dhedhh1me/s h3e/ngwn_-kwe swe/h2dt. me/h2te:r moy k'o/d bhe/h2ti se:/h2l se/lpesos priHo/s. nu moy h1e/y priHo/s. k'e/ywe sme:/ysi.

kwi/h1 me:/ms h2linHte/? h1i/d se/lpesh1 h2linHme/s h1i/d-kwe ph2we/ni h1e/pi pe/kwomes. pe/h2wr_ toy we/ydsoh2. gwm_dhi/ wide/-kwe. h1i/d dhe/gwheti.

kwi/d me/dhu? me/dhu me/lit wo/dr_-kwe. h1yo/s h1i/d pi/ph3eti so/ gwe/Hyeti. nu moy me/dhu dh3dhi/ gwe/Hyo:h2-kwe. k'e/ywe ph3dhi/ se/ngwhe-kwe.

kwi/d k'o/ymom h2re/ydhete? gho/steyes n_sme/ gwm_sk'o/nti. k'o/m h1e/domes pi/ph3o:mes-kwe. kwi/s gwm_sk'e/ti? bhre/h2teres moy h1e/yso:m-kwe suHne/wes gwm_sk'o/nti. k'o/m gwe/moh2. gwm_ye/. gho/stis nos h1sdhi/.

we/wke moy kwi/d dhg'hye/s h1e/dte? me:/ms dhg'hye/s h1e/dme me/dhu-kwe ph3me/. ph2te:/r moy gwe/Hyet sme/yme-kwe. swe/so:r toy sme:/yt? sme:/yt h1i/m-kwe gwr_ne/Ht.

kwi/d deywo/ybhos dedh3te/? h1e/ymos g'huto/m me/dhu se/lpos-kwe dedh3me/s. deywo:/s gwr_h3o/nti? h1n_gwni/s nos de/h3reh2 gwr_h3e/ti h1i/h2-kwe deywo/ns bhe/reti. h1we/suh2 wos h1a/yn_t? suHnu/ns nos dhugh2te/rn_s-kwe h1a/yn_t.

tr_syo/h2. pri/He me/dhu ph3dhi/. h1i/d dluku/. k'o/smi dino/y h1e:/ds? ne h1e:/dm_. h2use/si h1e/g'hs h1e:/m. nu gwr_h3e/.

suHne/wes moy k'o/smi dino/y ne h1e/dn_t. h1i/ns h1n_do/ we/dhe. po/tnih2 moy pe/kweti. tu/H nos h1sume/ne:s. dye:/ws ph2te:/r gho/stins pe/h2sti. ph2sye/h1t te.

gwhre/h1yeti tr_syo/h2-kwe. ku/dhe ste/yghes? sh2we/ni ste/yghom. sddhi/. dhugh2te:/r moy wo/dr_ bhe/reti. dhugh2te:/r toy h1sume/ne:s. ph3dhi/ tr_pso/-kwe.

gho/stipotey toy do/h3r_ bhe/rom. kwi/d h1i/d? to/d h2r_g'n_to/m. to/d me/g'h2 do/h3r_. h1e/k'wom toy we/dho:h2. h1e/k'wos. tu/H moy k'e/ywos. pri/He n_sme/ gwm_ye/.

kwi/d ghosti/bhos de/h3reh2 de/deh3si? h1yo/s de/h3nom h1a/yto so/ de/h3nom h1a/yt. ph2te:/r moy polh1u/h2 de/h3o:r de/h3t polh1e/wes-kwe priHo:/s h1e/smey h1se/nti. nu toy do/h3r_ de/h3oh2. gho/stey toy moy we/kwos sweh2du/.

kwi/d toy me/h2tr_s h1we/dmn_ h1e/st? h1e/syeh2s h1we/dmn_ de/k'm_ gwo/wes h1e/st. to/d me/g'h2 wo/sn_. me/h2te:r moy me/ne ph2tre/y lewbhe/h2. bhe/h2ti h1i/h2 moy h2e/h2usom. h1i/h2m_ lubhye/ti.

kwi/d h1we/sus h2ne:/r ghoste/yey de/deh3ti? h1e/smey me/dhu h1ope/yeti h1i/m-kwe ph2we/ni h1e/pi sode/yeti. kwi/d gho/stis h1ope/yeti? sweh2du/h2 we/kwesh2 we/rh1yeti. h1yo/d line/kwti de/h3rom de/deh3ti.

kwi/s toy k'a:/m h2wl_/h1neh2m de/h3t? swe/so:r moy h1i/h2m_ de/h3t. h1i/h2 moy lewbhe/h2. h1i/h2 ne/weh2 h2wl_/h1neh2. suHnu/ns moy wose/yo:h2. suHne/wes toy gwhre/h1yo:nti.

gwo:/m kwrine/h2mi. kwi/d k'o/syeh2s gwe/ws wo/sn_? de/k'm_ h2o/weyes. to/d polh1u/. swe/k's toy h2o/wins de/h3oh2. ok'to:/w ta:/s dh3dhi/ h1i/h2m_-kwe toy de/h3oh2. nu h1i/h2n_s h1a/yso. pri/He toy h1i/h2m_ h2e/g'rom we/dhe.

h2wl_/h1neh2m moy pe/rh2esi? h1i/h2m_ toy pe/rh2oh2. kwi/d moy h1ope/ye:si? kwe/rtrom toy h1ope/yo:h2. kwe/rtrom? kwe/rtreh2 ne wo/ydh2e. h2r_g'n_to/m moy dh3dhi/ k'wo/nm_-we: bhe/re. nu toy h1i/d bhe/ro:h2.

ku/dhe k'o/m h1e/k'wom kwre/yh2s? h1i/m poh2imne/s kwre/yh2m_ to/smey kwe/kwlom de/h3m_. h1e/y HeHk'u/s? h1e/y-ge HeHk'u/s suHne/wes-kwe moy h1i/m lubhyo/nti.

kwi/d k'o/syo k'une/s wo/sn_? h1e/syo wo/sn_ ti/sres h2o/weyes. h2o/weyes moy ne h1se/nti. nu moy h1e/k'wom h1ope/ye. h1e/k'wos? to/d polh1u/. so/ h1we/sus k'wo:/ kwe/kwlom moy h2le/kseti.

kwi/d dhh2bhro/s? dhh2bhro/s ph2we/ni h1e/pi wr_g'ye/ti. h2r_g'n_to/m gwhr_ne/wti h3re:g'bho/s-kwe de/h3reh2 te:/tk'ti. dhh2bhro/s toy k'o/ymoy h1e/sti? me/h2tr_s moy bhre/h2te:r dhh2bhro/s.

kwi/s wos wose/yeti? me/h2te:r moy n_sme/ h2wl_/h1neh2h1 wose/yeti. h2wl_/h1neh2m we/sth2er. h1i/h2 wr_g'ye/ti gwe/Hyeti-kwe. h1i/h2 nos priHe/h2.

kwi/s h1e/nom kwe/kwlom kwe/rt? k'o/ymosyo nos dhh2bhro/s h1i/m kwe/rt. bhre/h2te:r moy h1e/smey misdho/m me/dhu de/h3t. kwe/kwlom moy kwe/reti? h1i/m pre/k'sdhi. h2r_g'n_to/m lubhye/ti.

kwi/h1 me:/ms skindte/? h1i/d sokso/h1 skindme/s. we/y kwe/rtroh1 skindme/s. kwe/rtrom? kwi/d to/d? ph2te/res nos sokso:/ys skinde/nt.

kwi/h1 pe/kwete? wo/dr_ ph2we/ni h1e/pi gwhr_nume/s ye/seti-kwe. nu moy po/tnih2 se/lpos h1i/d h1n_do/ dhe/dheh1ti. h1i/d sweh2du/? dhugh2te/res moy h1i/d gwr_h3o/nti sme/yn_ti-kwe.

polh1u/ toy h2e/h2usom h1e/sti? ne kwid moy h1e/sti. gwo/wes moy h2o/weyes h1e/k'wo:s-kwe h1se/nti. h2ne/res h2e/h2usom ne h2k'h3e/nt. nu toy to/y h2e/h2usom. h2e/lonti-ge nos.

ku/dhe h2r_g'n_to/m gwm_sk'e/ti? wiHro:/s h1i/d neh2ubhi/s we/g'honti. kwi/d h1i/h1 kwrinh2te/? kwe/kwlons kwrinh2me/s bhre/h2te:r-kwe moy swesre/y me/dhu kwrine/h2ti.

kwi/h1 k'o/d mle/wHmi? se/h2lm_ moy dh3dhi/. se/h2lm_ moy dh3dhi/. to/d wr_h1dhi/ h1i/m-kwe toy bhe/ro:nti.

kwi/h1 k'o/d bhe/h2mi? me/lit dluku/. me/lit dluku/. me/dhu swe/h2dyos. nu me/dhu pe/h3oh2.

kwi/d h2use/si h1e:/dsi? me:/ms h3n_gwe/nh1 h2use/si h1e:/dmi po/tnih2 moy h1i/d pe/kweti. kwi/d tu/H h1e:/dsi? me/lit h1e:/dmi wo/dr_-kwe pi/ph3oh2. h1i/d h2use/si dluku/.

kwi/d gho/stis? so/ h2ne:/r h1yo/s nos k'o/ymom gwm_ye/ti. h1i/m h2e/lomes priHo/s-kwe nos bhuHye/ti. h1eg'o/h2 toy gho/stis? tu/H nos gho/stis tu/H-kwe nos lewbho/s.

pri/He moy kwid wr_h1dhi/. k'le/wse. wl_/kwos k'o/ymom h1n_do/ gwe/mt. me/lit wide/t h1i/d-kwe h2e/k'h3t. nu k'wo/nes wl_/kwom kwk'e/nt bhe/wgt-kwe. HeHk'e/wes k'wo/nes.

se/ngwhe moy. sweh2du/ me/dhu keh2ro/s-kwe nos gho/stis. deywo:/s h1e/smey h1we/suh2 dh3ih1e/nt. we/kwesh2 toy dluku/h2 me/wdm_-kwe.

kwi/h1 k'o/d we/wko:h2? h1e:/dm_ teto/rph2e-kwe. h1e:/dm_ teto/rph2e-kwe. me/h2trey moy to/d wr_h1dhi/ sme/yeti-kwe.

kwi/d se:/h2l? h1e/y se/lesos gwm_sk'e/ti. gwe/wbhos h1i/m dedh3me/s. h1i/m lubhyo/nti? h1i/m lubhyo/nti h1i/m-kwe lig'he/nti.

kwi/d h2e/h2usom? h3re/g'es h1i/d we/sn_tor deywo:/s-kwe h1i/d lubhyo/nti. h2e/h2usom we/sth2er? h1i/d ne we/sh2er. h1yo/d gho/steyes gwm_sk'o/nti po/tnih2 moy h1i/d we/stor. nu toy h1i/d po/tn_yeh2ey keh2ro/m. h1i/d h1e/syeh2ey keh2ro/m h1i/h2-kwe moy keh2re/h2.

pri/He moy kwid we/wke. wl_/kwos h2o/wins h2e/g'roy wide/t. nu k'wo:/ h1i/m k'le/wt wl_/kwos-kwe bhe/wgt. poh2ime:/n sme:/yt. we/kwesh2 toy moy k'e/yweh2.

kwi/s h1o/gwhim gwhe/nt? wiHro/s h1o/gwhim h2k'm_ne/h1 gwhe/nt. h1o/gwhis gwo:/s k'e/let wiHro/s-kwe to/ns spe/rt. nu me/g'h2 k'le/wos h1a/yto. kwi/s h1e/y h1e/st? h1e/y h1suk'le/we:s h1e/st.

kwi/s deywo:/m ph2te:/r? dye:/ws h1e/yso:m ph2te:/r dhe/g'ho:m-kwe me/h2te:r. dye:/ws dhg'he/mi h1e/pi le/ghet. nu h2e/wso:s gwe/mt He/h2mr_-kwe le/wkt. nu g'ne/h3m_.

k'e/ywe moy h2roh1dhe/ye. k'le/wsoh2. wo/rmos bho/lyom bhe/ret. nu su/Hs gwe/mt h1i/d-kwe h2e/k'h3t. wo/rmos Hre/wdHt. nu kwi/d wo/rmos dhe/h1t? ne/wom bho/lyom h2isk'e/t.

kwi/s pe/rkwunos? h1e/y me/g'h2s deywo/s. gwhe/nt h1o/gwhim h2k'm_ne/h1 we/do:r-kwe h1ne/dht. h1e/smo:d dedwo/yth2e? h1e/smo:d dedwo/yh2e gwo:/s-kwe nos h2le/kseti.

ke/h2nmn_ moy gwe/Hye. h2use/y diwe/s dhugh2tre/y gwe/Hyoh2. h2e/wsos h2usdhi/ le/wkm_-kwe nos bhe/re. to/d ke/h2nmn_ h1we/su.

kwi/d we/kwsperoy dhe/dheh1si? we/kwsperoy ph2we/ni h1e/pi se/dyomes. bhre/h2te:r moy se/ngwheti suHne/wes-kwe moy k'l_nwe/nti. kwi/d se/ngwheti? h2n_ro:/m k'le/wesh2 se/ngwheti.

h1n_gwni/m g'huHe/yesi? h1i/m g'huHe/yoh2 h1i/m-kwe gwr_ne/Hmi. h1n_/gwney h1use/wo:m de/h3tor. k'o/ymom nos h2le/kse. h1i/m k'o/m gwr_ne/Homes.

tu/H ke/h2nto:r? h1eg'o/h2 ne ke/h2nto:r. swe/so:r moy we/kwsperoy gwe/Hyeti h2o/weyes-kwe h1i/h2m_ k'le/wsonti. h2o/weyes sme/yn_ti? ne sme/yn_ti le/ghyonti-kwe.

kwo/ti h2ste/res dye/wi h1se/nti? ne kwis wo/yde. me/h2te:r moy mle/wHti h2ste/res diwe/s gwo/wes. kwi/s h1i/ns pe/h2sti? lo/wksneh2 h1i/ns pe/h2sti.

ku/dhe so/h2wl_ ne/kwti h1e/yti? so/h2wl_ dhe/g'ho:m Hn_dhe/r h1e/yti. nu h2e/wso:s h1i/d h2ew we/dheti. to/d h1so/nt? me/h2te:r moy to/d we/rh1t.

kwi/d lo/wksneh2? lo/wksneh2 ne/kwts le/wks. dye/wi ste/ygheti so/h2wl_-kwe se/kwetor. h1i/d h1r_h1pye/ti? h1i/d ne h1r_h1pye/ti. nu h1i/d h2ew se/h2gyeti.

dhg'hye/s h2we/rst? dhg'hye/s ne h2we/rst. h2e/g'ro:s nos h2susyo/nti. pe/rkwunom g'huHe/yo:mes. pe/rkwune nos h2wr_sdhi/.

deywo:/s nos k'lush1yo/nti? deywo:/s nos k'lush1yo/nti h1yo/d h1e/ymos kh2no/mes. dhro/wghom ne k'lush1yo/nti. nu h1so/nth2 we/kwesh2 bhe/h2oh2.

deywo:/m h1no/mn_h2 wo/ydth2e? h1i/h2 wo/ydh2e. h1n_gwni/s nos k'o/ymoy tk'e/yti. ne/bhtus we/dn_su tk'e/yti. kwo/m deywo/m lubhye/si? h2e/wsosm_ lubhyo/h2. h1i/h2 He/h2mr_ bhe/reti.

nu k'o/ymom h1e/ymi. gho/stey ke/h2ilos h1sdhi/. deywo:/s toy h1we/suh2 dh3ih1e/nt. h2ew gwm_dhi/. deywo:/s te ph2sih1e/nt.

kwi/d deywo/ybhos mle/wHsi? mle/wHmi de/ywo:s moy gwo:/s h2o/wins-kwe ph2ste/. nu h1e/ymos h1erkwo/m de/deh3mi. k'lush1yo/nti te? k'lush1yo/nti me gwo/wes-kwe moy ple/h1dhontor.

ne/h2wi h1e/ymi. ne/bhtus te ph2sye/h1t. me: tre/ses. we/dn_s dedwo/yh2e deywo/ybhos-kwe bhebho/ydhh2e. nu h1idhi/ ke/h2ilos-kwe h1sdhi/.

kwi/d wiHro:/s h2isk'o/nti? wiHro:/s n_/dhgwhitom k'le/wos h2isk'o/nti. m_re/nt h1e/yso:m-kwe k'le/wos gwi/h3weti. kwi/d tu/H h2isk'e/si? gwo:/m moy h2isk'o/h2. lelo/ykwe.

kwi/s tu/H? h1eg'o/h2 deywo/k'lewe:s. ph2te:/r moy k'o/d h1no/mn_ dhe/h1t. k'luto/m h1no/mn_. k'le/wos moy me/g'h2 bhuHye/h1t.

kwi/d k'le/wos? k'le/wos we/kwos h2ne/res h1yo/d k'lush1yo/nti. h1we/sus h2ne:/r h1suk'le/we:s. nu h1we/suh2 dhe/dheh1oh2.

swo/pniyom ne/kwti wido/m. kwi/d wide/s? wl_/kwos me se/kweto. bhe/yh2m_. me: bhe/yh2s. swo/pniyom ne wl_/kwos. swo/pniyosyo kwim wl_/kwos ne h1e:/dti.

kwi/d su/pniyom? h1yo/d ne/kwti le/ghesi me/nos toy h1e/g'hs h1e/yti ne/weh2-kwe spe/k'yeti. to/d su/pniyom. we/kwesh2 toy h1so/nth2.

swo/pniyeh2 wide/h1yesi? h1i/h2 wide/h1yoh2. ph2te/rm_ moy swo/pniyoy dhg'hye/s wido/m. h1e/y pe/ruti me/rt. sme:/yt gwe/Hyet-kwe moy. h1e/y te lubhye/ti.

kwi/h1 k'o/d mle/wHmi? ne/kwts dedwo/yh2e. ne/kwts dedwo/yh2e. me/h2trey toy k'o/d skwdhi/. h1i/h2 te h2le/kseti.

kwi/d sme:/ysi? suHnu/s moy dhg'hye/s g'n_h1ye/to. k'e:/r moy mo/wdoh1 pl_nh1to/r. gwi/h3woyh1t h2uksye/h1t-kwe. gwm_dhi/ ph3dhi/-kwe.

kwi/h1 k'o/d bhe/h2mi? k'e/mh2m_. k'e/mh2m_. sddhi/ ph3dhi/-kwe. tu/H moy k'e/ywos.

kwi/d toy swe/so:r Hre/wdHti? h1e/syeh2s k'wo:/ lelo/ykwe. h1i/m h2isk'e/ti h1i/m-kwe ne wide/t. k'wo/nm_ k'o/m se/h2gyo:mes. tu/H h1we/sus priHo/s.

ph2te:/r toy h2eh2o/ghe? h2eh2o/ghe. g'hwe:/r nos dwo/yh1 h2o/wih1 gwhe/nt. deywo:/s h1e/smey ne/wa:s h2o/wins dh3ih1e/nt.

k'o/ymom toy gwegwo/mh2e. gwr_/Hte gho/stey. k'e:/r moy me/wdt. ph2we/ni h1e/pi sddhi/ h1ddhi/-kwe. pri/He moy tu/H keh2ro/s.

ke/h2nmn_ memo/nth2e h1yo/d dhg'hye/s se/ngwhome. h1i/d memo/nh2e. h1i/d h2ew se/ngwho:mes. we/kwesh2 ne memo/nh2e. h1i/h2 toy sokwe/yo:h2. k'l_nudhi/.

dn_g'hwe/h2m me toy dok'e/ye. we/kwesh2 moy k'le/wse h1i/h2-kwe we/rh1ye. k'o/d wo/dr_. k'o/d pe/h2wr_. h1e/wkm_.

kwi/s te dok'e/yet? me/h2te:r me ke/h2nmn_h2 dok'e/yet. swe/so:r moy k'luto/s ke/h2nto:r. h1e/syeh2s moy ke/h2nmn_ kh2ne/. nu h2k'h2owsye/.

kwi/d mn_ye/th2er? bhre/h2term_ moy mn_yo/h2er. h3re:g'e/s k'o/ymom gwe/h2t h2ew-kwe ne gwegwo/me. mok's h2ew gwm_ye/h1t. we/kwesbhos toy bhebho/ydhh2e.

kwi/s h2ste/rn_s h2e/n dhe/h1t? ne wo/ydh2e. bhre/h2te:r moy sokwe/yeti deywo:/s h1i/ns dhh1e/nt. ku/dhe h1e/yes g'n_h1yo/nto? polh1u/h2 pre:/k's. bhre/h2term_ moy pre/k'sdhi.

we/kwos toy h1so/nt? we/kwos moy h1so/nt. bhoydhe/h2m toy de/deh3mi. deywo:/s to/d k'luwih1e/nt. nu toy bhebho/ydhh2e.

h2ne:/r me dhre/wght. kwi/d h2e/g't? h2e/g't k'o/s h1e/k'wos HeHk'u/s. so/ h1e/k'wos ne dre/mt. to/syo h2n_re/s we/kwesh2 dhro/wgho:s. h1e/smey kwid me: de/h3s.

k'm_to/m h1e/k'wons dhg'hye/s wido/m. to/d h1so/nt? mn_yo/h2er to/d dhro/wghos. HeHk'e/wes h1e/k'wo:s h2e/g'roy dr_me/nt. nu me/g'h2h2 dedo/rk'th2e.

sme/ymn_ moy we/wke. su/Hs h3re:g'e/s k'o/ymom gwe/h2t. h3re:/g's we/wket kwi/s tu/H. su/Hs h2e/g't h1eg'o/h2 toy ne/wos h3re:/g's. ne/wos h3re:/g's.

k'wo:/ toy pe/rdetor. ne pe/rdetor. tu/H pe/rdeth2er. dhrughye/si me. pri/He sme:/ymi. to/d sme/ymn_.

pri/He ke/h2ilos h1sdhi/. kwi/s tu/H? h1eg'o/h2 poh2ime:/n. h1eg'o/h2 h1suk'le/we:s. kwi/d toy gho/stey h1no/mn_? dweh2ro:/d gwegwo/mh2e. nu moy ph2we/ni h1e/pi sddhi/.

bhre/h2teres toy swe/sores-kwe h1se/nti? tre/yes moy bhre/h2teres dwo/yh1-kwe swe/sorh1e h1se/nti. bhre/h2teres moy ma/rkons ph2se/nti swe/sorh1e-kwe moy h2wl_/h1neh2m tkste/s. swe/so:r toy h1e/sti? swe/so:r moy h1e/sti dweh2ro/y-kwe tk'e/yti. dye:/ws h1i/h2m_ h2le/ksoyh1t.

wi/k'potey h1idhe/ gwm_dhi/. kwi/d h2use/si h1e:/ds? h2me/lg'os pe/h3m_. nu gwo:/s h2e/g'rom h2e/g'om. h2e/n wr_g'ye/si? ne wr_g'yo/h2 si/sdoh2 se/ngwhoh2-kwe dye:/m-kwe spe/k'yoh2. kwi/d h1e:/ds?

tu/H k'o/syo k'o/ymosyo po/tnih2? h1eg'o/h2 po/tnih2 po/tis moy wik'po/tis. suHne/wes toy h1se/nti? dwo/h1 moy suHnu/h1 dhugh2te:/r-kwe h1se/nti. dhugh2te:/r moy h1eg'o/h2-kwe tkswo/s suHnu/h1-kwe po:/lHons h2e/g'etes. te/ksi? ne te/ksmi. nu h1idhe/ sddhi/ h1ukdhi/-kwe.

h2ne/r h1we/sus h1sye/h1s. ku/dhe tk'e/ysi? k'o/ymoy we/dni h1e/pi tk'e/ymi. h2e/nHos moy h1idhe/ h1e/sti spe/leh2-kwe ne/kwti mle/wHti. ku/dhe toy h2e/wh2os h1e/sti? pe/ruti me/rt nu dhg'he/mi Hn_dhe/r k'e/yor. h2e/nHos toy gwi/h3weti? gwi/h3weti h1i/h2-kwe moy k'e/yweh2 h1e/sti.

kwi/d we/kwsperoy dhedhh1te/? k'o/m se/dyomes. h2e/wh2os moy deywo/ybhos h1erkwo/ns gwr_ne/Hti k'le/wsomes-kwe. nu le/ghyomes supno/s-kwe nos gwm_sk'e/ti. we/kwsperoy se/dyoh2 ne-kwe kh2no/h2. kwi/d ne kh2ne/si? kh2ne/ nos.

po/h2imen ke/h2ilos h1sdhi/. kwe/yes toy deywo:/s? ph2te:/r dye:/ws upe/r stesto/h2e me/h2te:r-kwe dhe/g'ho:m Hn_dhe/r k'e/yor. h2e/wso:s diwe/s dhugh2te:/r He/h2mr_ bhe/reti. kwi/m gwr_ne/Hsi? deywo/ns ne gwr_ne/Hmi. nu toy me: ghre/moyh1ent.

wi/k'potey. kwi/d h2ste/res? h2ste/res ne/kwts dhugh2te/res. no/kwts h1i/ns we/g'heti h2use/si-kwe h2epo/ ih2e/nti. h2ste/rn_s toy k'o/ymoy dedo/rk'th2e? h1i/ns ne dedo/rk'h2e ne/kwti ne h1regwesye/ti. nu h1idhe/ gwm_dhi/ h2ste/rn_s-kwe spe/k'ye.

h1yo/d h2ne:/r me/reti ku/dhe h1e/yti. dweh2ro/y me/g'h2i we/dni upe/r h1e/yti. swo/ns ph2te/rn_s h2e/n wide:/ti dh2pno/y-kwe h1e/pi se/dyonti ne-kwe ke/nkonti. mr_te/ys dedwo/yth2e? ne dedwo/yh2e k'le/wos moy gwi/h3we:ti. kwo/smo:d tu/H dedwo/yth2e? mr_te/ys dedwo/yh2e.

po/tey ke/h2ilos h1sdhi/. kwi/d h2n_re/y h1we/su? polh1e/wes ma/rko:s suHne/wes k'le/wos-kwe. h1suk'le/we:s wiHro/s ghosti/bhos de/deh3ti dhe/h1sn_s-kwe gwr_ne/Hti. h1yo/d me/reti h1e/syo k'le/wos n_/dhgwhitom. kwi/d toy h1we/su? priHo:/s moy ke/h2nmn_h2-kwe. nu moy h1i/h2 kh2ne/.

po/tey. h3re:/g's wos h1e/sti? h3re:/g's nos h1e/sti so/ h2nr_me/ne:s. me/g'h2 dh2pno/m dhg'hye/s de/h3t polh1u/h2 nos pe/h3tleh2 de/h3t ph3me/-kwe gwe/Hyome-kwe. h2e/n h1e/s? h2e/n h1e/sm_ k'o/smi-kwe dino/y k'e/mh2m_. h3re:/g's wos h1e/sti? h3re:/g's nos ne h1e/sti. nu wos kwi/s dh2pne/h2 de/deh3ti?

deywo:/s toy h1we/suh2 dh3ih1e/nt. ko/ryom h1e/ysi? bhre/h2teres moy ko/ryom h1ye/nti h1eg'o/h2 moy me/h2term_ pe/h2smi. gho/steyes nos gwo:/s pe/ruti ste/h2yont h1i/ns-kwe h2ew h2e/g'ome. h1i/ns gwhne/nt? dwo/h1 wiHro/h1 gwhne/nt tre/yes-kwe wiHro:/s bhuge/nt. ko/ryos wos h1e/sti? ko/ryos kwelso/y h1e/sti.

kwi/h1 k'o/d mle/wHmi? ke/nkoh2. ke/nkoh2. me/h2trey toy to/d we/wke h2me/lg'os-kwe toy de/deh3ti. kwi/h1 k'o/d mle/wHmi? tr_syo/h2. tr_syo/h2. tr_sye/si? wo/dr_ ph3dhi/. ne tr_syo/h2 pre:/k'sm_. nu k'o/m pe/h3omes.

po/h2imen. kwo/ti toy h2o/weyes h1se/nti? ti/sres h2o/weyes kwe/tesres h2o/weyes pe/nkwe-kwe h2o/weyes. nu moy supno/s h1ludhe/t. h1so/nt moy we/wke. septm_/ moy gwo/wes h1se/nti k'm_to/m-kwe moy ma/rko:s h2e/nHo:y h1se/nti. kwi/d toy h1e/sti? k'wo:/ moy h1e/sti.

kwi/d ghoste/yey we/rh1yesi? k'o/d we/rh1yoh2. pri/He gho/stey gwm_dhi/. ph2we/ni h1e/pi sddhi/ ph3dhi/-kwe. kwi/d gho/stis we/rh1yeti? k'o/d we/rh1yeti. tu/H h1use/wo:m de/h3to:r. nu moy tu/H to/d we/wke. tu/H h1use/wo:m de/h3to:r. to/d h1e/wks.

pri/He moy ke/h2nmn_ gwe/Hye. so/h2wl_ gwe/Hyo:h2. sh2we/ns kwe/kwlos dhg'he/mi upe/r we/rtetor. po:/lHo:s h2e/g'rom ih2e/nti poh2ime:/n-kwe se/ngwheti. to/d moy ke/h2nmn_ k'e/ywom. kwi/s h1i/d te:/tk't? h2e/wh2os moy h1i/d te:/tk't h1i/d-kwe se/ngwhoh2. nu moy tu/H ke/h2nmn_ gwe/Hye.

wl_/kwosyo moy spe/lom we/wke. wl_/kwos h2e/g'rom ne/kwti gwe/mt. h2o/wins wide/t k'wo:/-kwe bhe/wdhet. k'wo:/ h1i/m h1e/pt g'hwe:/r-kwe no/kwtm_ bhe/wgt. to/d spe/lom h1so/nt? so/ k'wo:/ h1idhe/ k'e/yor.

lewbho/m moy we/kwos wr_h1dhi/. so/h2wl_ toy lukye/h1t k'e:/r-kwe toy mudye/h1t. to/d moy keh2ro/m. ne/wom we/kwos wr_h1dhi/. h1no/mn_ toy k'luto/m h1sye/h1t k'le/wos-kwe toy n_/dhgwhitom h1sye/h1t. nu tu/H bhh2dhi/. pe/h2wr_ toy dhe/gwhoyh1t. k'e:/r moy me/wdt.

ke/h2ilos h1sdhi/. kwi/d to/d? to/d we/kwos ne wo/ydh2e. dhugh2tro:/m toy moy suHne/wes ph2te/res. nu moy tu/H g'e/nh1os. gwo:/s wos dino/y h2e/g'ete? h1i/ns h2e/g'onti h1eg'o/h2 h1i/ns ne h2e/g'oh2. nu toy kwi/s h2me/lg'os de/deh3ti? we/kwesh2 toy moy ne/weh2.

kwi/d to/d? to/d ne k'le:/m. so/ wo/g'hos h1e/k'wo:s-kwe h1i/m ne duke/nti. nu kwi/s h1i/m de/wkti? bha/gos? dhe/gwhonts wo/g'hos. ne h1e/wsetor? ne h1e/wsetor. ma/rko:s moy to/d me: k'luwih1e/nt.

te/h2 we/kwesh2 ne wo/ydh2e. ne/kwti nos k'o/ymoy le/wkt pe/h2wr_-kwe ne dhe/gwheti. h2e/wso:s h1e/sti? h2ste/rm_ toy g'hesr_su/ bhe/resi? h2ste/rm_ ne bhe/roh2 h2ne/res to/d swe/h2bhis g'hesr_bhi/s te/tk'n_ti. nu yu/H dhe/h1ses. h2ste/res nos ne/kwti h1idhe/ ph2se/nti.

kwi/d to/d? me/h2te:r moy dweh2ro/y tk'e/yti k'lush1ye/ti-kwe me. h1i/h2m_ g'huHe/yesi? ne g'huHe/yoh2. dhe/h1s h1e/smi h1e/n h1e/sti? dhe/h1s h1e/smi h1e/n ne h1e/sti. swe/sorm_ moy h2e/g'roy g'huHe/yoh2 k'lush1ye/ti-kwe me.

mo/ri dedo/rk'th2e? h1i/d ne dedo/rk'h2e h2e/wh2os moy h1i/d dedo/rk'e. mo/ri me/g'h2 wo/dr_. kwi/d to/d? diwe/s wo/g'hos. ph2te/rm_ dye:/m wide/s? h1i/m ne wido/m. nu me mo/ri we/dhe.

po/tey. to/d we/kwos ne wo/ydh2e. to/ti moy h2ne/res k'o/ymoy tk'iye/nti h1yo/ti h2ste/res h1se/nti. to/ti h2ne/res. kwi/s h1i/ns h2e/leti? kwelso/y h2e/rh3yonti wo/g'ho:s-kwe nos h2me/lg'os we/g'honti. ku/dhe wos gwo/wes h1se/nti? kwelso/y h1se/nti. h2e/n me: tk'iye/h1m_.

wi/k'potey. tu/H h1so/nts? h1eg'o/h2 h1so/nts g'he/sorm_ moy te/th2ge. gwhre/h1yeti? gwhre/h1yeti. h1eg'o/h2 poh2ime:/n h1e:/dmi pi/ph3oh2-kwe. tu/H h1so/nts? sme:/ymi.

wi/k'potey. tu/H deywo/s? sme:/ymi. h1eg'o/h2 ne deywo/s bha/go:s ne m_re/nt me/roh2-kwe. polh1u/h2 wo/ydth2e. ma/rkons ke/h2nmn_h2-kwe wo/ydh2e. tu/H dhe/h1s? h1eg'o/h2 ne dhe/h1s h1eg'o/h2 wiHro/s.

po/h2imen h1idhe/ gwm_sk'e/. kwi/d dhg'hye/s wide/s? me/g'h2m_ h1o/gwhim we/dni h1e/pi wido/m h2e/k'monm_-kwe gwr_h3e/t. to/d h1so/nt? bhoydhe/h2m toy moy de/deh3mi. bhre/h2te:r toy h2e/n bhe/h2ti. le/ghet supno/s-kwe h1i/m kapye/t.

kwi/d te/bhye bhebhe/ydhoh2? wik'po/tim pre/k'sdhi ne dhrughye/ti. kwi/d toy wik'po/teyey bhebhe/ydhoh2? nu moy h2e/wh2om pre/k'sdhi. dhg'he/mi Hn_dhe/r k'e/yor bha/gons pre/k'sdhi. sme:/ymi bhebho/ydhh2e-kwe toy.

ke/h2re. tu/H su/pniyom? su/pniyom h2me/lg'os ne pi/ph3eti spe/k'ye pi/ph3oh2. nu ku/dhe h1e/smi? k'o/ymoy moy h1e/si. h1eg'o/h2 toy su/pniyom? ne wo/ydh2e. nu h1e/domes su/pniyom ne ke/nketi.

po/tey ke/h2ilos h1sdhi/. k'o/smi dino/y h1idhe/ h2we/rst. h2we/rst toy? ne h2we/rst so/h2wl_ le/wkt h2e/g'ros-kwe h2susye/ti. h1idhe/ h2wr_sye/h1t. h2wr_sye/h1t toy. tu/H moy k'e/ywos.

spe/lom moy we/wke sme/yoh2-kwe. h2e/wh2os moy h1e/k'wom ne/kwti h1e/pt. nu h1i/m h2use/si kwe/k't so/ gwo:/ws h1e/st. h2e/nHos-kwe moy sme:/yt. to/d moy spe/lom lewbho/m. nu moy tu/H spe/lom we/wke.

h1e/k'wo:s toy HeHk'e/wes. to/y moy mo/wdos. k'o/s po:/lHos dhg'hye/s g'n_h1ye/to. h2uksye/h1t. tu/H h1sume/ne:s. h1i/m Hmolk'e/ye. g'he/sorm_ moy le/yg'hti.

tu/H wl_/kwos. dh2pno/m gwr_h3e/s. tu/H h2o/wis ne kwid h1e:/ds tre/msi-kwe. h1ddhi/ wl_/kwos-we: te gwr_h3e:/ti. wl_/kwoybhos ne dedwo/yh2e. nu sddhi/ pe/h3tlo:d-kwe moy ph3dhi/.

h1eg'o/h2 lug'ro/s. kwi/d pri/He tu/H lug'ro/s? me/h2te:r moy pe/ruti me/rt. k'e/h2dos toy me/g'h2. h1i/h2 toy k'e/rdi h1e/sti h1i/h2m_-kwe memo/nth2e. h1e/syeh2s h1no/mn_ gwr_nHdhi/ h1e/syeh2s-kwe k'le/wos gwi/h3we:ti. h1i/d gwr_ne/Hoh2. tu/H moy lewbho/s.

nu h1e/ymi ke/h2ilos h1sdhi/. dye:/ws te ph2sye/h1t. h2ew gwm_dhi/ ke/h2nmn_h2-kwe toy bhe/re. h2ew gwm_yo:/h2 tu/H moy k'e/ywos. nu gwh2dhi/ memo/nh2e te.

po/tey. suHnu/s moy g'n_h1ye/to. kwo/d h1no/mn_ h1e/smey dhe/h1oh2? k'o/d me/g'h2s mo/wdos. h1e/smey h1no/mn_ h1suk'le/wesm_ dhh1dhi/. h1yo/d dhugh2te:/r g'n_h1ye:/tor? nu h1e/syeh2ey h1no/mn_ h1suh1e/k'weh2m dhh1dhi/. kwi/d toy h1no/mn_?

ne/wos moy k'wo:/ h1e/sti k'luto/m moy h1no/mn_ dh3dhi/. so/ HeHk'u/s? so/ HeHk'u/s h2me/lg'os-kwe ste/h2yeti. nu h1e/smey h1no/mn_ HeHk'uwl_/kwom dhh1dhi/. wl_/kwo:s h1e/syo h1ne/mn_s tre/sonti. to/d moy h1no/mn_ keh2ro/m. h1i/m moy we/dhe h1i/m we/ydsoh2.

bhre/h2te:r moy ghre/meti ne-kwe moy h2roh1dhe/yeti. kwi/d ghre/meti? h1e/syo h1e/k'wom sle/h2gwm_ bhe/wgt-kwe. h1i/m gwh2dhi/ h1e/smey-kwe swo/m po:/lHom dh3dhi/. po:/lHos moy ne h1e/sti. nu h1e/k'wom h1i/h1 k'o/m h2isk'e/.

pri/He ne/kwts bhebho/yh2h2e. kwi/d bhebho/yh2th2e? no/kwts h2stro:/m me/h2te:r. g'hwe:/res h1o/gwheyes-kwe ne/kwti gwm_yo/nti. k'wo:/ bhe/wdheti pe/h2wr_-kwe dhe/gwheti h2le/ksetes te. no/kwts h2use/si h2epo/ ye/h2ti. nu ph2we/ni h1e/pi le/ghoh2. l_ghdhi/ supno/s-kwe toy gwm_ye/h1t.

mo/ri h1e/ymi kwi/h1 gwe/h2oh2? mo/ri dweh2ro/m HeHk'u/m h1e/k'wom sl_h2gwdhi/. pe/h3tlom bhe/re ph2te/rm_-kwe dye:/m g'huHe/ye. ste/yghoh2. nu moy po:/lHom h1a/yso so/ HeHk'u/s. tu/H moy k'e/ywos. h1i/m moy h2ew we/dhe wo/dr_-kwe moy mre/ys bhe/re.
